Текст представлен в латинской транслитерации IAST. Источник: sanskrit-kosha/kosha. Оцифровка: Dr. Dhaval Patel и Shree Ramkrishna Knowledge Foundation (SRKKF). Лицензия GNU GPL v3.0. # Medinī-kośa

## Opening

## Opening / 1 \[unit 1\]

\|\| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ \|\|

atha nānārthaśabdakośo nāma

medinīkośaḥ \|

vṛṣāṅkāya namastasmai yasya maulivilambinī \|

jaṭāveṣṭanajāṃ śeābhāṃ vibhāvayati jāhnavī \|\| 1 \|\|

## Opening / 2 \[unit 2\]

pātu(1) vo madakālimnā dhavalimnā radasya ca \|

gaṅgāyamunyoḥ saṅgaṃ vahanniva gajānanaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## Opening / 3 \[unit 3\]

pūrvācāryakṛtīrvīkṣya śabdaśāstraṃ nirūpya ca \|

nānārthaśabdkośo'yaṃ liṅgabhedena kathyate \|\| 3 \|\|

## Opening / 4 \[unit 4\]

prāyaśo rūpabhedena viśeṣaṇavaśāt kvacit \|

strīpunnapuṃsakaṃ jñeyaṃ viśeṣokteśca kutracit \|\| 4 \|\|

## Opening / 5 \[unit 5\]

triliṅgyāṃ triṣviti padaṃ mithune tu dvayoriti \|

niṣiddhaliṅgaṃ śeṣārthaṃ tvantāthādi na pūrvabhāk \|\| 5 \|\|

## Opening / 6 \[unit 6\]

rūpādvyaktaṃ liṅgamuktaṃ lipibhrānticchide kvacit \|

viśeṣyanighne'nukte'pi vijñeyā vācyaliṅgatā \|\| 6 \|\|

## Opening / 7 \[unit 7\]

guṇe śuklādikaṭvādyāḥ puṃsi syustadvati triṣu \|

tīkṣṇādyāśca guṇe klībe guṇiliṅgāstu tadvati \|\| 7 \|\|

## Opening / 8 \[unit 8\]

klībapuṃsorapi strītvaṃ kvāpyalpatvavivakṣayā \|

jātivācakabdānāmapi tat strīvivakṣayā \|\| 8 \|\|

## Opening / 9 \[unit 9\]

udbhidaḥ prasave klībe harītakyādayaḥ striyām \|

(1) idaṃ padyaṃ ka. pustake nāsti \|

puṣpe jātīprabhṛtayaḥ svaliṅgā vrīhayaḥ phale \|\| 9 \|\|

## Opening / 10 \[unit 10\]

prāṅ nānārthānna (1) talliṅgaṃ dvayordvandvena caikatā \|

śabdāvṛttirna liṅgaikye saptamī na viśeṣaṇe \|\| 10 \|\|

## Opening / 11 \[unit 11\]

klībe napuṃsake puṃsi striyāṃ yoṣiti ca dvayoḥ \|

triṣu cetyādi yadrūpaṃ talliṅgasyaiva vācakam \|\| 11 \|\|

## Opening / 12 \[unit 12\]

nānārthaḥ prathamānto'tra sarvatrādau pradarśitaḥ \|

(2)saptamyanto'bhidheyeṣu vartamāno viniścitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## Opening / 13 \[unit 13\]

ekadvitricatuḥpañcaṣaḍvarṇānukramāt kṛtaḥ \|

svarakādyādikādyāntavargairnānārthasaṅgrahaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## Opening / 14 \[unit 14\]

nānārthakośapustakabhārārjanaduḥkhahānaye kṛtinaḥ \|

medinikarakṛtakośo viśuddhaliṅgo'bhilikhyatāmekaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaikam

## kāntavargaḥ / kaikam / 15 \[unit 15\]

kaikam

ko brahmaṇi samīrātmayamadakṣeṣu bhāskare \|

kāmagranthau cakriṇi ca (3)patatriṇi ca pārthive \|\| 15 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaikam / fragment 16

mayūre'gnau (4)na puṃsi syāt sukhaśīrṣajaleṣu kam \|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 16 \[unit 17\]

kadvikam

arko'rkaparṇe sphaṭike ravau tāmre divaspatau \|\| 16 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 17 \[unit 18\]

aṅko rūpakabhedāṅgacihnarekhājibhūṣaṇe \|

rūpakāṃśā'ntikotsaṅgasthāne'kaṃ pāpaduḥkhayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 18 \[unit 19\]

ekaṃ saṃkhyāntare śreṣṭhe kevaletarayostriṣu \|

karkaḥ karke'nile vahnau śuklāśve darpaṇe ghaṭe \|\| 18 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 19 \[unit 20\]

kaṅkaśchadmadvije khyāto lohapṛṣṭhakṛtāntayoḥ \|

kalko'strī ghṛtatailādiśeṣe dambhe bibhītake \|\| 19 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 20 \[unit 21\]

(5)viṭaviṭṭayośca pāpe ca triṣu pāpāśaye punaḥ \|

kākaḥ syādvāyase vṛkṣaprabhede pīṭhasarpiṇi \|\| 20 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 21 \[unit 22\]

(1) nānārtho'nu-kha. pu. \| ; (2) saptamyantābhidheyeṣu-ka. pu. \| ; (3) patatripārthive tathā-ka. \| ; (4) ca-kha. \| ; (5) kiṭṭa-kha. \|

śiro'vakṣālane mānaprabhedadvīpabhedayoḥ \|

kākā syāt kākanāsāyāṃ kākolīkākajaṅghayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 22 \[unit 23\]

raktikāyāṃ malapvāṃ ca kākamācyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

kākaṃ suratabandhe syāt kākānāmapi saṃhatau \|\| 22 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 23 \[unit 24\]

kiṣkurdvayeārvitastau ca prakoṣṭhe'pi kare'pi ca \|

kokaścakre vṛke jyeṣṭhyāṃ kharjūrīdrumadardure \|\| 23 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 24 \[unit 25\]

cheko gṛhāśritamṛgapakṣiṇornāgare triṣu \|

ṭaṅko nīlakapitthe ca khanitre ṭaṅkane'striyām \|\| 24 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 25 \[unit 26\]

jaṅghāyāṃ strī pumān kope kośāsigrāvadāraṇe \|

tarkaḥ kāṅkṣāvitarkohahetuśāstreṣu kathyate \|\| 25 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 26 \[unit 27\]

trikā kūpasya nemau syāt kaṃ pṛṣṭhādhare traye \|

tokaṃ putre sutāyāṃ ca dvikaḥ syāt kākakokayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 27 \[unit 28\]

nyaṅkurmunau mṛge puṃsi nākastu tridive'mbare \|

nākurmunyantare pṛthvīdharavalmīkayeāḥ pumān \|\| 27 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 28 \[unit 29\]

niṣkamastrī sāṣṭahemaśataṃ dīnārakarṣayoḥ \|

vakṣeā'laṅkaraṇe hemapātre hemapale'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 29 \[unit 30\]

paṅko'strī kardame pāpe pākaḥ pariṇatau śiśau \|

keśasya jarasā śauklye sthālyādau pacane'pi ca \|\| 29 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 30 \[unit 31\]

bakastu bakapuṣpe syāt kahve śrīde ca rakṣasi \|

bhūkaṃ chidre ca kāle ca bheko maṇḍūkameghayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 31 \[unit 32\]

muṣko mokṣakavṛkṣe syāt (1)saṃhate vṛṣaṇe'pi ca \|

mūkastvavāci nā daitye raṅkaḥ (2)kṛpaṇamandayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 32 \[unit 33\]

rākā nadyantare kacchvāṃ navajātarajaḥstriyām \|

sampūrṇendutithau rekaḥ śaṅkānīcavirecane \|\| 32 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 33 \[unit 34\]

rokastu krayabhiddīptyo rokaṃ nāvi cale bile \|

laṅkā rakṣaḥpurīśākhāśākinīkulaṭālu ca \|\| 33 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 34 \[unit 35\]

(1) saṅghāte-kha. \| ; (2) rekaḥ-ka. \|

loko jane'pi bhuvane valkaṃ valkalaśalkayoḥ \|

vaṅkaḥ paryāṇabhāge nā nadīpātre ca bhaṅgure \|\| 34 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 35 \[unit 36\]

śalkaṃ tu śakale valke śako jātyantare nṛpe \|

śaṅkuḥ saṃkhyā'strayādobhitkīleśakaluṣeṣu nā \|\| 35 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 36 \[unit 37\]

śaṅkā trāse vitarke ca śāko dvīpāntare'pi ca \|

śaktau drumaviśeṣe ca pumān haritake'striyām \|\| 36 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 37 \[unit 38\]

śuko vyāsasute kīre rāvaṇasya ca mantriṇi \|

śirīṣapādape puṃsi granthiparṇe napuṃsakam \|\| 37 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 38 \[unit 39\]

śulkaṃ ghaṭṭādideye syādvarārthagrahe'striyām \|

śūko'strī śuṅgadalayoḥ ślokaḥ padye yaśasyapi \|\| 38 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kadvikam / 39 \[unit 40\]

śaukaṃ śukagaṇe strīṇāṃ karaṇe sūka ityapi(1) \|

bāṇavātotpale (2)stokastriṣvalpe cātake pumān \|\| 39 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 40 \[unit 41\]

katrikam

aśokastriṣu niḥśoke puṃsi kaṅkellipādape \|

striyāṃ tu kaṭurohiṇyāṃ pārade syānnapuṃsakam \|\| 40 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 41 \[unit 42\]

alakā kuberapuryāmastriyāṃ cūrṇakuntale \|

abhīkaḥ kāmuke krūre nirbhaye triṣu nā kavau \|\| 41 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 42 \[unit 43\]

anīko'strī raṇe sainye'pyaṇuko nipuṇālpayoḥ \|

alīkamapriye'pi syāddivyasatye napuṃsakam \|\| 42 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 43 \[unit 44\]

anūkaṃ tu kule śīle puṃsi syād gatajanmani \|

aṃśukaṃ ślakṣṇavastre syādvastramātrottarīyayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 44 \[unit 45\]

antikaṃ nikaṭe vācyaliṅgaṃ strī śātalauṣadhau \|

cullyāṃ jyeṣṭhabhaginyāṃ ca nāṭyoktyāṃ kathyate'ntikā \|\| 44 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 45 \[unit 46\]

alarko dhavalārke syādyogonmāditakukkure \|

ambikā pārvatīmātrordhṛtarāṣṭrasya mātari \|\| 45 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 46 \[unit 47\]

andhikā dyūtabhede ca rajanyāmapi yoṣiti \|

(1) ityayam-kha. \| ; ( 2 ) stokaṃ stri0-kha. \|

amlikā tintiḍīkāmlodgāracāṅgerikāsu ca \|\| 46 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 47 \[unit 48\]

arbhakaḥ kathito bāle mūrkhe'pi ca kṛśe'pi ca \|

ānakaḥ paṭahe bheryāṃ mṛdaṅge dhvanadambude \|\| 47 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 48 \[unit 49\]

āḍhakī tu tuvaryāṃ strī parimāṇāntare triṣu \|

ālokastu pumān dyote darśane bandibhāṣaṇe \|\| 48 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 49 \[unit 50\]

āhnikaṃ dinanirvartyaṃ triliṅgamatha na dvayoḥ \|

nityakriyābhojanayorvṛnde prakaraṇasya ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 50 \[unit 51\]

ātaṅko rogasantāpaśaṅkāsu murajadhvanau \|

ikṣvākuḥ kuṭutumbyāṃ strī sūryavaṃśanṛpe pumān \|\| 50 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 51 \[unit 52\]

ulūkaḥ puṃsi kākārāvindre bhāratayodhini \|

udarka (1)eṣyatkālīnaphale madanakaṇṭake \|\| 51 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 52 \[unit 53\]

uṣmakastu nidāghe syādāture kṣiprakāriṇi \|

uṣṭrikā mṛttikābhāṇḍabhede'pi karabhastriyām \|\| 52 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 53 \[unit 54\]

ūrmikā cāṅgulīye syādvastrabhaṅgataraṅgayoḥ \|

kanakaṃ hemni puṃsi syāt kiṃśuke nāgakesare \|\| 53 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 54 \[unit 55\]

dhatūre kāñcanāre ca kālīye campake'pi ca \|

karakastu pumān pakṣiviśeṣe dāḍime'pi ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 55 \[unit 56\]

(2)dvayormeghopale na strī karaṅke ca kamaṇḍalau \|

kramukastu pumān bhadramustake brahmadāruṇi \|\| 55 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 56 \[unit 57\]

phale kārpāsikāyāśca paṭṭikālodhrapūgayoḥ \|

kaṭako'strī nitambe'drerdantināṃ dantamaṇḍane \|\| 56 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 57 \[unit 58\]

sāmudralavaṇe rājadhānīvalayayorapi \|

kaṭukā kaṭurohiṇyāṃ striyāṃ vyoṣe napuṃsakam \|\| 57 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 58 \[unit 59\]

kaṇṭako na striyāṃ kṣudraśatrau matsyādikīkase \|

naiyāyikādidoṣoktau syādromāñcadrumāṅgayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 59 \[unit 60\]

karaṅko mastake'śasyanālikeraphalāsthani \|

(1) eṣyatkāle tatphale-kha. \| ; (2) dvayorvarṣopale-kha. \|

kalaṅko'ṅke'pavāde ca kālāyasamale'pi ca \|\| 59 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 60 \[unit 61\]

karṇikā karihastāgre karamadhyāṅgulāvapi \|

kramukādicchaṭāṃśe'bjavarāṭe karṇabhūṣaṇe \|\| 60 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 61 \[unit 62\]

kaṇikā kathyate'tyantasūkṣmavastvagnimanthayoḥ \|

kacākustu durādharṣe duḥśīle ca (1)bileśaye \|\| 61 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 62 \[unit 63\]

kañcuko vārabāṇe syānnirmoke karabhe'pi ca \|

vardhāpakagṛhītāṅgasthitavatastre ca colake \|\| 62 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 63 \[unit 64\]

kañcukyoṣadhibhede'tha kārikā naṭayoṣiti \|

kṛtau vivaraṇaśloke śilpayātanayorapi \|\| 63 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 64 \[unit 65\]

napuṃsakaṃ tu karmādau kārakaṃ kartari triṣu \|

kārmukaḥ kamane'śokapādape cātimuktake \|\| 64 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 65 \[unit 66\]

kārmukaṃ dhanuṣi syānnā veṇī karmakṣame'nyavat \|

kāvṛkaḥ kṛkavākau syāt pīnamastakakokayoḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 66 \[unit 67\]

kṣārakaḥ pakṣimatsyādipiṭake jālake'pi ca \|

kālikā caṇḍikābhede (2)kārṣṇyavṛścikapatrayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 67 \[unit 68\]

kramadeyavastumūlye dhūsarīnavameghayoḥ \|

paṭolaśākhāromālīmāṃsīkākīśivāsu ca \|\| 67 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 68 \[unit 69\]

meghāvalau ca kimpāko mahākālaphalājñayoḥ \|

kīcako datyabhidvātāhatasasvanavaṃśayoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 69 \[unit 70\]

kīṭakaḥ kṛmijātau nā niṣṭhure punaranyavat \|

kulakaṃ tu paṭole syāt sambaddhaślokasaṃhatau \|\| 69 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 70 \[unit 71\]

puṃsi valmīkakākendukulaśreṣṭheṣu kathyate \|

kṣullakastriṣu nīce'lpe kuśiko munisarjayoḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 71 \[unit 72\]

kuṣākuḥ kapivahnyarke nā parottāpini triṣu \|

kuliko nāgabhede syād drubhede kulasattame \|\| 71 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 72 \[unit 73\]

kṣurakaḥ kākilākṣe syād gokṣure tilakadrume \|

(1) nā-kha. \| ; (2) kārtsnya -ka. \|

kūpako guṇavṛkṣe syāttailapātre kukundare \|\| 72 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 73 \[unit 74\]

udapāne'cyu(1)tāyāṃ tu kūpikā'mbhogatopale \|

kūlakaṃ na striyāṃ stūpe puṃsi syāt kṛmiparvate \|\| 73 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 74 \[unit 75\]

kūrcikā sūcikāyāṃ ca tūlikāyāṃ ca kuḍmale \|

(2)kapāṭodbhedane kṣīravikṛtāvapi yoṣiti \|\| 74 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 75 \[unit 76\]

kṛṣakaḥ puṃsi phāle syāt karṣake tvabhidheyavat \|

kārako'strī kuḍmale syāt kakkolakamṛṇālayoḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 76 \[unit 77\]

kautukaṃ tvabhilāṣe syādutsave narmaharṣayoḥ \|

tathā paramparāyātamaṅgale ca kutūhale \|\| 76 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 77 \[unit 78\]

vivāhasūtragītādibhogayorapi na dvayoḥ \|

kauśiko nakule vyālagrāhe gugguluśukrayoḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 78 \[unit 79\]

koṣajñolūkayośca syādviśvāmitramunāvapi \|

kauṣikī caṇḍikāyāṃ ca nadībhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 78 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 79 \[unit 80\]

khanako nondurau sandhicaure triṣvavadārake \|

khaḍgiko mahiṣīkṣīre phenaśaunikayorapi \|\| 79 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 80 \[unit 81\]

syāt kholakastu pāke śirastravalmīkapūgakośeṣu \|

gaṇikā yūthīveśyebhītarkārīṣu nā tu daivajñe \|\| 80 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 81 \[unit 82\]

granthikaṃ pippalīmule guggulugranthiparṇayoḥ \|

karīre puṃsi daivajñe sahadevākhyapāṇḍave \|\| 81 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 82 \[unit 83\]

gaṇḍakaḥ puṃsi khaḍge syāt saṃkhyāvidyāprabhedayoḥ \|

avacchede'ntarāye ca gaṇḍakī saridantare \|\| 82 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 83 \[unit 84\]

grāhako ghātivihage vyā(3)dhānāṃ ca grahītari \|

gāndhiko lekhake'pi syāt sugandhivyavahāriṇi \|\| 83 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 84 \[unit 85\]

guṇḍako maline dhūlau kaloktisnehapātrayoḥ \|

gṛhyako nighnake cheke gairikaṃ dhāturukmayoḥ \|\| 84 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 85 \[unit 86\]

(1) citāyāṃ ca-kha. \| ; (2) kavāṭakuṭake-kha., kapāṭāṅkuṭṭake-ka. \| ; (3) vyālānāṃ-kha., dhānyānāṃ ka. \|

golako vidhavāputre jārāt syānmaṇike guḍe \|

goraṅkuḥ syāt pumān pakṣibhede lagnakabandinoḥ \|\| 85 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 86 \[unit 87\]

caṣako'sstrī surāpātre madhumadyaprabhedayoḥ \|

calukaḥ prasṛtau bhāṇḍabhede culukavat pumān \|\| 86 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 87 \[unit 88\]

catuṣkī maśakaharyāṃ puṣkariṇyantare'pi ca \|

cārakaḥ pālake'śvādeḥ syāt sañcārakabandhayoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 88 \[unit 89\]

citrakaṃ tilake nā tu vyāghrabhiccañcupāṭhiṣu \|

cumbakaścumbanapare dhūrtāyaskāntayorapi \|\| 88 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 89 \[unit 90\]

bahugranthaikadeśajñe ghaṭasyorddhvāvalambane \|

cullakī śiśumāre'pi kuṇḍībhede kulāntare \|\| 89 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 90 \[unit 91\]

cūlikā nāṭakasyāṅge karṇamūle ca hastinām \|

cūtakaḥ kūpake'pyāmre janakaḥ pitṛbhūbhujoḥ \|\| 90 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 91 \[unit 92\]

jambūkaḥ pherave nīce paścimāśāpatāvapi \|

jatukā jinapatrāyāṃ jatukaṃ hiṅgulākṣayoḥ \|\| 91 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 92 \[unit 93\]

jāhako ghoṅghamārjārakhaṭvākāruṇḍikāsu ca \|

jālakaṃ korake dambhe kulāyānāyayorapi \|\| 92 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 93 \[unit 94\]

na puṃsi mocakaphale striyāṃ tu vasanāntare \|

girisāre jalaukāyāmapi syādvidhavāstriyām \|\| 93 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 94 \[unit 95\]

bhaṭānāmaśmaracitāṅgarakṣiṇyāṃ ca jālikā \|

jāliko vācyavad grāmajālijālopajīvinoḥ \|\| 94 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 95 \[unit 96\]

jīvakaḥ prāṇake pītaśāla(1)kṛpaṇayorapi \|

kūrcaśīrṣe'pi puṃsi syādājīve jīvikā matā \|\| 95 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 96 \[unit 97\]

triṣu sevini vṛddhyāśijīvirāhituṇḍike \|

(2)jhallikodvartanapaṭe striyāṃ dyote ca jhillīkā \|\| 96 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 97 \[unit 98\]

(3)ātapasya rucau jhiṇṭyāṃ ṭuṇṭukaḥ śoṇakālpayoḥ \|

(1) kṣapaṇayo-kha. \| ; (2) jhallikodvartanamale paṭe dīptau ca jhillikā-kha. \| ; (3) udvartanāṃśuke jhiṇṭyāṃ-kha. \|

ḍimbikā jalabimbe syāt śo(1)ṇake kāmukastriyām \|\| 97 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 98 \[unit 99\]

taṇḍakaḥ khañjane phene samāsaprāyavāci ca \|

gṛhadārutaruskandhamāyābahulakeṣvapi \|\| 98 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 99 \[unit 100\]

takṣakastu pumān nāgarājabhede ca varddhakau \|

tārako daityabhitkarṇadhārayosrna dvayordṛśi \|\| 99 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 100 \[unit 101\]

kanīnikāyāmṛkṣe ca na pumāṃstrātari triṣu \|

tilako drumarogāśvabhedeṣu tilakālake \|\| 100 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 101 \[unit 102\]

klībaṃ sauvarcalaklemnorna striyāṃ tu viśeṣake \|

triśaṅkurnā rājabhede śalabhe vṛṣadaṃśake \|\| 101 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 102 \[unit 103\]

turuṣkaḥ silhake mlecchajātau deśāntare'pi ca \|

tūlikā kurcikāyāṃ ca śayyopakaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 102 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 103 \[unit 104\]

darśakaḥ syāt pratīhāre darśayitṛpravīṇayoḥ \|

dārako bālake'pi syād bhedake tvabhidheyavat \|\| 103 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 104 \[unit 105\]

drāvako grāvabhede syādvidagdhe moṣake'pi ca \|

dīpakaṃ vāgalaṅkāre vācyavaddīptikārake \|\| 104 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 105 \[unit 106\]

dīpyakaṃ cājamodāyāṃ yavānībarhicūḍayoḥ \|

ducchako gandhakuṭyāṃ syādvihārādyavakāśake \|\| 105 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 106 \[unit 107\]

dūṣikā tūlikāyāṃ ca male syāllocanasya ca \|

dhanikā sādhunāryāṃ nā dhanyāke triṣu sādhudhaninośca \|\| 106 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 107 \[unit 108\]

syāddhenukā kariṇyāṃ dhenāvapi nā tu dānavaviśeṣe \|

dhenukaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ dhenūnāmapi saṃhatau \|\| 107 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 108 \[unit 109\]

nartakaḥ kelake(2) poṭagalacāraṇayornaṭe \|

nartakī lāsikāyāṃ ca kareṇvāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 108 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 109 \[unit 110\]

nagnikā'pi kumāryāṃ syāt pumān kṣapaṇabandinoḥ \|

narakaḥ puṃsi nirayadevārātiprabhedayoḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 110 \[unit 111\]

nandako harikhaḍge ca harṣake kulapālake \|

(1) moṇake-ka. kha. \| ; (2) kevale-ka. \|

nālīkaḥ śaraśalyāṅgeṣvabjakhaṇḍe napuṃsakam \|\| 110 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 111 \[unit 112\]

nāyako netari śreṣṭhe hāramadhyamaṇāvapi \|

nirmoko mocane vyomni sannāhe sarpakañcuke \|\| 111 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 112 \[unit 113\]

nīlikā nīlinīkṣudrarogaśephālikāsu ca \|

parākastu vrate khaḍge prasekaḥ secane cyutau \|\| 112 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 113 \[unit 114\]

pratīko'vayave'pi syāt pratikūlavilomayoḥ \|

padmakaṃ syāt padmakāṣṭhabindujālakayorapi \|\| 113 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 114 \[unit 115\]

pakṣakastu pumān pārśvadvāre ca pārśvamātrake \|

palyaṅko mañcaparyaṅkavṛṣīparyastikāsu ca \|\| 114 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 115 \[unit 116\]

patākā vaijayantyāṃ ca saubhāgyanāṭakāṅgayoḥ \|

pātukaḥ patayālau syāt prapātajalahastinoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 116 \[unit 117\]

prāṇakaḥ sattvajātīye jīvakadrumacolayoḥ \|

pāṭakaḥ syānmahākiṣkau kaṭakāntaravādyayoḥ \|\| 116 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 117 \[unit 118\]

akṣādicālane mūladravyāpacayarodhasoḥ \|

pālaṅkaḥ śallakīśākabhedayoḥ prājipakṣiṇi \|\| 117 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 118 \[unit 119\]

pāvako'gnau sadācāre vahnimanthe ca citrake \|

bhallātake viḍaṅge'tha priyakaḥ pītaśālake \|\| 118 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 119 \[unit 120\]

nīpe citramṛge cālau priyaṅgau kuṅkume'pi ca \|

piṇyāko'strī tilakalke hiṅaguvālhīkasilhake \|\| 119 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 120 \[unit 121\]

pināko'strī rudracāpe pāṃśuvarṣatriśūlayoḥ \|

piṭakastriṣu visphoṭe mañjūṣāyāṃ punaḥ pumān \|\| 120 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 121 \[unit 122\]

piṣṭako ghṛtapūpādau netrarogāntare'pi ca \|

pulakaḥ kṛmiprabhede prastarabhede ca maṇidoṣe \|\| 121 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 122 \[unit 123\]

romāñce haritāle gajānnapiṇḍe ca gandharve \|

pulākastucchadhānye syāt saṅkṣepe bhaktasikthake \|\| 122 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 123 \[unit 124\]

puṣpakaṃ rītipuṣpe ca vimāne dhanadasya ca \|

netraroge tathā ratnakaṅkaṇe ca rasāñjane \|\| 123 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 124 \[unit 125\]

lohakāṃsye mṛdaṅgāraśakaṭyāṃ ca napuṃsakam \|

syāt putrikā puttalikāduhitroryāvatūlake \|\| 124 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 125 \[unit 126\]

nā putre śarabhe dhūrte śailavṛkṣaprabhedayoḥ \|

pūrṇakaḥ svarṇacūḍe syānnāsācchinyāṃ tu pūrṇikā \|\| 125 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 126 \[unit 127\]

pṛdākurvṛścike vyāghre sarpacitrakayoḥ pumān \|

pṛthukaḥ puṃsi cipiṭe śiśau syādabhidheyavat \|\| 126 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 127 \[unit 128\]

pecako gajalāṅgulamūlopānte ca kauśike \|

(1)peṭakaṃ pustakādīnāṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ kadambake \|\| 127 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 128 \[unit 129\]

valmīko rogabhede ca nākau ca punnapuṃsakam \|

bandhūkaṃ bandhujīve syād bandhūkaḥ pītaśālake \|\| 128 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 129 \[unit 130\]

bandhakaḥ syādvinimaye puṃścalyāṃ syācca bandhakī \|

bahukaḥ karkaṭe cārke dātyūhe jalakhātake \|\| 129 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 130 \[unit 131\]

varākaḥ śaṅkare puṃsi śocanīye'bhidheyavat \|

bālakastu śiśāvajñe vāladhau hayahastinoḥ \|\| 130 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 131 \[unit 132\]

aṅgulīyakahrīberavalaye puṃsi bālikā \|

bālāyāṃ bālukāpatrakāhalākarṇabhūṣaṇe \|\| 131 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 132 \[unit 133\]

vārako'śvagatau puṃsi vācyavat syānniṣedhake \|

bālukā sikatāsu syād bālukaṃ tvelabāluke(2) \|\| 132 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 133 \[unit 134\]

bharmakaṃ rogabhede syādviḍaṅgakaladhautayoḥ \|

bhrāmako jambuke dhūrte sūryāvarttāśmabhedayoḥ \|\| 133 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 134 \[unit 135\]

bhālāṅkaḥ karapatre syācchākabhede ca rohite \|

mahālakṣaṇasampannapuruṣe kacchape hare \|\| 134 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 135 \[unit 136\]

bhūmikā racanāyāṃ syādveśāntaraparigrahe \|

bhūtīkamapi bhūnimbe dīpyakarpūrake tṛṇe \|\| 135 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 136 \[unit 137\]

madhukā madhuparṇyāṃ syā(3)nmadhukaṃ klītake khage \|

velyantare nā maśako rogakīṭaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 136 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 137 \[unit 138\]

(1) peṭakaḥ–ka. \| ; (2) bālukāstvelavāluke - kha. \| ; (3) strī - kha. \|

maṇḍūkaḥ śoṇake munyantare syād gūḍhavarcasi \|

maṇḍūkaparṇyāṃ maṇḍūkī malliko haṃsabhidyapi \|\| 137 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 138 \[unit 139\]

māllikā tṛṇaśūnye'pi mīnamṛtpātrabhedayoḥ \|

māmakaṃ syānmadīyārthe triṣu puṃsi tu mātule \|\| 138 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 139 \[unit 140\]

mātṛkā dhātṛkāmātrordevībhidvarṇamālayoḥ \|

mālikā saptalāputrīgrīvālaṅkaraṇeṣu ca \|\| 139 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 140 \[unit 141\]

puṣpamālye nadībhede pakṣibhede tu mālikaḥ \|

mecakastu mayūrasya candrake śyāmale pumān \|\| 140 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 141 \[unit 142\]

tadyukte vācyavat klībaṃ srotoñjanāndhakārayoḥ \|

mocakaḥ kadalīśigrunirmocakavirāgiṣu \|\| 141 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 142 \[unit 143\]

modakaḥ khādyabhede'strī harṣuke punaranyavat \|

yamakaṃ yamaje śabdālaṅkāre puṃsi saṃyame \|\| 142 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 143 \[unit 144\]

yājakastu gaje rājño yājñike'pyatha yājñikaḥ \|

yājake ca kuśe cātha yutakaṃ saṃśaye yuge \|\| 143 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 144 \[unit 145\]

nārīvastrāñcale yukte calanāgre'pi yautake \|

yūthikā mlānake puṣpaviśeṣe'pi ca yoṣiti \|\| 144 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 145 \[unit 146\]

rasikā strī rasālekṣurasayoḥ sarase triṣu \|

raktako'mlānabandhūkaraktavastrānurāgiṣu \|\| 145 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 146 \[unit 147\]

rājikā'pi ca kedāre rājasarṣaparekhayoḥ \|

rātrakaṃ pañcarātre nā veśyāveśmābdavāsini \|\| 146 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 147 \[unit 148\]

rucako bījapūre ca niṣke dantakapotayoḥ \|

na dvayoḥ sarjikākṣāre'pyaśvābharaṇamālyayoḥ \|\| 147 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 148 \[unit 149\]

sauvarcale'pi māṅgalyadravye cāpyutkaṭe'pi ca \|

ruṇḍikā dvārapiṇḍyāṃ ca dūtikāyāṃ raṇakṣitau \|\| 148 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 149 \[unit 150\]

rūpakaṃ nāṭake mūrtte kāvyālaṅkaraṇe'pi ca \|

reṇukā'pi hareṇau ca jāmadagnyasya mātari \|\| 149 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 150 \[unit 151\]

rohakaḥ pretabhede nā roḍhari triṣu viśrutaḥ(1) \|

(1) idamardhaṃ padyaṃ nāsti - kha0 \|

lampāko lampaṭe deśe lāsako lāsyakāriṇi \|\| 150 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 151 \[unit 152\]

mayūre lasake cātha lūnako bhedite paśau \|

locako māṃsapiṇḍe'kṣitārakāyāṃ ca kajjale \|\| 151 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 152 \[unit 153\]

lalāṭābharaṇe strīṇāṃ kadalīnīlavastrayoḥ \|

nirbuddhau karṇapūre ca maurvyāṃ bhūślathacarmaṇi \|\| 152 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 153 \[unit 154\]

vasukaṃ raumake puṃsi śivamallyarkaparṇayoḥ \|

varṇakaścāraṇe'strī tu candane ca vilepane \|\| 153 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 154 \[unit 155\]

dvayornīlyādiṣu strī syādutkarṣe kathanasya ca \|

vartakastu khure'śvasya vihage vartakī dvayoḥ \|\| 154 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 155 \[unit 156\]

vañcakastu khale dhūrte gṛhababhrau ca jambuke \|

vyalīkamapriyākāryavailakṣyeṣvapi pīḍane \|\| 155 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 156 \[unit 157\]

nā nāgare'tha bālhīkaṃ bālhikaṃ dhīrahiṅgunoḥ \|

dvāvetau puṃsi deśasya prabhede turagāntare \|\| 156 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 157 \[unit 158\]

vārddhakaṃ vṛddhasaṅghāte vṛddhasya bhāvakarmaṇoḥ \|

vārṣikaṃ trāyamāṇāyāṃ klībaṃ varṣābhave triṣu \|\| 157 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 158 \[unit 159\]

bālako'strī pārihāryye triṣu syādaṅgagurīyake \|

vitarkastu pumānūhe saṃśaye ca nigadyate \|\| 158 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 159 \[unit 160\]

vipākaḥ pacane svāde karmaṇo visadṛkphale \|

vivekaḥ syājjaladroṇyāṃ pṛthagbhāvavicārayoḥ \|\| 159 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 160 \[unit 161\]

vṛścikaśca druṇe rāśau śūkakīṭauṣadhībhidoḥ \|

vṛṣāṅkaḥ śaṅkare sādhau bhallātakamahattvayoḥ \|\| 160 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 161 \[unit 162\]

vaijikaṃ śigrutaile syāddhetau sadyo'ṅkure tu nā \|

śalākā śalyamadanaśārikāśallakīṣu ca \|\| 161 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 162 \[unit 163\]

chatrādikāṣṭhīśarayoḥ śallakī paśuvṛkṣayoḥ \|

śambūko gajakumbhānte ghoṅge ca śūdratāpase \|\| 162 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 163 \[unit 164\]

jalajantuviśeṣe ca śambūkā na napuṃsake \|

śaṅkhaṃ valaye kambāvastrī puṃsi śiroruji \|\| 163 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 164 \[unit 165\]

śārkakaḥ syād dugdhaphenaśarkarāpiṇḍayoḥ pumān \|

śiśukaḥ (1)śiśumāre syādvālakolūpinorapi \|\| 164 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 165 \[unit 166\]

śītakaḥ śītakāle ca susthite dīrghasūtriṇi \|

śūkakaḥ prāvaṭe'pi syādrase'pi parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 165 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 166 \[unit 167\]

sasyako maṇibhede'sau samparko melake ratau \|

sampākastarkake dhṛṣṭe triṣu nā caturaṅgule \|\| 166 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 167 \[unit 168\]

syamīkā nīlikāyāṃ strī syamīko nākuvṛkṣayoḥ \|

svastiko maṅgaladravye catuṣkagṛhabhedayoḥ \|\| 167 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 168 \[unit 169\]

sarako'strī śīdhupāne śīdhupātrekṣuśīdhunoḥ \|

acchinnādhvagapaṅktau ca sāyakaḥ śarakhaḍgayoḥ \|\| 168 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 169 \[unit 170\]

sthāsakaḥ puṃsi cārcikye jalāderapi budbude \|

sūcakaḥ sīvanadravye bodhake piśune śuni \|\| 169 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 170 \[unit 171\]

otau kāke sūtako'strī pārade na dvayorjanau \|

sṛdākurnā'nile vajre jvalane pratisūryake \|\| 170 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 171 \[unit 172\]

sevakastu praseve nā vācyaliṅgo'nujīvini \|

secakaḥ syāt pumān meghe vācyaliṅgastu sektari \|\| 171 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 172 \[unit 173\]

sainikaḥ sainyarakṣe ca syāt senāsamavetake \|

hārakaḥ kitave caure gadyavijñānabhedayoḥ \|\| 172 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / katrikam / 173 \[unit 174\]

huḍukko vādyabhede ca mattadātyūhapakṣiṇi \|

heruko buddhabhede syānmahākālagaṇe'pi ca \|\| 173 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 174 \[unit 175\]

kacatuṣkam

bhavedalimako bheke pike'lau padyakesare \|

madhūke'pyathālipako bhṛṅgakokilakukkure \|\| 174 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 175 \[unit 176\]

aṅgārakaḥ kuje'pi syādulmukāṃśe kuruṇṭake \|

bhavedaṅgārikā cekṣukāṇḍe(2) kiṃśukakorake \|\| 175 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 176 \[unit 177\]

syādaśmantakamuddhāne mallikācchadane'pi ca \|

ākalpakastamomohagranthāvutkalikāmadoḥ \|\| 176 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 177 \[unit 178\]

(1) śūkakaḥ-kha. \| ; (2) daṇḍe-kha. \|

ākṣepako'nilavyādhau vyādhe nindākare'pi ca \|

bhavedākhanikaścaure śūkare mūṣike'pi ca \|\| 177 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 178 \[unit 179\]

kathitotkalikotkaṇṭhāhelāsalilavīciṣu \|

eḍamūko'nyaliṅgaḥ syācchaṭhe vākśrutivarjite \|\| 178 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 179 \[unit 180\]

kaṭhillakastu parṇāśe varṣābhūkāravellayoḥ \|

kapardako varāṭe syājjaṭājūṭe ca dhūrjaṭeḥ \|\| 179 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 180 \[unit 181\]

karkoṭakaḥ syānmālūrakādraveyaprabhedayoḥ \|

kalaviṅkaḥ pumān grāmacaṭake'pi kaliṅgake \|\| 180 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 181 \[unit 182\]

kanīnikā tārako'kṣṇaḥ syāt kaniṣṭhāṅgulāvapi \|

kāpaṭiko'nyamarmajñe chātre puṃsi śaṭhe triṣu \|\| 181 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 182 \[unit 183\]

kākarūko nagnadambhastrājitolūkabhīruṣu \|

niḥsve kurubakaḥ śoṇāmlānajhiṇṭīprabhedayoḥ \|\| 182 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 183 \[unit 184\]

kuraṇṭakaḥ pītapuṣpāmlānajhiṇṭīkayoḥ pumān \|

kṛkavākurmayūre'pi saraṭe caraṇāyudhe \|\| 183 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 185\]

kośātakaḥ (1)kaṭhe puṃsi paṭolyāṃ ghoṣake striyām \|

atha kaukkuṭiko'dūrapreritākṣe ca dāmbhike \|\| 184 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 185 \[unit 186\]

kauleyakaḥ sārameye kulīne'tha kharālikaḥ \|

grāmaṇībhaṇḍinārāce'pyupadhāne ca puṃsyayam \|\| 185 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 186 \[unit 187\]

bhaved guṇanikā nṛtyaśūnyāṅke pāṭhaniścitau \|

gokaṇṭako gokṣurake sthapuṭe ca gavāṃ khure \|\| 186 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 187 \[unit 188\]

gomedakaṃ pītamaṇau kākole patrake'pi ca \|

syād gokuliko valire paṅkasthagavyupekṣake \|\| 187 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 188 \[unit 189\]

atha ghargharikā kṣudraghaṇṭāvāditradaṇḍayoḥ \|

carcarīko mahākāle keśavinyāsaśākayoḥ \|\| 188 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 189 \[unit 190\]

caṇḍālikā kinnarāyāmumāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|

atha cāturakaścakragaṇḍau puṃsyabhidheyavat \|\| 189 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 190 \[unit 191\]

(1) kace-kha. \|

gocare locanasyāpi cāṭukāre niyantari \|

jarjarīkaṃ bahucchidre jarāture'pi vācyavat \|\| 190 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 191 \[unit 192\]

jīvantikā guḍūcyāṃ ca jīvākhyaśākavandayoḥ \|

jaivātṛkaḥ pumān some dīrghāyuḥkṛśayāstriṣu \|\| 191 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 192 \[unit 193\]

tartarīkaṃ vahitre syānna dvayoḥ pārage triṣu \|

tiktaśākastu khadire varuṇe patrasundare \|\| 192 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 193 \[unit 194\]

trivarṇakaṃ gokṣurake triphalāyāṃ kaṭutrike \|

dalāḍhakaḥ svayaṃ jātatile pṛśnyāṃ ca gairike \|\| 193 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 194 \[unit 195\]

phenakhātakayornāgakesare ca mahattare \|

dandaśūkastu pulliṅgo rākṣase ca sarīsṛpe \|\| 194 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 195 \[unit 196\]

dāserakastu karabhe dāsīputre ca dhīvare \|

niyāmakaḥ karṇadhāre potavāhe niyantari \|\| 195 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 196 \[unit 197\]

niścārakaḥ purīṣasya kṣaye svaire samīraṇe \|

nirgranthakaḥ syāt kṣapaṇe niṣphale'pyaparicchade \|\| 196 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 197 \[unit 198\]

pracalākaḥ śarāghāte śikhaṇḍe ca bhujaṅgame \|

prakīrṇakaṃ cāmare syādvistāre nā turaṅgame \|\| 197 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 198 \[unit 199\]

pañcālikā striyāṃ vastraputrikāgītibhedayoḥ \|

syāt pippalakaṃ vakṣojavṛnte sīvanasūtrake \|\| 198 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 199 \[unit 200\]

piṇḍītakaḥ syāttagare madanākhyamahīruhe \|

atha puṣkalako gandhamṛge kṣapaṇakīlayoḥ \|\| 199 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 200 \[unit 201\]

puṇḍarīkaṃ sitāmbhoje sitacchatre ca bheṣaje \|

puṃsi vyāghre'gnidiṅnāge kośakārāntare'pi ca \|\| 200 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 201 \[unit 202\]

bhavet pūrṇānakaṃ varddhāpane ca paṭahe'pi ca \|

pharpharīkaścapeṭe syāt pharpharīkaṃ tu mārdave \|\| 201 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 202 \[unit 203\]

balāhako girau meghe daityanāgaviśeṣayoḥ \|

varāṭakaḥ padmabījakośe rajjau kapardake \|\| 202 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 203 \[unit 204\]

bakerukā balākābhidvātāvarjitaśākhayoḥ \|

varaṇḍakastu mātaṅgavedyāṃ yauvanakaṇṭake \|\| 203 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 204 \[unit 205\]

varttule'tho vāṇijako vāḍavāgnau vaṇijyapi \|

vṛndārakaḥ sure puṃsi manojñaśreṣṭhayostriṣu \|\| 204 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 205 \[unit 206\]

atha bhramarako bhṛṅge girike cā'lakāntare \|

bhaṭṭārako nṛpe nāṭyavācā deve tapodhane \|\| 205 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 206 \[unit 207\]

bhayānakaḥ smṛto vyāghre rase rāhau bhayaṅkare \|

bhāryāṭikaḥ pumān bhāryānirjite hariṇāntare \|\| 206 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 207 \[unit 208\]

bhavenmarubakaḥ (1)puṣpaviśeṣe madanadrume \|

maṇḍodakaṃ cittarāge klībamātarpaṇe'pi ca \|\| 207 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 208 \[unit 209\]

bhavenmaṇḍalakaṃ bimbe kuṣṭhabhede ca darpaṇe \|

atha markaṭakaḥ sasyabhede vānaralūtayoḥ \|\| 208 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 209 \[unit 210\]

mayūrako'pyapāmārge klībaṃ tutthāñjane punaḥ \|

māṇavako hārabhede bāle kupuruṣe'pi (2)ca \|\| 209 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 210 \[unit 211\]

mṛṣṭeruko vadānye'pi miṣṭāśinyatithidviṣi \|

ratarddhikaṃ syāddivase sukhasthāneṣṭamaṅgale \|\| 210 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 211 \[unit 212\]

(3)rādharekaḥ pumān sīre sīrake ca ghanopale \|

lālāṭikaḥ prabhorbhāvadarśinyāśleṣaṇāntare \|\| 211 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 212 \[unit 213\]

kāryākṣame lekhanikaḥ kathito lekhahārake \|

lekheṣu parahastena svahastasya ca lekhake \|\| 212 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 213 \[unit 214\]

vartarūko nadībhede kākanīḍe jalāvaṭe \|

vināyakastu herambe tārkṣye vighne jine gurau \|\| 213 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 214 \[unit 215\]

vitunnakastu dhānyāke jhāṭāmalamayūrake \|

viśeṣako'strī tilake viśeṣayitari triṣu \|\| 214 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 215 \[unit 216\]

vidūṣakaścāṭubaṭau paranindākare'pi ca \|

vaināśikaḥ syāt kṣaṇike paratantrorṇanābhayoḥ \|\| 215 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 216 \[unit 217\]

vaidehako vāṇijake śūdrādvaiśyāsute'pi ca \|

vaitālikaḥ pumān kheṭṭitāle bodhakare triṣu \|\| 216 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 217 \[unit 218\]

(1) puṣpabhicchalyadruphaṇijjhake-kha. \| ; (2) baṭau-kha. \| ; (3) rādharaṅkaḥ-kha. \|

śatānīkastu vṛnde syānmuni(1)rājaprabhedayoḥ \|

śālāvṛkaḥ śṛgāle syāt sārameye valīmukhe \|\| 217 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 218 \[unit 219\]

śilāṭakaḥ pumānaṭṭe (2)vilepe parikīrtitaḥ \|

śṛgālikā śivāyāṃ syāt trāsādapi palāyane \|\| 218 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 219 \[unit 220\]

śṛṅgāṭakaṃ bhaved vārikaṇṭake ca catuṣpathe \|

santānikā'pi kṣīrādisāre markaṭajālake \|\| 219 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 220 \[unit 221\]

sandaṃśikā'pi sucuṭīlohayantraviśeṣayoḥ \|

sañcārikā tu yugale kuṭṭanīghrāṇayorapi \|\| 220 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 221 \[unit 222\]

saṅghāṭikā striyāṃ yugme kuṭṭinyāṃ jalakaṇṭake \|

supratīkaḥ śobhanāṅge bhavedīśānadiggaje \|\| 221 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 222 \[unit 223\]

atha sakatikaṃ mātṛyātrāmaṅgalasūtrake \|

nā saṃnyaste kṣapaṇake triṣu sandehajīvini \|\| 222 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / 223 \[unit 224\]

somavalkastu dhavalakhadire kaṭphale'pi ca \|

saugandhikaṃ tu kalhāre padmarāge'pi kattṛṇe \|\| 223 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kacatuṣkam / fragment 225

puṃliṅgo gandhapāṣāṇe sugandhivyavahāriṇi \|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 224 \[unit 226\]

kapañcakam

aneḍamūka uddiṣṭaḥ śaṭhe vākśrutivarjite \|\| 224 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 225 \[unit 227\]

syādācchuritakaṃ hāsanakharāghātabhedayoḥ \|

upakārikopakartryāṃ piṣṭabhede nṛpālaye \|\| 225 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 226 \[unit 228\]

kakṣyāvekṣaka ityeṣa śuddhāntodyānapālayoḥ \|

raṅgājīve kaṇe(3) khaḍge dvāḥsthe'tha kaṭakhādakaḥ \|\| 226 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 227 \[unit 229\]

khādake kācakalaśe balipuṣṭe ca jambuke \|

kṛmikaṇṭakaṃ tu citrāṅgaviḍaṅgodumbareṣu ca \|\| 227 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 228 \[unit 230\]

gojāgarikaṃ maṅgale puṃsi syāt kaṇṭakārake \|

cilamīlikā tu kaṇṭhībhede khadyotavidyutoḥ \|\| 228 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 229 \[unit 231\]

(1) maṇi-ka. \| ; (2) vile'pi-kha. \| ; (3) kavau-kha. \|

atho jalakaraṅkaḥ syānnālikeraphale'mbuje \|

śaṅkhe jalalatāyāṃ ca vārivāhe ca kīrttitaḥ \|\| 229 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 230 \[unit 232\]

jalatāpika illīśa(1)kākacījhaṣayośca puṃsi syāt \|

navaphalikā strī navye navajātarajo'ṅganāyāñca \|\| 230 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 231 \[unit 233\]

nāgavārika uddiṣṭo rājakuñjarahastipe \|

gaṇastharāje garuḍe citramekhalake'pi ca \|\| 231 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 232 \[unit 234\]

syād vrīhirājikaḥ kaṅgudhānyacīnakadhānyayoḥ \|

vyavahārikā syāllokayātrāsammārjanīṅgude \|\| 232 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 233 \[unit 235\]

śataparvikā dūrvāyāṃ vacāyāmapi yoṣiti \|

śītacampaka ityeṣa syādātarpaṇadīpayoḥ \|\| 233 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kapañcakam / 234 \[unit 236\]

suvasantaka uddi(2)ṣṭo vāsantyāṃ madanotsave \|

syāddhemapuṣpikā yūthyāṃ campake hemapuṣpakaḥ \|\| 234 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / 235 \[unit 237\]

kaṣaṭkam

grāmamadgurikā grāmayuddhe śṛṅgījhaṣe striyām \|

madanaśalākā kāmoddīpakabhaiṣajyasārikayoḥ \|\| 235 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / 236 \[unit 238\]

mātulaputraka ityapi mā(2)makatanaye phale ca dhūrtasya \|

varṇaviloḍaka eṣa ślokastene ca sandhicaure ca \|\| 236 \|\|

## kāntavargaḥ / kaṣaṭkam / fragment 239

sindūratilaka ukto mataṅgaje strī tu kāminyām \|

iti kāntavargaḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khaikam

## khāntavargaḥ / khaikam / 1 \[unit 240\]

khaikam

khamindriye pure kṣetre śūnye bindau vihāyasi \|

saṃvedane devaloke śarmaṇyapi napuṃsakam \|\| 1 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khaikam / fragment 241

(1) ilīśakākocimatsyayoḥ-kha. \| ; (2) ākhyāto-kha. \| ; (3) mātula-kha. \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 2 \[unit 242\]

khadvikam

nakhī strīklībayoḥ śuktau nakhare punnapuṃsakam \|

nyuṅkhaḥ samyaṅ manojñe ca sāmnaḥ ṣaṭpraṇaveṣu ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 3 \[unit 243\]

preṅkhā paryaṭane'pyaśvagatau saṃveśanāntare \|

mukhaṃ niḥsaraṇe vaktre prārambhopāyayorapi \|\| 3 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 4 \[unit 244\]

sandhyantare nāṭakādeḥ śabde'pi ca napuṃsakam \|

lekho lekhye sure lekhā lipirājikayormatā \|\| 4 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 5 \[unit 245\]

riṅkhā'śvagatibhede'pi śūkaśimbyāṃ ca nartane \|

śaṅkhaḥ kambau na yoṣinnā bhālāsthinidhibhinnakhe \|\| 5 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 6 \[unit 246\]

śākhā pakṣāntare bāhau vedabhāgadrumāṅgayoḥ \|

śikhā śākhābarhicūḍālāṅgalikyagramātrake \|\| 6 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / 7 \[unit 247\]

cūḍāmātre śikhāyāṃ ca jvālāyāṃ prapade'pi ca \|

sakhā mitre sahāye nā vayasyāyāṃ sakhī matā \|\| 7 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khadvikam / fragment 248

sukhaṃ śarmaṇi nāke ca sukhā puryāṃ pracetasaḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 8 \[unit 249\]

khatrikam

gomukhaṃ kuṭilāgāre vādyabhāṇḍe ca lepane \|\| 8 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 9 \[unit 250\]

puṃsi mātaliputre ca mahādevagaṇāntare \|

triśikho rākṣase klībaṃ triśūle maṇḍalāntare \|\| 9 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 10 \[unit 251\]

durmukhaḥ kapibhinnāgabhidornā mukhare triṣu \|

pramukhaḥ prathame śreṣṭhe mayūkhaḥ kiraṇe'pi ca \|\| 10 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 11 \[unit 252\]

jvālāyāmapi śobhāyāṃ viśikhastāmare śare \|

viśikhā tu khanitryāṃ ca rathyānalikayorapi \|\| 11 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / 12 \[unit 253\]

viśākhastarkake skande striyāmṛkṣe kaṭhillake \|

vaiśākho māsabhede'pi manthāne ca prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khatrikam / fragment 254

sumukhastārkṣyatanayo śākanāgaprabhedayoḥ \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 13 \[unit 255\]

khacatuṣkam

bhavedagnimukhā deve vipre bhallātake striyām \|\| 13 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 14 \[unit 256\]

athāgniśikhamuddiṣṭaṃ kusumbhe kuṅkume'pi ca \|

lāṅgalikyākhyauṣadhau ca viśalyāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 14 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 15 \[unit 257\]

indulekhā'mṛtāsomalatāśaśikalāsu ca \|

atha baddhaśikhā strī syāduccaṭāyāṃ śiśau triṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 16 \[unit 258\]

mahāśaṅkho mānuṣāsthisaṅkhyābhedālikeṣu ca \|

bhavedvyāghranakhaṃ kandagandhadravyaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / 17 \[unit 259\]

nakhakṣatāntare klībaṃ śilīmukho'likāṇḍayoḥ \|

śaśilekhā kalābhāge guḍūcīvṛttabhedayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khacatuṣkam / fragment 260

bhavet svastimukho lekhe brāhmaṇe vandini triṣu \|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / 18 \[unit 261\]

khapañcakam

malinamukho'gnau golāṅgūle puṃsi triṣu krūre \|\| 18 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / 19 \[unit 262\]

śītamayūkhaścandre ghanasāre cāpi puṃliṅgaḥ \|

sarvatomukha ugre ca kṣetrajñabrahmaṇoḥ pumān \|\| 19 \|\|

## khāntavargaḥ / khapañcakam / fragment 263

napuṃsakaṃ tu pānīye suravartmanyapi smṛtam \|

iti khāntavargaḥ \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gaikam

## gāntavargaḥ / gaikam / 1 \[unit 264\]

gaikam

gauḥ svarge ca balībardai raśmau ca kuliśe pumān \|

(1)strī saurabheyīdṛgbāṇadigvāgbhūṣvapsu bhūmni ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 2 \[unit 265\]

gadvikam

(2)agaḥ syānnagavat pṛthvīdharapādapayoḥ pumān \|

aṅgaṃ gātre pratīkopāyayoḥ pumbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 2 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 3 \[unit 266\]

(1) gāyatryāmapi gambhīre jagatyāṃ bhuvane jale \| go gaṇeśe ca nāke ca viyatyapi pumānayam \|\| gaḥ sumerau samākhyāto gāyatrīgītayoḥ pumān

gandharve cāpi gaḥ khyāto gāyake cābhidheyavat \|\| iti kha. pustake'dhikam \| ; (2) ago mahīruhe śaile bhāskare pavanāśane \| kha. pu. pāṭhaḥ \| }

klībaikatve tvapradhāne triṣvaṅgavati cāntike \|

iṅgaḥ syādadbhute jñāne jaṅgameṅgitayorapi \|\| 3 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 4 \[unit 267\]

khagaḥ sūrye grahe deve mārgaṇe ca vihaṅgame \|

khaḍgo gaṇḍakaśṛṅgāsibuddhabhedeṣu gaṇḍake \|\| 4 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 5 \[unit 268\]

gāṅgastu gaṅgāsambhūte triṣu bhīṣme guhe pumān \|

caṅgastu śobhane dakṣe ṭaṅgo'strī syāt khanitrake \|\| 5 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 6 \[unit 269\]

khaḍgabhede ca jaṅghāyāṃ tyāgo dāne ca varjane \|

tuṅgī niśābarbarayoḥ punnāganagayoḥ pumān \|\| 6 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 7 \[unit 270\]

unnate triṣu durgomānīlyoḥ strī durgame triṣu \|

nāgaṃ napusakaṃ raṅge sīsake karaṇāntare \|\| 7 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 8 \[unit 271\]

nāgaḥ pannagamātaṅgakrūrācāriṣu toyade \|

nāgakesarapunnāganāgadantakamustake \|\| 8 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 9 \[unit 272\]

dehānilaprabhede ca śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthitaḥ \|

piṅgī gorocanāhiṅgunālikācaṇḍikāsu ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 10 \[unit 273\]

piṅgī śamyāṃ piśaṅge nā bālake tu napuṃsakam \|

pūgastu kramuke vṛnde phala(1)sāre'bhidheyavat \|\| 10 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 11 \[unit 274\]

nadībhede malapvāṃ strī bhaṅgo jayaviparyaye \|

bhedarogataraṅgeṣu bhaṅgā sasyāntare striyām \|\| 11 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 12 \[unit 275\]

bhagaṃ śrīyonivīryecchājñānavairāgyakīrtiṣu \|

mahātmyaiśvaryayatneṣu dharme mokṣe ca nā ravau \|\| 12 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 13 \[unit 276\]

bhāgo rūpārddhake bhāgyaikadeśayorbhṛguḥ pumān \|

munau hare taṭe śukre bhṛṅgo dhūmyāṭaṣiḍgayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 14 \[unit 277\]

madhuvrate bhṛṅgarāje puṃsi bhṛṅgaṃ guḍatvaci \|

bhogaḥ sukhe dhane cāheḥ śarīraphaṇayorapi \|\| 14 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 15 \[unit 278\]

pālane'bhyavahāre ca yoṣidādibhṛtāvapi \|

mārgo mṛgamade māsaprabhede'nveṣaṇadhvanoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 16 \[unit 279\]

(1) phalgu-kha. \|

mṛgaḥ paśau kuraṅge ca karinakṣatrabhedayoḥ \|

anveṣaṇe ca yācñāyāṃ mṛgī tu vanitāntare \|\| 16 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 17 \[unit 280\]

yugo rathahalādyaṅge na dvayostu kṛtādiṣu \|

yugme hastacatuṣke'pi vṛddhināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 18 \[unit 281\]

yogo'pūrvārthasamprāptau saṅgatidhyānayuktiṣu \|

vapuḥsthairye prayoge ca viṣkambhādiṣu bheṣaje \|\| 18 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 19 \[unit 282\]

viśrabdhaghātake dravyopāyasannahaneṣvapi \|

kārmaṇe'pi ca raṅgo nā rāge nṛtye raṇakṣitau \|\| 19 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 20 \[unit 283\]

astrī trapuṇi rāgastu mātsarye lohitādiṣu \|

kleśādāvanurāge ca gāndhārādau nṛpe'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 21 \[unit 284\]

rogaḥ kuṣṭhauṣadhe vyādhau laṅgastu saṅgaṣiḍgayoḥ \|

liṅge cihne'numāne ca sāṅkhyoktaprakṛtāvapi \|\| 21 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 22 \[unit 285\]

śivamūrtiviśeṣe'pi mehane'pi napuṃsakam \|

vyaṅgo bheke ca hīnāṅge vaṅgaṃ sīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 23 \[unit 286\]

vāttoke'pi ca kārpāse pumbhūmni nīvṛdantare \|

valguḥ syācchagale puṃsi sundare cābhidheyavat \|\| 23 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 24 \[unit 287\]

vego jave pravāhe ca mahākālaphale'pi ca \|

śārṅgaḥ kārmukamātre'pi viṣṇorapi śarāsane \|\| 24 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 25 \[unit 288\]

śuṅgo vaṭāmrātakayoḥ parkaṭyāmapi cāstriyām \|

śṛṅgaṃ prabhutve śikhare cihne krīḍāmbuyantrake \|\| 25 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / 26 \[unit 289\]

viṣāṇotkarṣayoścātha śṛṅgaḥ syāt kūrcaśīrṣake \|

strī viṣāyāṃ svarṇamīnabhedayo ṛṣabhauṣadhau \|\| 26 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gadvikam / fragment 290

sargastu niścayādhyāyamohotsāhātmasṛṣṭiṣu \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 27 \[unit 291\]

gatrikam

ayogo vidhure kūṭe viśleṣe kaṭhinodyame \|\| 27 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 28 \[unit 292\]

anaṅge madane'naṅgamākāśamanasorapi \|

apāṅgastvaṅgahīne syānnetrānte tilake'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 29 \[unit 293\]

ābhogo varuṇacchatre pūrṇatāyatnayorapi \|

āyogo vyāpṛtau gandhamālyopahārarodhayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 30 \[unit 294\]

āśugo mārute bāṇe'pyudvegaṃ kramukīphale \|

udvego'pyudbāhulakodvejanodgamaneṣu ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 31 \[unit 295\]

utsargaḥ puṃsi sāmānye nyāye ca tyāgadānayoḥ \|

kaliṅgaḥ pūtikaraje dhūmyāṭe bhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 31 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 32 \[unit 296\]

na dvayoḥ kauṭajaphale mahilāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

kāliṅgo bhūmikarkārau dantāvalabhujaṅgayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 33 \[unit 297\]

kāliṅgī rājakarkaṭyāṃ cakrāṅgo mānasaukasi \|

cakrāṅgī kaṭurohiṇyāṃ jihmago'hau ca mandage \|\| 33 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 34 \[unit 298\]

taḍāgo'strī jalādhāraviśeṣe yantrakūṭake \|

tātaguḥ kṣudratāte nā janakasya hite triṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 35 \[unit 299\]

trivargo dharmakāmārthe triphalāyāṃ kaṭutrike \|

vṛddhisthānakṣaye sattvarajastamasi ceṣyate \|\| 35 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 36 \[unit 300\]

turagī cāśvagandhāyāṃ turagaścittavājinoḥ \|

dhārāṅgo'sau ca tīrthe ca naraṅgastu varaṇḍake \|\| 36 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 37 \[unit 301\]

mehane na dvayoścātha nāraṅgaḥ pippalīrase \|

yamajaprāṇini viṭe nāgaraṅgadrume'pi ca \|\| 37 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 38 \[unit 302\]

niṣaṅgaḥ saṅgate tūṇe nisargo rūpasargayoḥ \|

nīlaṅguḥ syātkṛmau puṃsi bhambharālyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 38 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 39 \[unit 303\]

patrāṅgaṃ na dvayorbhūrje padmake raktacandane \|

pannagaścauṣadhībhede tathaiva pavanāśane \|\| 39 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 40 \[unit 304\]

plavago vānare bheke sārathau coṣṇadīdhiteḥ \|

parāgaḥ sumanoreṇau dhūlisnānīyayorapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 41 \[unit 305\]

giriprabhede vikhyātāvuparāge ca candane \|

prayāgastīrthabhede syādyajñe śatamakhāśvayoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 42 \[unit 306\]

prayogaḥ kārmaṇe'pi syāt prayuktau ca nidarśane \|

pataṅgaḥ śalabhe śāliprabhede pakṣisūryayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 43 \[unit 307\]

klībaṃ sūte priyaṅguḥ strī rājikākaṇayorapi \|

phalinyāṃ kaṅgusasye ca punnāgastu sitātpale \|\| 43 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 44 \[unit 308\]

jātīphale naraśreṣṭhe pāṇḍunāge drumāntare \|

varāṅgaṃ yonimātaṅgamastakeṣu guḍatvaci \|\| 44 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 45 \[unit 309\]

bhujaṅgo'hau ca ṣiḍge ca mātaṅgaḥ śvapace gaje \|

mṛdaṅgaḥ paṭahe ghoṣe raktāṅgastu mahīsute \|\| 45 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 46 \[unit 310\]

kampille strī tu jīvantyāṃ klībaṃ vidrumadhīrayoḥ \|

rathāṅgaṃ na dvayoścakre nā cakrāṅgavihaṅgame \|\| 46 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 47 \[unit 311\]

vātigaḥ puṃsi bhaṇṭākyāṃ dhātuvādini cānyavat \|

viḍaṅgastriṣvabhijñe syāt kṛmighne punnapuṃsakam \|\| 47 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 48 \[unit 312\]

visargastu pumān dāne tyāge ca malanirgame \|

visarjanīye'pyayanabhede'pi ca vibhāvasoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 49 \[unit 313\]

vihaṅgatu triliṅgaḥ syādāśuge ca vihaṅgame \|

sarvagaṃ salile klībaṃ sarvagaḥ śaṅkare vibhau \|\| 49 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / 50 \[unit 314\]

sambhogastu pumān bhoge surate jinaśāsane \|

sāraṅgaḥ puṃsi hariṇe cātake ca mataṅgaje \|\| 50 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gatrikam / fragment 315

śabale triṣu hemāṅgo garuḍe parameṣṭhini \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 51 \[unit 316\]

gacatuṣkam

syādapavargastyāge mokṣe kāryāvasānasāphalye \|\| 51 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 317\]

abhiṣaṅgaḥ puṃliṅgaḥ parābhavākrośaśapatheṣu \|

īhāmṛgastu puṃsi syāt kokarūpakabhedayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 318\]

uparāgastu puṃsi syādrāhu(1)grastārkacandrayoḥ \|

durnaye grahakallole vyasane'pi nigadyate \|\| 53 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 319\]

upasargaḥ pumān rogabhedopaplavayorapi \|

kaṭabhaṅgastu śasyānāṃ hastacchede nṛpātyaye \|\| 54 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 320\]

chatrabhaṅgo'pi vaidhavye svātantryanṛpanāśayoḥ \|

dīrghādhvagaḥ pumānuṣṭre lekhyahāre tu bhedyavat \|\| 55 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 321\]

(1) rāhugrāse'rkacandrayoḥ-kha. \|

mallanāgo'bhramātaṅge vātsyāyanamunāvapi \|

samāyogastu saṃyoge samavāye prayojane \|\| 56 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gacatuṣkam / fragment 322

samprayogo rate'pi syādanvitau kārmaṇe'pi ca \|

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam / 57 \[unit 323\]

gapañcakam

kathāprasaṅgo vātūle viṣavaidye ca vācyavat \|\| 57 \|\|

## gāntavargaḥ / gapañcakam / fragment 324

nāḍītaraṅgaḥ kākole hiṇḍake ratahiṇḍake \|

iti gāntavargaḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghaikam / fragment 325

ghekam

gho ghaṇṭāyāṃ gharghare nā striyāṃ tu kāñcidhātayoḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 1 \[unit 326\]

ghadvikam

aghaṃ tu vyasane duḥkhe durite ca napuṃsakam \|\| 1 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 2 \[unit 327\]

arghaḥ pūjāvidhau mūlye'pyudghaḥ syāddehajānile \|

agnau hastapuṭe śaste'pyogho vege jalasya ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 3 \[unit 328\]

vṛnde paramparāyāṃ ca drutanṛtyopadeśayoḥ \|

maghā maghī ca nakṣatre dhānyabhede yathākramam \|\| 3 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 4 \[unit 329\]

magho dvīpāntare megho mustājaladayoḥ pumān \|

moghā strī pāṭalāyāṃ syāddhīnaniṣphalayostriṣu \|\| 4 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / 5 \[unit 330\]

laghuragurau ca manojñe niḥsāre vācyavat klībam \|

śīghre kṛṣṇāguruṇi ca pṛkkānāmauṣadhau tu strī \|\| 5 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghadvikam / fragment 331

ślāghā striyāṃ praśaṃsāyāṃ paricaryā'bhilāṣayoḥ \|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 6 \[unit 332\]

ghatrikam

anagho nirmalāpāpamanojñeṣvabhidheyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 7 \[unit 333\]

amoghaḥ saphale vācyavat strī pathyāviḍaṅgayoḥ \|

ullāgho'pi śucau kṛṣṇe dakṣanīrogayostriṣu \|\| 7 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 8 \[unit 334\]

kācighaḥ kāñcane'pi syācchemaṇḍe mūṣake'pi ca \|

nidāgho grīṣmakāle syāduṣṇasvedāmbunorapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 9 \[unit 335\]

palighaḥ kācakalaśe ghaṭe prākāragopure \|

parigho yogabhede'straviśeṣe'rgalaghātayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / 10 \[unit 336\]

pratighaḥ pratighāte syāt krodhe'pi parikīrtitaḥ \|

mahārghastu mahāmūlye triṣu syāllāvake pumān \|\| 10 \|\|

## ghāntavargaḥ / ghatrikam / fragment 337

sarvaugho gurubhede ca sarvasannahane pumān \|

iti ghāntavargaḥ \|

## ṅāntavargaḥ / ṅaikam

## ṅāntavargaḥ / ṅaikam / fragment 338

ṅaikam

ṅaḥ pumān viṣaye khyātaḥ spṛhāyāṃ viṣayasya ca \|

iti ṅāntavargaḥ \|

## cāntavargaḥ / caikam

## cāntavargaḥ / caikam / fragment 339

caikam

caścaṇḍeśe pumānuktaḥ kacchape candracaurayoḥ \|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 1 \[unit 340\]

cadvikam

arcā pūjāpratimayoḥ kacaḥ keśe guroḥ sute \|\| 1 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 2 \[unit 341\]

bandhe śuṣkavraṇe puṃsi kariṇyāṃ tu kacā striyām \|

kācaḥ śikye maṇau netrarogabhede mṛdantare \|\| 2 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 3 \[unit 342\]

kāñcā syānmekhalādāmni prabhede nagarasya ca \|

kūrcamastrī bhruvormadhye kaṭhinaśmaśrukaitave \|\| 3 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 4 \[unit 343\]

krauñco dvīpaviśeṣe syāt pakṣiparvatabhedayoḥ \|

carcā cintāsthāsakayoścarcikāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 4 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 5 \[unit 344\]

cañcā tu nalanirmāṇe tṛṇanirmitapūruṣe \|

cañcustroṭyāṃ striyāṃ puṃsi gonāḍīke vyaḍambake \|\| 5 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 6 \[unit 345\]

tvak strī carmaṇi valke ca guḍatvaci viśeṣataḥ \|

nyaṅ nīcani(1)mnayornīcaḥ pāmare vāmane'pi ca \|\| 6 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 7 \[unit 346\]

prācchabdo diśi deśe ca kāle ca vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

picurnā kuṣṭhabhede ca karṣe tūle'surāntare \|\| 7 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 8 \[unit 347\]

mocaḥ śobhāñjane puṃsi mocā śālmalirambhayoḥ \|

(1) mandayo-kha. \|

ruciḥ strī dīptau śobhāyāmabhiṣvaṅgābhilāṣayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 9 \[unit 348\]

ruk strī śobhādyutīcchāsu vacaḥ kīre vacauṣadhau \|

śārikāyāṃ ca vāgvāce bhāratyāṃ vacane striyau \|\| 9 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 10 \[unit 349\]

vīciḥ svalpataraṅge syādavakāśe sukhe dvayoḥ \|

śacīndrāṇyāṃ śatāvaryāṃ tathā strīkaraṇāntare \|\| 10 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / 11 \[unit 350\]

śucirgrīṣmāgniśṛṅgāreṣvāṣāḍhe śuddhamantriṇi \|

jyeṣṭhe ca puṃsi dhavale śuddhe'nupahate triṣu \|\| 11 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cadvikam / fragment 351

sūcī tu sīvanadravye'pyāṅgikābhinayāntare \|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 12 \[unit 352\]

catrikam

udagdigdeśakāleṣu vācyavat tritaye'vyayam \|\| 12 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 13 \[unit 353\]

kaṇīcī puṣpitalatāguñjayoḥ śakaṭe striyām \|

kavaco gardabhāṇḍe ca sannāhe paṭahe'pi ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 14 \[unit 354\]

krakacaḥ karapatre'strī granthilākhyatarau pumān \|

namucistu pumān daityabhede kusumakārmuke \|\| 14 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 15 \[unit 355\]

nārācyeṣaṇikāyāṃ nā lohabāṇāmbuhastinoḥ \|

prapañcaḥ sañcaye'pi syādvistāre ca pratāraṇe \|\| 15 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 16 \[unit 356\]

pratyagdigdeśakāleṣu vācyavattritaye'vyayam \|

marīcirmunibhede nā gabhastāvanapuṃsakam \|\| 16 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 17 \[unit 357\]

mārīco rakṣaso bhede kakkole yājakadvije \|

mārīcī devatābhede vipañcī kelivīṇayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 18 \[unit 358\]

vikacaḥ kṣapaṇe ketau nā'keśe sphuṭite'nyavat \|

virañcirnā viriñcaśca vaikuṇṭhe parameṣṭhini \|\| 18 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / catrikam / 19 \[unit 359\]

saṅkoco mīnabhede syād bandhe klībaṃ tu kuṅkume \|

samyak syādvācyaliṅgaṃ tu manojñe saṅgate'pi ca \|\| 19 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacatuṣkam

## cāntavargaḥ / cacatuṣkam / 20 \[unit 360\]

cacatuṣkam

jalasūciḥ kaṅkatrāṭimatsyaśṛṅgāṭayorapi \|

śiśumāre ca puṃliṅgo jalaukāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 20 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / cacatuṣkam / fragment 361

malimluco māsabhede caurajvalanayoḥ pumān \|

## cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam

## cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam / 21 \[unit 362\]

capañcakam

ratanārīco nārīṇāṃ sītkāre ca śuni smare \|\| 21 \|\|

## cāntavargaḥ / capañcakam / fragment 363

iti cāntavargaḥ \|

## chāntavargaḥ / chaikam

## chāntavargaḥ / chaikam / fragment 364

chaikam

chā(1) chādane striyāṃ khyātā nirmale'nyavadiṣyate \|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 1 \[unit 365\]

chadvikam

acchaḥ sphaṭikabhallūkanirmaleṣvacchamavyayam \|\| 1 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 2 \[unit 366\]

ābhimukhye'tha kacchaḥ syādanūpe tunnakadrume \|

naukāṅge pusi vārāhyāṃ cīrikāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 2 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 3 \[unit 367\]

syād gucchaḥ stabake stambe hārabhedakalāpayoḥ \|

picchā pūgacchaṭākośamocāśālmaliveṣṭake \|\| 3 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 4 \[unit 368\]

bhaktasambhūtamaṇḍe ca paṅktāvaśvapadāmaye \|

striyāṃ puṃsi tu lāṅgūle na dvayorbarhacūḍayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chadvikam / 5 \[unit 369\]

pucchaḥ paścātpradeśe syāllāṅgūle pucchamiṣyate \|

mlecchaḥ pāmarabhede ca pāparakte'pabhāṣaṇe \|\| 5 \|\|

## chāntavargaḥ / chacatuṣkam

## chāntavargaḥ / chacatuṣkam / fragment 370

chacatuṣkam

mahākacchastu puṃsi syāt samudraṃ ca pracetasi \|

iti chāntavargaḥ \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jaikam

## jāntavargaḥ / jaikam / 1 \[unit 371\]

jaikam

jā nā mṛtyuñjaye janyāṃ tātamātre janārdane \|

(2)jūstvarāgamane proktā sāmānyagamane striyām \|\| 1 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jaikam / fragment 372

jūrākāśasarasvatyāṃ piśācyāṃ javane striyām \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 2 \[unit 373\]

jadvikam

ajaśchāge haribrahmavidhusmarahare nṛpe \|\| 2 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 3 \[unit 374\]

(1) tarale chaṃ prakīrtitaṃ māhātmye parame'pi ca - kha. pustake'dhikam \| ; (2) tvarite jaḥ samākhyāto jayane jiḥ prakīrtitaḥ - kha. pustake'dhikam \|

abjo'strī śaṅkhe nā vicule dhanvantarau ca himakiraṇe \|

klībaṃ padme'thājiḥ strī samabhūmau ca saṃgrāme \|\| 3 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 4 \[unit 375\]

ūrjjastu kārttikotsāhavaleṣu prāṇane'pi ca \|

kañjaḥ keśe viriñce ca kañjaṃ pīyūṣapadmayoḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 5 \[unit 376\]

kujā kātyāyanīdevyāṃ kujo ranakabhaumayoḥ \|

kubjo vṛkṣaprabhede nā nyubje syādvācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 6 \[unit 377\]

kuñjo'striyāṃ nikuñje'pi hanau dante'pi hastinām \|

khañjā chandaḥprabhede strī kuṭilāṅghrau tu vācyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 7 \[unit 378\]

khajā manthe prahaste'tha kharjūḥ kīṭāntare smṛtā \|

kharjūrāpādape kaṇḍvāṃ gajo māne mataṅgaje \|\| 7 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 8 \[unit 379\]

vāstunaḥ sthānabhede'pi gañjā khanau surāgṛhe \|

gañjaḥ syāt puṃsi rīḍhāyāṃ bhāṇḍāgāre tu na striyām \|\| 8 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 9 \[unit 380\]

guñjā tu kākaciñcāyāṃ paṭahe ca kaladhvanau \|

dvijaḥ syād brāhmaṇakṣatravaiśyadantāṇḍajeṣu nā \|\| 9 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 10 \[unit 381\]

dvijā bhārgyāṃ hareṇau ca dhvajaḥ syācchauṇḍike pumān \|

na striyāṃ tu patākāyāṃ khaṭvāṅge meḍhracihnayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 11 \[unit 382\]

nijaṃ svīye ca nitye ca nyubjo darbhamayasruci \|

karmaraṅgaphale klībaṃ kubjādhomukhayostriṣu \|\| 11 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 12 \[unit 383\]

prajā loke ca santāne piñjā tūlaharidrayoḥ \|

bale klībe vadhe puṃsi vyākule triṣvatho bhujā \|\| 12 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 13 \[unit 384\]

dvayorbāhau kare marjūḥ strī śuddhau dhāvake'pi ca \|

rajo reṇau parāge syādārtave ca guṇāntare \|\| 13 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 14 \[unit 385\]

rajjurveṇyāṃ guṇe yoṣidrājiḥ strī paṅktilekhayoḥ \|

rujā rāge ca bhaṅge'tha lājaḥ syādārdrataṇḍule \|\| 14 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 15 \[unit 386\]

napuṃsakamuśīre ca striyāṃ pumbhūmni cākṣate \|

vrajo gāṣṭhādhvavṛndeṣu vājo niḥsvanapakṣayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 16 \[unit 387\]

vege pumānatha klībe ghṛtayajñānnavāriṣu \|

vyājaḥ śāṭhye'padeśe'tha bījamaṅkurakāraṇe \|\| 16 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / 17 \[unit 388\]

hetutattvādhānaśukre sarjūrvaṇiji vidyuti \|

striyāṃ svañjo vidhau rudre svajaḥ prasvedaputrayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jadvikam / fragment 389

klībaṃ rakte'tha sajjaḥ syāt sannaddhe sambhṛte triṣu \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 18 \[unit 390\]

jatrikam

aṇḍajo mṛganābhau syāt saraṭe'hau khage jhaṣe \|\| 18 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 19 \[unit 391\]

aṅgajaṃ rudhire'naṅgakeśaputragadeṣu nā \|

ambujo nicule puṃsi kamale tu napuṃsakam \|\| 19 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 20 \[unit 392\]

asṛk syādyogabhede nā rakte'pi syānnapuṃsakam \|

kambojo hastibhede'pi śaṅkhadeśaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 21 \[unit 393\]

karajaṃ syādvyāghranakhe karañjanakhayoḥ pumān \|

kārujaḥ śilpināṃ citre vāmalūre gajārbhake \|\| 21 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 22 \[unit 394\]

kāmbojo'śvāntare somavalke punnāgapādape \|

kāmbojī māṣaparṇyāṃ ca valakṣakhadire striyām \|\| 22 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 23 \[unit 395\]

kuṭajo vṛkṣabhede syādagastyadroṇayorapi \|

girijaṃ tvabhrake'pi syācchilājatuni śaislaje \|\| 23 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 24 \[unit 396\]

lohe'pi girijāgaurīmātuluṅgyośca yoṣiti \|

jalajaṃ kamale śaṅkhe nīrajaṃ kuṣṭhapadmayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 25 \[unit 397\]

parañjastailayantre syācchurikāphalasenayoḥ \|

valajaṃ gopure kṣetre śasyasaṅgarayorapi \|\| 25 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 26 \[unit 398\]

valajā varayoṣāyāṃ yūthyāmatha vaṇik striyām \|

vaṇijyāyāṃ pumān vāṇijyake ca karaṇāntare \|\| 26 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 27 \[unit 399\]

bāhujaḥ kṣatriye kīre svayañjātatile'pi ca \|

bhūmijo narake'ṅgāre sītādevyāṃ tu bhūmijā \|\| 27 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 28 \[unit 400\]

vanajā mudgaparṇyāṃ nā mustake klībamambuje \|

sahajastu nisarge nā sahotthe punaranyavat \|\| 28 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / 29 \[unit 401\]

samajaḥ paśuvṛnde nā vipine tu napuṃsakam \|

sāmajastu gaje puṃsi sāmotthe punaranyavat \|\| 29 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jatrikam / fragment 402

himajā tu śacīgauryoḥ puṃsi mainākaparvate \|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 30 \[unit 403\]

jacatuṣkam

ahibhug barhiṇe tārkṣye puṃsi kāśmīrajaṃ punaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 31 \[unit 404\]

kuṣṭhakuṅkamayoḥ klībe'tiviṣāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

kṣīrābdhijaṃ tu sāmudralavaṇe mauktike'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 32 \[unit 405\]

pumāṃstuṣārakiraṇe kamalāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

graharājo ravau candre jaghanyajaḥ kanīyasi \|\| 32 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 33 \[unit 406\]

vṛṣale dvijarājastu candre'nante garutmati \|

dharmarājo yame buddhe yudhiṣṭhiranṛpe pumān \|\| 33 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 34 \[unit 407\]

bharadvājo guroḥ putre vyāghrāṭākhyavihaṅgame \|

bhāradvājī vanakārpāsyāṃ nā droṇaṛṣibhedayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / 35 \[unit 408\]

atha bhṛṅgarāja uktaḥ pakṣiviśeṣe ca mārkave bhramare \|

yakṣarāṭ puṃsi dhanade mallānāṃ raṅgacatvare \|\| 35 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / jacatuṣkam / fragment 409

rājarājaḥ kubere'pi sārvabhaume sudhākare \|

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam / 36 \[unit 410\]

japañcakam

ṛṣabhadhvaja eṣo'pi śaṅkare cārhadantare \|\| 36 \|\|

## jāntavargaḥ / japañcakam / fragment 411

(1)munibheṣajamāgastyaharītakyāṃ ca laṅghane \|

iti jāntavargaḥ \|

## jhāntavargaḥ / jhaikam

## jhāntavargaḥ / jhaikam / fragment 412

jhaikam

jho jhaṇṭīśe suragurau daityarāje dhvanāvapi \|

## jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam

## jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam / 1 \[unit 413\]

jhadvikam

jhañjhā vāte tāravāyau naṣṭe'pi kīrtyate budhaiḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## jhāntavargaḥ / jhadvikam / fragment 414

jhañjhā dhvaniviśeṣe ca syādambukaṇavarṣaṇe \|

iti jhāntavargaḥ \|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñaikam / fragment 415

ñaikam

ñaḥ pumān syād balīvarde śuke vāmamatāvapi \|

(1) vasundharīśajaścandre padme'pi parikīrtitaḥ - kha. pustake'dhikam \|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam / 1 \[unit 416\]

ñadvikam

jño brahmabudhavidvatsu syādajño jaḍamūrkhayoḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam / 2 \[unit 417\]

prajñastu paṇḍite vācyaliṅgo buddhau ca yoṣiti \|

rājñī rājapriyāyāṃ ca bhāryāyāṃ bhāskarasya ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñadvikam / 3 \[unit 418\]

saṃjñā nāmani gāyatryāṃ cetanāraviyoṣitoḥ \|

arthasya sūcanāyāṃ ca hastādyairapi yoṣiti \|\| 3 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam / 4 \[unit 419\]

kṛtajñaḥ kukkure puṃsi maryādinyabhidheyavat \|

kṣetrajña ātmani ccheke daivajño gaṇake pumān \|\| 4 \|\|

## ñāntavargaḥ / ñatrikam / fragment 420

daivajñekṣaṇikāyāṃ strī sarvajñaḥ śivabuddhayoḥ \|

iti ñāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭaikam / fragment 421

ṭaikam

(1)ṭaḥ pumān vāmane pāde niḥsvane'pi kvacinmataḥ \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 1 \[unit 422\]

ṭadvikam

aṭṭaṃ bhakte ca śuṣke nā kṣaume'tyarthe gṛhāntare \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 2 \[unit 423\]

iṣṭamāśaṃsite'pi syāt pūjite preyasi triṣu \|

saptatantau pumān klībe saṃskāre ṛtukarmaṇi \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 3 \[unit 424\]

iṣṭirmatā'bhilāṣe'pi saṅgrahaślokayāgayoḥ \|

kaṭaḥ śroṇau dvayoḥ puṃsi kiliñje'tiśaye śive(2) \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 4 \[unit 425\]

samaye gajagaṇḍe ca pippalyāṃ tu kaṭī matā \|

kaṭuḥ strī kaṭurohiṇyāṃ latārājikayorapi \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 5 \[unit 426\]

napuṃsakamakārye syāt puṃliṅgo rasamātrake \|

triṣu tadvatsugandhyośca matsare'pi khare'pi ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 6 \[unit 427\]

(1) ṭā pṛthivyāṃ karaṅke ṭī dhvanau ca parikīrtitaḥ \| ṭo maheśvara ākhyātaḥ khyāte tribhuvane'pi ca \| idaṃ padyaṃ kha. pustake'dhikam \| ; (2) śare-kha., śire-ka. \|

kaṣṭaṃ tu gahane kṛcchre kruṣṭaṃ rodanarāvayoḥ \|

kuṭaḥ koṭe pumānastrī ghaṭe strīpuṃsayorgṛhe \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 7 \[unit 428\]

kuṭī syāt kumbhadāsyāṃ ca murāyāṃ citragucchake \|

kūṭo'strī niścalaṃ rāśau lohamudgaradambhayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 8 \[unit 429\]

māyādriśṛṅgayostucche sīrāvayavayantrayoḥ \|

anṛte cātha kṛṣṭiḥ syādākarṣe strī budhe pumān \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 9 \[unit 430\]

kāṭiḥ strī dhanuṣo'gre'śrau saṃkhyābhedaprakarṣayoḥ \|

khaṭo'ndhakūpakaphayoḥ prahārāntaraṭaṅkayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 10 \[unit 431\]

khāṭistvasadgrahe'pi syāt kiṇe śavarathe striyām \|

kheṭaḥ kaphe grāmabhede carmaṇyastryarvati triṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 11 \[unit 432\]

atha gṛṣṭiḥ sakṛtsūtagavībadarayoḥ striyām \|

ghaṭaḥ samādhibhedebhaśiraḥkūṭakuṭeṣu ca \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 12 \[unit 433\]

ghaṭā ghaṭanagoṣṭhībhaghaṭanāsu ca yoṣiti \|

ghṛṣṭiḥ strī gharṣaṇasparddhāviṣṇukrāntāsu nā kirau \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 13 \[unit 434\]

ghoṇṭā tu badarīpūgavṛkṣayorapi yoṣiti \|

caṭuścāṭau piciṇḍe(1) ca vratināmāsane pumān \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 14 \[unit 435\]

jaṭā lagnakace mūle māṃsyāṃ plakṣe punarjaṭī \|

juṣṭaṃ tu klībamucchiṣṭe sevite vācyaliṅgakam \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 15 \[unit 436\]

jhāṭo nikuñje kāntāre vraṇādīnāṃ ca mārjane \|

taṭaṃ napuṃsakaṃ kṣetre pratīre tu taṭī triṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 16 \[unit 437\]

tvaṣṭā pumān devaśilpitakṣṇorādityabhidyapi \|

truṭiḥ strī saṃśaye svalpe sūkṣmailākālamānayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 17 \[unit 438\]

troṭiḥ strī kaṭphale cañcvāṃ khage mīnāntare'pi ca \|

diṣṭaṃ daivai pumān kāle diṣṭirmutparimāṇayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 18 \[unit 439\]

striyāṃ dṛṣṭiḥ striyāṃ buddhau locane darśane'pi ca \|

dhaṭo divyatulāyāṃ syāddhaṭī cīre ca vāsasaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 19 \[unit 440\]

naṭī nalyauṣadhe strī syāt śailūṣāśīkayoḥ pumān \|

(1) 'tha-kha. \|

paṭaścitrapaṭe vastre'strī priyāladrume pumān \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 20 \[unit 441\]

paṭurdakṣe ca nīroge cature'pyabhidheyavat \|

paṭole tu pumān klībe chatrālavaṇayorapi \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 21 \[unit 442\]

paṭṭaḥ peṣaṇapāṣāṇe vraṇādīnāṃ ca bandhane \|

catuṣpathe ca rājādiśāsanāntarapīṭhayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 22 \[unit 443\]

paṭiḥ strī paṭabhede syādvāgulau kumbhikādrume \|

puṣṭiḥ strī poṣaṇe vṛddhau phaṭā tu phaṇadantayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 23 \[unit 444\]

baṭī triṣu guṇe puṃsi syānnyagrodhakapardayoḥ \|

bhaṭaḥ syāt puṃsi vīre ca viśeṣe pāmarasya ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 24 \[unit 445\]

bhṛṣṭiḥ syādbharjane śūnyavāṭikāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

mliṣṭaṃ triṣvavyaktavāci mlāne muṣṭirdvayoḥ phale \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 25 \[unit 446\]

baddhapāṇau tsarau yaṣṭiḥ puṃsi syād bhujadaṇḍake \|

dvayorhāralatābhārgyormadhukāśastrabhedayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 26 \[unit 447\]

riṣṭaṃ kṣamāśubhābhāve puṃsi khaḍge ca phenile \|

riṣṭiḥ khaḍge nā śubhe strī lāṭo deśāntareṃ'śuke \|\| 26 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 27 \[unit 448\]

vāṭo mārge vṛtisthāne syāt kuṭīvāstunoḥ striyām \|

viṭo'drau lavaṇe ṣiḍge mūṣike khadire'pi ca \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 28 \[unit 449\]

viṣṭistriṣu karmakare stryājūvetanakarmasu \|

vyuṣṭiḥ phale samṛddhau strī vyuṣṭaṃ kalye triṣūṣite \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 29 \[unit 450\]

saṭā jaṭākesarayoḥ sphuṭo vyaktapraphullayoḥ \|

vyāpte triṣu sphuṭiryoṣit pādasphoṭāmaye'pi ca \|\| 29 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 30 \[unit 451\]

nirbhinnakarkaṭīśasye'pi syāt sṛṣṭaṃ tu nirmite \|

yuktaniścitayoḥ prājye triṣu sṛṣṭistu nirmitau \|\| 30 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭadvikam / 31 \[unit 452\]

svabhāve cāpi kathitā hṛṣṭo romāñcite'pi ca \|

jātaharṣe prahasite vismite'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 31 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 32 \[unit 453\]

ṭatrikam

avaṭaḥ syāt khile garte kūpe kuhakajīvini \|

ariṣṭo laśune nimbe phenile kākakaṅkayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 33 \[unit 454\]

ariṣṭamaśubhe takre sūtikāgāra āsave \|

śubhe maraṇacihne cādiṣṭamādeśite triṣu \|\| 33 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 34 \[unit 455\]

ājñaptocchiṣṭayoḥ klībamutkaṭatīvramattayoḥ \|

udbhaṭaḥ kacchape(1) śūrpe karkaṭo vihagāntare \|\| 34 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 35 \[unit 456\]

rāśibhede kulīre ca vāluṅkyāṃ śālmalīphale \|

kardaṭaḥ karahāṭe syāt paṅkapaṅkārayorapi \|\| 35 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 36 \[unit 457\]

karaṭo gajagaṇḍe syāt kusumbhe nindyajīvane(2) \|

ekādaśāhādiśrāddhe durdurūḍhe'pi vāyase \|\| 36 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 37 \[unit 458\]

karaṭo vādyabhede'tha kārpaṭo jatukāryiṇoḥ \|

kīkaṭaḥ kṛpaṇe niḥsve triṣu pumbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 37 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 38 \[unit 459\]

kuruṇṭo dāruputryāṃ nā jhiṇṭyamlānaprabhedayoḥ \|

kukkuṭyanṛtacaryāyāṃ puṃsi syāccaraṇāyudhe \|\| 38 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 39 \[unit 460\]

niṣādaśūdrayoḥ putre tṛṇīlkāyāṃ ca kukkubhe \|

kunaṭī manaḥśilāyāṃ naipālyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 39 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 40 \[unit 461\]

kṛpīṭamudare toye keśaṭo haryajotkaṭe \|

carpaṭaḥ sphāravipule capeṭe parpaṭe'pi ca \|\| 40 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 41 \[unit 462\]

cakrāṭo viṣavaidye'pi dhūrtadīnārayorapi \|

cipiṭaḥ khādyabhede nā triṣu piṭṭitavistṛte \|\| 41 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 42 \[unit 463\]

ciraṭī tu suvāsinyāṃ syād dvitīyavayaḥstriyām \|

jakuṭaṃ vārttākupuṣpe jakuṭo malaye śuni \|\| 42 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 43 \[unit 464\]

tryaṅkaṭaṃ śikyabhede'pi dhautāñjanyāṃ ca na dvayoḥ \|

trikūṭaṃ sindhulavaṇe trikūṭaḥ parvatāntare \|\| 43 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 44 \[unit 465\]

tripuṭā mallikāyāṃ ca sūkṣmailātrivṛtoḥ striyām \|

satīnake ca tīre ca tripuṭaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 45 \[unit 466\]

drohāṭaḥ kathito gāthāprabhede mṛgalubdhake \|

vaiḍālavratike cā'tha dhārāṭaścātakāśvayoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 46 \[unit 467\]

(1) sūrye-kha. \| ; (2) jīvini-kha. \|

nirdaṭastu dayāśūnye kathito niṣprayojane \|

parāpavādarakte ca vācyaliṅgo'yamiṣyate \|\| 46 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 47 \[unit 468\]

niṣkuṭastu gṛhodyāne syāt kedārakapāṭayoḥ \|

parkaṭī nūtanaphale pūgādeḥ plakṣapādape \|\| 47 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 48 \[unit 469\]

parīṣṭiḥ paricaryāyāṃ prākāmye'nveṣaṇe striyām \|

parpaṭī piṣṭabhede syāt parpaṭo bheṣajāntare \|\| 48 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 49 \[unit 470\]

pātraṭaḥ (1)karpare puṃsi kṛśe syādabhidheyavat \|

piccaṭo netraroge syāt klībaṃ sīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 50 \[unit 471\]

varaṭā dvayorvaraṭyāṃ strī haṃsyāṃ tatpatau pumān \|

barvaṭī paṇyayoṣāyāṃ vrīhibhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 50 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 51 \[unit 472\]

vekaṭaḥ syādvaikaṭike matsyabhede ca yūni ca \|

bhāvāṭo bhāvake sādhuniveśe kāmuke naṭe \|\| 51 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 52 \[unit 473\]

bhākūṭaḥ śailajhaṣayorbhede syādatha markaṭī \|

karañjabhicchūkaśibyoḥ puṃsi vānaralūtayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 53 \[unit 474\]

mocāṭaḥ kṛṣṇajīre ca rambhāsthni malayodbhave \|

moraṭaṃ tu bhavedikṣumūlāṅkoṭhaprasūnayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 54 \[unit 475\]

saptarātrātparakṣīre mūrvikāyāṃ tu moraṭā \|

varṇāṭo gāyane citrakare strīkṛtajīvane \|\| 54 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 55 \[unit 476\]

cikaṭā vajravārāhyāṃ triṣūruvikarālayoḥ \|

śākaṭaṃ śakaṭasyedamityarthe tasya voḍhari \|\| 55 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭatrikam / 56 \[unit 477\]

śailāṭo devale siṃhe śuklakācakirātayoḥ \|

saṃsṛṣṭaṃ triṣu saṃsarga saṃśuddhe vasanādinā \|\| 56 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 478\]

ṭacatuṣkam

ucciṅgaṭastṛṇagaḍamatsye(2) kopanapūruṣe \|

karahāṭaḥ śiphākande padmasya madanadrume \|\| 57 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 479\]

syāt kāryapuṭaḥ kṛpaṇonmattānarthakareṣu ca \|

(1) karpaṭe-kha. \| ; (2) matsyakopanayoḥ pumān-kha. \|

kāmakūṭastu veśyāyāḥ priyavibhramayoḥ pumān \|\| 58 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 480\]

kuṭannaṭaṃ tu kaivarte mustake puṃsi śoṇake \|

kuṇḍakīṭastu cārvākavacanābhijñapūruṣe \|\| 59 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 481\]

patitabrāhmaṇīputradāsīkāmukayorapi \|

khaḍgārīṭastu phalakāsidhārāvratadhāriṇoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 482\]

gāḍhamuṣṭiḥ kṛpāṇe nā kṛpaṇe tvabhidheyavat \|

cakravāṭaḥ kriyārohe paryyante ca śikhātarau \|\| 61 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 483\]

catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ kalāsaṃkhyābhedayorbahvṛci striyām \|

nārakīṭo'śmakīṭe syāt svadattāśāvihantari \|\| 62 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 63 \[unit 484\]

parapuṣṭaḥ parabhṛte vānarāryāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

pratikṛṣṭaṃ mataṃ guhye dvirāvṛttyā ca karṣite \|\| 63 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 64 \[unit 485\]

pratisṛṣṭaḥ preṣite syāt pratyākhyāte ca vācyavat \|

varkarāṭaḥ kaṭākṣe syāttaruṇādityarociṣi \|\| 64 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / 65 \[unit 486\]

nārīpayodharotsaṅgakāntadattanakhakṣate \|

śipiviṣṭastu khalatau śive duścarmaṇi smṛtaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭacatuṣkam / fragment 487

atha śrutikaṭaḥ prāñcallohe'hau pāpaśodhane \|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam / 66 \[unit 488\]

ṭapañcakam

daśanocchiṣṭaḥ puṃsi syānniśvāsādharacumbayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## ṭāntavargaḥ / ṭapañcakam / fragment 489

iti ṭāntavargaḥ \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhaikam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhaikam / fragment 490

ṭhaikam

ṭho maṇḍale candrabimbe śūnye ca lokagocare \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 1 \[unit 491\]

ṭhadvikam

kaṭho munau tadākhyātavedādhyetṛjñayoḥ svare \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 2 \[unit 492\]

kaṇṭho gale sannidhāne dhvanau madanapādape \|

kāṣṭhā dāruharidrāyāṃ kālamānaprakarṣayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 3 \[unit 493\]

sthānamātre diśi ca strī dāruṇi syānnapuṃsakam \|

kuṣṭhaṃ roge puṣkare'strī kuṇṭho'karmaṇyamūrkhayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 4 \[unit 494\]

koṣṭhaḥ kusūle cātmīye madhye kukṣergṛhasya ca \|

goṣṭhaṃ gosthānake goṣṭhī sabhāsaṃlāpayoḥ striyām \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 5 \[unit 495\]

jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhe'tivṛddhe ca triṣu māsāntare pumān \|

jyeṣṭhā tu gṛhagodhā''khyajantunakṣatrabhedayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 6 \[unit 496\]

niṣṭhā niṣpattināśāntayāñcānirvahaṇeṣu ca \|

praṣṭhastriṣvagrage śreṣṭhe puṃsi cāṇḍālikauṣadhau \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 7 \[unit 497\]

pāṭhaśca paṭhane khyāte viddhakarṇyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

pṛṣṭhaṃ paramagātre'pi dehasyāvayavāntare \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 8 \[unit 498\]

vaṇṭhaḥ syādakṛtodvāhe kharve kuntāyudhe'pi ca \|

śaṭho madhyasthapuruṣe dhūrttadhūstūrayorapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / 9 \[unit 499\]

śreṣṭho vare kubere ca śoṭho murkhe'lase'pi ca \|

ṣaṣṭhī kātyāyanītithyostriṣu ṣaṇṇāṃ ca pūraṇe \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhadvikam / fragment 500

haṭhaḥ syāt prasabhe pṛśnyāṃ heṭho bādhāpiheṭhayoḥ \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 10 \[unit 501\]

ṭhatrikam

apaṣṭhuḥ puṃsi kāle ca vāme syādanyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 11 \[unit 502\]

ambaṣṭho deśabhede'pi viprādvaiśyāsute'pi ca \|

ambaṣṭhā cāmlaloṇyāṃ syāt pāṭhāyūthikayorapi \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 12 \[unit 503\]

kamaṭhaḥ kacchape puṃsi bhāṇḍabhede napuṃsakam \|

kaniṣṭho'tiyuvātyalpānuje strī dubalāṅgulau \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 13 \[unit 504\]

jaraṭhaḥ karkaśe pāṇḍau kaṭhine'pyabhidheyavat \|

narmaṭhaścibuke ṣiḍge pratiṣṭhā gaurave kṣitau \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 14 \[unit 505\]

sthāne ca yāganiṣpatticaturakṣarapadyayoḥ \|

prakoṣṭho maṇibandhasya kūrparasyāntare'pi ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 15 \[unit 506\]

bhūpakakṣāntare'pi syādvariṣṭhaṃ marice'pi ca \|

tāmre klībaṃ tittirau nā varorutamayostriṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / 16 \[unit 507\]

makuṣṭho vrīhibhede nā manthare punaranyavat \|

laghiṣṭho'tyalpake bhele vaikuṇṭhaḥ kṛṣṇaśakrayoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhatrikam / fragment 508

śrīkaṇṭho deśabhidyugre sādhiṣṭho'tidṛḍhārthayoḥ \|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 17 \[unit 509\]

ṭhacatuṣkam

kalakaṇṭhaḥ kaladhvāne haṃse pārāvate pike \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 18 \[unit 510\]

kālakaṇṭhaśca dātyūhe kalaviṅke ca khañjane \|

mayūre pītasāre ca syāt khaṇḍaparaśau pumān \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 19 \[unit 511\]

kālapṛṣṭhaṃ karṇacāpe puṃsi kaṅkavihaṅgame \|

syāddantaśaṭho jambīre kapitthe karmaraṅgake \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 20 \[unit 512\]

nāgaraṅge'pi ca pumān syāccāṅgeryāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

pūtikāṣṭhaṃ ca saraladevadārumahīruhoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / 21 \[unit 513\]

sūtrakaṇṭhaḥ pumān vipre khañjarīṭakapotayoḥ \|

hārikaṇṭhaḥ pike puṃsi hārānvitagale triṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṭhāntavargaḥ / ṭhacatuṣkam / fragment 514

iti ṭhāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍaikam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍaikam / fragment 515

ḍaikam

ḍaḥ pumān vāḍavāgnau syāḍḍākinyāṃ strī nigadyate \|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 1 \[unit 516\]

ḍadvikam

aṇḍaṃ muṣke ca peśyāṃ syādiḍā tu budhayoṣiti \|\| 1 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 2 \[unit 517\]

saurabheyyāṃ ca vacane vasumatyāmapi striyām \|

kāṇḍaḥ stambe taruskandhe bāṇe'vasaranīrayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 3 \[unit 518\]

kutsite vṛkṣabhinnāḍīvṛnde rahasi na striyām \|

krīḍā keliprakāre syāt khelāvajñānayorapi \|\| 3 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 4 \[unit 519\]

kuṇḍamagnyālaye mānabhede devajalāśaye \|

kuṇḍī kamaṇḍalau jārāt pativatnīsute pumān \|\| 4 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 5 \[unit 520\]

piṭhare tu na nā kṣveḍo dhvanau karṇāmaye viṣe \|

kṣveḍā vaṃśaśalākāyāṃ siṃhanāde ca yoṣiti \|\| 5 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 6 \[unit 521\]

lohitārkaparṇaphale ghoṣapuṣpe napuṃsakam \|

durāsade ca kuṭile vācyaliṅgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 7 \[unit 522\]

kroḍaḥ śanau śūkare nā na pumānaṅkarakṣasoḥ \|

khaṇḍo'strī śakale nekṣuvikāramaṇidoṣayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 8 \[unit 523\]

khaṇḍaḥ pānāntare bhede gaṇḍaḥ syāt puṃsi khaḍgini \|

grahayogaprabhede ca vīthyaṅge piṭake'pi ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 9 \[unit 524\]

cihnavīrakapoleṣu hayabhūṣaṇabudbude \|

gaḍuḥ pṛṣṭhaguḍe kubje gaḍo mīnāntarāyayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 10 \[unit 525\]

guḍaḥ syād golake hastisannāhekṣuvikārayoḥ \|

guḍā snuhyāṃ ca kathitā guḍikāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 10 \|\|
