## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 11 \[unit 526\]

goṇḍaḥ pāmarajātau ca vṛddhanābhau ca tadvati \|

caṇḍo nā tintiḍīvṛkṣe yamakiṅkaradaityayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 12 \[unit 527\]

caṇḍī kātyāyanīdevyāṃ hiṃsrakopanayoṣitoḥ \|

caṇḍā dhanaharīśaṅkhapuṣpyostriṣvatikopane \|\| 12 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 13 \[unit 528\]

tīvre'pi cūḍā vaḍabhau śikhāyāṃ bāhubhūṣaṇe \|

coḍaḥ prāvaraṇe bhūmni deśabhede jaḍā striyām \|\| 13 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 14 \[unit 529\]

śūkaśiṃbyāṃ himagrastamūkāprajñeṣu tu triṣu \|

tāḍastu tāḍane ghoṣe muṣṭimeyatṛṇādiṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 15 \[unit 530\]

tāḍī patradrume cātha daṇḍo'strī laguḍe pumān \|

vyūhabhede prakāṇḍe'śve koṇamanthānayorapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 16 \[unit 531\]

sainye kāle mānabhede caṇḍāṃśoḥ pāripārśvike \|

dame yame'bhimāne ca nāḍī nāle vraṇāntare \|\| 16 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 17 \[unit 532\]

śirāyāṃ gaṇḍadūrvāyāṃ caryāyāṃ kuhanasya ca \|

tathā ṣaṭkṣaṇakāle'pi nīḍaṃ sthānakulāyayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 18 \[unit 533\]

paṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍe dhiyi strī syāt pāṇḍurnā nṛpatau site \|

piṇḍo bole bale sāndre dehāgāraikadeśayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 19 \[unit 534\]

dehamātre nivāpe ca golasihlakayorapi \|

āḍrapuṣpe ca puṃsi syāyāt klībamājīvanāyasoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 20 \[unit 535\]

piṇḍī tu piṇḍītagare'lābukharjūrabhedayoḥ \|

pīḍā kṛpāśiromālā'pamardasaraladruṣu \|\| 20 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 21 \[unit 536\]

bhāṇḍaṃ pātre vaṇiṅmūladhane bhūṣā'śvabhūṣayoḥ \|

maṇḍaḥ pañcāṅgule śākabhede klībaṃ tu mastuni \|\| 21 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 22 \[unit 537\]

āmalakyāṃ striyāṃ maṇḍāthāstriyāṃ sārapicchayoḥ \|

muṇḍo daityāntare rāhugrahe nā muṇḍite triṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 23 \[unit 538\]

muṇḍā muṇḍīrikāyāṃ syāt striyāmastrī tu mūrddhani \|

raṇḍā mūṣikaparṇyāṃ ca vidhavāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 23 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 24 \[unit 539\]

roḍaḥ kṣode bhavet puṃsi tṛpte tu vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

(1)raṇḍā tu pāṃsulāyāṃ strī triṣu hastādivarjjite \|\| 24 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 25 \[unit 540\]

vyāḍo hiṃsrapaśau sarpe śuṇḍā pānagṛhe matā \|

apyambuhastinīveśyāhastihastamurāsu ca \|\| 25 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍadvikam / 26 \[unit 541\]

śauṇḍo matte ca vikhyātaḥ pippalyāṃ ca bhavet striyām \|

ṣaṇḍaṃ padmādisaṅghāte na strī syād gāpatau pumān \|\| 26 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 27 \[unit 542\]

ḍatrikam

karaṇḍo madhukośāsikāraṇḍeṣu dalāḍhake \|

kuṣmāṇḍyumāyāṃ strī puṃsi karkārau ca gaṇāntare \|\| 27 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 28 \[unit 543\]

bhrūṇāntare'tha kodaṇḍaṃ cāpe nā nīvṛdantare \|

bhruvyatho garuḍaṃ kṣveḍamantre marakate'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 29 \[unit 544\]

taraṇḍo baḍiśīsūtrabaddhakoṣṭhādike plave \|

naukāyāmapi na strī syāttintiḍīḍimbaciñcayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 30 \[unit 545\]

drāviḍo deśabhijjāte saṃkhyābhidvedhamukhyayoḥ \|

nirguṇḍī nīlaśephālyāṃ sindhuvāradrume'pi ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 31 \[unit 546\]

pracaṇḍo durvahe śvetakaravīre pratāpini \|

prakāṇḍo na strī viṭape mūlaśākhāntare taroḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 32 \[unit 547\]

śaste picaṇḍa udare paśoravayave pumān \|

pūtyaṇḍo gandhakīṭe'pi tathā jantvantare pumān \|\| 32 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 33 \[unit 548\]

(1) vaṇḍā-kha. \|

varaṇḍo'pyantarāvedau samūhamukharogayoḥ \|

vāruṇḍī dvārapiṇḍyāṃ strī phaṇināṃ rājake pumān \|\| 33 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 34 \[unit 549\]

na striyāṃ sekapātre ca male'kṣṇaḥ śravaṇasya ca \|

bheruṇḍā devatābhedayakṣiṇyantarayoḥ striyām \|\| 34 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 35 \[unit 550\]

bhayānake vācyavatsyānmārttaṇḍaḥ kroḍasūryayoḥ \|

māruṇḍo'ṇḍe bhujaṅgīnāṃ mārge gomayamaṇḍale \|\| 35 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 36 \[unit 551\]

varaṇḍā sārikāvarttiśastrabhede ca yoṣiti \|

vitaṇḍā vādabhede syāt kaccīśāke śilāhvaye \|\| 36 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍatrikam / 37 \[unit 552\]

karavīryāmapi strī syācchikhaṇḍo barhacūḍayoḥ \|

(1)śaraṇḍastu pumān dhūrte śaraṭe bhūṣaṇāntare \|\| 37 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 553\]

ḍacatuṣkam

(2)apogaṇḍastu balibhe vikalāṅgaśiśāvapi \|

(3)atigaṇḍo yogabhede bṛhadgaṇḍe tu vācyavat \|\| 38 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 39 \[unit 554\]

cakravāḍo'dribhede syāccakravāḍaṃ tu maṇḍale \|

jalaruṇḍo jalāvarte payoreṇau bhujaṅgame \|\| 39 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / 40 \[unit 555\]

devatāḍaḥ saiṃhikeye jīmūte ca hutāśane \|

atha vātahuḍā(4) vātyāvātaśoṇitayorapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## ḍāntavargaḥ / ḍacatuṣkam / fragment 556

picchilasphoṭikāyāṃ ca vāmāyāmapi yoṣiti \|

iti ḍāntavargaḥ \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhaikam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhaikam / fragment 557

ḍhaikam

ḍho ḍhakkāyāṃ pumānuktaḥ śuni tasya ca lāṅgule \|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam / 1 \[unit 558\]

ḍhadvikam

gūḍhaṃ rahasi guhye ca na dvayoḥ saṃvṛte triṣu \|\| 1 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam / 2 \[unit 559\]

dṛḍhaṃ sthūle nitānte ca pragāḍhabalavatyapi \|

(1) saraṇḍastu pumān dhūrte saraṭe-kha. \| ; (2) apogaṇḍaṃ tu-kha, \| ; (3) idaṃ padyārthaṃ kha. pustake nāsti \| ; (4) vātathuḍā-kha. \|

māḍhiḥ strī patrabhaṅge ca dainyasyāpi prakāśane \|\| 2 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam / 3 \[unit 560\]

mūḍhastu tandrite bāle rāḍhā strī suhmaśobhayoḥ \|

rūḍhaṃ jāte prasiddhe ca bāḍhe dṛḍhapratijñayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam / 4 \[unit 561\]

vyūḍhaḥ saṃhatavinyastapṛthuleṣvabhidheyavat \|

ṣoḍhā nā bhārike sūte ṣaṇḍhaḥ syāt puṃsi gopatau \|\| 4 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhadvikam / 5 \[unit 562\]

ākṛṣṭāṇḍe varṣavare tṛtīyāprakṛtāvapi \|

soḍhā titikṣāsaṃyukte śakte cāpyabhidheyavat \|\| 5 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 6 \[unit 563\]

ḍhatrikam

adhyūḍhā kṛtasāpatnyanāryāmadhyūḍha īśvare \|

āṣāḍho vratināṃ daṇḍe māse malayaparvate \|\| 6 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 7 \[unit 564\]

strī pūrṇimāyāmālīḍhaṃ pādanyāse'śite triṣu \|

udū(1)ḍhamūḍhe sthūle syādupoḍho nikaṭoḍhayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 8 \[unit 565\]

pragāḍhaḥ kṛcchradṛḍhayoḥ pramīḍho mūtrite ghane \|

prarūḍho jaṭhare baddhamūle vārūḍha ityayam \|\| 8 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 9 \[unit 566\]

vastrāñcale kapāṭe'gnau pañjare śambale'pi ca \|

virūḍho'ṅkurite jāte vigūḍho garhite'pi ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhatrikam / 10 \[unit 567\]

gupte'pi triṣu saṃrūḍhaḥ prauḍhe cāṅkurite triṣu \|

samūḍhaḥ puñjite bhugne sadyojāte'nupaplute \|\| 10 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam / 11 \[unit 568\]

ḍhacatuṣkam

adhyārūḍhaṃ samārūḍhe'bhyadhike cābhidheyavat \|

khaṭvārūḍhaḥ śrite khaṭvāmavinīte ca vācyavat \|\| 11 \|\|

## ḍhāntavargaḥ / ḍhacatuṣkam / fragment 569

pratyālīḍhaṃ tu caraṇanyāsabhede'śite triṣu \|

iti ḍhāntavargaḥ \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaikam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaikam / 1 \[unit 570\]

ṇaikam

ṇaḥ pumān bindudeve syād bhūṣaṇe guṇavarjite \|

( 1 ) udūḍha ūḍhe-kha. \|

pānīyanilaye'pīti kecidūcurvipaścitaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 2 \[unit 571\]

ṇadvikam

aṇurvrīhiviśeṣe syātpuṃsi sūkṣme'bhidheyavat \|

aṇirāṇivadakṣāgrakīlāśrisīmasu dvayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 3 \[unit 572\]

uṣṇo grīṣme pumān dakṣāśītayoranyaliṅgakaḥ \|

ūrṇā meṣādilomni syādantarāvartake bhruvoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 4 \[unit 573\]

karṇaḥ pṛthājyeṣṭhasute suvarṇālau śrutāvapi \|

kṣaṇaḥ parvotsavavyāpāreṣu māne'pyanehasaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 5 \[unit 574\]

kaṇā jīrakakumbhīramakṣikāpippalīṣu ca \|

kaṇo'tisūkṣme dhānyāṃśe kāṇaḥ kākaikacakṣuṣoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 6 \[unit 575\]

kīrṇaṃ (1)datte ca vikṣipte hiṃsite'pyabhidheyavat \|

kuṇistunnakavṛkṣe nā kukare tvabhidheyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 7 \[unit 576\]

kṛṣṇaḥ satyavatīputre vāyase keśave'rjune \|

kṛṣṇā syād draupadīnīlīkaṇādrākṣāsu yoṣiti \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 8 \[unit 577\]

mecake vācyaliṅgaṃ syāt klībe maricalohayoḥ \|

koṇo vādyaprabhede syādvīṇādīnāṃ ca vādane \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 9 \[unit 578\]

ekadeśe gṛhādīnāmaśrau ca laguḍe'pi ca \|

gaṇaḥ pramathasaṃkhyaughe caṇḍāsainyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 10 \[unit 579\]

guṇo maurvyāmapradhāne rūpādau sūda indriye \|

tyāgaśauryādisattvādisandhyādyāvṛttirajjuṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 11 \[unit 580\]

śuklādāvapi vaṭyāṃ ca geṣṇurnā gāyane naṭe \|

ghrāṇaṃ klībaṃ nāsikāyāṃ ghrāte syādvācyaliṅgakam \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 12 \[unit 581\]

ghṛṇā jugupsākṛpayoratha cūrṇī kapardake \|

cūrṇo dhūlau kṣārabhede cūrṇānivāsayuktiṣu \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 13 \[unit 582\]

jarṇaścandre ca vṛkṣe ca jiṣṇurnā vāsave'rjune \|

jitvare vācyavajjīrṇaṃ paripakvapurāṇayoḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 14 \[unit 583\]

(1) channe-kha. \|

jhaṇiḥ kramukabhede'pi duṣṭadaivaśrutau striyām \|

trāṇaṃ tu trāyamāṇāyāṃ rakṣaṇe rakṣite triṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 15 \[unit 584\]

tīkṣṇaṃ sāmudralavaṇe viṣalohājimuṣkake \|

klībaṃ, yavāgraje puṃsi tigmātmatyāginoḥ triṣu \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 16 \[unit 585\]

tūṇī nīlyāṃ niṣaṅge nā tṛṣṇā syāttarṣalipsayoḥ \|

dīrṇaṃ vidārite bhīte druṇaṃ cāpe'lini druṇaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 17 \[unit 586\]

druṇyambudroṇīkacchapyordeṣṇurdātari durgame \|

droṇo'striyāmāḍhake syādāḍhavāpacatuṣṭaye \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 18 \[unit 587\]

pumān kṛpīpatau dagdhakāke strī nīvṛdantare \|

tathā kāṣṭhāmbuvāhinyāṃ gavādanyāmapīṣyate \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 19 \[unit 588\]

paṇo varāṭamāne syānmūlye kārṣāpaṇe glahe \|

krayyaśākāṭṭikādyūtavyavahāre bhṛtau dhane \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 20 \[unit 589\]

parṇaṃ patre kiṃśuke nā pārṣṇiḥ syādunmadastriyām \|

striyāṃ dvayoḥ sainyapṛṣṭhe pādagranthyadhare'pi ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 21 \[unit 590\]

prāṇo hṛnmārute bole kāvyajīve'nile bale \|

puṃliṅgaḥ pūrite vācyaliṅgaḥ pumbhūmni cāsuṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 22 \[unit 591\]

pūrṇaḥ śakte samagre nā pūrite tvabhidheyavat \|

phāṇirguḍe karambe ca striyāṃ bāṇī tu yoṣiti \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 23 \[unit 592\]

vyūtāvapi sarasvatyāṃ bhrūṇaḥ strīgarbhaḍimbhayoḥ \|

maṇiḥ strīpuṃsayoraśmajātau muktādike'pi ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 24 \[unit 593\]

kaṇṭhadeśastane'jāyaliṅgāgre'liñjare'pi ca \|

moṇaḥ śuṣkaphale nakre makṣikā'hikaraṇḍayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 25 \[unit 594\]

raṇaḥ koṇe kvaṇe puṃsi samare puṃnapuṃsakam \|

reṇuḥ strīpuṃsayordhūlau puṃliṅgaḥ parpaṭe punaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 26 \[unit 595\]

varṇo dvijādiśuklādiyaśoguṇakathāsu ca \|

stutau nā na striyāṃ bhedarūpākṣaravilepane \|\| 26 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 27 \[unit 596\]

bāṇaḥ syād gostane daityabhede kevalakāṇḍayoḥ \|

bāṇā tu bāṇamūle strī nīlajhiṇṭyāṃ punardvayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 28 \[unit 597\]

vīṇā vidyuti vallakyāṃ vṛṣṇiḥ pākhaṇḍacandrayoḥ \|

triṣu nā yādave meṣe veṇī keśasya bandhane \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 29 \[unit 598\]

nadyāderantare devatāle(1) veṇurnupāntare \|

tvaksāre'pi ca puṃsi syācchāṇo māsacatuṣṭaye \|\| 29 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 30 \[unit 599\]

lohādīnāṃ ca nikaṣe śāṇī prāvaraṇāntare \|

śrāṇaṃ pakve yavāgvāṃ strī śīrṇaṃ tanuviśīrṇayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 31 \[unit 600\]

śreṇiḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ paṅktau samānaśilpisaṃhatau \|

śoṇaḥ kṛśānau śyonāke lohitāśve nade pumān \|\| 31 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇadvikam / 32 \[unit 601\]

triṣu kākanadacchāye sthāṇuḥ kīle hare pumān \|

astrī dhruve'tha sthūṇā syāt sūrmyāṃ stambhe gṛhasya ca \|\| 32 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 33 \[unit 602\]

ṇatrikam

aruṇo'vyaktaroge'rke sandhyārāge'rkasārathau \|

niḥśabde kapile kuṣṭhabhede nā guṇini triṣu \|\| 33 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 34 \[unit 603\]

aruṇā'tiviṣāśyāmāmañjiṣṭhātrivṛtāsu ca \|

araṇirvahnimanthe nā dvayornirmanthyadāruṇi \|\| 34 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 35 \[unit 604\]

abhīkṣṇaṃ tu bhṛśe nityatadyuktakriyayostriṣu \|

indrāṇī karaṇe strīṇāṃ paulomīsindhuvārayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 36 \[unit 605\]

īriṇaṃ tūṣare śūnye'pīkṣaṇaṃ darśane dṛśi \|

ūṣaṇaṃ marice klībaṃ kaṇāyāmūṣaṇā striyām \|\| 36 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 37 \[unit 606\]

eṣaṇī vraṇamārgānusāriṇyāṃ ca tulābhidi \|

karuṇastu rase vṛkṣe kṛpāyāṃ karuṇā matā \|\| 37 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 38 \[unit 607\]

kattṛṇaṃ tṛṇabhitpṛśnyoḥ karaṇaṃ hetukarmaṇoḥ \|

(2)vīṇajādau hastalepe nṛtyagītaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 39 \[unit 608\]

kriyābhedendriyakṣetrakāyasaṃveśaneṣu ca \|

kāyasthe sādhane klībaṃ puṃsi śūdrāviśoḥ sute \|\| 39 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 40 \[unit 609\]

kalyāṇamakṣayasvarge maṅgale'pi napuṃsakam \|

(1) devatāḍe-kha. \| ; (2) bālavādī-kha. \|

kaṅkaṇaṃ karabhūṣāyāṃ sūtramaṇḍanayorapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 41 \[unit 610\]

kareṇurgajayoṣāyāṃ striyāṃ puṃsi mataṅgaje \|

kārmaṇaṃ mantratantrādiyojane karmaṭhe'pi ca \|\| 41 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 42 \[unit 611\]

kākiṇī paṇaturyāṃśe mānadaṇḍe ca dṛśyate \|

kṛṣṇalaikavarāṭyoḥ syādunmānasyāṃśake'pi ca \|\| 42 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 43 \[unit 612\]

kāraṇaṃ karaṇe hetuvadhayośca napuṃsakam \|

strī yātanāyāṃ kurvāṇo bhṛtyakārakayostriṣu \|\| 43 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 44 \[unit 613\]

kṛpāṇaḥ khāḍage churikākarttaryorapi yoṣiti \|

kṛpaṇastu kṛmau puṃsi mandakutsitayostriṣu \|\| 44 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 45 \[unit 614\]

kṣepaṇaṃ preraṇe naukādaṇḍajālabhidoḥ striyām \|

grahaṇaṃ svīkārādarakaroparāgopalabdhivandiṣu (ca) \|\| 45 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 46 \[unit 615\]

syād grāmaṇīḥ pradhāne'dhipatau triṣu nāpite puṃsi \|

grāmīṇā nīlikāyāṃ strī grāmodbhūte'bhidheyavat \|\| 46 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 47 \[unit 616\]

gokarṇo'śvatare sarpe sāraṅge ca gaṇāntare \|

aṅguṣṭhānāmikonmāne gokarṇī mūrvikauṣadhau \|\| 47 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 48 \[unit 617\]

caraṇo'strī bahvṛcādau mūle gotre pade'pi ca \|

bhramaṇe bhakṣaṇe cā(1)pi napuṃsaka udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 49 \[unit 618\]

jaraṇaṃ jīrake(2)'jājīhiṅgusauvarcale'pi ca \|

taruṇaṃ kubjapuṣpe nā rubuke yūni tu triṣu \|\| 49 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 50 \[unit 619\]

taraṇirdyumaṇau puṃsi kumārīnaukayoḥ striyām \|

dakṣiṇo dakṣiṇodbhūtasaralacchandavarttiṣu \|\| 50 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 51 \[unit 620\]

ārāme triṣu yajñādividhidāne diśi striyām \|

draviṇaṃ na dvayorvitte kāñcane ca parākrame \|\| 51 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 52 \[unit 621\]

dāruṇo rasabhede nā triṣu tu syādbhayāvahe \|

drughaṇo mudgare'pi syāt druhiṇe ca paraśvadhe \|\| 52 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 53 \[unit 622\]

durvarṇaṃ triṣvasadvarṇe klībamaileyarūpyayoḥ \|

(1) vāpi-kha. \| ; (2) jīrake'pi syāt kṛṣṇa0-kha. \|

daurvīṇaṃ mṛṣṭaparṇaṃ syād dūrvāyāṃ svarase'pi ca \|\| 53 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 54 \[unit 623\]

dharmaṇastu pumān vṛkṣabhedasarpaprabhedayoḥ \|

dharaṇaṃ mānabhede'pi dhāraṇe dharaṇī bhuvi \|\| 54 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 55 \[unit 624\]

dharṣaṇaṃ syāt paribhave rate'satyāṃ tu dharṣiṇī \|

dharuṇaḥ sammate nīre svarloke parameṣṭhini \|\| 55 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 56 \[unit 625\]

dhāraṇī nāḍikāyāṃ syād buddhoktamantrabhidyapi \|

dhāraṇā'ṅge ca yogasya na puṃsi syād vidhāraṇe \|\| 56 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 57 \[unit 626\]

dhiṣaṇastridaśācārye dhiṣaṇā dhiyi yoṣiti \|

niḥśreṇiradhirohiṇyāṃ kharjūrīpādape striyām \|\| 57 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 58 \[unit 627\]

niryāṇaṃ vāraṇāpāṅgadeśe mokṣe'dhvanirgame \|

nirmāṇaṃ nirmitau sāre samañjase napuṃsakam \|\| 58 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 59 \[unit 628\]

nirvāṇamastaṅgamane nirvṛtau gajamajjane \|

saṅgame'pyapavarge ca pravaṇo nā catuṣpathe \|\| 59 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 60 \[unit 629\]

kramanimnamahībhāgodaraprahveṣu ca triṣu \|

praghaṇastāmrakumbhe syādalinde lohamudgare \|\| 60 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 61 \[unit 630\]

pramāṇaṃ nityamaryādāśāstreṣu satyavādini \|

iyattāyāṃ ca hetau ca klībaikatve pramātari \|\| 61 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 62 \[unit 631\]

patrorṇaṃ dhautakauśeye klībaṃ syācchoṇake pumān \|

pakṣiṇī pūrṇimāyāṃ syādvihaṅgyāṃ śākinībhidi \|\| 62 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 63 \[unit 632\]

āgāmivarttamānāharyuktarātrāvapi striyām \|

praveṇiḥ strī kuthāveṇyoḥ purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇe \|\| 63 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 64 \[unit 633\]

paṇe puṃsi triṣu pratne pūraṇaḥ pūrake triṣu \|

klībaṃ piṇḍaprabhede ca pūraṇī śālmalidrume \|\| 64 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 65 \[unit 634\]

paṭārambhakasūtreṣu prokṣaṇaṃ secane vadhe \|

varuṇastarubhede'psu paścimāśāpatāvapi \|\| 65 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 66 \[unit 635\]

varaṇastu pumān tiktaśākaprākārayorapi \|

klībaṃ kanyādivaraṇe veṣṭane strī nadībhidi \|\| 66 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 67 \[unit 636\]

vāraṇaṃ pratiṣedhe syādvāraṇastu mataṅgaje \|

brāhmaṇaṃ brahmasaṅghāte vedabhāge napuṃsakam \|\| 67 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 68 \[unit 637\]

bhūmideve tu puṃliṅgaḥ phañjikāpṛkkayoḥ striyām \|

vāruṇī gaṇḍadūrvāyāṃ pratīcīsurayorapi \|\| 68 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 69 \[unit 638\]

bharaṇī ghoṣake ṛkṣe bharaṇaṃ vetane bhṛtau \|

bhramaṇī kāruṇḍikāyāṃ krīḍādyāyāmadhīśituḥ \|\| 69 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 70 \[unit 639\]

bhīṣaṇo rase sallakyāṃ nā gāḍhe dāruṇe triṣu \|

masṛṇo'karkaśe snigdhe triṣūmāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 70 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 71 \[unit 640\]

matkuṇo nirviṣāṇebhe niḥśmaśrupuruṣe'pi ca \|

uddeśe nārikele ca mārgaṇo yācake śare \|\| 71 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 72 \[unit 641\]

yācñā'nveṣaṇayoḥ klībaṃ yantraṇaṃ rakṣaṇe smṛtam \|

bandhane syānniyamane ravaṇaḥ śabdane khare \|\| 72 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 73 \[unit 642\]

ramaṇaṃ paṭolamūle nāryāṃ strī nā dhave smare \|

roṣaṇaḥ pārade hemaharṣaṇopalayoḥ pumān \|\| 73 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 74 \[unit 643\]

krodhane vācyaliṅgaḥ syādrohiṇī kaṇṭharugbhidi \|

taḍitkaṭambharāsomavalkeṣu lohitāgavoḥ \|\| 74 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 75 \[unit 644\]

lavaṇaṃ saindhavādo nā sindhurakṣobhido rase \|

tadyukte vācyaliṅgaḥ syānnadībhedatviṣoḥ striyām \|\| 75 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 76 \[unit 645\]

lakṣaṇaṃ nāmni cihne ca sārasyāṃ lakṣaṇā kvacit \|

lakṣmaṇā tvoṣadhībhede sārasyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 76 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 77 \[unit 646\]

rāmabhrātari puṃsi syāt saśrīke cābhidheyavat \|

viṣāṇī kṣīrakākolyāmajaśṛṅgyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 77 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 78 \[unit 647\]

kuṣṭhanāmauṣadhe klībaṃ paśuśṛṅgebhadantayoḥ \|

vipaṇiḥ paṇyavīthyāṃ ca bhavedāpaṇapaṇyayoḥ \|\| 78 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 79 \[unit 648\]

śaraṇaṃ gṛharakṣitrorvadharakṣaṇayorapi \|

striyāṃ sudarśanāmāṃsyormuṇḍīrīśabarībhidoḥ \|\| 79 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 80 \[unit 649\]

śravaṇaṃ ca śrutau karṇe nakṣatre na napuṃsakam \|

śramaṇo yatibhede nā nindyajīvini tu triṣu \|\| 80 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 81 \[unit 650\]

śrāvaṇo māsi pāṣaṇḍe dadhyālyāṃ śrāvaṇā striyām \|

śiṅghāṇaṃ kācapātre ca lohanāsikayormale \|\| 81 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 82 \[unit 651\]

śrīparṇī kāśmarīkumbhyoḥ klībaṃ padmāgnimanthayoḥ \|

saṅkīrṇaṃ nicite'pi syādaśuddhe cāpi bhedya(1)vat \|\| 82 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 83 \[unit 652\]

saraṇiḥ paṅktau mārge ca sāraṇo rākṣasāntare \|

rugbhede nā prasāraṇyāṃ svalpanadyāṃ ca sāraṇī \|\| 83 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 84 \[unit 653\]

suṣeṇaḥ karamarde syādviṣṇusugrīvavaidyayoḥ \|

suvarṇo nā svarṇakarṣe suvarṇākhyamakhāntare \|\| 84 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 85 \[unit 654\]

śrīkṛṣṇāguruṇi klībe kāñcane haricandane \|

suparṇaḥ svarṇacūḍe ca garuḍe kṛtamālake \|\| 85 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 86 \[unit 655\]

suparṇā kamalinyāṃ ca vainateyasya mātari \|

hareṇurnā satīle strī reṇukākulayoṣitoḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 87 \[unit 656\]

haraṇaṃ yautakādīnāṃ dravye bhuje hṛtāvapi \|

hariṇaḥ puṃsi sāraṅge viṣade tvabhidheyavat \|\| 87 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 88 \[unit 657\]

hariṇī haritāyāṃ ca nārībhidvṛttabhedayoḥ \|

suvarṇapratimāyāṃ ca harpaṇo'kṣirugantare \|\| 88 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇatrikam / 89 \[unit 658\]

harṣake yogabhede ca śrāddhadeve'pi ca kvacit \|

hiraṇaṃ retasi svarṇe varāṭe ca napuṃsakam \|\| 89 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 90 \[unit 659\]

ṇacatuṣkam

aṅgāriṇī hasantyāṃ ca bhāskaratyaktadiśyapi \|

atharvaṇirnā'tharvajñabrāhmaṇe ca purodhasi \|\| 90 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 91 \[unit 660\]

ātarpaṇaṃ prīṇane syānmaṅgalālepane'pi ca \|

ārohaṇaṃ syāt sopāne samārohe prarohaṇe \|\| 91 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 92 \[unit 661\]

utkṣepaṇaṃ tu vyajane dhānyamardanavastuni \|

syāduddharaṇamuddhāravāntānnonmūlaneṣvapi \|\| 92 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 93 \[unit 662\]

kārṣāpaṇo'strī kārṣike paṇaṣoḍaśake'pi ca \|

atha kāmaguṇo rāge viṣayābhogayorapi \|\| 93 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 94 \[unit 663\]

(1) vācyavat-kha. \|

cīrṇapaṇastu nimbe'pi kharjūrībhūruhe pumān \|

cūḍāmaṇiḥ śiroratne kākaciñcāphale pumān \|\| 94 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 95 \[unit 664\]

juhu(6)vāṇo'pi cādhvaryāvapi syājjātavedasi \|

taṇḍurīṇaḥ kīṭamātre barbare taṇḍulodake \|\| 95 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 96 \[unit 665\]

trāyamāṇā vārṣike strī rakṣyamāṇe'bhidheyavat \|

tailaparṇī malayaje śrīvāse sihlake'pi ca \|\| 96 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 97 \[unit 666\]

dākṣāyaṇī tvaparṇāyāmaśvinyādyuḍuṣu striyām \|

atha devamaṇirbhaga'śvāvarte kaustubhe'pi (2)ca \|\| 97 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 98 \[unit 667\]

nārāyaṇo'cyute bhīrugauryornārāyaṇī matā \|

atho nigaraṇaṃ klībaṃ bhojane syādgale pumān \|\| 98 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 99 \[unit 668\]

atha niḥsaraṇaṃ mṛtyūpāyamokṣeṣu nirgame \|

syānnistaraṇamupāye nistāre taraṇe'pi ca \|\| 99 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 100 \[unit 669\]

nirūpaṇaṃ syādāloke vicāre ca nidarśane \|

parparīṇaśca parṇasya śirāyāṃ dyūtakambale \|\| 100 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 101 \[unit 670\]

parṇacūrṇarase'pi syāt parparīṇaṃ tu parvaṇi \|

pravāraṇaṃ niṣedhe syāt kāmyadāne ca na dvayoḥ \|\| 101 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 102 \[unit 671\]

parīraṇaḥ syāt kamaṭhe daṇḍe ca paṭṭaśāṭake \|

parāyaṇamabhīṣṭe syāttatparāśrayayorapi \|\| 102 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 103 \[unit 672\]

paravāṇiḥ pumān dharmādhyakṣavatsarayorapi \|

pārāyaṇaṃ samāsaṅge kārtsnye pāragatāvapi \|\| 103 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 104 \[unit 673\]

pīluparṇī bimbikāyāṃ mūrvāyāmoṣadhībhidi \|

atha puṣkariṇī cebhyāṃ sarojinyāṃ jalāśaye \|\| 104 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 105 \[unit 674\]

madhuparṇī tu gāmbhīryāṃ nīlīsaṃjñauṣadhāvapi \|

mīnāmrīṇastu puṃliṅgo dardurāmre ca khañjane \|\| 105 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 106 \[unit 675\]

raktareṇustu sindūre palāśakorake pumān \|

rāgacūrṇaḥ pumān dantadhāvane makaradhvaje \|\| 106 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 107 \[unit 676\]

(1) juhurāṇo'pi-kha. \| ; (2) nā–kha. \|

rerihāṇastu puṃsi syānmahādeve'mbare'pi ca \|

lambakarṇaḥ smṛto'ṅkoṭhapādape chagale'pi ca \|\| 107 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 108 \[unit 677\]

vidāraṇaṃ viḍambe ca bhede klībaṃ raṇe dvayoḥ \|

atha vaitaraṇī dadyāṃ pretānāṃ yātumātari \|\| 108 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 109 \[unit 678\]

śaravāṇiḥ śaramukhe pāyike śarajīvini \|

pumānatho śikhariṇī rasālavṛttabhedayoḥ \|\| 109 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 110 \[unit 679\]

nārīratne mallikāyāṃ romāvalyāmapi striyām \|

atha saṃsaraṇaṃ klībamasambādhacamūgatau \|\| 110 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / 111 \[unit 680\]

ghaṇṭāpathe ca saṃsāre raṇārambhe ca kutracit \|

samīraṇaḥ syāt pavane pathike ca phaṇijjake \|\| 111 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇacatuṣkam / fragment 681

hastikarṇaścorubūke palāśagaṇabhedayoḥ \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 112 \[unit 682\]

ṇapañcakam

avagrahaṇamityetat pratirodhe'pyanādare \|\| 112 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 113 \[unit 683\]

avatāraṇaṃ bhūtādigrahe vastrāñcale'rcane \|

pravidāraṇamākhyātaṃ samparāye'vadāraṇe \|\| 113 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 114 \[unit 684\]

paribhāṣaṇaṃ sanindopālambhe niyame'pi ca \|

ukto mattavāraṇastu praklinnakaṭakuñjare \|\| 114 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 115 \[unit 685\]

klībaṃ prāsādavīthīnāṃ kundavṛkṣavṛtāvapi \|

maṇḍūkaparṇī mañjiṣṭhābrāhmaṇyornā tu śoṇake \|\| 115 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / 116 \[unit 686\]

roma(1)harṣaṇaṃ romāñce nā sūte ca bibhītake \|

vātarāyaṇa unmatte niṣprayojanapūruṣe \|\| 116 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇapañcakam / fragment 687

kāṇḍe ca karapatre ca kūṭe ca parasaṅkrame \|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭhakam

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭhakam / 117 \[unit 688\]

ṇaṣaṣṭhakam

dohadalakṣaṇamuktaṃ vayasaḥ sandhau ca garbhe ca \|\| 117 \|\|

## ṇāntavargaḥ / ṇaṣaṣṭhakam / fragment 689

yauvanalakṣaṇamapyākhyātaṃ lāvaṇyakucayośca \|

iti ṇāntavargaḥ \|

(1) lomaharṣaṇaṃ-kha. \|

## tāntavargaḥ / taikam

## tāntavargaḥ / taikam / 1 \[unit 690\]

taikam

taścaurāmṛtapuccheṣu kroḍe mlecche ca kutracit \|

apumāṃstaraṇe puṇye kathitaḥ śabdavedibhiḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 2 \[unit 691\]

tadvikam

astaṃ kṣipte'pyavasite triṣu nā paścimācale \|

antaṃ svarūpe nāśe nā na strī śeṣe'ntike triṣu \|\| 2 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 3 \[unit 692\]

ārttiḥ pīḍādhanuṣkoṭyoḥ stryāpto labdhe svasatyayoḥ \|

āptiḥ strī yogasamprāptyoritaṃ smṛte gate triṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 4 \[unit 693\]

ītirḍimbe pravāse'tivṛṣṭyādiṣaṭsu ca striyām \|

uktamekākṣaracchandasyuktaṃ syādbhāṣite triṣu \|\| 4 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 5 \[unit 694\]

ūtiḥ strī rakṣaṇe syūtāvṛtiḥ kalyāṇavartmanoḥ \|

jugupsāsparddhayoḥ strī syādrutamuñchaśile jale \|\| 5 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 6 \[unit 695\]

satye dīpte pūjite syādruturvarṣādiṣaṭsu ca \|

ārtave bhāsi ca pumānetaḥ karbura āgate \|\| 6 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 7 \[unit 696\]

kṣattā śūdrāt kṣatriyāje pratīhāre ca sārathau \|

bhujiṣyātanaye'pi syānniyuktavedhasoḥ pumān \|\| 7 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 8 \[unit 697\]

karttā tu kārake vācyaliṅgo nā parameṣṭhini \|

ṛturyajñe munau puṃsi kāntiḥ śobhecchayoḥ striyām \|\| 8 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 9 \[unit 698\]

kāntā nāryāṃ priyaṅgau strī śobhane triṣu nā dhave \|

lohe ca candrasūryāyaḥparyāyāntaḥśilāsu ca \|\| 9 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 10 \[unit 699\]

kṣitirnivāse medinyāṃ kālabhede kṣaye striyām \|

kīrtiḥ prasādayaśasoryoṣit kunto gavedhikāyāṃ syāt \|\| 10 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 11 \[unit 700\]

prāsāyudhe ca kuntī guggulupṛthayośca śallakyām \|

kṛtaṃ yuge'lamarthe syādvihite hiṃsite triṣu \|\| 11 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 12 \[unit 701\]

kṛttaṃ tu ceṣṭite channe kṛtiḥ karaṇahiṃsayoḥ \|

kṛttiścarmatvacorbhūrje kṛttikāyāṃ dvayaṃ striyām \|\| 12 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 13 \[unit 702\]

keturnā rukpatākāvigrahotpāteṣu lakṣmaṇi \|

gartastrigarttabhede syādavaṭe ca kukundare \|\| 13 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 14 \[unit 703\]

grastaṃ grāsīkṛte'pi syālluptavarṇapade'pi ca \|

gatiḥ strī mārgadaśayorjñāne yātrā'bhyupāyayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 15 \[unit 704\]

nāḍīvraṇasaraṇyāṃ ca gataṃ vijñānayātayoḥ \|

gāturnā kokile bhṛṅge gandharve triṣu roṣaṇe \|\| 15 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 16 \[unit 705\]

gītiśchandasi gāne strī gītaṃ śabditagānayoḥ \|

guptiḥ stryavakarasthāne kārāgāre ca rakṣaṇe \|\| 16 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 17 \[unit 706\]

guptaṃ syādrakṣite gūḍhe ghātaḥ kāṇḍaprahārayoḥ \|

ghṛtamājye jale klībaṃ pradīpte tvabhidheyavat \|\| 17 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 18 \[unit 707\]

citaṃ channe triṣu citā cityāyāṃ saṃhatau striyām \|

citiścityāvṛndayoḥ strī jātaṃ vyaktaughajanmasu \|\| 18 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 19 \[unit 708\]

klībaṃ triliṅgamutpanne jātiḥ strī gotrajanmanoḥ \|

aśmantikāmalakyośca sāmānyacchandasorapi \|\| 19 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 20 \[unit 709\]

jātīphale ca mālatyāṃ jñātistātasagotrayoḥ \|

pumānatha tataṃ vyāpte vistṛte ca triliṅgakam \|\| 20 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 21 \[unit 710\]

klībaṃ vīṇādivādye syāt puṃliṅgastu samīraṇe \|

tāto'nukampye janake tikto rasasugandhayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 22 \[unit 711\]

tiktā tu kaṭurohiṇyāṃ parpaṭe tu napuṃsakam \|

tūstaṃ reṇau ca vikhyā(1)taṃ jaṭāyāṃ ca napuṃsakam \|\| 22 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 23 \[unit 712\]

tretā yuge'gnitritaye dattaṃ viśrāṇite'rcite \|

danto'drikaṭake kuñje daśane'thauṣadhau striyām \|\| 23 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 24 \[unit 713\]

dāntastu damite'pi syāttapaḥkleśasahe triṣu \|

ditirdaityajananyāṃ ca khaṇḍane'pi ca yoṣiti \|\| 24 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 25 \[unit 714\]

dīptaṃ nirbhāsite dagdhe jvalite'tha drutaṃ triṣu \|

śīghre vilīne vidrāṇe dyutiḥ strī raśmiśobhayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 26 \[unit 715\]

dṛtiścarmapuṭe matsye nā dhāturnendriyeṣu ca \|

śabdayonimahābhūtatadguṇeṣu rasādiṣu \|\| 26 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 27 \[unit 716\]

(1) pāpe. ca. kha. \|

manaḥśilādau śleṣmādau viśeṣād gairike'sthni ca \|

dhātā hiraṇyagarbhe nā pālake triṣvatho dhutam \|\| 27 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 28 \[unit 717\]

tyakte vidhūte dhūtaṃ tu kampite bhartsite triṣu \|

dhūrttaṃ tu khaṇḍalavaṇe dhustūre nā viṭe triṣu \|\| 28 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 29 \[unit 718\]

dhṛtirneṣṭau striyāṃ tuṣṭau yogabhiddhairyadhāraṇe \|

nataṃ tagarapādyāṃ syāt klībaṃ kuṭilanamrayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 30 \[unit 719\]

triṣu nītirnaye'pi syāt prāpaṇe'pi ca yoṣiti \|

paktiḥ strī gaurave pāke pattirnā padage striyām \|\| 30 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 31 \[unit 720\]

gatāvekarathaikebhatryaśvapañcapadātike \|

paṅktirdaśākṣaracchandodaśasaṃkhyāliṣu striyām \|\| 31 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 32 \[unit 721\]

patirdhave nā triṣvīśe prāptaṃ labdhe samañjase \|

pāto nā patane trāte triṣu prāptirmahodaye \|\| 32 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 33 \[unit 722\]

lābhe'pi ca striyāṃ prātiḥ pūrttiprādeśayoḥ striyām \|

pītirnā'śve striyāṃ pāne prītaṃ hṛṣitanarmaṇoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 34 \[unit 723\]

pītaṃ pāne haridrāyāṃ striyāṃ gaure'bhidheyavat \|

prītiryogāntare premṇi smarapatnīmudoḥ striyām \|\| 34 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 35 \[unit 724\]

pustaṃ klībe pustake ca lepyādiśilpakarmaṇi \|

plutaṃ turaṅgamagatau klībaṃ puṃsi trimātrake \|\| 35 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 36 \[unit 725\]

pūtaṃ triṣu pavitre ca śaṭhite bahulīkṛte \|

pūrttaṃ triṣu pūrite syāt klībaṃ khātādikarmaṇi \|\| 36 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 37 \[unit 726\]

protaṃ napuṃsakaṃ vastre khacite vācyaliṅgakam \|

preto bhūtāntare puṃsi mṛte syādvācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 38 \[unit 727\]

potaḥ śiśau vahitre ca gṛhasthāne ca vāsasi \|

vṛtistu varaṇe'pi syādveṣṭane'pi ca yoṣiti \|\| 38 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 39 \[unit 728\]

bharttā svāmini puṃsi syāt triṣu dhātari poṣṭari \|

bhaktirvibhāge sevāyāṃ striyāṃ bhrāntistu yoṣiti \|\| 39 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 40 \[unit 729\]

mithyāmatau ca bhramaṇe bhittiḥ kuḍyapradeśayoḥ \|

bhītirbhaye striyāṃ bhītaṃ bhaye'tha bhīyute triṣu \|\| 40 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 41 \[unit 730\]

bhūtirbhasmani sampattau hastiśṛṅgārayoḥ striyām \|

bhūtaṃ kṣmādau piśācādau jantau klībaṃ triṣūcite \|\| 41 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 42 \[unit 731\]

prāpte vitte same satye devayonyantare tu nā \|

kumāre'pi bhṛtiḥ strī syānmūlye'pi bharaṇe'pi ca \|\| 42 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 43 \[unit 732\]

mataṃ tu sammate jñāte matiḥ strīcchādhiyoḥ smṛtau \|

mantuḥ puṃsyaparādhe'pi manuṣye'pi prajāpatau \|\| 43 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 44 \[unit 733\]

mātā gauryādijananīgobrahmāṇyādibhūmiṣu \|

(1)mātirmāne'pyavacchede muktirmokṣe ca mokṣaṇe \|\| 44 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 45 \[unit 734\]

mātrādayaḥ striyāṃ muktaḥ prāptamokṣe ca mocite \|

triṣu strī mauktike mūrttiḥ kāyakāṭhinyayoḥ striyām \|\| 45 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 46 \[unit 735\]

mūrttaṃ syāt triṣu mūrcchāle kaṭhine mūrttimatyapi \|

mṛtaṃ tu yācite mṛtyau klībaṃ mṛtyumati triṣu \|\| 46 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 47 \[unit 736\]

yatiḥ strī (2)pāṭhavikchede nikārayatinoḥ pumān \|

yantā (3)meṭhe ca sūte nā yuktiḥ strī nyāyayogayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 48 \[unit 737\]

yuto yukto'pṛthagbhūte klībaṃ hastacatuṣṭaye \|

rakto'nurakte nīlyādirañjite lohite triṣu \|\| 48 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 49 \[unit 738\]

klībaṃ tu kuṅkume tāmre prācīnāmalake sṛji \|

ratiḥ strī smaradāreṣu rāge surataguhyayoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 50 \[unit 739\]

ratnaṃ vartmani nadyāṃ strī rataṃ surataguhyayoḥ \|

ratastriṣvanurakte syāt tatpare cābhidheyavat(4) \|\| 50 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 51 \[unit 740\]

riktaṃ śūnye vane rītiḥ striyāṃ syandapracārayoḥ \|

pittale lohakiṭṭe ca latā priyaṅguśākhayoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 52 \[unit 741\]

pṛkkājyotiṣmatīvallīlatākastūrikāsu ca \|

mādhavīdūrvayorliptaṃ viliptaviṣadigdhayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 53 \[unit 742\]

bhukte lūtā tūrṇanābhe pipīlikāgadāntare \|

(1) mitiḥ-kha. \| ; (2) pāda-ka. \| ; (3) yantā hastipake sūte-kha. \| ; (4) adhikaṃ padyārdhamidam-kha. \|

vaptā tu janake puṃsi vāpake'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 53 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 54 \[unit 743\]

vaktā tu paṇḍite'pi syādvāgminyapyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

vyastaṃ tu vyākule vyāpte vyaktaḥ sphuṭamanīṣiṇoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 55 \[unit 744\]

vastirdvayornirūhe nābhyadhobhūmni daśāsu ca \|

varttibhaiṣajanirmāṇe nayanāñjanalekhayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 56 \[unit 745\]

gātrānulepanīdīpadaśādīpeṣu yoṣiti \|

vārttā tu varttane vātiṅgaṇe kṛṣyādyudantayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 57 \[unit 746\]

niḥsārārogyayoḥ klībaṃ vṛttirmallīrujoḥ striyām \|

vyāptaṃ khyāte samākrānte vyāptirvyāpanarambhayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 58 \[unit 747\]

striyāṃ vittirvicāre ca lābhasambhāvayoḥ striyām \|

vittaṃ klībaṃ dhane vācyaliṅgaṃ khyāte vicārite \|\| 58 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 59 \[unit 748\]

vītamasāragaje syādaṅkuśakarmaṇyasāraturage'pi \|

vītirgatau ca dīptau prajanāśanadhāvaneṣu strī \|\| 59 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 60 \[unit 749\]

vṛttirvivaraṇājīvakaiśikyādipravartane \|

vṛntaṃ prasabandhe'strī ghaṭīdhārākucāgrayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 61 \[unit 750\]

vṛtto'dhītepyatīte'tivartule'pi vṛte mṛte \|

dṛḍhe'nyaliṅgaṃ vā klībaṃ chandaścāritravṛttiṣu \|\| 61 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 62 \[unit 751\]

śaktirastrāntare gauryāmutsāhādau bale striyām \|

śastaṃ kṣeme praśaste ca śāntiḥ strī maṅgale śame \|\| 62 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 63 \[unit 752\]

śāstā samantabhadre nā śāsane punaranyavat \|

śānto'bhiyuktarasayoḥ puṃsi triṣu śamānvite \|\| 63 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 64 \[unit 753\]

avyayaṃ vāraṇe śāntaṃ śāntaṃ śitaṃ ca durbale \|

niśite ca śitirbhūrje nā sitāsitayostriṣu \|\| 64 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 65 \[unit 754\]

śītaṃ himaguṇe klībaṃ śītalālasayostriṣu \|

vānīre bahuvāre nā śuktaṃ pūtāmlaniṣṭhure \|\| 65 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 66 \[unit 755\]

śrutiḥ śrautre ca tatkarmaṇyāmnāyavā(1)tayoḥ striyām \|

(1) vārtayoḥ-kha. \|

śuktiḥ kapālaśakale śaṅkhe śaṅkhanakhe'pi ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 67 \[unit 756\]

nakhyaśvāvartadurnāmamuktāsphoṭeṣu ca striyām \|

śrutamākarṇite śāstre śveto dvīpādribhedayoḥ \|\| 67 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 68 \[unit 757\]

śvetā varāṭikākāṣṭhapāṭalāśaṅkhinīṣu ca \|

klībaṃ rūpye'nyavacchukle san sādhau dhīraśastayoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 69 \[unit 758\]

mānye satye vidyamāne triṣu sādhvyumayoḥ striyām \|

sātirdāne'vasāne strī svāntaṃ cetasi gahvare \|\| 69 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 70 \[unit 759\]

sthitaṃ pratijñātavati cordhvaniścalayostriṣu \|

sthitiḥ striyāmavasthāne maryādāyāṃ ca sīmani \|\| 70 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 71 \[unit 760\]

sitamavasite ca baddhe dhavale triṣu dhavalāyāṃ strī \|

sītā lāṅgalapaddhativaidehīsvargagaṅgāsu \|\| 71 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 72 \[unit 761\]

sutastu pārthive putre stryapatye tu sutā smṛtā \|

suptiḥ sparśājñatānidrāviśrambhe śayane striyām \|\| 72 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 73 \[unit 762\]

sūtastu sārathau takṣṇi kṣatriyād brāhmaṇīsute \|

bandipāradayoḥ puṃsi prasūte prerite triṣu \|\| 73 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 74 \[unit 763\]

syūtiḥ sīvanasantatyoḥ striyāṃ syūtaḥ prasevake \|

nā triṣūte smṛtirdharmaśāstrasmaraṇayoḥ striyām \|\| 74 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 75 \[unit 764\]

sṛtiḥ strī gamane mārge heturnā''lau kumārake \|

hastaḥ kare karikare saprakoṣṭhakare'pi ca \|\| 75 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tadvikam / 76 \[unit 765\]

ṛkṣe keśāt paro vrāte hitaṃ pathye gate dhṛte \|

hetiḥ syādāyudhajvālāsūryatejaḥsu yoṣiti \|\| 76 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 77 \[unit 766\]

tatrikam

amṛtaṃ yajñaśeṣe syāt pīyūṣe salile ghṛte \|

ayācite ca mokṣe ca nā dhanvantaridevayoḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 78 \[unit 767\]

amṛtā māgadhīpathyāguḍūcyāmalakīṣu ca \|

anṛtaṃ kṛṣāvasatye'pyajito nā harau triṣu \|\| 78 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 79 \[unit 768\]

anirjite cācyutastu harau puṃsi triṣu sthire \|

arditaṃ yācite'pi syādvātavyādhau ca hiṃsite \|\| 79 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 80 \[unit 769\]

akṣataṃ na dvayoḥ paṇḍe lājeṣu triṣvahiṃsite \|

yave'pi kvacidvyakto harau smarahare pumān \|\| 80 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 81 \[unit 770\]

paramātmamahadādyoḥ klībaṃ syādasphuṭe triṣu \|

anantaḥ keśave śeṣe pumānanavadhau triṣu \|\| 81 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 82 \[unit 771\]

anantā ca viśalyāyāṃ śārivādūrvayorapi \|

kaṇādurālabhāpathyāpārvatyāmalakīṣu ca \|\| 82 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 83 \[unit 772\]

viśvambharāguḍūcyoḥ syādanantaṃ suravartmani \|

āśmantamaśubhe culyāṃ maraṇe'navadhāvapi \|\| 83 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 84 \[unit 773\]

kṣetre'pyathāyatiḥ puṃsi himadīdhitikālayoḥ \|

arhastu kṣapaṇe buddhe puṃsi mānye'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 84 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 85 \[unit 774\]

arhantaścāpi sugate kṣapaṇe'pi ca dṛśyate \|

agastiḥ kumbhayonau ca (1)vaṅkasenatarau pumān \|\| 85 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 86 \[unit 775\]

arvatī kumbhadāsyāṃ ca va vaḍavāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

aṅgatiḥ puṃsyagnihotṛbrahmavahniṣvathāditiḥ \|\| 86 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 87 \[unit 776\]

bhūdevamātroraṃhatistyāge roge'pyubhe striyau \|

āpātaḥ puṃsi patane tadātve ca prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 88 \[unit 777\]

aghrātaṃ śiṅghite krānte'pyākhyātaṃ bhāṣite tiṅi \|

ādhmātaḥ śabdite dagdhe vātarugbhedasaṃyute \|\| 88 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 89 \[unit 778\]

āplutaḥ snātake snāte'pyādṛtaḥ sādare'rcite \|

ācitaḥ śakaṭonmeye palānāmayutadvaye \|\| 89 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 90 \[unit 779\]

puṃliṅgaḥ saṅgṛhīte'pi channe'pi syāttriliṅgakaḥ \|

āhataṃ guṇite cāpi tāḍite ca mṛṣā'rthake \|\| 90 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 91 \[unit 780\]

syāt purātanavastre'pi navavastre ca nā''nake \|

ānatoṃ deśabhede'pi nṛtyasthāne jale raṇe \|\| 91 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 92 \[unit 781\]

āvartaścintane vāribhrame cāvartane pumān \|

āsphotaṃ tu pumānarkaparṇe syāt kovidārake \|\| 92 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 93 \[unit 782\]

(1) vaṅgasena0-kha. \|

āsphotā girikarṇyāṃ ca vanamallyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

āyastastejite kṣipte kleśite kupite hate \|\| 93 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 94 \[unit 783\]

āyatistu striyāṃ dairghye prabhāvāgāmikālayoḥ \|

āyattistu striyāṃ snehe vaśitve vāsare bale \|\| 94 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 95 \[unit 784\]

ākṛtistu striyāṃ rūpe sāmānyavapuṣorapi \|

āsattiḥ saṅgame lābhe striyāmāpattiropadi \|\| 95 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 96 \[unit 785\]

prāpaṇe'pi ca yoṣitsyādudantaḥ sādhuvārtayoḥ \|

uṣitaṃ vyuṣite dagdhe'pyudāttastu svarāntare \|\| 96 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 97 \[unit 786\]

dayātyāgādisampanne kāvyālaṅkārakṛtyayoḥ \|

uttaptaṃ śuṣkamāṃse'tha triṣu tapte pariplute \|\| 97 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 98 \[unit 787\]

ucitaṃ tu bhave nyaste mite jñāte samañjase \|

uddhṛtaṃ syāttriṣūtkṣipte paribhuktojjhite'pi ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 99 \[unit 788\]

utthitaṃ syāttriṣūtpanne prodyate vṛddhimatyapi \|

udghātastu pumān pādaskhalane samupakrame \|\| 99 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 100 \[unit 789\]

(1)pavanābhyāsayogasya kālabhede ca kīrtitaḥ \|

uttuṅge mudgare'pi syāduditaṃ tūkta āgate \|\| 100 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 101 \[unit 790\]

ucchritaṃ triṣu sañjāte samu(2)nnaddhapravṛddhayoḥ \|

unmatta unmādavati dhūstūramucukundayoḥ \|\| 101 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 102 \[unit 791\]

udvānto nirmadagaje pumānudvamite triṣu \|

edhatuḥ puruṣe'gnau nā kalitaṃ viditāptayoḥ \|\| 102 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 103 \[unit 792\]

kapotaḥ syāccitrakaṇṭhapārāvatavihaṅgayoḥ \|

atha kranditamāhvāne rudite'pi napuṃsakam \|\| 103 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 104 \[unit 793\]

kṣāritaḥ srāvite kṣāre cābhiśaste'pi ca triṣu \|

kāpoto rucake klībe kapotaughe'ñjanāntare \|\| 104 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 105 \[unit 794\]

kirāto mlecchabhede syāt bhūnimbe'lpatanāvapi \|

striyāṃ cāmaradhāriṇyāṃ kuṭṭinīdurgayorapi \|\| 105 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 106 \[unit 795\]

(1) pavanābhyāsayogāya kumbhakāditraye'pi ca-kha. \| ; (2) samunnata-kha.

kṛtānto yamasiddhāntadavākuśalakarmasu \|

garjitaṃ vārivāhādidhvanau nā mattakuñjare \|\| 106 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 107 \[unit 796\]

grathitaṃ gumphite krānte hiṃsite ca triliṅgakam \|

gabhastiḥ kiraṇe sūrye nā svāhāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 107 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 108 \[unit 797\]

garmut strī svarṇa(1)latayorgopatiḥ śivaṣaṇḍhayoḥ \|

nṛpabhāskarayoḥ puṃsi ceṣṭitaṃ gaticeṣṭayoḥ \|\| 108 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 109 \[unit 798\]

jagat syāt viṣṭape klībaṃ vāyau nā jaṅgame triṣu \|

jagatī bhuvane kṣmāyāṃ chandobhede jane'pi ca \|\| 109 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 110 \[unit 799\]

jayantī vṛkṣabhidgauryorindraputrīpatākayoḥ \|

pumānaindre hare bhīme jvalitaṃ dugdha ujvale \|\| 110 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 111 \[unit 800\]

jāmātā duhituḥ patyau sūryāvartte dhave pumān \|

jīmūto'drau dhṛtikare devatāḍe payodhare \|\| 111 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 112 \[unit 801\]

jīvāturastriyāṃ bhakte jīvite jīvanauṣadhe \|

jīvantī jīvanīśamyorguḍūcīvṛndayorapi \|\| 112 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 113 \[unit 802\]

jṛmbhitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāmīhāsphoṭitayorapi \|

tvaritaṃ prajave śīghre trigarto gaṇitāntare \|\| 113 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 114 \[unit 803\]

deśe'pi nā ghurghurikākāmukāṅganayoḥ striyām \|

tṛṇatā dhanuṣi tṛṇatve daṃśitamapi jātadaṃśe syāt \|\| 114 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 115 \[unit 804\]

kavacācite dvijātirviprāṇḍajayośca puṃliṅgaḥ \|

durjātaṃ vyasane klībe samyagjāte'nyaliṅgakam \|\| 115 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 116 \[unit 805\]

durgatirnarake naissvye strī dṛṣṭānta udāhṛtau \|

śāstre ca maraṇe dhīmān paṇḍite ca bṛhaspatau \|\| 116 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 117 \[unit 806\]

nirṛtiḥ syādalakṣmyāṃ strī diśāpālāntare pumān \|

nikṛtaṃ vipralambhe (2)syānnīce viprakṛte'pi ca \|\| 117 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 118 \[unit 807\]

nirmuktastyaktasaṅge syānmuktakañcukabhogini \|

nirastastriṣu niṣṭhyūte preṣiteṣau drutodite \|\| 118 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 119 \[unit 808\]

(1) naḍayoḥ-kha. \| (2) syāt śaṭhe viprakṛte triṣu-kha. \|

santyakte ca pratihate nimittaṃ hetulakṣmaṇoḥ \|

nivāto dṛḍhasannāhe vātaśūnye'pi cāśraye \|\| 119 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 120 \[unit 809\]

niśāntaṃ triṣu śānte syāt klībaṃ tu bhavanoṣasoḥ \|

nikṛtirbhartsane kṣepe śaṭhe śāṭhye'pi ca striyām \|\| 120 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 121 \[unit 810\]

nirvṛtiḥ susthitāvastagamane ca sukhe striyām \|

niyatirniyame daive strī patan pātuke khage \|\| 121 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 122 \[unit 811\]

prahataṃ vitate kṣuṇṇe paryastaḥ patite hate \|

prabhūtamudgate prājye paṇḍitaḥ silhake kavau \|\| 122 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 123 \[unit 812\]

palitaṃ śailaje tāpe keśapāke ca kardame \|

prasṛtaḥ suprasāre syādvinīte vegite triṣu \|\| 123 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 124 \[unit 813\]

arddhāñjalau tu puṃliṅgo jaṅghāyāṃ prasṛtā matā \|

prasūtaṃ kusume klībaṃ triṣu sañjātasūtayoḥ \|\| 124 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 125 \[unit 814\]

praṇītaḥ saṃskṛtāgnau nā yajñapātrāntare striyām \|

triṣu kṣiptopasampannavihiteṣu praveśite \|\| 125 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 126 \[unit 815\]

pratītaḥ sādare jñāte hṛṣṭaprakhyātayostriṣu \|

pramītaṃ vācyaliṅgaṃ syāt prokṣite'pi mṛte'pi ca \|\| 126 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 127 \[unit 816\]

pareto vācyaliṅgaḥ syānmṛte bhūtāntare pumān \|

parvataḥ pādape puṃsi śākamatsyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 128 \[unit 817\]

devamunyantare śaile prapāto nirjhare taṭe \|

paryāptaṃ tu yatheṣṭe syāttṛptau śakte nivāraṇe \|\| 128 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 129 \[unit 818\]

pañcatā pañcabhāve syānmaraṇe'pi ca yoṣiti \|

pakṣatiśca bhavet pakṣamūle ca pratipattithau \|\| 129 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 130 \[unit 819\]

prasūtirudbhave'pi syāttanaye duhitaryapi \|

pratatirvistṛtau valyāṃ paddhatiḥ paṅktivartmanoḥ \|\| 130 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 131 \[unit 820\]

pravṛttistu pravāhe syādudante ca pravarttane \|

avantyādau ca paryāptiḥ prakāmaprāptirakṣaṇe \|\| 131 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 132 \[unit 821\]

prakṛtirguṇasāmye syādamātyādisvabhāvayoḥ \|

yonau liṅge pauravarge'mī pakṣatyādayaḥ striyām \|\| 132 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 133 \[unit 822\]

pārvatī sallakīdurgāgopālaputrikāsu ca \|

prārthitaṃ yācite śatrusaṃruddhe'bhihate triṣu \|\| 133 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 134 \[unit 823\]

pitsan vihaṅgame puṃsi patanecchau punastriṣu \|

piśitaṃ māṃse māṃsyāṃ strī piṇḍitaṃ guṇite ghane \|\| 134 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 135 \[unit 824\]

pīḍitaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ yantrite bādhite'pi ca \|

puṭitaṃ syā(1)dahipuṭe pāṭitasyūtayorapi \|\| 135 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 136 \[unit 825\]

pṛṣan mṛge pumān bindau na dvayoḥ pṛṣato'pi nā \|

anayośca triṣu śvetabinduyukte'pyubhāvimau \|\| 136 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 137 \[unit 826\]

prokṣitaṃ nihate sikte bṛhatī vasanāntare \|

chandobhitkṣudravārtākīvāridhānīṣu vāci ca \|\| 137 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 138 \[unit 827\]

kaṇṭakārīmahatyośca bhavedyuṣmatsatostriṣu \|

strī bāṇabhede bharato nāṭyāśāstramunau taṭe \|\| 138 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 139 \[unit 828\]

rāmānuje ca dauṣyantau bhāsvān bhāsvarasūryayoḥ \|

bhārataṃ granthabhede syādvarṣabhede'tha bhāratī \|\| 139 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 140 \[unit 829\]

vacane ca sarasvatyāṃ pakṣivṛttiprabhedayoḥ \|

bhāsantaḥ sundarākāre triṣu nā bhāsapakṣiṇi \|\| 140 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 141 \[unit 830\]

bhāvitaṃ vāsite prāpte bhūbhṛnnā'drau mahīpatau \|

mahatī vallakībhede rājye syāttu napuṃsakam \|\| 141 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 142 \[unit 831\]

tattvabhede pumān vṛddhe vācyavanmathitaṃ punaḥ \|

klībaṃ nirjalaghole syāt triṣvāloḍitaghṛṣṭayoḥ \|\| 142 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 143 \[unit 832\]

maruddeve samīre nā granthiparṇe napuṃsakam \|

muṣitaṃ hṛtakhaṇḍitayormūrcchitamapi socchraye ca mūḍhe ca \|\| 143 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 144 \[unit 833\]

rajataṃ triṣu śukle syāt klībaṃ hāre ca durvarṇe \|

ramatirnāyake nāke puṃsi syādrasitaṃ punaḥ \|\| 144 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 145 \[unit 834\]

rutastanitayoḥ klībaṃ svarṇādikhacite triṣu \|

revatī halipatnyāṃ syāttārābhinmātṛbhedayoḥ \|\| 145 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 146 \[unit 835\]

(1) syāt hastapuṭe-kha. \|

raivatastu suvarṇālau śailabhede ca śaṅkare \|

rohitaṃ kuṅkume rakte ṛjuśakraśarāsane \|\| 146 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 147 \[unit 836\]

puṃsi syānmīnamṛgayorbhede rohitakadrume \|

rohin mṛgyāṃ latābhede strī nā'rke lalitaṃ triṣu \|\| 147 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 148 \[unit 837\]

lalite ceśite'pi syāddhārabhede tu na dvayoḥ \|

lohitaṃ raktagośīrṣe kuṅkume raktacandane \|\| 148 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 149 \[unit 838\]

pumān nadāntare bhaume varṇe ca triṣu tadvati \|

varddhitaṃ prasite chinne pūrite vahatirgavi \|\| 149 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 150 \[unit 839\]

sacive puṃsi vahatuḥ pathike vṛṣabhe pumān \|

vanitā jātarāgastrīstriyostriṣu ca yācite \|\| 150 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 151 \[unit 840\]

sevite vāpitaṃ bījākṛtamuṇḍitayostriṣu \|

vasatiḥ syāt striyāṃ vāse yāminyāṃ ca niketane \|\| 151 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 152 \[unit 841\]

vyāyataṃ vyāpṛte dīrghe dṛḍhe cātiśaye'nyavat \|

vyāghāto yogabhede syādantarāyaprahārayoḥ \|\| 152 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 153 \[unit 842\]

vāsitā kariṇīnāryorvāsitaṃ bhāvite rute \|

vāsantī mādhavīyūthyoruṣṭre nā'vahite triṣu \|\| 153 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 154 \[unit 843\]

viviktaṃ triṣvasampṛkte rahaḥpūtavivekiṣu \|

vasunande nā vihastaḥ paṇḍite vyākule triṣu \|\| 154 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 155 \[unit 844\]

vinītaḥ suvahāśve syādvaṇijyapi pumāṃstriṣu \|

jitendriye'panīte ca nibhṛte vinayānvite \|\| 155 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 156 \[unit 845\]

viśvasto jātaviśvāse viśvastā vidhavāstriyām \|

vidyuttaḍiti sandhyāyāṃ striyāṃ triṣu tu niṣprabhe \|\| 156 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 157 \[unit 846\]

vivṛtā kṣudrarugbhede vistṛte tvabhidheyavat \|

vinetā'deśake rājñi nā vidhātā tu vedhasi \|\| 157 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 158 \[unit 847\]

smare nā vinatā tārkṣyajananyāṃ paṭakābhidi \|

vinataḥ praṇate bhugne śikṣite cābhidheyavat \|\| 158 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 159 \[unit 848\]

vikṛtaṃ triṣu bībhatsarāgite saṃskṛte'pi ca \|

vicchittiraṅgarāge vicchede hārabhede strī \|\| 159 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 160 \[unit 849\]

viditaṃ budhitārthitayorvipattirapi yātanā''padoryoṣit \|

vṛttāntaḥ prakriyāyāṃ syāt kārtsnyavārttāprabhedayoḥ \|\| 160 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 161 \[unit 850\]

prastāve veṣṭitaṃ ruddhe lāsake karaṇāntare \|

vellitaṃ gamane klībaṃ kuṭile vidhute triṣu \|\| 161 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 162 \[unit 851\]

(1)vivarttaḥ samudāye syādapavartananṛtyayoḥ \|

śakuntaḥ kīṭabhede syādbhāsapakṣivihaṅgayoḥ \|\| 162 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 163 \[unit 852\]

śrīmāṃstilakavṛkṣe nā manojñe dhanike triṣu \|

śrīpatiḥ puṃsi pṛthivīnāthe ca madhusūdane \|\| 163 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 164 \[unit 853\]

śuddhānto'ntaḥpure kṣmābhṛdrahaḥkakṣāntare'pi ca \|

sravantī tu taraṅgiṇyāṃ gulmasthānauṣadhībhidoḥ \|\| 164 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 165 \[unit 854\]

saṃvarttaḥ pralaye munyantare karṣaphale'pi ca \|

skhalitaṃ calite bhreṣe saṃhataṃ saṅgate dṛḍhe \|\| 165 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 166 \[unit 855\]

saṃskṛtaḥ kṛtrime śaste bhūṣite'ṣvanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

klībaṃ tu lakṣaṇopete sthapatiḥ kañcukinyapi \|\| 166 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 167 \[unit 856\]

jīveṣṭiyājake śilpibhede nā sattame triṣu \|

saṅghātaḥ puṃsi ghāte ca saṃhatau narakāntare \|\| 167 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 168 \[unit 857\]

saṃvittiḥ pratipattau syādavivāde janasya ca \|

santatiḥ syāt paṃktau gotre pāramparye ca putrayoḥ \|\| 168 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 169 \[unit 858\]

(2)saṅgatiḥ saṅgame jñāne sannatiḥ praṇatau dhvanau \|

samitiḥ samparāye syātsabhāyāṃ saṅgame'pi ca \|\| 169 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 170 \[unit 859\]

samāptiravasāne syātsamarthane'tha sammatiḥ \|

abhilāṣe'pyanujñāyāṃ saṃvityādyāstu yoṣiti \|\| 170 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 171 \[unit 860\]

sthāpitaṃ niścite nyaste (3)stimito'cañcalārdrayoḥ \|

sikatā strī sikatile vālukāyāṃ tu bhūmani \|\| 171 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 172 \[unit 861\]

surataṃ syānnidhuvane devatve suratā smṛtā \|

(1) idamardhaṃ nāsti-kha. \| ; (2) idamardhaṃ padyaṃ nāsti-kha. \| ; (3) niścalā-kha. \|

sukṛtaṃ tu śubhe puṇye klībaṃ suvihite triṣu \|\| 172 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 173 \[unit 862\]

suvratā sukhasandohyāśobhanavratayorapi \|

sunītiḥ śobhananaye dhruvamātari yoṣiti \|\| 173 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 174 \[unit 863\]

sūnṛtaṃ maṅgale'pi syāt priyasatye vacasyapi \|

hariddiśi striyāṃ puṃsi hayavarṇaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 174 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 175 \[unit 864\]

astriyāṃ syāttṛṇe cātha harmitaṃ kṣiptadagdhayoḥ \|

haritā strī ca dūrvāyāṃ haridvarṇayute'nyavat \|\| 175 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / 176 \[unit 865\]

hasantyaṅgāradhānyāṃ ca mallikāśākinībhidoḥ \|

hārītaḥ pakṣibhede syānmunibhede ca kaitave \|\| 176 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tatrikam / fragment 866

hṛṣitaṃ vismite prīte prahate hṛṣṭalomani \|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 177 \[unit 867\]

tacatuṣkam

avasitamṛddhe jñāte'pyavasānagate ca vācyaliṅgaṃ syāt \|\| 177 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 178 \[unit 868\]

(1)arthapatiḥ puṃliṅgo dhanādhināthe ca naranāthe \|

avadātaḥ site gaure viśuddhe'pyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 178 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 179 \[unit 869\]

avagītaṃ tu nirvāde du(2)ṣṭagarhitayorapi \|

antargataṃ vismṛte syānmadhyaprāpte ca vācyavat \|\| 179 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 180 \[unit 870\]

aṅgāritaṃ tu dagdhe syātpalāśakalikodgame \|

(3)hasantyāṃ ca latāmātre nadībhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 180 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 181 \[unit 871\]

apāvṛtastu pihite svatantre'pi ca vācyavat \|

atyāhitaṃ mahābhītau jīvānapekṣikarmaṇi \|\| 181 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 182 \[unit 872\]

abhinītaṃ triṣu nyāyye saṃskṛtāmarṣiṇorapi \|

abhijātaḥ kulīne syāt nyāyyapaṇḍitayostriṣu \|\| 182 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 183 \[unit 873\]

abhiyuktaḥ parai ruddhe tatpare'pyabhidheyavat \|

atimuktastu niḥsaṅge vāsantyāṃ tiniśe'pi ca \|\| 183 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 184 \[unit 874\]

avadhvastaḥ parityakte nindite'pyavacūrṇite \|

(1) idamardhaṃ nāsti-ka. pu. \| ; (2) tvavda-ka. \| ; (3) idamardhaṃ nāsti-kha. \|

adhikṣiptaḥ prasṇihite kutsite bhartsite triṣu \|\| 184 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 185 \[unit 875\]

aṃśumān bhāskare śālaparṇyāmaṃśumatī smṛtā \|

bhavedapacitiḥ pūjāvyayaniṣkṛtihāniṣu \|\| 185 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 186 \[unit 876\]

athānumatirūnendupūrṇimā'nujñayorapi \|

abhiśastiḥ prārthanā'pavādayośca trayaṃ striyām \|\| 186 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 187 \[unit 877\]

āyuṣmān yogabhede nā vācyavaccirajīvini \|

upāhito'nalotpāte pumānāropite triṣu \|\| 187 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 188 \[unit 878\]

upākṛto'dhvarahatapaśau nopadrute triṣu \|

udāsthitaḥ pratīhāre pravrajyā'vasite care \|\| 188 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 189 \[unit 879\]

syādullikhitamutkīrṇe tanūkṛte ca vācyavat \|

udgrāhitamudīrṇe syādbaddhagrāhitayostriṣu \|\| 189 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 190 \[unit 880\]

bhavedupacitaṃ dagdhe samṛddhe vācyaliṅgakam \|

ujjṛmbhitaṃ triṣūtphulle ceṣṭāyāṃ tu napuṃsakam \|\| 190 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 191 \[unit 881\]

uparakto vyasanārte rāhugrastendusūryayoḥ \|

upasattiḥ saṅgamātre sevāyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 191 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 192 \[unit 882\]

ṛṣyaproktā śatāvaryatibalāśūkaśibiṣu \|

airāvato'bhramātaṅge nāraṅge lakucadrame \|\| 192 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 193 \[unit 883\]

nāgabhede ca puṃsi syādvidyuttadbhedayoḥ striyām \|

napuṃsakaṃ mahendrasya ṛjudīrghaśarāsane \|\| 193 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 194 \[unit 884\]

kaladhautaṃ suvarṇe syādrajate ca napuṃsakam \|

bhavet kuharitaṃ klībaṃ pikālāpe ratadhvanau \|\| 194 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 195 \[unit 885\]

kumudvān kumudaprāyadeśe syādvācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

kumudvatī kumudinyāṃ kuśapatnyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 195 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 196 \[unit 886\]

kṛṣṇavṛntā pāṭalāyāṃ māṣaparṇyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

atha gandhavatī pṛthvīpurībhidvyāsamātṛṣu \|\| 196 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 197 \[unit 887\]

surāyāṃ ca garutmāṃśca pakṣimātre khagādhipe \|

bhaved gṛhapatiḥ puṃsi gṛhasthe'pi ca mantriṇi \|\| 197 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 198 \[unit 888\]

candrakāntastu puṃsi syānmaṇibhede ca kairave \|

carmaṇvatī nadībhede kadalīpādape striyām \|\| 198 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 199 \[unit 889\]

citraguptastu puṃsi syādyame tasya ca lekhake \|

divābhītaḥ kumbhile syādulūke kumudākare \|\| 199 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 200 \[unit 890\]

divākīrtistu puṃsi syānnāpitāntāvasāyinoḥ \|

dvīpavān sindhunadayordvīpavatyāpagābhuvoḥ \|\| 200 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 201 \[unit 891\]

dṛṣadvatī nadībhede kātyāyanyāmapi striyām \|

syād dhūmaketurutpātabhede vaiśvānare pumān \|\| 201 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 202 \[unit 892\]

nandyāvartaḥ pumān veśmaprabhede bhagavaddrume \|

nadīkāntaḥ samudre syād hijjale sinduvārake \|\| 202 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 203 \[unit 893\]

nadīkāntā striyāṃ jambvāṃ kākajaṅghauṣadhāvapi \|

nāgadanto dviparade gṛhānnirgatadāruṇi \|\| 203 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 204 \[unit 894\]

nāgadantī tu kumbhāyāṃ śrīhastinyāmapi striyām \|

niṣkāsito nirgamite'pyāhite'dhikṛte'pi ca \|\| 204 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 205 \[unit 895\]

atha nistuṣitaṃ tyakte tvagnihīne laghūkṛte \|

nirākṛtiranākāre'svādhyāye triṣu vāraṇe \|\| 205 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 206 \[unit 896\]

striyāṃ parigataṃ prāpte vismṛtajñānaceṣṭite \|

atha praṇihitaṃ nyaste prāpte'pi ca samāhite \|\| 206 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 207 \[unit 897\]

atha pallavitaṃ lākṣārakte sapallave tate \|

bhavet pratihitaṃ dviṣṭe pratiskhalitaruddhayoḥ \|\| 207 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 208 \[unit 898\]

pratikṣiptaṃ vārite syātpreṣite pañcakaṃ triṣu \|

parivarto viniyame kūrmarāje'pavartane \|\| 208 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 209 \[unit 899\]

parisrutā strī vāruṇyāṃ syanne syādabhidheyavat \|

pradhūpitā kleśitāyāṃ sūryagantavyadiśyapi \|\| 209 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 210 \[unit 900\]

pañcaguptastu cārvākadarśane kamaṭhe pumān \|

prajāpatiśca dakṣādau mahīpāle vidhātari \|\| 210 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 211 \[unit 901\]

pratipattiḥ pravṛttau ca prāgalbhye gaurave'pi ca \|

samprāptau ca prabodhe ca padaprāptau ca yoṣiti \|\| 211 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 212 \[unit 902\]

atha pravrajitā māṃsīmuṇḍīrītāpasīṣu ca \|

pratikṛtirathārcāyāṃ pratinidhipratīkārayośca strī \|\| 212 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 213 \[unit 903\]

pāśupato bakapuṣpe syāt paśupatyadhidaivate ca tadbhakte \|

pārijātaḥ suratarau pāribhadratarāvapi \|\| 213 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 214 \[unit 904\]

pārāvatī gopagīte nadībhillavalīphale \|

pārāvataḥ kalarave tathā markaṭatinduke \|\| 214 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 215 \[unit 905\]

puṣpadantastu diṅnāgabhede vidyādharāntare \|

puraskṛto'bhiśastārigrastāgre kṛtapūjite \|\| 215 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 216 \[unit 906\]

bhagavānnā jine gauryāṃ striyāṃ pūjye tu vācyavat \|

atha bhogavatī nāgapurīnadyorahau pumān \|\| 216 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 217 \[unit 907\]

raṅgamātā tu kuṭṭanyāṃ vṛkṣarogāntare striyām(1) \|

lakṣmīpatiḥ pumān vāsudeve narapatāvapi \|\| 217 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 218 \[unit 908\]

vyatīpāto mahotpāte yogabhedāpamānayoḥ \|

vanaspatirnā drumātre vinā puṣpaphaladrume \|\| 218 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 219 \[unit 909\]

vinipāto nipāte syād daivādivyasane pumān \|

vijṛmbhitaṃ tu ceṣṭāyāṃ klībaṃ triṣu vikasvare \|\| 219 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 220 \[unit 910\]

vivasvān vibudhe(2) sūrye tannagaryāṃ vivasvatī \|

vaijayanto mahendrasya dhvajaprāsādayoḥ pumān \|\| 220 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 221 \[unit 911\]

vaijayantī patākāyāṃ jayantīpādape (3)striyām \|

bhavecchatadhṛtiḥ puṃsi śukre ca parameṣṭhini \|\| 221 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 222 \[unit 912\]

śubhradantī sudantyāṃ syāt puṣpadantebhayoṣiti \|

samuddhataṃ samutkīrṇe'pyapanīte ca vācyavat \|\| 222 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 223 \[unit 913\]

sadāgatirnākavātanirvāṇeṣu sadīśvare \|

sarasvāṃśca nade cābdhau mānyavadrasike striyām \|\| 223 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 224 \[unit 914\]

vāṇī strī ratnavāgdevīgonadīṣu nadībhidi \|

manupatnyāmapi satyavatī vyāsasya mātari \|\| 224 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 225 \[unit 915\]

nāradarcīkayoḥ patnyāṃ striyāṃ puṃsi nṛpāntare \|

(1) pumān-kha. \| ; (2) vivasvāṃtu sure sūrye-kha. \| ; (3) pumān-ka. \|

samudrāntā tu kārpāsīpṛkkādurālabhāsu ca \|\| 225 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 226 \[unit 916\]

samāhitaḥ samādhisthe'pyuktasiddhānta āhite \|

nirvivādīkṛte'pi syātpratijñāte ca vācyavat \|\| 226 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 227 \[unit 917\]

samāghāto vadhe yuddhe saṃkhyāvān paṇḍite pumān \|

saṃkhyāyukte'nyaliṅgo'tha sudhāsūtiḥ pumān makhe \|\| 227 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 228 \[unit 918\]

candre subhāṣito buddhe pumān sūkte napuṃsakam \|

sūryabhaktastu bandhūke puṃsi triṣvarkapūjake \|\| 228 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / 229 \[unit 919\]

senāpatiḥ kārtikeye'pyanīkādhipatau pumān \|

himārātiḥ pumān vītihotre kiraṇamālini \|\| 229 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tacatuṣkam / fragment 920

haimavatyabhayāsvarṇakṣīryoḥ śvetavacomayoḥ \|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 230 \[unit 921\]

tapañcakam

avalokito nā lokanāthe triṣu nirīkṣite \|\| 230 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 231 \[unit 922\]

aparājita īśājeṣṭyantare nā'jite triṣu \|

girikarṇījayādurgāśanaparṇīṣu yoṣiti \|\| 231 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 232 \[unit 923\]

upadhūpita āsaṅgamaraṇe paridhūpite \|

syādgaṇādhipatiḥ puṃsi śaṅkare'pi gajānane \|\| 232 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 233 \[unit 924\]

bhavet pipatiṣan pitsau triṣu puṃsi vihaṅgame \|

pṛthivīpatistu bhūpe ṛṣabhākhyauṣadhau pumān \|\| 233 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 234 \[unit 925\]

mūrdhābhiṣikto bhūpāle mantriṇi kṣatriye'pi ca \|

yādasāmpatirambhodhau paścimāśāpatāvapi \|\| 234 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tapañcakam / 235 \[unit 926\]

vasantadūtaścaitre syāt pikapañcamarāgayoḥ \|

vasantadūtī pāṭalyāmabhiyukte ca yoṣiti \|\| 235 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṭkam

## tāntavargaḥ / taṣaṭkam / fragment 927

taṣaṭkam

ardhapārāvataścitrakaṇṭhe tittiripakṣiṇi \|

## tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam

## tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam / 236 \[unit 928\]

tasaptakam

samudranavanītaṃ syāt klībaṃ pīyūṣacandrayoḥ \|\| 236 \|\|

## tāntavargaḥ / tasaptakam / fragment 929

iti tāntavargaḥ \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thaikam

## thāntavargaḥ / thaikam / 1 \[unit 930\]

thaikam

thaṃ rakṣaṇe maṅgale ca sādhvase ca napuṃsakam \|

śiloccaye pumāneva kvacittu bhayarakṣake \|\| 1 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 2 \[unit 931\]

thadvikam

artho viṣayārthanayordhanakāraṇavastuṣu \|

abhidheye ca śabdānāṃ nivṛttau ca prayojane \|\| 2 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 3 \[unit 932\]

āsthā tvālambanāsthānayatnāpekṣāsu yoṣiti \|

kanthā mṛṇmayabhittau ca tathā prāvaraṇāntare \|\| 3 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 4 \[unit 933\]

kvathaḥ syādatiduḥkhe'pi niṣpāke'pi dravasya ca \|

kuthaḥ strīpuṃsayorvarṇakambale puṃsi barhiṣi \|\| 4 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 5 \[unit 934\]

kotho nā netrarogasya bhede ca śaṭhite triṣu \|

grantho granthanādhanayoḥ syācchāstrākṣarasaṃkhyayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 6 \[unit 935\]

granthistu granthiparṇe nā bandhe rugbhedaparvaṇoḥ \|

gāthā śloke saṃskṛtānyabhāṣāyāṃ geyavṛttayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 7 \[unit 936\]

tīrthaṃ śāstrādhvarakṣetropāyanārīrajaḥsu ca \|

avatārarṣijuṣṭāmbupātropādhyāyamantriṣu \|\| 7 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 8 \[unit 937\]

tutthamañjanabhede syānnīlasūkṣmailayostriyām \|

duḥsthaḥ syād durgate mūrkhe duḥkhena tiṣṭhati triṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 9 \[unit 938\]

prastho'striyāṃ mānabhede sānāvunmitavastuni \|

pātho'rke'gnau jale klībaṃ pṛthuḥ syānmahati triṣu \|\| 9 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 10 \[unit 939\]

tvakparṇyāṃ kṛṣṇajīre strī pumānagnau nṛpāntare \|

prātho'strī hayaghoṇāyāṃ nākaṭyāmadhvage triṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 11 \[unit 940\]

manthaḥ syātpuṃsi manthāne sāktave ca divākare \|

yūthaṃ tiryaksamūhe'strī puṣpabhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 11 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 12 \[unit 941\]

rathaḥ pumānavayave syandane vetase'pi ca \|

vīthī paṅktau gṛhāṅge ca rūpakāntaravartmanoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / 13 \[unit 942\]

saṃsthaścare'vasthite strī sthitau sādṛśyanāśayoḥ \|

sārtho vaṇiksamūhe syādapi saṅghātamātrake \|\| 13 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thadvikam / fragment 943

sikyo bhaktapulāke nā madhūcchiṣṭe napuṃsakam \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 14 \[unit 944\]

thatrikam

atithiḥ kuśaputre syātpumānāgantuke triṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 15 \[unit 945\]

aśvatthaḥ pippaladrau syādaśvatthā pūrṇimātithau \|

avyatho nirvyathe sarpe cāraṭīpathyayoḥ striyām \|\| 15 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 16 \[unit 946\]

unmāthaḥ kūṭayantre syānmāraṇe ghātake pumān \|

upasthaḥ śephasi kroḍe tathā madanamandire \|\| 16 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 17 \[unit 947\]

udratho rathakīle syāttāmracūḍākhyapakṣiṇi \|

kṣavathurnā kṣute kāse kāyasthaḥ paramātmani \|\| 17 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 18 \[unit 948\]

narajātiviśeṣe nā harītakyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

kāpathaḥ kutsitapathe uśīre klībamiṣyate \|\| 18 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 19 \[unit 949\]

gogranthirnā karīṣe syād goṣṭhagojihvikauṣadhau \|

damathastu pumān daṇḍe dāme ca parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 20 \[unit 950\]

niśīthastu pumānardharātre syādrātrimātrake \|

nirgrantho nagnake'pi syānniḥsvabāliśayorapi \|\| 20 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 21 \[unit 951\]

pramathā syād harītakyāṃ harapāriṣade pumān \|

varūtho rathaguptau syāt varūthaṃ carmaveśmanoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 22 \[unit 952\]

manmathaḥ kāmacintāyāṃ kapitthe kusumāyudhe \|

vayasthā tu striyāṃ brāhmīguḍūcyāmalakīṣu ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 23 \[unit 953\]

sūkṣmailāyāṃ ca kākolyāṃ pathyā(1)lītaruṇe triṣu \|

vamathuḥ puṃsi vamane gajasya karaśīkare \|\| 23 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 24 \[unit 954\]

vidatho yogikṛtinoḥ śamathaḥ śāntimantriṇoḥ \|

ṣaḍgranthā tu vacāyāṃ strī syātkarañjāntare pumān \|\| 24 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / 25 \[unit 955\]

samarthastu hite śakte sambaddhe'pyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

siddhārthastu pumān śākyasiṃhe ca sitasarṣape \|\| 25 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thatrikam / fragment 956

(1) pathyāyāṃ taruṇe triṣu-kha. \|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 26 \[unit 957\]

thacatuṣkam

anīkastho raṇagate hastiśikṣāvicakṣaṇe \|

rājarakṣiṇi cihne ca vīramardanake'pi ca \|\| 26 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 958\]

bhaveditikathā'pārthavācyaśraddheyanaṣṭayoḥ \|

catuṣpathaṃ caturmārge saṅgame brāhmaṇe'pi nā \|\| 27 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 28 \[unit 959\]

atha citrarathaḥ sūryagandharvāntarayoḥ pumān \|

daśamīstho naṣṭavīrye sthavire'pyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / 29 \[unit 960\]

prāṇanātho yame nā syādiṣṭe prāṇādhike mataḥ \|

vānaprastho madhūke'pi syāt tṛtīyāśrame pumān \|\| 29 \|\|

## thāntavargaḥ / thacatuṣkam / fragment 961

bhavedudarathiḥ puṃsi samudre ca viyanmaṇau \|

iti thāntavargaḥ \|

## dāntavargaḥ / daikam

## dāntavargaḥ / daikam / 1 \[unit 962\]

daikam

daḥ pumānacale datte striyāṃ śodhanadānayoḥ \|

chedopatāparakṣāsu pumāṃstu dātari smṛtaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 2 \[unit 963\]

dadvikam

abdaḥ saṃvatsare vārivāhamustakayoḥ pumān \|

andūḥ striyāṃ syānnigaḍe prabhede bhūṣaṇasya ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 3 \[unit 964\]

kando'strī sūraṇe śasyamūle jaladhare pumān \|

kundo māghye'strī mukundabhraminiddhyantareṣu nā \|\| 3 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 4 \[unit 965\]

kṣodaḥ syātpuṃsi rajasi peṣaṇe ca prakīrtitaḥ \|

gado bhrātari viṣṇoḥ syādāmaye nā yudhe gadā \|\| 4 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 5 \[unit 966\]

chadaḥ palāśe garuti granthiparṇatamālayoḥ \|

chando vaśe'pyabhiprāye dhīdā putrīmanīṣayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 6 \[unit 967\]

nandirdyūtāṅga ānande'strī nandikeśvare pumān \|

nadī sariti śoṇādau nā nandā syādaliñjare \|\| 6 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 7 \[unit 968\]

gauryāṃ tithiviśeṣe strī nidhirājabhidoḥ pumān \|

nindā syādapavāde'pi kutsāyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 7 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 8 \[unit 969\]

padaṃ śabde ca vākye ca vyavasāyapradeśayoḥ \|

pādataccihnayoḥ sthānatrāṇayoraṅkavastunoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 9 \[unit 970\]

ślokapāde'pi ca klībaṃ puṃliṅgaḥ kiraṇe punaḥ \|

pādo bradhne turīyāṃśe śailapratyantaparvate \|\| 9 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 10 \[unit 971\]

caraṇe ca mayūkhe ca vandā latāntare smṛtā \|

bhikṣukyāmapi vandyāṃ ca vindurdantakṣatāntare \|\| 10 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 11 \[unit 972\]

bhruvormadhye rūpakāryaprakṛtau pṛṣate pumān \|

veditaryanyaliṅgaḥ syāt bhadraṃ kalyāṇaśarmaṇoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 12 \[unit 973\]

bhedā dvaidhe viśeṣe syādupajāpe vidāraṇe \|

mado retasi kastūryāṃ garve harṣebhadānayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 13 \[unit 974\]

mando'tīkṣṇe ca mūrkhe ca svaire cābhāgyarogiṇoḥ \|

alpe ca triṣu puṃsi syāddhastijātyantare śanau \|\| 13 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 14 \[unit 975\]

mṛduḥ syātkomale'tīkṣṇe rado dante vilekhane \|

vidā jñāne ca nirdiṣṭā manīṣāyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 14 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 15 \[unit 976\]

vedaḥ śrutau ca vṛtte ca vediḥ syātpaṇḍite pumān \|

striyāmaṅgulimudrāyāṃ syātpariṣkṛtabhūtale \|\| 15 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / 16 \[unit 977\]

śādaḥ syātkardame śaṣpe svādurmiṣṭamanojñayoḥ \|

sūdastu kathitaḥ sūpakāre ca vyañjanāntare \|\| 16 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dadvikam / fragment 978

svedastu svedane gharme hṛt klībaṃ bukkacittayoḥ \|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 17 \[unit 979\]

datrikam

aṅgadaḥ kapibhede nā keyūre tu napuṃsakam \|\| 17 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 18 \[unit 980\]

aṅgadā yāmyadigdantihastinyāmapi yoṣiti \|

arddhendurarddhacandre syādgalahastanakhāṅkayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 19 \[unit 981\]

arddhenduḥ syādatiprauḍhastrīyonyaṅguliyojane \|

(1)arbudo māṃsakīle syātpuruṣe daśakoṭiṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 20 \[unit 982\]

(1) arbudo māṃsapuruṣe daśakoṭiṣu na striyām-kha. \|

mahīdharaviśeṣe nā'thāspadaṃ padakṛtyayoḥ \|

āsando vāsudeve syāt khaṭvābhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 20 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 21 \[unit 983\]

ākrandaḥ krandane hvāne mitradāruṇayuddhayoḥ \|

bhrātaryapi ca puṃsi syādāmodo gandhaharṣayoḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 22 \[unit 984\]

kakud strī kakudo'pyastrī vṛṣāṅke rāḍdhvaje vare \|

kṣaṇado gaṇake rātrau kṣaṇadā kṣaṇadañjale \|\| 22 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 23 \[unit 985\]

kapardaḥ khaṇḍaparaśau jaṭājūṭe varāṭake \|

karṇāndurutkṣiptikāyāṃ karṇapālyāmapi striyām \|\| 23 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 24 \[unit 986\]

kravyādrakṣasi puṃsi syānmāṃsāśinyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

kāmadā kāmadhenau strī kāmadātari vācyavat \|\| 24 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 25 \[unit 987\]

kumud triṣu syātkṛpaṇe kairave tu napuṃsakam \|

kumudaṃ kairave raktapadme strī kumbhikauṣadhau \|\| 25 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 26 \[unit 988\]

gābhyāryāṃ puṃsi diṅnāge nāge śākhāmṛgāntare \|

kusīdaṃ jīvane vṛddhyā klībaṃ triṣu kusīdake \|\| 26 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 27 \[unit 989\]

kaumudaḥ syātkārtikike candrikāyāṃ tu kaumudī \|

goṣpadaṃ gopadaśvabhre gavāñca gatigocare \|\| 27 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 28 \[unit 990\]

govindā vāsudeve syād gavādhyakṣe bṛhaspatau \|

gonardamapi kaivartīmustake puṃsi sārase \|\| 28 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 29 \[unit 991\]

jalado mustake meghe jīvado vaidyavidviṣoḥ \|

tarad striyāṃ plave'pi syāt kāraṇḍe ca vihaṅgame \|\| 29 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 30 \[unit 992\]

toyado mustake meghe pumānājye napuṃsakam \|

darad striyāṃ prapāte'pi bhayaparvatayorapi \|\| 30 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 31 \[unit 993\]

dārado viṣabhede syātpārade hiṅgule pumān \|

dāyādastu bhavetpuṃsi sapiṇḍe tanaye'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 32 \[unit 994\]

dṛṣad niṣpeṣaṇaśilāpaṭṭaprastarayoḥ pumān \|

dhanadastu kubere syātpuṃliṅgo dātari triṣu \|\| 32 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 33 \[unit 995\]

naladaṃ syātpuṣparasośīramāṃsīṣu na dvayoḥ \|

narmadaḥ kelisacive narmadā saridantare \|\| 33 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 34 \[unit 996\]

nirvādaḥ syāllokavāde pariniṣṭhitavādayoḥ \|

niṣādaḥ svarabhede syāccaṇḍāle dhīvarāntare \|\| 34 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 35 \[unit 997\]

pramadaḥ sammade matte striyāmuttamayoṣiti \|

prasādo'nugrahe kāvyaguṇasvāsthyaprasattiṣu \|\| 35 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 36 \[unit 998\]

praṇādastu pumāṃstāraśabde ca śravaṇāmaye \|

prāsādo devanṛpayorgṛhe'tha varadā striyām \|\| 36 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 37 \[unit 999\]

kanyāyāṃ vācyaliṅgastu prasanne ca samarddhake \|

bhasad strī bhāsvare yonau maryādā sīmani sthitau \|\| 37 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 38 \[unit 1000\]

mākandaḥ sahakāre'strī dhātrīnagarabhedayoḥ \|

mukundo nidhibhidviṣṇuratnabhede ca kundurau \|\| 38 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 39 \[unit 1001\]

menādastu pumān kekimārjāracchāgaleṣu ca \|

viśadaḥ pāṇḍure rakte śarad strī vatsare'pyṛtau \|\| 39 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 40 \[unit 1002\]

śārado'bde striyāṃ toyapippalīsaptaparṇayoḥ \|

śasye klībaṃ śarajjātanūtanāpratibhe triṣu \|\| 40 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 41 \[unit 1003\]

ṣaḍbinduḥ syātkīṭabhedapuṇḍarīkākṣayoḥ pumān \|

sampad bhūtau guṇotkarṣe hārabhede'pi ca striyām \|\| 41 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / 42 \[unit 1004\]

saṃvid striyāṃ pratijñāyāmācārajñānasaṅgare \|

sambhāṣaṇe kriyākāre saṅkete nāmni toṣaṇe \|\| 42 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / datrikam / fragment 1005

sambhedaḥ sphuṭane saṅge sunandā rocanomayoḥ \|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 43 \[unit 1006\]

dacatuṣkam

abhiṣyando'tivṛddhau syādāsrāve'kṣigade'pi ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 44 \[unit 1007\]

aṣṭāpado'strī kanake śārīṇāṃ phalake'pi ca \|

aṣṭāpadī candramallyāṃ śarabhe markaṭe pumān \|\| 44 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 45 \[unit 1008\]

apavādastu nindāyāmājñāviśrambhayorapi \|

abhimardastu puṃsi syādavamarde samparāye ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 46 \[unit 1009\]

ekapadaṃ tatkāle napuṃsakaṃ vartmani strī syāt \|

kaṭukandaḥ pumān śigrau śṛṅgaverarasonayoḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 47 \[unit 1010\]

kuruvindaṃ ratnabhede mustākulmāṣayoḥ pumān \|

atha kokanadaṃ raktakumude raktapaṅkaje \|\| 47 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 1011\]

catuṣpadī tu padye nā paśau ca karaṇāntare \|

bhavejjanapado jānapado'pi janadeśayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 49 \[unit 1012\]

tamonudagnīndvarke nā pratipad strī tithau matau \|

parivādo'pavāde syādvīṇāvādanavastuni \|\| 49 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 50 \[unit 1013\]

priyaṃvadaḥ khecare nā priyavāci tu vācyavat \|

pīṭhamardo'tidhṛṣṭe syānnāyakasya priye'pi ca \|\| 50 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 51 \[unit 1014\]

puṭabhedo nadīvakrapatanātodyayorapi \|

mahānādaḥ kuñjare syād varṣukābde mahāsvane \|\| 51 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 1015\]

mucukundo vṛkṣabhede māndhātṛtanaye'pi ca \|

meghanādastu varuṇe tanaye rāvaṇasya ca \|\| 52 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 1016\]

viśāradaḥ paṇḍite ca dhṛṣṭe viṣṇupadaṃ tu khe \|

kṣīrode ca striyāṃ gaṅgāravisaṅkrāntibhedayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 1017\]

śaśabinduḥ pumānvāsudeve rājāntare'pi ca \|

śatānando munerbhede devakīnandane'pi ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 1018\]

śatahradā striyāṃ vajre saudāminyāṃ ca kīrtitā \|

samaryādaḥ samīpe nā maryādāsahite triṣu \|\| 55 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam / 56 \[unit 1019\]

dapañcakam

bhavedupaniṣad dharme vedānte vijane striyām \|

sahasrapādaḥ kāruṇḍe mārtaṇḍe yatnapūruṣe \|\| 56 \|\|

## dāntavargaḥ / dapañcakam / fragment 1020

iti dāntavargaḥ \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhaikam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhaikam / 1 \[unit 1021\]

dhaikam

dhā nā dharme kubere ca klībaṃ tu vasuni smṛtam \|

dho dhā ca brahmaṇi khyāto dhā tu syād dhārake'pi ca \|\| 1 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhaikam / fragment 1022

dhīrjñānabhede buddhau ca dhūḥ smṛtā dhūnane striyām \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 2 \[unit 1023\]

dhadvikam

arddhaṃ samāṃśe khaṇḍe nā'bdhirnā sarasi vāridhau \|\| 2 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 3 \[unit 1024\]

andhaṃ syāttimire klībaṃ cakṣurhīne'bhidheyavat \|

ādhiḥ pumāṃścittapīḍāpratyāśābandhakeṣu ca \|\| 3 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 4 \[unit 1025\]

vyasane cāpyadhiṣṭhāne'pīddhamātapadīptayoḥ \|

ṛddhaṃ sampannadhānye ca susamṛddhe ca vācyavat \|\| 4 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 5 \[unit 1026\]

ṛddhiḥ syādauṣadhībhede samṛddhāvapi yoṣiti \|

gandhaḥ prativeśyāmodaleśasambandhagandhake \|\| 5 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 6 \[unit 1027\]

gādhaḥ sthāne ca lipsāyāṃ godhā talanihākayoḥ \|

dadhi kṣīrottarā(1)vasthābhāve śrīvāsasa(2)rjayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 7 \[unit 1028\]

dagdhaṃ pluṣṭe'nyaliṅgaṃ syāt sthitārkadiśi ca striyām \|

digdhā viṣāktabāṇe syātpuṃsi lipte'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 8 \[unit 1029\]

dugdhaṃ prapūrite kṣīre dugdhī kṣīrāvikauṣadhau \|

dogdhā'rthopajīvikavau vatsagopālayoḥ pumān \|\| 8 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 9 \[unit 1030\]

naddho baddhe tathodvṛtte bandha ādhau ca bandhane \|

bandhuḥ syātpuṃsi bandhūke mitre trātari bāndhave \|\| 9 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 10 \[unit 1031\]

bādhā duḥkhe niṣedhe ca budhaḥ saumye ca paṇḍite \|

buddho jine labdhavarṇe puṃsi syād budhite'nyavat \|\| 10 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 11 \[unit 1032\]

bodhiḥ puṃsi samādheśca bhede pippalapādape \|

madhupuṣparase kṣaudre madye nā tu madhudrume \|\| 11 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 12 \[unit 1033\]

vasantadaityabhiccaitre syājjīvantyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

middhaṃ cittābhisaṅkṣepe klībamālasyanidrayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 13 \[unit 1034\]

mugdhastu sundare mūḍhe medhā budhau kratau pumān \|

rādho māsāntare rādhā citrabhede ca dhanvinām \|\| 13 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 14 \[unit 1035\]

gopīviśākhāmalakīviṣṇukāntāsu vidyuti \|

lubdha ākāṅkṣiṇi vyādhe vadhūḥ strī śārivauṣadhau \|\| 14 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 15 \[unit 1036\]

snuṣāśaṭīnavoḍhāsu cāryāpṛkkāṅganāsu ca \|

vyādhiḥ kuṣṭhe ca roge nā vyādho mṛgayuduṣṭayoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 16 \[unit 1037\]

(1) kṣīrāntarāvasthā0-kha. \| ; (2) śrīvāsavāsayoḥ-kha. \|

tārāmayeśe ca vidhurnā karpūrenduviṣṇuṣu \|

viddhaṃ syādvedhite kṣipte sadṛśe bādhite triṣu \|\| 16 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 17 \[unit 1038\]

vidhirnā niyatau kāle vidhāne parameṣṭhini \|

vidhā gajānne ṛddhau ca prakāre vetane vidhau \|\| 17 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 18 \[unit 1039\]

vṛddho jīrṇe pravṛddhe jñe triṣu klībaṃ tu śailaje \|

vṛddhistu varddhane yoge'pyaṣṭavargauṣadhāntare \|\| 18 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 19 \[unit 1040\]

kālāntare cābhyudaye samṛddhāvapi yoṣiti \|

śraddhā''dare ca kāṅkṣāyāṃ śrāddhaṃ śraddhā'nvite triṣu \|\| 19 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 20 \[unit 1041\]

havyakavyavidhau klībaṃ śuddhaṃ syāttriṣu kevale \|

nirdoṣe ca pavitre ca sandhā sthitipratijñayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 21 \[unit 1042\]
