sparddhā saṃharṣaṇe'pi syātsāmye kramasamunnatau \|

sandhiḥ pumān suruṅgāyāṃ bhage saṅghaṭane'pi ca \|\| 21 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 22 \[unit 1043\]

rūpakāṇāṃ mukhādyaṅge sāvakāśe'pi kīrtitaḥ \|

skandhaḥ syānnṛpatāvaṃse samparāyasamūhayoḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 23 \[unit 1044\]

kārye taruprakāṇḍe ca bhadrādau chandaso bhidi \|

sādhurvārdhuṣike cārau sajjane cābhidheyavat \|\| 23 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 24 \[unit 1045\]

siddho nā vyāsādau bhede yogasya devayoneśca \|

triṣu niṣpanne snigdhaṃ snehayute cikkaṇe'pi syāt \|\| 24 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / 25 \[unit 1046\]

sindhurvamathudeśābdhinade nā sariti striyām \|

siddhiḥ strī yoganiṣpattipādukāntarddhivṛddhiṣu \|\| 25 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhadvikam / fragment 1047

sudhā strī lepane mūrvyāṃ snuhīgaṅgaṣṭakāmṛte \|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 26 \[unit 1048\]

dhatrikam

avadhistvavadhāne syātsīmni kāle bile pumān \|\| 26 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 27 \[unit 1049\]

agādhamatalasparśe triṣu śvabhre napuṃsakam \|

ānaddhaṃ murajādau ca klībaṃ syātsandite triṣu \|\| 27 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 28 \[unit 1050\]

āviddho vācyaliṅgaḥ syātkuṭile ca parāhatam \|

ābaddho dṛḍhabandhe syātpremālaṅkārayoktrayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 29 \[unit 1051\]

utsedhastūcchraye na strī klībaṃ saṃhanane'pi ca \|

upādhirdharmacintāyāṃ kuṭumbavyāpṛte chale \|\| 29 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 30 \[unit 1052\]

viśeṣaṇe puṃsyupadhiḥ puṃsi vyājarathāṅgayoḥ \|

kabandho'strī kriyāyuktavyapamūrdhakalevare \|\| 30 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 31 \[unit 1053\]

klībaṃ jale puṃsyudare bāhurakṣoviśeṣayoḥ \|

durvidho vācyaliṅgaḥ syād durgate'pi khale'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 32 \[unit 1054\]

nyagrodhastu pumān vyāmavaṭayośca śamītarau \|

nyagrodho tūpacitrāyāṃ nirodho nāśarodhayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 33 \[unit 1055\]

niṣadhaḥ kaṭhine deśe tadrāje parvatāntare \|

paridhirnā yajñiyadruśākhāyāmupasūryake \|\| 33 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 34 \[unit 1056\]

prasiddho bhūṣite khyāte praṇidhirnā'rthane care \|

māgadhī strī kaṇāyūthyorvācyavanmagadhodbhave \|\| 34 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 35 \[unit 1057\]

puṃsi vaiśyāt kṣatriyāje śuklajīrakabandinoḥ \|

vivadho vīvadhaścāpi paryāhāre'dhvabhārayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 36 \[unit 1058\]

viśrabdho'nudbhaṭe'pi syād gāḍhaviśvastayostriṣu \|

vibudho jñe sure vīrudh latāviṭapayoḥ striyām \|\| 36 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 37 \[unit 1059\]

sannaddho barmite vyūḍhe sambādhaḥ saṅkaṭe bhage \|

sannidhiḥ sannidhāne'pi pumānindriyagocare \|\| 37 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 38 \[unit 1060\]

saṃsiddhiḥ prakṛtau siddhau madogrāyāmapi striyām \|

sambodho bodhane kṣepe samādhirnā samarthane \|\| 38 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhatrikam / 39 \[unit 1061\]

dhyānanīvākaniyame kāvyasya ca guṇāntare \|

(1)sugandhiḥ syādiṣṭagandhe triṣu klībaṃ tu vāluke \|\| 39 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 40 \[unit 1062\]

dhacatuṣkam

avarodhastirodhāne rājadāreṣu tadgṛhe \|

avaṣṭabdho vidūre syādākrānte cāvalambite \|\| 40 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 41 \[unit 1063\]

anubandhastu bandhe syāddoṣotpāde vinaśvare \|

mukhyānuyāyini śiśau prakṛtasyānuvarttane \|\| 41 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 42 \[unit 1064\]

(1) idamardhaṃ nāsti ka. \|

anubandhī tu hikkāyāṃ tṛṣṇāyāmapi yoṣiti \|

aniruddha uṣānāthe puṃsi (1)cābandhane'nyavat \|\| 42 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 43 \[unit 1065\]

āśābandhaḥ samāśvāse puṃsi markaṭajālake \|

iṣṭagandhaḥ sugandhau syāt triṣu klībaṃ tu bāluke \|\| 43 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 44 \[unit 1066\]

ikṣugandhā kokilākṣe kroṣṭyāṃ kāśe ca gokṣure \|

upalabdhirmitau prāptāvapi jñāne ca yoṣiti \|\| 44 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 45 \[unit 1067\]

ugragandhā'jamodāyāṃ vacāyāṃ chikkikauṣadhau \|

kālaskandhastamāle syāttinduke jīvakadrume \|\| 45 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 46 \[unit 1068\]

tīkṣṇagandhā vacārājikayoḥ śobhāñjane pumān \|

tṛṇagodhā citrakole kṛkalāse'pi yoṣiti \|\| 46 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 47 \[unit 1069\]

pārivyādhastu puṃsi syādvetase ca drumotpale \|

brahmabandhuradhikṣipte nirdeśe brāhmaṇasya nā \|\| 47 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 1070\]

mahauṣadhaṃ tu śuṇṭhyāṃ syādviṣāyāṃ laśune'pi ca \|

samunnaddhaḥ samudbhūte paṇḍitammanyagarvite \|\| 48 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam / 49 \[unit 1071\]

dhapañcakam

yojanagandhā kastūryāṃ sitāyāṃ vyāsamātāri \|\| 49 \|\|

## dhāntavargaḥ / dhapañcakam / fragment 1072

iti dhāntavargaḥ \|

## nāntavargaḥ / naikam

## nāntavargaḥ / naikam / fragment 1073

naikam

naḥ pumān sugate bandhe dviraṇḍe prastute'pi ca \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 1 \[unit 1074\]

nadvikam

agnirvaiśvānare'pi syāccitrakākhyauṣadhau pumān \|\| 1 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 2 \[unit 1075\]

annaṃ bhakte ca bhukte syādinaḥ patyau nṛpārkayoḥ \|

unnaṃ klinne ca surate kṛtsnaṃ sarvāmbukukṣiṣu \|\| 2 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 3 \[unit 1076\]

ghanaṃ syāt kāṃsyatālādivādyamadhyamanṛtyayoḥ \|

nā mustābdhaughadārḍhyeṣu vistāre lohamudgare \|\| 3 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 4 \[unit 1077\]

(1) cānargale-kha. \|

triṣu sāndre dṛḍhe cātha cihnaṃ lakṣmapatākayoḥ \|

cīno deśāṃśukavrīhibhede tantau mṛgāntare \|\| 4 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 5 \[unit 1078\]

channaṃ tu rahasi klībaṃ chādite vācyaliṅgavat \|

chinnaṃ kṛtte triliṅgaṃ syād guḍūcyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 5 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 6 \[unit 1079\]

jano loke maharlokātparaloke ca pāmare \|

janī sīmantinīvadhvorutpattāvauṣadhībhidi \|\| 6 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 7 \[unit 1080\]

jahnuḥ syāt puṃsi rājarṣibhede ca madhusūdane \|

jyānirjīrṇe ca hānau ca taṭinyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 7 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 8 \[unit 1081\]

jino'rhati ca buddhe ca puṃsi syājjitvare triṣu \|

jyotsnā candrātape'pi syājjyotsnāyuktaniśi smṛtā \|\| 8 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 9 \[unit 1082\]

jyotsnī paṭolikāyāṃ syājjyotsnāyuktaniśi striyām \|

tanuḥ kāye tvaci strī syāt triṣvalpe virale kṛśe \|\| 9 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 10 \[unit 1083\]

dānaṃ gajamade tyāge pālanacchedaśuddhiṣu \|

dānurdātari vikrānte dīnā strī mūṣikastriyām \|\| 10 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 11 \[unit 1084\]

vācyavad durgate bhīte dyumnaṃ vitte bale'pi ca \|

dhanuḥ pumān piyāladrau rāśibhede śarāsane \|\| 11 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 12 \[unit 1085\]

dhanaṃ tu godhane vitte dhānā bhṛṣṭayave'pi ca \|

dhanyāke'bhinavodbhinne dhunī nadyāṃ nade pumān \|\| 12 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 13 \[unit 1086\]

nagno bandikṣapaṇayoḥ puṃsi triṣu vivāsasi \|

nyūnaṃ garhyonayoḥ pānaṃ pītibhājanarakṣaṇe \|\| 13 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 14 \[unit 1087\]

bhānurnā kiraṇe sūrya bhinnaḥ syāttriṣu dārite \|

saṅgate'nyatra phulle ca mānaścittonnatau grahe \|\| 14 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 15 \[unit 1088\]

klībaṃ pramāṇe prasthādau mīno rāśyantare jhaṣe \|

muniḥ puṃsi vasiṣṭhādau vaṅkasenatarau jine \|\| 15 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 16 \[unit 1089\]

mṛtsnā mṛtsātuvaryoḥ strī yānaṃ syādvāhane gatau \|

yoniḥ strīpuṃsayośca syādākare smaramandire \|\| 16 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 17 \[unit 1090\]

ratnaṃ svajātiśreṣṭhe'pi maṇāvapi napuṃsakam \|

rāsnā ca syādbhujaṅgākṣyāmelāparṇyāmapi striyām \|\| 17 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 18 \[unit 1091\]

lagnaṃ rāśyudaye klībaṃ saktalajjitayostriṣu \|

vasnastvavakraye puṃsi vetane syānnapuṃsakam \|\| 18 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 19 \[unit 1092\]

vanaṃ napuṃsakaṃ nīre nivāsālayakānane \|

vahnirvaiśvānare'pi syāccitrakākhyauṣadhau pumān \|\| 19 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 20 \[unit 1093\]

vānaṃ śuṣkaphale śuṣkasyūtikarmakaṭe gatau \|

vinnaṃ vicārite labdhe budhno nā mūlarudrayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 21 \[unit 1094\]

śvā pumān kukkure khyātaḥ sthānabhede ca vāstunaḥ \|

śīno mūrkhe cājagare śyenaḥ pakṣiṇi pāṇḍure \|\| 21 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 22 \[unit 1095\]

svapnaḥ svāpe prasuptasya vijñāne darśane pumān \|

sānurastrī vane prasthe vātyāmārgāgrakovide \|\| 22 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 23 \[unit 1096\]

sthānaṃ sādṛśye'vakāśe sthitau vṛddhikṣayetare \|

styānaṃ snigdhe pratidhvāne dhanatvālasyayorapi \|\| 23 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 24 \[unit 1097\]

snānaṃ snānīye'bhiṣave sūnaṃ prasavapuṣpayoḥ \|

sūnā putryāṃ vadhasthāne galaśuṇḍikayoḥ striyām \|\| 24 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / 25 \[unit 1098\]

sūnuḥ putre'nuje'rke nā sūnuḥ kiraṇasūryayoḥ \|

hanurhaṭṭavilāsinyāṃ mṛtyāvastre gade striyām \|\| 25 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nadvikam / fragment 1099

dvayoḥ kapolāvayave hīnaṃ garhyonayostriṣu \|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 26 \[unit 1100\]

natrikam

añjanaṃ kajjale cāktau sauvīre ca rasāñjane \|\| 26 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 27 \[unit 1101\]

puṃsi jyeṣṭhādiggajayorañjanā vānarībhidi \|

añjanī lepyanāryāṃ cāvanaṃ prītau ca rakṣaṇe \|\| 27 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 28 \[unit 1102\]

ayanaṃ pathi bhānorapyudagdakṣiṇato gatau \|

apānaṃ tu gude klībaṃ puṃsi syāttasya mārute \|\| 28 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 29 \[unit 1103\]

asanaṃ kṣepaṇe klībaṃ puṃsi syāñjīvakadrume \|

aṅganaṃ prāṅgaṇe yāne kāminyāmaṅganā matā \|\| 29 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 30 \[unit 1104\]

arjunaḥ kakubhe pārthe kārtavīryamayūrayoḥ \|

māturekasute'pi syāt puṃliṅgo dhavale'nyavat \|\| 30 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 31 \[unit 1105\]

napuṃsakaṃ tṛṇe netragade cāpyarjunī gavi \|

uṣāyāṃ bāhudānadyāṃ kuṭṭinyāmapi ca kvacit \|\| 31 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 32 \[unit 1106\]

amlāno mahāsahāyāṃ nānyaliṅgastu nirmale \|

arśoghnī tālamūlyāṃ syādarśoghnaḥ sūraṇe'pi ca \|\| 32 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 33 \[unit 1107\]

asiknī syādavṛddhāntaḥpurapreṣyānadībhidoḥ \|

asaniḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ syāccañcalāyāṃ pavāvapi \|\| 33 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 34 \[unit 1108\]

aratnirnā saprakoṣṭhatatāṅgulikare'pi ca \|

kaphoṇāvapyathālau syād vṛścike bhramare pumān \|\| 34 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 35 \[unit 1109\]

arthī pumān yācake syāt sevake ca vivādini \|

adhvā nā pathi saṃsthāne syādavaskandakālayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 36 \[unit 1110\]

arvā turaṅgame puṃsi kutsite vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

āsanaṃ dviradaskandhe pīṭhe yātrānivartane \|\| 36 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 37 \[unit 1111\]

āsanī vipaṇau sthityāmāsano (1)jīvakadrume \|

ādānaṃ grahaṇe'pi syālaṅkāre ca vājinām \|\| 37 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 38 \[unit 1112\]

sāpannaḥ savipattau ca prāpte vācyavadīritaḥ \|

ātmā puṃsi svabhāve'pi prayatnamanasorapi \|\| 38 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 39 \[unit 1113\]

dhṛtāvapi manīṣāyāṃ śarīrabrahmaṇorapi \|

ālānaṃ kariṇāṃ bandhastambhe rajjau ca na striyām \|\| 39 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 40 \[unit 1114\]

īśānaṃ jyotiṣi klībaṃ puṃliṅgaḥ syāt trilocane \|

uttānamagabhīre syādūrdhvāsyaśayite triṣu \|\| 40 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 41 \[unit 1115\]

utthānamudyame tantre pauruṣe pustake raṇe \|

prāṅgaṇodgamaharṣeṣu malaroge'pi na dvayoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 42 \[unit 1116\]

udyānaṃ syānniḥsaraṇe vanabhede prayojane \|

udāno'pyudarāvarte vāyubhede bhujaṅgame \|\| 42 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 43 \[unit 1117\]

uddhānamudgate vācyaliṅgaṃ culyāṃ napuṃsakam \|

odanaṃ na striyāṃ bhakte balāyomodanī striyām \|\| 43 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 44 \[unit 1118\]

kamanaḥ kāmuke kāme'bhirūpe'śokapādape \|

(1) jīrakadrume-kha., nā tu jīrake - ka. \|

kampanaṃ na dvayoḥ kampe kampre syādabhidheyavat \|\| 44 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 45 \[unit 1119\]

kaṭhinamapi niṣṭhure syātstabdhe'pi triṣu napuṃsakaṃ sthālyām \|

kaṭhinī khaṭikāyāmapi kaṭhinā guḍaśarkarāyāṃ ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 46 \[unit 1120\]

krandanaṃ rodane'pi syādāhvāne'pyatha kalpanā \|

kariṇaḥ sajjanāyāṃ strī klībaṃ luptau ca kalpane \|\| 46 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 47 \[unit 1121\]

karttanaṃ na dvayośchede nārīṇāṃ sūtranirmitau \|

karmāstrī vyāpyakriyayoḥ kadanaṃ mardapāpayoḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 48 \[unit 1122\]

kāñcanaḥ kāñcanāle syāccampake nāgakesare \|

udumbare ca dhattūre haridrāyāṃ ca kāñcanī \|\| 48 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 49 \[unit 1123\]

klībe'bjakesare hemni kāminī bhīruvandayoḥ \|

kāmī tu kāmuke cakravāke pārāvate'pi ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 50 \[unit 1124\]

kānīnaḥ kanyakājātatanaye vyāsakarṇayoḥ \|

kākinī paṇapāde'pi mānapāde varāṭake \|\| 50 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 51 \[unit 1125\]

kānanaṃ vipine gehe parameṣṭhimukhe'pi ca \|

kuhanaṃ mṛttikābhāṇḍaviśeṣe kācabhājane \|\| 51 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 52 \[unit 1126\]

kuhanā dambhacaryāyāmīrṣyālau kuhanaṃ triṣu \|

kṛtī syāt paṇḍite yogye ketanaṃ tu nimantraṇe \|\| 52 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 53 \[unit 1127\]

gṛhe ketau ca kṛtye'tha keśī keśavati triṣu \|

daitye nā caurapuṣpyāṃ strī kaulīnaṃ guhyajanyayoḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 54 \[unit 1128\]

kukarmaṇi kulīnatve yuddhe paśvahipakṣiṇām \|

kaupīnaṃ syādakārye ca cīraguhyapradeśayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 55 \[unit 1129\]

khalinī khalavṛnde syāttālaparṇyāmapi striyām \|

khañjanaḥ khañjarīṭe strī sarṣapyāṃ khañjanaṃ gatau \|\| 55 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 56 \[unit 1130\]

khaḍgī nā gaṇḍake mañjughoṣe khaḍgadhare triṣu \|

garjanaṃ ninade kope gahanaṃ kalile triṣu \|\| 56 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 57 \[unit 1131\]

napuṃsakaṃ gahvare syādduḥkhakānanayorapi \|

gandhanamutsāhe syātprakāśane sūcane'pi hiṃsāyām \|\| 57 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 58 \[unit 1132\]

grāmīṇaḥ śuni kāke puṃsi triṣu sambhṛte grāmaiḥ \|

grāvā tu prastare pṛthvīdhare puṃsyatha gṛñjanam \|\| 58 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 59 \[unit 1133\]

viṣadagdhapaśormāṃse klībaṃ puṃsi rasonake \|

gostano hārabhede nā drākṣāyāṃ gostanī striyām \|\| 59 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 60 \[unit 1134\]

gomī śṛgāle puṃsi syāttriṣu gomatyupāsake \|

ghaṭṭanā calanāvṛttyoścandanī tu nadībhidi \|\| 60 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 61 \[unit 1135\]

candano'strī malayaje bhadrakālyāṃ napuṃsakam \|

calanaṃ bhramaṇe kampe kampre tu vācyaliṅgakam \|\| 61 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 62 \[unit 1136\]

calanī vastrayodhinyāṃ vāribhede'pi ca kvacit \|

cakrī tailikabhitkokakulālājāhisūcake \|\| 62 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 63 \[unit 1137\]

carmī phalakapāṇau syādbhūrje bhṛṅgariṭāvapi \|

carmakṛttau ca phalake cetanā saṃvidi striyām \|\| 63 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 64 \[unit 1138\]

vācyavatprāṇayukte'tha chadma vyājāpadeśayoḥ \|

chardanaṃ vamane klībaṃ nimbālambuṣayoḥ pumān \|\| 64 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 65 \[unit 1139\]

chadanaṃ ca dale pakṣe pidhāne chedanaṃ bhidi \|

kartane ca januḥ puṃsi jantau vaiśvānare'pi ca \|\| 65 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 66 \[unit 1140\]

javanaṃ tu syade vege haye nā vegini triṣu \|

jananī tu dayāmātrorjananaṃ vaṃśajanmanoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 67 \[unit 1141\]

jayanaṃ syātturaṅgādisannāhe vijaye'pi ca \|

jaghanaṃ ca striyāḥ śreṇipurobhāge kaṭāvapi \|\| 67 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 68 \[unit 1142\]

jñānī syātpuṃsi daivajñe jñānayukte'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|

jīvanaṃ varttane jīvaprāṇadhāraṇayorjale \|\| 68 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 69 \[unit 1143\]

jīvanī jīvanā cāpi jīvantībhedayoḥ kramāt \|

tapano'ruṣkare'pi syādbhāskare nirayāntare \|\| 69 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 70 \[unit 1144\]

talunaḥ pavane yūni yuvatyāṃ talunī smṛtā \|

talinaṃ virale stoke svacche'pi vācyaliṅgakam \|\| 70 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 71 \[unit 1145\]

tamoghnaḥ sūryavahnīndubuddhaśaṅkaraviṣṇuṣu \|

tyāgī dātari śūre'tha tevanaṃ kelikānane \|\| 71 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 72 \[unit 1146\]

krīḍāyāṃ temanaṃ tvārdrīkaraṇe vyañjane'pi ca \|

tejanaḥ puṃsi vaṃśe syāttejanī tṛṇapūlake \|\| 72 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 73 \[unit 1147\]

todanaṃ vyathane totre daṃśanaṃ daṃśavarmaṇoḥ \|

darśanaṃ nayanasvapnabuddhidharmopalabdhiṣu \|\| 73 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 74 \[unit 1148\]

śāstradarpaṇayoścātha damanaḥ puṣpavīra(1)yoḥ \|

daśanaḥ śikhare dante kavace tu napuṃsakam \|\| 74 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 75 \[unit 1149\]

dahanaścitrake bhallātake'gnau duṣṭaceṣṭite \|

dṛśānaṃ jyotiṣi klībaṃ puṃliṅgaḥ syādvirocane \|\| 75 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 76 \[unit 1150\]

devanaṃ vyavahāre syājjigīṣākrīḍayorapi \|

devano'kṣeṣu puṃsi syād dhvajī tu pṛthivīdhare \|\| 76 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 77 \[unit 1151\]

rathabrāhmaṇayoścāpi bhujaṅgamaturaṅgayoḥ \|

dhanvī dhanurdhare'pi syādarjune kakubhadrume \|\| 77 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 78 \[unit 1152\]

dhanvā tu marudeśe nā klībaṃ cāpe sthale'pi ca \|

dhamano nā'nale bhastrā'dhmāpakakrūrayostriṣu \|\| 78 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 79 \[unit 1153\]

dhamanī tu śirāhaṭṭavilāsinyośca yoṣiti \|

dhāvanaṃ gamane śuddhau pṛśniparṇyāṃ tu dhāvanī \|\| 79 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 80 \[unit 1154\]

dhāma dehe gṛhe raśmau sthāne janmaprabhāvayoḥ \|

nandanaṃ vāsavodyāne nandane harṣake sute \|\| 80 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 81 \[unit 1155\]

nandī harapratīhāre gardabhāṇḍe vanadrume \|

nandinyumāyāṃ gaṅgāyāṃ nanāndṛdhenubhedayoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 82 \[unit 1156\]

nalinī padminīvyomanimnagākamalākare \|

napuṃsakaṃ nīlikāyāṃ na puṃsi sarasīruhe \|\| 82 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 83 \[unit 1157\]

nidānaṃ kāraṇe vatsadāmādikāraṇakṣaye \|

nidhanaṃ syātkule nāśe pradhanaṃ dāraṇe raṇe \|\| 83 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 84 \[unit 1158\]

pavanaṃ kumbhakārasya pākasthāne napuṃsakam \|

niṣpāvamarutoḥ puṃsi prajñānaṃ buddhicihnayoḥ \|\| 84 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 85 \[unit 1159\]

pradhānaṃ syānmahāmātre prakṛtau paramātmani \|

(1) dhīrayoḥ- ka \|

prajñāyāmapi ca klībamekatve tūttame sadā \|\| 85 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 86 \[unit 1160\]

padminī padmasaṅghāte strīviśeṣe saroruhe \|

prasannā strī surāyāṃ syātsvacchasantuṣṭayostriṣu \|\| 86 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 87 \[unit 1161\]

prasūno vācyavajjāte klībaṃ tu phalapuṣpayoḥ \|

patrī śyene patrarathe kāṇḍadrurathikādriṣu \|\| 87 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 88 \[unit 1162\]

parva klībaṃ mahe granthau prastāve lakṣaṇāntare \|

darśapratipadoḥ sandhau viṣuvatprabhṛtiṣvapi \|\| 88 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 89 \[unit 1163\]

pakṣma sūtrādisūkṣmāṃśe kiñjalke netralomani \|

pāṭhīno mīnabhede syāt pāṭhake gugguludrume \|\| 89 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 90 \[unit 1164\]

pāvanaṃ tu jale kṛcchre nā vyāse pāvake'pi ca \|

pācanaṃ daśamūlyādau prāyaścitte ca nā'nale \|\| 90 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 91 \[unit 1165\]

vācyavatpācayitari harītakyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

pāśī vyādhe ca varuṇe piśunaṃ kuṅkume'pi ca \|\| 91 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 92 \[unit 1166\]

kapivaktre ca kāke nā sūcakakrūrayostriṣu \|

pṛkkāyāṃ piśunā strī syāt pītanaṃ pītadāruṇi \|\| 92 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 93 \[unit 1167\]

kuṅkume haritāle ca pumānāmrātake mataḥ \|

pūtanā tu harītakyāṃ dānavīrogabhedayoḥ \|\| 93 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 94 \[unit 1168\]

pṛtanā tu striyāṃ senāmātrasenāviśeṣayoḥ \|

premā nā vāsave vāte premāstrī snehanarmaṇoḥ \|\| 94 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 95 \[unit 1169\]

phalinyagniśikhāyāṃ ca phalyāṃ strī phalini triṣu \|

phālgunastu guḍākeśe nadījārjunabhūruhe \|\| 95 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 96 \[unit 1170\]

tapasyasaṃjñāmāse tatpūrṇimāyāṃ tu phālgunī \|

brahma tattvatapovede na dvayoḥ puṃsi vedhasi \|\| 96 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 97 \[unit 1171\]

ṛtvigyāgabhidorvipre bandhanaṃ vadhabandhayoḥ \|

bandanī natijīvātukuṭīyācanakarmasu \|\| 97 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 98 \[unit 1172\]

vāhinī syāttaraṅgiṇyāṃ senāsainyaprabhedayoḥ \|

vāṇinī narttakīmattāvidagdhavanitāsu ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 99 \[unit 1173\]

budhānastu gurau vijñe bodhanaṃ gandhadīpane \|

bodhanī bodhapippalyorbharma syāt kāñcane bhṛtau \|\| 99 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 100 \[unit 1174\]

bhaṇḍanaṃ kavace yuddhe khalīkāre'pi na dvayoḥ \|

bhaṭṭinī dvijabhāryāyāṃ nāṭyoktyāṃ rājayoṣiti \|\| 100 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 1 \[unit 1175\]

bhavanaṃ syād gṛhe bhāve bhājanaṃ yogyapātrayoḥ \|

bhāvanā tu na nā dhyāne paryāloce'dhivāsane \|\| 1 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 2 \[unit 1176\]

bhuvanaṃ viṣṭape'pi syāt salile gagane jane \|

bhogī bhujaṅgame'pi syād grāmapātre nṛpe pumān \|\| 2 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 3 \[unit 1177\]

vihāya mahiṣīmanyarājayoṣiti bhoginī \|

madanaḥ smaravasantadrubhiddhūstūrasikthake \|\| 3 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 4 \[unit 1178\]

malanaḥ paṭavāse nā mardane tu napuṃsakam \|

malinaṃ dūṣite kṛṣṇe ṛtumatyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 4 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 5 \[unit 1179\]

maṇḍanaṃ bhūṣaṇe klībaṃ syāttriṣvalaṅkariṣṇuni(1) \|

mārjanaṃ na dvayormārṣṭau puṃsi syāllodhraśākhini \|\| 5 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 6 \[unit 1180\]

mālinī mātṛkāvṛttabhidormālikayoṣiti \|

gaurīcampānagaryośca mandākinyāṃ nadībhidi \|\| 6 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 7 \[unit 1181\]

māninī tu striyāṃ phalyāṃ mānī mānavati triṣu \|

mithunaṃ na dvayo rāśibhede strīpuṃsayugmake \|\| 7 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 8 \[unit 1182\]

muṇḍanaṃ vapane trāṇe mehanaṃ mūtraśiśnayoḥ \|

maithunaṃ surate'pi syāt saṅgate'pi napuṃsakam \|\| 8 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 9 \[unit 1183\]

yamanaṃ bandhane coparatau klībaṃ yame pumān \|

yavano deśabhede nā vegivegādhikāśvayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 10 \[unit 1184\]

yavānyauṣadhibhede strī vācyavadvegini smṛtā \|

yāpanaṃ varttane kālakṣepe nirasane'pi ca \|\| 10 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 11 \[unit 1185\]

(1) itaḥ paraṃ sārdhamidaṃ padyaṃ nāsti-ka. \| masanaṃ gaṇḍagole syāttapasye parameṣṭhini \| maḍḍanaṃ tārkike ramye gṛhe dehe jalāśaye \|\| masūno'strī jitvare syānmeṣādau mahiṣe'pi ca \|

yuvā syāttaruṇe śreṣṭhe nisargabalaśālini \|

yuñjānaḥ sārathau vipre yojanaṃ paramātmani \|\| 11 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 12 \[unit 1186\]

catuṣkrośyāṃ ca yoge'tha rasanaṃ svadane dhvanau \|

jihvāyāṃ tu na puṃsi syādrāsnāyāṃ rasanā striyām \|\| 12 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 13 \[unit 1187\]

rañjano rāgajanane rañjanaṃ raktacandane \|

śuṇḍārocanikānīlīmañjiṣṭhāsu ca rañjanī \|\| 13 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 14 \[unit 1188\]

rajanī nalinīrātriharidrājatukāsu ca \|

rādhanaṃ sādhane prāptau rādhanā bhāṣaṇe striyām \|\| 14 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 15 \[unit 1189\]

rājā prabhau ca nṛpatau kṣatriye rajanīpatau \|

yakṣe śakre ca puṃsi syādrāgī rakte'pi kāmuke \|\| 15 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 16 \[unit 1190\]

recanī trivṛtādantyo rocanī karkaśe striyām \|

rocanā raktakalhāre gopittavārayoṣitoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 17 \[unit 1191\]

rocanaḥ kūṭaśālmalyāṃ puṃsi syādrocake triṣu \|

rodanaṃ krandane'sre'pi durālabhauṣadhau striyām \|\| 17 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 18 \[unit 1192\]

rohī rohitake'śvatthavaṭapādapayoḥ pumān \|

laṅghanaṃ tūpavāse syāt (1)kramaṇe plavane'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 19 \[unit 1193\]

lalanā kāminīnārībhedajihvāsu yoṣiti \|

lakṣma cihne pradhāne ca lāñchanaṃ nāmacihnayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 20 \[unit 1194\]

lekhanaṃ chardane bhūrje lipinyāse'tha varjanam \|

hiṃsātyāgayorvapanaṃ bījādhāne ca muṇḍane \|\| 20 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 21 \[unit 1195\]

vasanaṃ chādane vastre vamanaṃ chardane'rdane \|

varddhanaṃ vṛddhivarddhiṣṇucchede ghaṭyāṃ tu varddhanī \|\| 21 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 22 \[unit 1196\]

vyañjanaṃ temane cihne śmaśruṇyavayave'hani \|

vyasanaṃ tvaśubhe saktau pānastrīmṛgayādiṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 23 \[unit 1197\]

daivāniṣṭaphale pāpe vipattau niṣphalodyame \|

vartano vāmane klībaṃ vṛttau strī preṣaṇādhvanoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 24 \[unit 1198\]

(1) caṅkrame-kha. \|

na puṃsi tūlanālāyāṃ tarkupīṭhe ca jīvane \|

varttiṣṇau triṣu vajrī tu buddhe devādhipe pumān \|\| 24 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 25 \[unit 1199\]

vacaknustu pumān vipre vāvadūke'bhidheyavat \|

varṇī syāllekhake citrakare'pi brahmacāriṇi \|\| 25 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 26 \[unit 1200\]

varṣma dehapramāṇātisundarākṛtiṣu smṛtam \|

vartma netracchade mārge vājī bāṇāśvapakṣiṣu \|\| 26 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 27 \[unit 1201\]

vāmano'pyatikharve ca triṣu puṃsi tu diggaje \|

harāvaṅkoṭavṛkṣe'tha vāsanaṃ dhūpane'pi ca \|\| 27 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 28 \[unit 1202\]

vāridhānyāṃ ca vastre ca pratyāśājñānayoḥ striyām \|

vāgmī paṭau surācārye vijñānaṃ jñānakarmaṇoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 29 \[unit 1203\]

vitunnaṃ suniṣaṇṇe ca śaivāle ca napuṃsakam \|

vitāno yajña ulloce vistāre punnapuṃsakam \|\| 29 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 30 \[unit 1204\]

klībaṃ vṛttaviśeṣe syāttriliṅgo mattatucchayoḥ (1) \|

vimāno vyomayāne ca sārvabhaumagṛhe'pi ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 31 \[unit 1205\]

ghoṭake yānapātre ca punnapuṃsakayormataḥ \|

viklinno jarasā jīrṇe śīrṇe cārdre ca vācyavat \|\| 31 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 32 \[unit 1206\]

vipannaṃ vipadākrānte triṣu puṃsi bhujaṅgame \|

vicchinnaṃ ca samālabdhe vibhakte'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 32 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 33 \[unit 1207\]

vilagnaṃ na striyāṃ madhye triṣu syāllagnamātrake \|

viṣaghnastu śirīṣe nā guḍūcītrivṛtoḥ striyām \|\| 33 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 34 \[unit 1208\]

vyutthānaṃ svātantryakṛtye virodhācaraṇe'pi ca \|

vṛjinaṃ kalmaṣe klībaṃ keśe nā kuṭile triṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 35 \[unit 1209\]

vṛṣā karṇe mahendre nā vedanā jñānaduḥkhayoḥ \|

veṣṭanaṃ karṇaśakulyāmuṣṇīṣe mukuṭe vṛtau \|\| 35 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 36 \[unit 1210\]

vyoma vāriṇi cākāśe bhāskarasyānāśraye \|

śakuniḥ puṃsi vihage saubale karaṇāntare \|\| 36 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 37 \[unit 1211\]

(1) manda- kha. \|

śayanaṃ surate nidrāśayyayośca napuṃsakam \|

śamanaṃ śāntivadhayoḥ śamanaḥ śrāddhadaivate \|\| 37 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 38 \[unit 1212\]

śvasanaṃ svasite puṃsi mārute madanadrume \|

śakunastu pumān pakṣimātrapakṣiviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 39 \[unit 1213\]

śubhaśaṃsinimitte ca śakunaṃ syānnapuṃsakam \|

śaṅkhinī śvetavṛndāyāṃ caurapuṣpyāṃ vadhūbhidi \|\| 39 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 40 \[unit 1214\]

śaṅkhī harau samudre nā śaṅkhavatyabhidheyavat \|

śataghnī śastrabhede syāt vṛścikālyāṃ karañjake \|\| 40 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 41 \[unit 1215\]

śāsanaṃ rājadattorvyāṃ lekhājñāśāstraśāstiṣu \|

śākhī syāt pādape vede turuṣkākhyajane pumān \|\| 41 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 42 \[unit 1216\]

śikhī vahnau balīvarde śare ketugrahe drume \|

mayūre kukkuṭe puṃsi śikhāvatyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 43 \[unit 1217\]

śilpī tu vācyavat kārau striyāṃ koladalauṣadhau \|

śūlī syācchaṅkare puṃsi śūlarogiṇi vācyavat(1) \|

śṛṅgī śṛṅgayute nāge vṛṣaparvatayorapi \|\| 43 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 44 \[unit 1218\]

śleṣmaghnī mallikāyāṃ ca jyotiṣmatyāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

śobhano yogabhede nā sundare vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 45 \[unit 1219\]

savanaṃ tvadhvare snāne somanirdalane'pi ca \|

stananaṃ dhvanimātre syānmeghaśabde ca kunthite \|\| 45 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 46 \[unit 1220\]

samānaḥ satsamaikeṣu triṣu nā nābhimārute \|

santānaḥ santatau gotre syādapatye suradrume \|\| 46 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 47 \[unit 1221\]

sparśano mārute puṃsi dāne sparśe napuṃsakam \|

saṃsthānamākṛtau mṛtyau sanniveśe catuṣpathe \|\| 47 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 48 \[unit 1222\]

syandanaṃ tu srutau nīre tiniśe nā rathe'striyām \|

sajjanaṃ tu bhavet klībamuparakṣaṇaghaṭṭayoḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 49 \[unit 1223\]

vācyaliṅgaṃ kulīne syāt kalpanāyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

(1) idamardhaṃ nāsti-ka. \|

sandhānaṃ syādabhiṣave tathā saṅghaṭane'pi ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 50 \[unit 1224\]

sampannaṃ sādhite'pi syāt sampattisahite'nyavat \|

sandhinī vṛṣabhākrāntākāladugdhosrayoḥ striyām \|\| 50 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 51 \[unit 1225\]

sadma syānmandire nīre saṃvyānaṃ chādaneṃ'śuke \|

sadanaṃ mandire toye svāmī prabhuviśākhayoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 52 \[unit 1226\]

sādī turaṅgamātaṅgarathāroheṣu dṛśyate \|

sthāpanaṃ ropaṇe puṃsavane pāṭhauṣadhau striyām \|\| 52 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 53 \[unit 1227\]

sāvano yajñakarmānte yajamānapracetasoḥ \|

sādhanaṃ mṛtasaṃskāre sainye siddhauṣadhau gatau \|\| 53 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 54 \[unit 1228\]

nirvartanopāyameḍhre dāpane'nugame dhane \|

sāma klībamupāyasya bhede vedāntare'pi ca \|\| 54 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 55 \[unit 1229\]

sīmā ghāṭasthitikṣetreṣvaṇḍakoṣe'pi ca striyām \|

sūcanā vyadhane dṛṣṭau gandhane'bhinaye'pi ca \|\| 55 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 56 \[unit 1230\]

secanaṃ rakṣaṇe seke naukāyāḥ sekabhājane \|

senānīḥ syātpumān kārtikeye senāpatāvapi \|\| 56 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 57 \[unit 1231\]

sevanaṃ sīvanopāstyorhalī rāme ca karṣake \|

hastinī gajayoṣāyāṃ jātibhede ca yoṣitaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / 58 \[unit 1232\]

hasanaṃ na dvayorhāse'ṅgāradhānyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|

hāyano na striyāṃ varṣe puṃsyarcirvrīhibhedayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / natrikam / fragment 1233

hrādinī vajrataḍitorhiṇḍanaṃ bhramaṇe rate \|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 1234\]

nacatuṣkam

avalagno'striyāṃ madhye triṣu syāllagnamātrake \|\| 59 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 1235\]

anūcāno vinīte syāt sāṅgavedavicakṣaṇe \|

apācīnamavāgarthe viparyaste ca vācyavat \|\| 60 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 1236\]

abhipanno'parādhe'bhigraste cāpadgate triṣu \|

avadānaṃ khaṇḍane syādativṛtte ca karmaṇi \|\| 61 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 1237\]

abhimāno'rthādidarpe'jñāne praṇayahiṃsayoḥ \|

anīkinī striyāṃ senāmātrasenāviśeṣāyoḥ(1) \|\| 62 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 63 \[unit 1238\]

adhiṣṭhānaṃ pure cakre prabhāve'dhyāsane'pi ca \|

anvāsanaṃ snehavastāvupāstāvanuśocane \|\| 63 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 64 \[unit 1239\]

aryamā tu pumān sūrye pitṛdevāntare'pi ca \|

atharvā brāhmaṇe puṃsi vede tu syānnapuṃsakam \|\| 64 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 65 \[unit 1240\]

bhavedabhijanaḥ khyātau janmabhūmyāṃ kuladhvaje \|

kule'pi ca pumānāyodhanaṃ yuddhe vadhe'pi ca \|\| 65 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 66 \[unit 1241\]

syādākalanamākāṅkṣā parisaṅkhyānabandhane \|

ātañcanaṃ pratīvāpajavanāpyāyaneṣu ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 67 \[unit 1242\]

ārādhanaṃ ca pacane prāptau santeāṣaṇe'pi ca \|

ācchādanaṃ sampidhāne vastre'pavṛtimātrake \|\| 67 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 68 \[unit 1243\]

āskandanaṃ tiraskāre raṇe saṃśoṣaṇe'pi ca \|

āveśanaṃ śilpiśāle bhūtāveśapraveśayoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 69 \[unit 1244\]

ātmayoniḥ pumān kāmadeve ca parameṣṭhini \|

upadhānaṃ viṣe gaṇḍau praṇaye'pi napuṃsakam \|\| 69 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 70 \[unit 1245\]

utsādanaṃ samullekhodvāhanodvartaneṣu ca \|

syādutpatanamutpattau tathordhvagamane'pi ca \|\| 70 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 71 \[unit 1246\]

udvarttanamutpatane vilepane gharṣaṇe klībam \|

udayanamudaye klībaṃ vatseśāgastyayoḥ puṃsi \|\| 71 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 72 \[unit 1247\]

udvāhanaṃ (2)dvisītye syādrajjvāmudvāhanī matā \|

upāsanaṃ śarābhyāse'pyupāstāvāsane'pi ca \|\| 72 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 73 \[unit 1248\]

kapītano gardabhāṇḍaśirīṣāmrātakeṣu ca \|

aśvatthe ca kalāpī tu plakṣabarhiṇayoḥ pumān \|\| 73 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 74 \[unit 1249\]

kaladhvaniḥ pumān pārāvate pikamayūrayoḥ \|

kañcukyantaḥpurādhyakṣe ṣiḍge'hau joṅgakadrume \|\| 74 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 75 \[unit 1250\]

kātyāyano vararucau viśeṣe ca muneḥ pumān \|

(1) prabhedayoḥ-kha.; (2) praṇīte-ka. \|

kāṣāyavastre vidhavārddhajaratyumayoḥ striyām \|\| 75 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 76 \[unit 1251\]

kumbhayoniragastye syādvasiṣṭhadroṇayoḥ pumān \|

kucandanaṃ ca patrāṅge drubhede raktacandane \|\| 76 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 77 \[unit 1252\]

kuṇḍalī varuṇe kekibhoginośca sakuṇḍale \|

kesarī turage siṃhe punnāge nāgakesare \|\| 77 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 78 \[unit 1253\]

krauñcādanaṃ tu pippalyāṃ ciñcoṭakamṛṇālayoḥ \|

khaḍgadhenuḥ striyāṃ khaḍgaputrikāgaṇḍakastriyoḥ \|\| 78 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 79 \[unit 1254\]

gavādanīndravāruṇyāṃ gavāṃ ghāsādanāśraye \|

gadayitnuḥ pumānkāme jalpāke kāmuke'pi ca \|\| 79 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 80 \[unit 1255\]

ghanāghano varṣukābde mattadhātukakuñjare \|

cakre ca ghoṣayitnustu viprākokilayoḥ pumān \|\| 80 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 81 \[unit 1256\]

citrabhānuḥ pumān vaiśvānare cāhaskare'pi ca \|

colakī tu karīre syānnāraṅge kiṣkuparvaṇi \|\| 81 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 82 \[unit 1257\]

jalāṭano lohapṛṣṭhe jalaukāyāṃ jalāṭanī \|

tapasvī tāpase cānukampye triṣvatha yoṣiti \|\| 82 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 83 \[unit 1258\]

māṃsikākaṭurohiṇyostarasvī śūraveginoḥ \|

tapodhanastāpase syānmuṇḍīryāṃ tu tapodhanā \|\| 83 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 84 \[unit 1259\]

dvijanmā daśane vipre durnāma klībamarśasi \|

syāddīrghakoṣikāyāṃ strī devasenā tu vajriṇaḥ \|\| 84 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 85 \[unit 1260\]

sutāyāmapi senāyāṃ devatānāṃ ca yoṣiti \|

nāgāñjanebhasundaryāṃ nāgayaṣṭau ca yoṣiti \|\| 85 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 86 \[unit 1261\]

bhavennidhuvanaṃ kampe surate ca napuṃsakam \|

nirvāsanaṃ hiṃsane syānnagarāderbahiṣkṛtau \|\| 86 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 87 \[unit 1262\]

nirbhartsanaṃ khalīkāre'laktake'pi napuṃsakam \|

niryātanaṃ vairaśuddhau dāne nyāsārpaṇe'pi ca \|\| 87 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 88 \[unit 1263\]

niśāmanaṃ darśane syādālocane ca na dvayoḥ \|

niśamanamuktaṃ dṛṣṭau śrutau nirasanaṃ vadhe pratikṣepe \|\| 88 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 89 \[unit 1264\]

niṣṭhīvane prajananaṃ yonau janmanyapi klībam \|

prasahanamapi bhaṅge syāt kṣepe pañcānanaḥ śive siṃhe \|\| 89 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 90 \[unit 1265\]

prahasanamapi prahāse rūpakabhede ca parihāse \|

praṇidhānaṃ prayatne syāt samādhau ca praveśane \|\| 90 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 91 \[unit 1266\]

prayojanaṃ kāryahetvoḥ palāśī vṛkṣarakṣasoḥ \|

atha pravacanaṃ vede prakṛṣṭavacane'pi ca \|\| 91 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 92 \[unit 1267\]

pratipanno'nyaliṅgaḥ syādvijñāte'ṅgīkṛte'pi ca \|

prasphoṭanaṃ tu śūrpe syāttāḍane ca vikāśane \|\| 92 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 93 \[unit 1268\]

pratimānaṃ praticchāyāgaja(1)kumbhāntarālayoḥ \|

pratiyatnastu saṃskāralipsopagrahaṇeṣu ca \|\| 93 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 94 \[unit 1269\]

pratyarthī vācyaliṅgaḥ syācchatrau ca prativādini \|

parijvā tu pumānindau yājñike paricārake \|\| 94 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 95 \[unit 1270\]

prasādhanī tu kaṅkatyāṃ siddhau veśe prasādhanam \|

payasvinī nadīdhenvorvibhāvaryāṃ ca yoṣiti \|\| 95 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 96 \[unit 1271\]

atha puṇyajano yakṣe rākṣase sajjane'pi ca \|

pṛthagjanastu puṃliṅgaḥ kathito nīcamūrkhayoḥ \|\| 96 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 97 \[unit 1272\]

phalakī matsyabhede nā vācyavatphalakānvite \|

vārakī śatrucitrāśvaparṇījīvapayodhiṣu \|\| 97 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 98 \[unit 1273\]

bhūtātmā puṃsi nirdiṣṭo deve ca parameṣṭhini \|

mahādhanaṃ mahāmūlye (2)suvarṇe silhake'pi ca \|\| 98 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 99 \[unit 1274\]

mahāsenaḥ kārttikeye mahāsainyapatāvapi \|

madayitnurmada(3)yute pumān madye napuṃsakaḥ \|\| 99 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 100 \[unit 1275\]

mahāmuniragastye nā kustumburuṇi na dvayoḥ \|

mātulānī kalāye syādbhaṅgāyāṃ mātulastriyām \|\| 100 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 101 \[unit 1276\]

māludhāno mātulāhau māludhānī latā'ntare \|

medhāvī nā śuke brāhmyāṃ striyāṃ medhā'nvite triṣu \|\| 101 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 102 \[unit 1277\]

rasāyanaṃ viṣe'pi syājjarāvyādhijidauṣadhau \|

(1) danta-kha.; (2) suvastre-kha.; (3) kāmadeve-kha. \|

puṃliṅgaḥ pakṣirāje ca viḍaṅgākhyauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 102 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 103 \[unit 1278\]

rājādanaṃ kṣīrikāyāṃ priyāle kiṃśuke'pi ca \|

lalāma syāt prabhāve ca lāñchanadhvajavājiṣu \|\| 103 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 104 \[unit 1279\]

śṛṅge pradhāne bhūṣāyāmapi bāladhipuṇḍrayoḥ \|

lāṅgalī nālikeradrau revatīramaṇe pumān \|\| 104 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 105 \[unit 1280\]

vanaśvā gandhamārjāre vañcakavyāghrayoḥ pumān \|

vardhamānaḥ praśnabhede śarāvairaṇḍaviṣṇuṣu \|\| 105 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 106 \[unit 1281\]

vismāpanaḥ syātkuhake gandharvanagare smare \|

visarjanaṃ parityāge dāne sampreṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 106 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 107 \[unit 1282\]

virocanaḥ prahlādasya tanaye'rke'gnicandrayoḥ \|

viheṭhanaṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ mardane ca viḍambane \|\| 107 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 108 \[unit 1283\]

bhavedvihananaṃ vighnahiṃsayostūlapiñjane \|

viṣayī viṣayāsakte vācyavat klībamindriye \|\| 108 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 109 \[unit 1284\]

puṃsi syānnṛpatau kāmadeve vaiṣayike'pi ca \|

viṣvaksenā phalinyāṃ syādviṣvakseno janārdane \|\| 109 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 110 \[unit 1285\]

vilepanī suveṣastrīyavāgvorapi yoṣiti \|

vilāsī bhogini vyāle visvapsā nā'gnicandrayoḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 111 \[unit 1286\]

vṛkṣādanaścaladale madhucchatrakuṭhārayoḥ \|

vṛkṣādanī tu vandāyāṃ vidārīkandake'pi ca \|\| 111 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 112 \[unit 1287\]

vairocanistu sugate balidaityārkaputrayoḥ \|

śikharī syādapāmārge śailapādapayoḥ pumān \|\| 112 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 113 \[unit 1288\]

śikhaṇḍinī yūthikāyāṃ guñjāyāmapi yoṣiti \|

śikhaṇḍī nā kalāpe syād gāṅgeyārimayūrayoḥ \|\| 113 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 114 \[unit 1289\]

śṛṅgārī pūgagajayoḥ saśṛṅgārasuveśayoḥ \|

atha śleṣmaghanā mallyāṃ ketakyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 114 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 115 \[unit 1290\]

sammūrcchanamabhivyāptāvucchrāyamohayorapi \|

samutthānaṃ samudyoge vyādhīnāṃ nirṇaye'pi ca \|\| 115 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 116 \[unit 1291\]

sadādāno'bhramātaṅge herambe gandhahastini \|

syātsaṃvadanamāloce vaśīkāre napuṃsakam \|\| 116 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 117 \[unit 1292\]

samādānaṃ samīcīnagrahaṇe saugatāhnike \|

saṃvādanaṃ vāhane syāddhārāderaṅgamardane \|\| 117 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 118 \[unit 1293\]

samāpanaṃ paricchede samāptivadhayorapi \|

sanātanaḥ śāśvate'je pitratithyantare hare \|\| 118 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 119 \[unit 1294\]

stanayitnuḥ pumān vāridhare'pi stanite'pi ca \|

sarojinī tu kamalākare padme ca yoṣiti \|\| 119 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 120 \[unit 1295\]

samāpannaṃ samāpte syāt prāpte kliṣṭe vadhe'pi ca \|

sambādhanaṃ dvāḥsadane śūlāgradvārapālayoḥ \|\| 120 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 121 \[unit 1296\]

saṃyamanī yamapuryyyāṃ saṃyamanaṃ bandhane vrate'pi syāt \|

sārasanamapyurastre tanutriṇāṃ mekhalāyāñca \|\| 121 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 122 \[unit 1297\]

sāmadhenī tu dhāyyāyāṃ samidhyapi ca yoṣiti \|

samayonistu sāmotthe brahmakuñjarayorapi \|\| 122 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 123 \[unit 1298\]

sudarśano hareścakre merujambudrume pumān \|

na dvayoḥ śakranagare ājñauṣadhibhidoḥ striyām \|\| 123 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 124 \[unit 1299\]

suyāmuno vatsarāje prāsāde'bhrāntare'cyute \|

sukarmā tu pumān yogabhede syād devaśilpini \|\| 124 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 125 \[unit 1300\]

sudhanvā prauḍhadhānuṣke triṣu nā viśvakarmaṇi \|

suparvā nā śare vaṃśe parvadhūmasureṣvapi \|\| 125 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / 126 \[unit 1301\]

sudāmā tu pumān vāridharaparvatabhedayoḥ \|

saudāmanyapsarobhede taḍittadbhedayoḥ striyām \|\| 126 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / nacatuṣkam / fragment 1302

harṣayitnuḥ pumān putre kāñcane ca napuṃsakam \|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 127 \[unit 1303\]

napañcakam

athātisarjanaṃ dāne vadhe'pi ca napuṃsakam \|\| 127 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 128 \[unit 1304\]

apavarjanantu dāne nirvāṇatyāgayorapi \|

apasarjanamāmnāte parivarjanadānayoḥ \|\| 128 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 129 \[unit 1305\]

agrajanmā dvije jyeṣṭhe bhrātari brahmaṇi smṛtaḥ \|

athānuvāsanaṃ snehavastidhūpanayorapi \|\| 129 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 130 \[unit 1306\]

abhiniṣṇāno'pyakṣaramātre visarjanīye'pi \|

antevāsī śiṣye'pi syāccaṇḍāle prāntage'pi ca \|\| 130 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 131 \[unit 1307\]

syādupasparśanaṃ sparśe snānācamanayorapi \|

upasampannamuddiṣṭaṃ nihate ca susaṃskṛte \|\| 131 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 132 \[unit 1308\]

kārandhamī kāṃsyakāre dhātuvādarate'pi ca \|

kāmacārī kāmuke syāt svacchandakalaviṅkayoḥ \|\| 132 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 133 \[unit 1309\]

kiṣkuparvā pumānikṣau veṇau poṭagale'pi ca \|

kṛṣṇavartmā pumānagnau durācāre vidhuntude \|\| 133 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 134 \[unit 1310\]

syād gandhamādano bhṛṅge gandhake vānarāntare \|

strī surāyāṃ nage na strī cirajīvī kakākayoḥ \|\| 134 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 135 \[unit 1311\]

tiktaparvā hilamocīguḍūcīyaṣṭiṣu striyām \|

dantadhāvanaḥ khadire dantaśuddhau napuṃsakam \|\| 135 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 136 \[unit 1312\]

syād dhūmaketanaḥ puṃsi ketugrahahutāśayoḥ \|

syānnandivarddhanoḥ bharge pakṣānte tanaye'pi ca \|\| 136 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 137 \[unit 1313\]

pratipādanantu dāne pratipattau ca bodhane \|

syāt pṛṣṭhahāyano dhānyaviśeṣe ca mataṅgaje \|\| 137 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 138 \[unit 1314\]

pracalākī mayūre syād bhujaṅge cāpi dṛśyate \|

syāt padmalāñchanā lokeśvare brahmaṇi bhāskare \|\| 138 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 139 \[unit 1315\]

dhanade ca striyāṃ tārālakṣmīvāgdevatāsu ca \|

pītacandanamuddiṣṭaṃ kālīyakaharidrayoḥ \|\| 139 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 140 \[unit 1316\]

pṛṣṭhaśṛṅgī pumān daṃśabhīrau ṣaṇḍe vṛkodare \|

varacandanamākhyātaṃ kālīye devadāruṇi \|\| 140 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 141 \[unit 1317\]

varavarṇinī tu lākṣāharidrārocanāsu ca \|

strīratne ca phalinyāñca sādhīyasyāmapi striyām \|\| 141 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 142 \[unit 1318\]

madhusūdanastu puṃsi syād bhramare vanamālini \|

mahārajanamuddiṣṭaṃ śātakumbhakusumbhayoḥ \|\| 142 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 143 \[unit 1319\]

syānmṛtyuvañcanaḥ śambhau śrīphaladroṇakākayoḥ \|

vanamālī ca govinde vārāhyāṃ vanamālinī \|\| 143 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 144 \[unit 1320\]

viṣāyī syāt pumān rājñi vaiṣayikajane'pi ca \|

indriye kāmadeve ca viṣayāsaktapūruṣe \|\| 144 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 145 \[unit 1321\]

viśvakarmā sahasrāṃśau munibhiddevaśilpinoḥ \|

vighnakārī smṛto ghoradarśane'pi vighātini \|\| 145 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 146 \[unit 1322\]

vṛṣaparvā pumān daityabhede'pyandhakasūdane \|

vyomacārī tu nirdiṣṭo devatāyāṃ vihaṅgame \|\| 146 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 147 \[unit 1323\]

śakulādanī striyāṃ kṛṣṇabhedīkaṭukaśākayoḥ \|

śālaṅkāyana uddiṣṭo munibhede ca nandini \|\| 147 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 148 \[unit 1324\]

śivakīrttanastu puṃsi (syād) bhṛṅgarīṭe'suradviṣi \|

śvetavāhana ityeṣa sudhādhāmni dhanañjaye \|\| 148 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 149 \[unit 1325\]

śvetadhāmā pumānindau ghanasārābdhiphenayoḥ \|

syāt ṣaṣṭihāyano dhānyaviśeṣe kuñjare pumān \|\| 149 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 150 \[unit 1326\]

samprayogī kalākelau kāmuke samprayojake \|

haricandanamastrī syād devatānāṃ mahīruhe \|\| 150 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / napañcakam / 151 \[unit 1327\]

napuṃsakantu gośīrṣe jyotsnākuṅkamayorapi \|

harivāhanastu puṃsi syāt śacīpativivasvatoḥ \|\| 151 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / 152 \[unit 1328\]

naṣaṭkam

antāvasāyī śvapace munibhede ca nāpite \|

kalānunādī rolambe kalaviṅke kapiñjale \|\| 152 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / 153 \[unit 1329\]

jāyānujīvī puṃsi syānnaṭe ca bakapakṣiṇi \|

sahasravedhi klībaṃ syād rāmaṭhe nāmbuvetase \|\| 153 \|\|

## nāntavargaḥ / naṣaṭkam / fragment 1330

iti nāntavargaḥ \|

## pāntavargaḥ / paikam

## pāntavargaḥ / paikam / 1 \[unit 1331\]

pā nā vātāṇḍapūteṣu pāne pātari kīrttitaḥ \|

striyāntu rakṣaṇe pāne pūte pūritake ca saḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 2 \[unit 1332\]

padvikam

kalpaḥ śāstre vidhau nyāye saṃvartte brahmaṇo dine \|

kapirnā sihlake śākhāmṛge ca madhusūdane \|\| 2 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 3 \[unit 1333\]

kūpaḥ kūpakamṛṇmāne garttāmbuguṇavṛkṣake \|

kṛpo droṇaśyālake syād droṇapatnyāṃ kṛpī matā \|\| 3 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 4 \[unit 1334\]

kṛpā dayāyāṃ kṣepastu nindāvikṣepalepane \|

vilambapreraṇe garve khaṣpaḥ krodhe balātkṛtau \|\| 4 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 5 \[unit 1335\]

gopo gopālake goṣṭhādhyakṣe pṛthvīpatāvapi \|

grāmaughādhikṛte puṃsi śārivākhyauṣadhau striyām \|\| 5 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 6 \[unit 1336\]

chupaḥ kṣupasparśanayostrapu sīsakaraṅgayoḥ \|

talpamaṭṭe kalatre ca śayanīye ca na dvayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 7 \[unit 1337\]

trapā lajjākulaṭayostāpaḥ santāpakṛcchrayoḥ \|

tāpī nadyantare darpaḥ kastūryyāmapyahaṅkṛtau \|\| 7 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 8 \[unit 1338\]

nīpaḥ kadambabandhūkanīlāśokadrumeṣvapi \|

puṣpaṃ vikāśakusumastrīrajaḥsu napuṃsakam \|\| 8 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 9 \[unit 1339\]

rūpaṃ svabhāve saundaryye nāmage paśuśabdayoḥ \|

granthāvṛttau nāṭakādāvākāraślokayorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 10 \[unit 1340\]

repaḥ syānnindite krūre ropo ropaṇabāṇayoḥ \|

lepaḥ pralepane jagdhau vapā vivaramedasoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 11 \[unit 1341\]

bāṣpa ūṣmaṇi nā'sre'tha śaṣpaṃ bālatṛṇe smṛtam \|

puṃsi syāt pratibhāhānau śāpa akrośadivyayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / padvikam / 12 \[unit 1342\]

śilpaṃ sruve kriyāyogye svāpaḥ śayananidrayoḥ \|

sparśājñatāyāmajñāne sūpo vyañjanasūdayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 13 \[unit 1343\]

patrikam

anūpo mahiṣe nā'mbuprāyadeśe tu vācyavat \|

asrapā tu jalaukāyāṃ ḍākinyāṃ rākṣase tu nā \|\| 13 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 14 \[unit 1344\]

āvāpo bhāṇḍe vapane parikṣepālavālayoḥ \|

ākṣepo bhartsanākṛṣṭikāvyālaṅkṛtiṣu smṛtaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 15 \[unit 1345\]

kākalpaḥ kalpane veśe uḍupantu plave'striyām \|

candre puṃsyulapo na strī gulminyāṃ nā tṛṇāntare \|\| 15 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 16 \[unit 1346\]

kacchapī vallakībhede dulau kṣudragadāntare \|

puṃsi nidhyantare kūrme mallabandhāntare'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 17 \[unit 1347\]

kalāpaḥ saṃhatau barhe kāñcyāṃ bhūṣaṇatūṇayoḥ \|

candre vidagdhe vyākaraṇabhede'pi kathyate budhaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 18 \[unit 1348\]

kaśipurbhaktācchādanayorekoktyā pṛthak tayoḥ puṃsi \|

kaśyapa ukto munimṛgayorbhede kāśyapī kṣmāyām \|\| 18 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 19 \[unit 1349\]

kutapo'striyāṃ dauhitre vādye chāgajakambale \|

kuśe dinasyāṣṭamāṃśe nā sūryye kuṇapī punaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 20 \[unit 1350\]

viṭśārikāyāṃ kuṇapaḥ pūtigandhau śave'pi ca \|

jihvāpaḥ śuni mārjāre pratāpastāpatejasoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 21 \[unit 1351\]

pādapaḥ pādapīṭhe'drau pādukāyāntu pādapā \|

raktapā syājjalaukāyāṃ ḍākinyāṃ nā tu rākṣase \|\| 21 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / patrikam / 22 \[unit 1352\]

viṭapo na striyāṃ stambaśākhāvistārapallave \|

viṭādhipe nā vikalpaḥ puṃsi bhrāntau ca kalpane \|\| 22 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 23 \[unit 1353\]

pacatuṣkam

abhirūpo budhe ramye'palāpaḥ premṇyapahnave \|

avalepastu garve syāllepane bhūṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 24 \[unit 1354\]

upatāpastvarāyāṃ syāduttāpagadayorapi \|

jalakūpī kūpagartte puṣkariṇyāñca yoṣiti \|\| 24 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 25 \[unit 1355\]

nāgapuṣpastu punnāganāgakeśaracampake \|

parivāpastu paryyuptau jalasthāne paricchade \|\| 25 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 26 \[unit 1356\]

paritāpastu puṃsi syād duḥkhe ca narakāntare \|

parikampo bhaye kampe prāptarūpo jñaramyayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 27 \[unit 1357\]

piṇḍapuṣpamaśoke ca javāyāñca kuśeśaye \|

bahurūpaḥ śive viṣṇau dhūnake saraṭe smare \|\| 27 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 28 \[unit 1358\]

meghapuṣpaṃ piṇḍābhrāmbunādeye nā harerhaye \|

vipralāpo virodhoktāvapārthavacane'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 29 \[unit 1359\]

bījapuṣpaṃ marubake tathā madanake'pi ca \|

vṛkadhūpastu saraladravakṛtrimadhūpayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / pacatuṣkam / 30 \[unit 1360\]

vṛṣākapiḥ pumān kṛṣṇe śaṅkare jātavedasi \|

hemapuṣpamaśoke'pi japāpuṣpe napuṃsakam \|\| 30 \|\|

## pāntavargaḥ / papañcakam

## pāntavargaḥ / papañcakam / fragment 1361

papañcakam

bhaveccāmarapuṣpantu pūge kāśāmraketake \|

iti pāntavargaḥ \|

## phāntavargaḥ / phaikam

## phāntavargaḥ / phaikam / 1 \[unit 1362\]

phaikam

pho yajñasādhane sphāne jhañjhāvāte ca puṃsyayam \|

phaṃ rūkṣoktau ca phūtkāre tathā niṣphalabhāṣaṇe \|\| 1 \|\|

## phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam

## phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / 2 \[unit 1363\]

phadvikam

gumphastu gumphane bāhoralaṅkāre ca kīrttyate \|

repho varṇe ca puṃsi syāt kutsite punaranyavat \|\| 2 \|\|

## phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / 3 \[unit 1364\]

śaphaṃ mūle tarūṇāṃ syād gavādīnāṃ khure'pi ca \|

śiphā jaṭāyāṃ sariti māṃsikāyāñca mātari \|\| 3 \|\|

## phāntavargaḥ / phadvikam / fragment 1365

iti phāntavargaḥ \|

## bāntavargaḥ / baikam

## bāntavargaḥ / baikam / 1 \[unit 1366\]

baikam

baḥ pumān varuṇe sindhau bhage toye gete'pi ca \|

gandhane tantusantāne puṃsyeva vapane smṛtaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 2 \[unit 1367\]

badvikam

kambuḥ śaṅkhe'striyāṃ puṃsi śambūke balaye gaje \|

kambiraṃśe ca vaṃśasya khajākāyāmapi striyām \|\| 2 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 3 \[unit 1368\]

klībaṃ syāt paṇḍake na strī vācyaliṅgastvavikrame \|

kharbaṃ saṅkhyantare klībaṃ nāce vāmanake triṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 4 \[unit 1369\]

garvo'bhimāne'valepe jambuḥ syāt pādapāntare \|

tathā sumerusariti dvīpabhede'pi ca striyām \|\| 4 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 5 \[unit 1370\]

ḍimbo bhayadhvanāvaṇḍe phupphusaplīhaviplave \|

darbiḥ striyāṃ khajākāyāṃ phaṇāyāmuragasya ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 6 \[unit 1371\]

pūrve tu pūrvajeṣu syuḥ pūrvaḥ prāgādyayostriṣu \|

lambā padmālayāgauryyostiktatumbyāmapi striyām \|\| 6 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 7 \[unit 1372\]

bimbastu pratibimbe syanmaṇḍale punnapuṃsakam \|

vimbikāyāḥ phale klībaṃ kṛkalāse punaḥ pumān \|\| 7 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / badvikam / 8 \[unit 1373\]

śambaḥ syānmusalāgrasthalohamaṇḍalake pavau \|

śubhānvite triṣu stambo'prakāṇḍadrumagucchayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 9 \[unit 1374\]

batrikam

kadambaṃ nikurambe syānnīpasarṣapayoḥ pumān \|

kalambī śākabhede syāt kadambaśarayoḥ pumān \|\| 9 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 10 \[unit 1375\]

kādambaḥ syāt pumān pakṣiviśeṣe sāyake'pi ca \|

gajāhvā gajapippalyāṃ gajāhvaṃ hastināpure \|\| 10 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 11 \[unit 1376\]

gandharvaḥ paśubhede syāt puṃskokilaturaṅgayoḥ \|

antarābhavasattve ca gāyane khecare'pi ca \|\| 11 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 12 \[unit 1377\]

goḍumbaḥ śīrṇavṛnte gavādanyāntu yoṣiti \|

dvijihvastu khale sarpe nitambaḥ skandharodhasoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 13 \[unit 1378\]

striyāḥ paścāt kaṭāvadreḥ kaṭake kaṭimātrake \|

pralambo daityabhede syāt trapuṣe'pi payodhare \|\| 13 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / 14 \[unit 1379\]

latāṅkure ca śākhāyāṃ hārabhede pralambane \|

bhūjambūrapi godhūme vikaṅkataphale striyām \|\| 14 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / batrikam / fragment 1380

herambo vighnarāje syānmahiṣe śauryyagarvite \|

## bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam

## bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam / 15 \[unit 1381\]

bacatuṣkam

rājajambūstu jambūbhitpiṇḍakharjūrayoḥ striyām \|\| 15 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / bacatuṣkam / 16 \[unit 1382\]

lalajihvā'nyavat hiṃsre kramelakaśunoḥ pumān \|

śataparvā tu dūrvāyāṃ vacābhārgavayoṣitoḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam

## bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam / 17 \[unit 1383\]

dhūlīkadambo nīpe syāt tiniśe varuṇadrume \|

śṛgālajambūrgoḍumbe tathā ghoṇṭāphale striyām \|\| 17 \|\|

## bāntavargaḥ / bapañcakam / fragment 1384

iti bāntavargaḥ \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhaikam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhaikam / 1 \[unit 1385\]

bhaikam

bhaṃ nakṣatre gabhastau strī puṃsi syād bhṛgunandane \|

bhūḥ sthānamātre kathitā dharaṇyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 1 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 2 \[unit 1386\]

bhadvikam

kumbho rāśyantare hastimūrddhāṃśe rākṣasāntare \|

kāmuke vāranāryyāñca ghaṭe klībantu guggulau \|\| 2 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 3 \[unit 1387\]

striyāmukhākaṭphalayoḥ pṛśnikāyāñca pāṭalau \|

garbho bhrūṇe'rbhake kukṣau sandhau panasakaṇṭake \|\| 3 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 4 \[unit 1388\]

jambho daityaviśeṣe nā dante jambīrabhakṣyayoḥ \|

jṛmbhā vikāśajṛmbhaṇayostriṣu ḍimbho'pi bāliśe bāle \|\| 4 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 5 \[unit 1389\]

dambhastu kaitake kalke dṛnbhūḥ strī sarpacakrayoḥ \|

nābhirmukhyanṛpe cakramadhyakṣatriyayoḥ pumān \|\| 5 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 6 \[unit 1390\]

dvayoḥ prāṇipratīke syāt striyāṃ kastūrikāmade \|

nibhastu kathito vyāje puṃliṅgaḥ sadṛśe triṣu \|\| 6 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 7 \[unit 1391\]

rambhā kadalyapsarasornā veṇau bānarāntare \|

vibhuḥ prabhau sarvagate śaṅkarabrahmaṇostu nā \|\| 7 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 8 \[unit 1392\]

śambhuḥ puṃsi mahādeve parameṣṭhini cā'rhati \|

śubho yoge śubhaṃ kṣeme vācyavat kṣemaśālini \|\| 8 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / 9 \[unit 1393\]

khasañcāripure strī tu śobhā kāntīcchayormatā \|

sabhā sāmājike goṣṭhyāṃ dyūtamandirayorapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhadvikam / fragment 1394

svabhūrnā brahmaṇi harau stambhaḥ sthūṇājaḍatvayoḥ \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 10 \[unit 1395\]

bhatrikam

ārambhastu tvarāyāṃ syādudyame vadhadarpayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 11 \[unit 1396\]

ātmabhūrnā vidhau kāme'pyṛṣabhastvauṣadhāntare \|

svarabhidvṛṣayoḥ karṇarandhrakumbhīrapucchayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 12 \[unit 1397\]

uttarasthaḥ smṛtaḥ śreṣṭhe strī narākārayoṣiti \|

śūkaśimbyāṃ śirālāyāṃ vidhavāyāṃ kvacinmatā \|\| 12 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 13 \[unit 1398\]

kakubh striyāṃ praveṇīdikśobhāsu campakasraji \|

kakubho rāgabhede'pi vīṇāṅge'rjunapādape \|\| 13 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 14 \[unit 1399\]

kaṭabhī vṛkṣabhede'pi jyotiṣmatyāmapi striyām \|

karabho maṇibandhādikaniṣṭhāntoṣṭratatsute \|\| 14 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 15 \[unit 1400\]

kusumbhaṃ hemani mahārajate nā kamaṇḍalau \|

gardabhaṃ śvetakumude gardabho gandhabhidyapi \|\| 15 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 16 \[unit 1401\]

rāsabhe gardabhī kṣudrarogajantuviśeṣayoḥ \|

durlabhastriṣu duṣprāpe karcūre dundubhiḥ pumān \|\| 16 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 17 \[unit 1402\]

varuṇe daityabheryyośca stryakṣabindutrikadvaye \|

nikumbhaḥ kumbhakarṇasya tanaye dantikauṣadhau \|\| 17 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 18 \[unit 1403\]

vallabho dayite'dhyakṣe sallakṣaṇaturaṅgame \|

varṣābhūḥ strī ca śeāthaghnyāṃ bhūlatāplavayoḥ pumān \|\| 18 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 19 \[unit 1404\]

viṣkambho yogabhede syādvistārapratibandhayoḥ \|

rūpakāṅgaprabhede ca bandhabhede ca yoginām \|\| 19 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 20 \[unit 1405\]

viśrambhaḥ kelikalahe viśvāse praṇaye'pi ca \|

viṣṭambhaḥ pratibandhe syāt prabhede cāmayasya ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 21 \[unit 1406\]

vṛṣabhaḥ śreṣṭhavṛṣayorvaidarbhī rītibhidyapi \|

vaidarbhaṃ vākyavakratve śarabhastu paśorbhidi \|\| 21 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 22 \[unit 1407\]

karabhe vānarabhidi sanābhirjñātitulyayoḥ \|

surabhiḥ śallakīmātṛbhinmurāgoṣu yoṣiti \|\| 22 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / 23 \[unit 1408\]

campake ca vasante ca tathā jātīphale pumān \|

svarṇe gandhotpale klībaṃ sugandhikāntayostriṣu \|\| 23 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhatrikam / fragment 1409

vikhyāte sacive dhīre caitre'pi ca pumānayam \|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam / 24 \[unit 1410\]

bhacatuṣkam

anuṣṭabh syāt sarasvatyāṃ chandobhede ca yoṣiti \|\| 24 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam / 25 \[unit 1411\]

avaṣṭambhaḥ suvarṇe ca stambhaprārambhayorapi \|

śātakumbhaṃ suvarṇe syāt śātakumbho'śvamārake \|\| 25 \|\|

## bhāntavargaḥ / bhacatuṣkam / fragment 1412

iti bhāntavargaḥ \|

## māntavargaḥ / maikam

## māntavargaḥ / maikam / 1 \[unit 1413\]

maikam

mo yame samaye'pi syāt viṣe ca madhusudane \|

mā strī padmālayāyāṃ syāt puṃliṅgaścandraśekhare \|\| 1 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 2 \[unit 1414\]

madvikam

āmo ruktadbhidoḥ puṃsi syādapakve'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|

umā'tasīhaimavatīharidrākīrttikāntiṣu \|\| 2 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 3 \[unit 1415\]

ūrmiḥ strīpuṃsayorvīcyāṃ prakāśe vegabhaṅgayoḥ \|

vastrasaṅkocarekhāyāṃ vedanāpīḍayorapi \|\| 3 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 4 \[unit 1416\]

kramaścānukrame śaktau kalpe cākramaṇe'pi ca \|

kṣamā bhūmau titikṣāyāṃ striyāṃ yukte napuṃsakam \|\| 4 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 5 \[unit 1417\]

vācyavat śaktahitayoḥ kāmaḥ smarecchayoḥ pumān \|

retasyapi nikāme ca kāmye'pi syānnapuṃsakam \|\| 5 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 6 \[unit 1418\]

kāmirnā kāmuke ratyāṃ strī kim kṣepavitarkayoḥ \|

nindāyāñca paripraśne vācyaliṅgamudāhṛtam \|\| 6 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 7 \[unit 1419\]

kṛmirnā kṛmivatkīṭe lākṣāyāṃ kṛmile khare \|

kirmī palāśe śālāyāṃ hemaputryāñca yoṣiti \|\| 7 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 8 \[unit 1420\]

kṣumā'tasīnīlikayoḥ kṣemaṃ syāllabdharakṣaṇe \|

caṇḍāyāṃ nā śubhe na strī kātyāyanyāñca yoṣiti \|\| 8 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 9 \[unit 1421\]

kṣaumamaṭṭe dukūle'strī kṣaumaṃ valkalajāṃśuke \|

śaṇaje'tasīje kharmaṃ pauruṣe koṣajāṃśuke \|\| 9 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 10 \[unit 1422\]

gamo nā'kṣavivartte syādaparyyālocane'dhvani \|

grāmaḥ svare saṃvasathe grīṣma ūṣmarttabhedayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 11 \[unit 1423\]

gulmaḥ senāghaṭṭabhidoḥ sainyarakṣaṇarugbhidoḥ \|

stambe striyāmāmalakyelāvanīvastraveśmasu \|\| 11 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 12 \[unit 1424\]

gharmaḥ syādātape grīṣme'pyūṣmasvedāmbhasorapi \|

camūḥ senāviśeṣe ca senāmātre ca yoṣiti \|\| 12 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 13 \[unit 1425\]

jālmaḥ syāt pāmare krūre'samīkṣyakāriṇi triṣu \|

jihmastu kuṭile mande klībaṃ tagarapādape \|\| 13 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 14 \[unit 1426\]

tokmaṃ karṇamale puṃsi harite ca haridyave \|

damastu damathe daṇḍe kardame damane pumān \|\| 14 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 15 \[unit 1427\]

dasmastu yajamāne syādapi caure hutāśane \|

drumo mahīruhe pārijāte kimpuruṣeśvare \|\| 15 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 16 \[unit 1428\]

dharmo'strī puṇya ācāre svabhāvopamayoḥ kratau \|

ahiṃsopaniṣannyāye nā dhanuryamasomape \|\| 16 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 17 \[unit 1429\]

dhyāmaṃ damanake gandhatṛṣṇe'tha śyāmale triṣu \|

nemirnā tiniśe kūpatrikācakrāntayoḥ striyām \|\| 17 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 18 \[unit 1430\]

nemaḥ kīle'vadhau gartte prākāre kaitave'pi ca \|

padmo'strī padmake vyūhanidhisaṅkhyāntare'mbuje \|\| 18 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 19 \[unit 1431\]

nā nāge strī phañjikāśrīcāraṭīpannageṣu ca \|

brahmī tu phañjikāyāṃ syāt śākamatsyaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 20 \[unit 1432\]

brāhmī tu bhāratīsomavallarībrahmaśaktiṣu \|

bhramo'mbunirgame bhrāntau kundabhramaṇayorapi \|\| 20 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 21 \[unit 1433\]

bhāmaḥ krodhe ravau dīptau bhīṣmo gāṅgeyaghorayoḥ \|

bhīmo'mlavetase ghore śambhau madhyamapāṇḍave \|\| 21 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 22 \[unit 1434\]

bhūmirvasundharāyāṃ syāt sthānamātre'pi ca striyām \|

bhaumaḥ kuje ca narake puṃsi bhūmibhave triṣu \|\| 22 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 23 \[unit 1435\]

yamo'nyaliṅgo yamaje nā kāke śamane śanau \|

śarīrasādhanāpekṣanityakarmaṇi saṃyame \|\| 23 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 24 \[unit 1436\]

yāmastu puṃsi prahare saṃyame ca prakīrttitaḥ \|

yāmiḥ kulastrīsvasroḥ strī yudhmo dhanuṣi saṃyuge \|\| 24 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 25 \[unit 1437\]

raśmiḥ pumān dīdhitau syāt pakṣmapragrahayorapi \|

ramā lakṣmyāṃ ramaḥ kānte raktāśokadrume smare \|\| 25 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 26 \[unit 1438\]

rāmā yoṣāhiṅgunadyoḥ klībaṃ vāstūkakuṣṭhayoḥ \|

nā rāghave ca varuṇe raiṇukeye halāyudhe \|\| 26 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 27 \[unit 1439\]

haye ca paśubhede ca triṣu cārau site'site \|

rumā sugrīvadāreṣu viśiṣṭalavaṇākare \|\| 27 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 28 \[unit 1440\]

rukmañca kāñcane lohe lakṣmīḥ sampattiśobhayoḥ \|

ṛddhauṣadhe ca padmāyāṃ vṛddhināmauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 28 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 29 \[unit 1441\]

phalinyāṃ strī vamirvāntau striyāṃ puṃsi hutāśane \|

vāmaṃ dhane paṃsi hare kāmadeve payodhare \|\| 29 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 30 \[unit 1442\]

valgupratīpasavyeṣu triṣu nāryyāṃ striyāmatha \|

vāmī śṛgālīvaḍavārāsabhīkarabhīṣu ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 31 \[unit 1443\]

śamī śaktuphalāyāñca śimbikāyāñca vāggulau \|

śyāmo vaṭe prayāgasya vāride vṛddhadārake \|\| 31 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 32 \[unit 1444\]

pike ca kṛṣṇaharitoḥ puṃsi syāt tadvati triṣu \|

marīce sindhulavaṇe klībaṃ strī śārivauṣadhau \|\| 32 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 33 \[unit 1445\]

aprasūtāṅganāyāñca priyaṅgāvapi bāgujau \|

yamunāyāṃ triyāmāyāṃ kṛṣṇatrivṛtikauṣadhau \|\| 33 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 34 \[unit 1446\]

nīlikāyāmatha śrāmī māse maṇḍapakālayoḥ \|

śuṣmaṃ tejasi sūryye nā samā saṃvatsare striyām \|\| 34 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 35 \[unit 1447\]

sarvasādhusamāneṣu samaṃ syādabhidheyavat \|

sīmā''ghāṭasthitikṣetre'ṣvaṇḍakoṣe'pi ca striyām \|\| 35 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 36 \[unit 1448\]

sūkṣmaṃ syāt kaitave'dhyātme puṃsyaṇau triṣu cālpake \|

sūmaṃ kṣīre ca nabhasi somastu himadīdhitau \|\| 36 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 37 \[unit 1449\]

vānare ca kubere ca pitṛdeve samīraṇe \|

vasuprabhede karpūre nīre somalatauṣadhau \|\| 37 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / madvikam / 38 \[unit 1450\]

himaṃ tuṣāramalayodbhavayoḥ syānnapuṃsakam \|

śītale vācyaliṅgo'tha homirnā pāvake ghṛte \|\| 38 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 39 \[unit 1451\]

matrikam

adhamaḥ syād garhyajane'pyāgamaḥ śāstra āgatau \|

āśramo brahmacaryādau vānaprasthe maṭhe vane \|\| 39 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 40 \[unit 1452\]

astriyāmuttamā dugdhikāyāṃ strī triṣu bhadrake \|

uddāmo bandharahite svatantre ca pracetasi \|\| 40 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 41 \[unit 1453\]

kalamaḥ puṃsi lekhanyāṃ śālau paṭaccare'pi ca \|

kusumaṃ strī rajonetrarogayoḥ phalapuṣpayoḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 42 \[unit 1454\]

kṛtrimaṃ lavaṇabhede nā sihlake racite triṣu \|

golomī śvetadūrvāyāṃ syād vacābhūtakeśayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 43 \[unit 1455\]

godhūmo nāgaraṅge syādoṣadhivrīhibhedayoḥ \|

gotamo nā munau buddhe rocanīdurgayoḥ striyām \|\| 43 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 44 \[unit 1456\]

talimaṃ kuṭṭime talpe candrahāse vitānake \|

dāḍimastu triliṅgaḥ syādelāyāṃ karake triṣu \|\| 44 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 45 \[unit 1457\]

niṣkramo buddhisampattau nirgame duṣkule'pi ca \|

nigamo vāṇije puryyāṃ kaṭe vede vaṇikpathe \|\| 45 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 46 \[unit 1458\]

niyamo mantraṇāyāñca pratijñāniścaye vrate \|

naigamaḥ syādupaniṣadvaṇijornāgare'pi ca \|\| 46 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 47 \[unit 1459\]

paramaṃ syādanujñāyāmavyayaṃ paramaṃ pare \|

prathamastu bhavedādau pradhāne'pi ca vācyavat \|\| 47 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 48 \[unit 1460\]

pratimā gajadantasya bandhe cānukṛtāvapi \|

pañcamo rāgabhede syāt svarabhede'tha pañcamī \|\| 48 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 49 \[unit 1461\]

pāṇḍavānāñca patnyāṃ strī pañcānāṃ pūraṇe triṣu \|

prakramaḥ krame'vasare madhyamo madhyaje'nyavat \|\| 49 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 50 \[unit 1462\]

pumān svare madhyadeśe'pyavalagne tu na striyām \|

striyāṃ dṛṣṭarajonāryyāṃ karṇikāṅgulibhedayoḥ \|\| 50 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 51 \[unit 1463\]

tryakṣaracchandasi tathā lalāmaṃ lāñchane dhvaje \|

śṛṅge pradhāne bhūṣāyāṃ tathā bāladhipuṇḍrayoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 52 \[unit 1464\]

turaṅge ca prabhāve'tha vyāyāmaḥ pauruṣe śrame \|

viṣame durgasañcāre vibhramo bhrāntihāvayoḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 53 \[unit 1465\]

vidrumo ratnavṛkṣe'pi prabāle'pi pumānayam \|

vilomastu pratīpe syādbhujaṅge varuṇe śuni \|\| 53 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 54 \[unit 1466\]

āmalakyāṃ vilomī ca vilomañcāraghaṭṭake \|

vikramastu pumān krāntimātre syāt śaktisampadi \|\| 54 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 55 \[unit 1467\]

saṃkramaḥ kramaṇe puṃsi na strī syād durgasañcare \|

sambhramaḥ sādhvase'pi syāt saṃvegādarayorapi \|\| 55 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 56 \[unit 1468\]

sattamaḥ syāt pūjyatame sādhīyasyuttame triṣu \|

saramā kukkurīdevaśunyoḥ syād rākṣasībhidi \|\| 56 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / matrikam / 57 \[unit 1469\]

suṣamā'tyantaśobhāyāṃ strī cārau ca same triṣu \|

suṣīmaḥ śītale cārau triṣu nā pannagāntare \|\| 57 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 1470\]

macatuṣkam

bhavedanupamā supratīkinyāṃ strī triṣūttame \|

abhyāgamo virodhājighātābhyudgamanāntike \|\| 58 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 1471\]

upakramastūpadhāyāṃ jñātrārambhe ca vikrame \|

cikitsāyāmupagamaḥ svīkāre'ntikasarpaṇe \|\| 59 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 1472\]

jalagulmo jalāvartte kacchape jalajatvare \|

daṇḍayāmastu kīnāśe divase kumbhasambhave \|\| 60 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 1473\]

plavaṅgamastu maṇḍūke tathā śākhāmṛge'pi ca \|

parākramo vikrame syāt sāmarthyodyogayorapi \|\| 61 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 1474\]

mahāpadmaḥ pumān nāganidhisaṅkhyāntareṣu ca \|

yātayāmo'nyavajjīrṇe paribhuktoñjhite'pi ca \|\| 62 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / macatuṣkam / fragment 1475

sārvabhaumastu diṅnāge sarvapṛthvīpatāvapi \|

## māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam

## māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam / 63 \[unit 1476\]

mapañcakam

abhyupagamastu puṃsi svīkāre nikaṭagamane ca \|\| 63 \|\|

## māntavargaḥ / mapañcakam / fragment 1477

nakṣatranemiruktā revatyāṃ nā dhruve candre \|

iti māntavargaḥ \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yaikam

## yāntavargaḥ / yaikam / 1 \[unit 1478\]

yaikam

yo nā vāyau yamane yā yātrāptidhūmitatyāgeṣu \|

vāraṇayogasamajñāyāneṣu pumāṃstu gantari vikhyātaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yaikam / 2 \[unit 1479\]

jyā mātari vasudhāyāṃ maurvyāṃ dyu klībamahni gamane ca \|

nā jvalane dyaustridaśālayatridaśavartmanoryoṣit \|\| 2 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 3 \[unit 1480\]

yadvikam

avyaḥ syāt paṅkaje puṃsi triṣu ramye ca nāvike \|

arthyaṃ śilājatunyārthye budhe nyāyye ca vācyavat \|\| 3 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 4 \[unit 1481\]

antyastu puṃsi mustāyāmantodbhūte'dhame triṣu \|

arghyamarghasya yogye syādarghārthe'pi ca vācyavat \|\| 4 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 5 \[unit 1482\]

anyo'sadṛśetarayoraryyaḥ syāt svāmivaiśyayoḥ \|

āsyaṃ mukhe ca tanmadhye tadbhave ca striyāṃ sthitau \|\| 5 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 6 \[unit 1483\]

ijyā dāne'dhvare'rccāyāṃ saṅgame strī gurau triṣu \|

ibhyā kareṇuśallakyoḥ striyāmāḍhye tu vācyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 7 \[unit 1484\]

kaśyaṃ triṣu kaśārhe syāt klībaṃ madyāśvamadhyayoḥ \|

kanyā kumārikānāryyoroṣadhīrāśibhedayoḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 8 \[unit 1485\]

kṣayo rogāntare veśmakalpāntāpacayeṣu ca \|

kalyaṃ prabhāte klībaṃ syāt kalyo vākśrutivarjite \|\| 8 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 9 \[unit 1486\]

sajjanīrogadakṣeṣu kalyāṇavacane'pi ca \|

upāyavacane'pi syāt triṣu madye tu yoṣiti \|\| 9 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 10 \[unit 1487\]

kakṣyo bṛhatikāyāṃ syāt kāñcyāṃ madhyebhabandhane \|

harmyādīnāṃ prakoṣṭhe'pi kāryyaṃ hetau prayojane \|\| 10 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 11 \[unit 1488\]

kāvyaṃ granthe pumān śukre kāvyā syāt pūtanādhiyoḥ \|

kāṃsyaṃ vādyāyantare pānapātre syāt taijasāntare \|\| 11 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 12 \[unit 1489\]

kāyaḥ kadaivate mūrttau saṅghe lakṣyasvabhāvayoḥ \|

manuṣyatīrthe kāyaṃ syāt kriyā tūpāyaceṣṭayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 13 \[unit 1490\]

ārambhe niṣkṛtau pūjāsampradhāraṇakarmasu \|

śikṣācikitsākaraṇe kuḍyaṃ syāttu napuṃsakam \|\| 13 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 14 \[unit 1491\]

vilepane ca bhittau ca tathā kautūhale'pi ca \|

kulyaṃ syāt kīkase'pyaṣṭadroṇīśūrpāmiṣeṣu ca \|\| 14 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 15 \[unit 1492\]

kulyā payaḥpraṇālyāñca nadyāṃ jīvantikauṣadhau \|

kulodbhave kulahite triṣu mānye punaḥ pumān \|\| 15 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 16 \[unit 1493\]

kṛtyā kriyādevatayostriṣu vidviṣṭakāryyayoḥ \|

gayaḥ syāt paśurājarṣyorbhede tīrthe punargayā \|\| 16 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 17 \[unit 1494\]

gavyaṃ napuṃsakaṃ jyāyāṃ rāgadravye'pyatha striyām \|

gosamūhe triliṅgantu godugdhādau ca gohite \|\| 17 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 18 \[unit 1495\]

grāmyaṃ strīkaraṇe klībe'ślīlaprākṛtayostriṣu \|

guhyaṃ rahasyupasthe ca guhyaḥ kamaṭhadambhayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 19 \[unit 1496\]

gṛhyaṃ gude granthabhede klībaṃ śākhāpure striyām \|

gṛhāsakte mṛgādau nā triṣu cāsvairipakṣayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 20 \[unit 1497\]

geyantu gīte geyaḥ syād gātavye gāyane triṣu \|

gopyo dāsīsute puṃsi rakṣaṇīye'bhidheyavat \|\| 20 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 21 \[unit 1498\]

cavyantu cavike klībaṃ vacāyāmapi yoṣiti \|

cayaḥ samūhe prākrāre mūlabandhe samāhṛtau \|\| 21 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 22 \[unit 1499\]

cityaṃ mṛtakacaitye syāccitrāmṛtacitau striyām \|

caityamāyatane buddhabimbe noddeśapādape \|\| 22 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 23 \[unit 1500\]

codyaṃ syādadbhute praśne codanārhe tu vācyavat \|

chāyā syādātapābhāve pratibimbārkayoṣitoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 24 \[unit 1501\]

pālanotkocayordīptisacchobhāpaṅktiṣu striyām \|

jayā jayantītithibhitpathyomātatsakhīṣu ca \|\| 24 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 25 \[unit 1502\]

agnimanthe nā jayante vijaye ca yudhiṣṭhire \|

janyaṃ haṭṭe parīvāde saṅgrāme ca napuṃsakam \|\| 25 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 26 \[unit 1503\]

janyā mātṛvayasyāyāṃ janyaḥ syājjanake pumān \|

triṣūtpādyajanitrośca navoḍhājñātibhṛtyayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 27 \[unit 1504\]

varasnigdhe'tha janyuḥ syāt puṃsi prāṇyagnidhātṛṣu \|

trayī trivedyāṃ tritaye na nā tārkṣyo'śvaśālayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 28 \[unit 1505\]

garuḍāgraje suparṇe puṃsi klībaṃ rasāñjane \|

tiṣyo nakṣatrabhede syāt kalau dhātryāñca yoṣiti \|\| 28 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 29 \[unit 1506\]

dravyantu pittale vitte pṛthivyādau vilepane \|

klībe ca bheṣaje bhavye drutikāre ca vācyavat \|\| 29 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 30 \[unit 1507\]

dasyuścaure ripau puṃsi dāyaḥ seālluṇṭhabhāṣaṇe \|

vibhaktavyapitṛdravye tathā haraṇadānayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 31 \[unit 1508\]

divyaṃ lavaṅge dhātryāṃ strī valgau divi bhave triṣu \|

dūṣyaṃ triṣu dūṣaṇīye klībaṃ vastre ca tadgṛhe \|\| 31 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 32 \[unit 1509\]

dūtyaṃ dūtasya bhāge ca bhāve karmaṇyapi smṛtam \|

daityo'sure surāyāntu daityā caṇḍauṣadhāvapi \|\| 32 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 33 \[unit 1510\]

dhanyā dhātryāmalakyoḥ syād dhanyaṃ puṇyavati triṣu \|

dhānyaṃ vrīhiṣu dhanyāke dhiṣṇyaṃ sthānāgnisadmasu \|\| 33 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 34 \[unit 1511\]

śaktāvṛkṣe'pyatha nayo nītidyūtaviśeṣayoḥ \|

nāṭyaṃ tauryyatrike lāsye nityaṃ syāt satate'pi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 35 \[unit 1512\]

śāśvate triṣu pathyā strī harītakyāṃ hite triṣu \|

padyaṃ śloke pumān śūdre padyā vartmani kīrttitā \|\| 35 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 36 \[unit 1513\]

pākyaṃ viṭākhyalavaṇe yavakṣāre punaḥ pumān \|

prāyo maraṇānaśane mṛtyau bāhulyatulyayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 37 \[unit 1514\]

priyo hṛdye'nyavat puṃsi vṛddhināmauṣadhe dhave \|

pīyuḥ kāle ravau ghūke nā puṇyaṃ śobhane triṣu \|\| 37 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 38 \[unit 1515\]

klībaṃ dharme ca sukṛte puṣyaḥ kaliyuge smṛtaḥ \|

nakṣatramāsayorbhede pūjyaḥ śvaśuravandyayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 39 \[unit 1516\]

peyaṃ pātavyapayasoḥ peyā śrāṇācchamaṇḍayoḥ \|

bandhyastvaphalavṛkṣādau striyāṃ syādaprajaḥ striyām \|\| 39 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 40 \[unit 1517\]

varyyā patiṃvarāyāṃ strī vareṇye triṣu nā smare \|

balyaṃ pradhānadhātau syāt klībaṃ balakare triṣu \|\| 40 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 41 \[unit 1518\]

vīryyaṃ śukre prabhāve ca tejaḥsāmarthyayorapi \|

bhayaṃ pratibhaye ghore prasūne kubjakasya ca \|\| 41 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 42 \[unit 1519\]

bhavyaṃ śubhe ca satye ca yogye bhāvini ca triṣu \|

karmaraṅgatarau puṃsi striyāṃ karikaṇomayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 43 \[unit 1520\]

klībamasthani bhāgyantu syāt śubhāśubhakarmaṇoḥ \|

bhṛtyo dāse bhṛtau bhṛtyā mayurnā kinnare mṛge \|\| 43 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 44 \[unit 1521\]

mayaḥ śilpini daityānāṃ karabhe'śvatare'pi ca \|

madhyaṃ valagne na strī syānnyāyye'ntare'dhame'nyavat \|\| 44 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 45 \[unit 1522\]

matsyo mīne'tha puṃbhūmni deśe manyuḥ pumān krudhi \|

dainye śoke ca yajñe ca mālyaṃ kusumatatsrajoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 46 \[unit 1523\]

māyā syāt śāmbarībuddhyormāyaḥ pītāmbare'sure \|

mūlyaṃ syād vetane praśne mṛtyurnā maraṇe yame \|\| 46 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 47 \[unit 1524\]

medhyaṃ triṣu śucau raktavacārocanayoḥ striyām \|

yayuḥ pumānaśvamedhaturaṅge ca turaṅgame \|\| 47 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 48 \[unit 1525\]

yāyyastu yāpanīye syānnindite'pyabhidheyavat \|

yāmyā'vācyāṃ bharaṇyāñca puṃsyagastye ca candane \|\| 48 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 49 \[unit 1526\]

yogyaḥ pravīṇayogārhopāyiśakteṣu vācyavat \|

klībamṛddhyauṣadhe puṣye nā stryabhyāsārkayoṣitoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 50 \[unit 1527\]

ramyā rātrau campake nā manojñe tvabhidheyavat \|

napuṃsakaṃ paṭolasya mūle sūribhiriṣyate \|\| 50 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 51 \[unit 1528\]

rathyā rathaughaviśikhāvarttanīṣu ca yoṣiti \|

rathavoḍhari puṃliṅgo rūpyaḥ syāt sundare triṣu \|\| 51 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 52 \[unit 1529\]

āhatasvarṇarajate rajate ca napuṃsakam \|

layo vināśe saṃśleṣe sāmye tauryyatrike matam \|\| 52 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 53 \[unit 1530\]

lakṣyaṃ syādapadeśe'pi śaravye'pi napuṃsakam \|

labhyaṃ yukte ca labdhavye lāsyaṃ tauryyatrike matam \|\| 53 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 54 \[unit 1531\]

nṛtye ca vrajyā prasthāne varge paryyaṭane'pi ca \|

vanyaṃ triṣu vanodbhūte strī vanāmbusamūhayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 55 \[unit 1532\]

vācyantu kutsite hīne vacanārhe ca vācyavat \|

vindhyā striyāṃ lavalyāṃ syāt puṃsi vyādhādribhedayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 56 \[unit 1533\]

vīkṣyantu vismaye dūśye puṃsi lāsakavājinoḥ \|

veśyaṃ veśyāgṛhe klībaṃ gaṇikāyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 56 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 57 \[unit 1534\]

śalyantu na striyāṃ śaṅkī klībaṃ kṣeḍeṣu tomare \|

madanadruśvāvidhornā śayaḥ śayyāhipāṇiṣu \|\| 57 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 58 \[unit 1535\]

śayyā syāt śayanīye ca gumphane'pi ca yoṣiti \|

śūnyaṃ syānnirjane vācyaliṅgaṃ nalyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 58 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 59 \[unit 1536\]

śauryyamārabhaṭīśaktyoḥ sahyaḥ śailāntare pumān \|

soḍhavye vācyaliṅgaḥ syādārogye tu napuṃsakam \|\| 59 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 60 \[unit 1537\]

saṅkhyaṃ samiti saṅkhyā syādekādyādivicārayoḥ \|

sandhyā pitṛprasūnadyantarayoryugasandhiṣu \|\| 60 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 61 \[unit 1538\]

satyaṃ kṛte ca śapathe tathye triṣu tu tadvati \|

savyaṃ vāme pratīpe ca smayo nā'dbhutagarvayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 62 \[unit 1539\]

sāyaḥ kāṇḍe dinānte ca sādhyo yogāntare śare \|

gaṇadevaviśeṣe ca sādhanīye tu vācyavat \|\| 62 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 63 \[unit 1540\]

sūryyo'rkapaṇeṃ tapane strī tadbhāryyauṣadhībhidoḥ \|

stheyo vivādapakṣasya nirṇetari purohite \|\| 63 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 64 \[unit 1541\]

sevyaṃ klībamuśīre syāt sevārhe punaranyavat \|

sainyaṃ klībaṃ bale senāsamavete tu vācyavat \|\| 64 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 65 \[unit 1542\]

saumyo jñe nā triṣvanugre manojñe somadaivate \|

hāryyo bibhītakatarau harttavye punaranyavat \|\| 65 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yadvikam / 66 \[unit 1543\]

hṛdyaṃ javane'tha triṣu hṛjjahṛddhitahṛtpriye \|

vaśakṛdvedamantre nā vṛddhināmauṣadhe striyām \|\| 66 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 67 \[unit 1544\]

yatrikam

anayastu vipaddaivāśubhayorvyasaneṣu ca \|

atyayā'tikrame daṇḍe vināśe doṣakṛcchrayoḥ \|\| 67 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 68 \[unit 1545\]

aśvīyamaśvasaṅghāte klībamaśvahite'nyavat \|

adhṛṣyā taṭinībhede pragalbhe vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 69 \[unit 1546\]

abhikhyā tvabhidhāne syāt śobhāyāñca yaśasyapi \|

abhayā tu harītakyāmuśīre tu napuṃsakam \|\| 69 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 70 \[unit 1547\]

nirbhaye vācyaliṅgaṃ syādapatyaṃ putrayormatam \|

agastyaḥ syāt kumbhayonau vaṅgasenatarāvapi \|\| 70 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 71 \[unit 1548\]

avadhyamavadhārhe syādanarthakavacasyapi \|

avyayo'strī śabdabhede nā viṣṇau nirvyaye triṣu \|\| 71 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 72 \[unit 1549\]

ahalyā tvapsarobhede bhāryyāyāṃ gautamasya ca \|

ahāryyaḥ parvate nā syādaharttavye tu vācyavat \|\| 72 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 73 \[unit 1550\]

āśayaḥ syādabhiprāye panasādhārayorapi \|

syādātithyamatithyarthye triliṅgamatithau pumān \|\| 73 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 74 \[unit 1551\]

ātreyī puṣpavatyāṃ syāt nadībhede ca nā munau \|

ādityo bhāskare deve'pyāmnāyo nigame'pi ca \|\| 74 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 75 \[unit 1552\]

upadeśe'pīndriyantu hṛṣīke cetasi smṛtam \|

udayastu pumān pūrvaparvate ca samunnatau \|\| 75 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 76 \[unit 1553\]

upāyaḥ sāmabhedādau tathaivopagatau pumān \|

ūrṇāyurnā kṣaṇabhaṅge meṣakambalameṣayoḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 77 \[unit 1554\]

aucityamucitatve syāt satye'pi ca napuṃsakam \|

kaṣāyo rasabhede'pi niryyāse ca vilepane \|\| 77 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 78 \[unit 1555\]

aṅgarāge ca na strī syāt surabhau lohite triṣu \|

kāleyo daityabhede syāt kālakhaṇḍe napuṃsakam \|\| 78 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 79 \[unit 1556\]

kṣipaṇyustu pumān dehe surabhau vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

kulāyastu pumān sthānamātre syāt pakṣivāsake \|\| 79 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 80 \[unit 1557\]

kṣetriyaṃ kṣetrajatṛṇe paradehacikitsayoḥ \|

paradāraratāsādhyarogayoḥ kṣetriyaḥ pumān \|\| 80 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 81 \[unit 1558\]

kaukṛtyamanutāpe syādayuktakaraṇe'pi ca \|

gāṅgeyaḥ syāt pumān bhīṣme klībaṃ svarṇakaśeruṇoḥ \|\| 81 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 82 \[unit 1559\]

cakṣuṣyaḥ ketake puṇḍarīkasaṃjñakapādape \|

kulatthikāsubhagayoḥ striyāmakṣihite'nyavat \|\| 82 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 83 \[unit 1560\]
