cāmpeyaścampake svarṇe kiñjalke nāgakeśare \|

jaghanyaṃ mehane klībaṃ carame garhite'nyavat \|\| 83 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 84 \[unit 1561\]

jaṭāyuḥ puṃsi sampāteḥ kanīyasi ca guggulau \|

jarāyurapi tatpakṣirāje garbhāśaye pumān \|\| 84 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 85 \[unit 1562\]

tapasyā vratacaryyāyāṃ tapasyaḥ phālgune pumān \|

dvitīyā tithibhitpatnyoḥ pūraṇe tūbhayostriṣu \|\| 85 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 86 \[unit 1563\]

devayurvācyaliṅgaḥ syāddhārmike lokayātrike \|

nādeyo nāgaraṅge syājjayāyāmambuvetase \|\| 86 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 87 \[unit 1564\]

bhūmijambbāṃ javāyāñca vyaṅguṣṭhe'pi ca yoṣiti \|

nikāyastu pumān lakṣye sadharmiprāṇisaṃhatau \|\| 87 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 88 \[unit 1565\]

samuccaye saṃhatānāṃ nilaye paramātmani \|

nepathyaṃ syādalaṅkāre raṅgajyāyāṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 88 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 89 \[unit 1566\]

praṇayaḥ praśraye premṇi yāñcāviśrambhayorapi \|

nirvāṇe'pyatha paryyāyaḥ prakāre'vasare krame \|\| 89 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 90 \[unit 1567\]

pralayo mṛtyukalpāntamūrcchāpāyeṣu puṃsyayam \|

pratyayaḥ prathitatve ca matvādijñānayorapi \|\| 90 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 91 \[unit 1568\]

ācāre śapathe randhraviśvāsādhīnahetuṣu \|

parjjanyo meghaśabde'pi dhvanadambudaśakrayoḥ \|\| 91 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 92 \[unit 1569\]

prasavyaṃ vācyaliṅgaṃ syāt pratikūlānukūlayoḥ \|

prakīryyaḥ pūtikaraje vinikīryyaṃ tu vācyavat \|\| 92 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 93 \[unit 1570\]

praṇāyyo'sammate'pi syādabhilāṣavivarjite \|

payasyantu payojātahitayorvācyaliṅgakam \|\| 93 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 94 \[unit 1571\]

dugdhikākṣīrakākolyoḥ svarṇakṣīryyāmapi striyām \|

pāruṣyaṃ paruṣatve ca durvākye puṃsi gīṣpatau \|\| 94 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 95 \[unit 1572\]

pānīyantu jale klībaṃ pātavye vācyaliṅgakam \|

paulastyastu kubere syād daśagrīve'pi puṃsyayam \|\| 95 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 96 \[unit 1573\]

valayaḥ kaṇṭharoge nā kaṭake punnapuṃsakam \|

brahmaṇyaḥ syād brahmasādhubrahmadāruśanaiścare \|\| 96 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 97 \[unit 1574\]

bāleyo gardabhe puṃsi mṛdau bālahite triṣu \|

brāhmaṇyaṃ brāhmaṇatve ca samūhe ca dvijanmanām \|\| 97 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 98 \[unit 1575\]

bhuvanyuḥ syāt pumān bhānau jvalane śaśalāñchane \|

bhujiṣyastu svatantre ca hastasūtrakadāsayoḥ \|\| 98 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 99 \[unit 1576\]

striyāṃ dāsīgaṇikayormalayaḥ parvatāntare \|

śailāṃśe deśa ārāme trivṛtāyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 99 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 100 \[unit 1577\]

maṅgalyaḥ syāt trāyamāṇāśvatthabilvamasūrake \|

striyāṃ śamyāmadhaḥpuṣpīmisīśuklavacāsu ca \|\| 100 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 101 \[unit 1578\]

rocanāyāmatho dadhni klībaṃ śivakare triṣu \|

mṛgayuḥ puṃsi gomāyau vyādhe ca parameṣṭhini \|\| 101 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 102 \[unit 1579\]

rahasyā strī nadībhede gopanīye'bhidheyavat \|

lauhityaṃ lohitatve ca klībaṃ puṃsi nadāntare \|\| 102 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 103 \[unit 1580\]

vadānyo dānaśauṇḍe ca cāruvādini vācyavat \|

vyavāyaḥ surate'ntarddhau puṃsi klībantu tejasi \|\| 103 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 104 \[unit 1581\]

vaktavyaṃ kutsite hīne vacanārhe tu vācyavat \|

vijayaḥ syājjaye pārthe striyāṃ tithyantare smṛtā \|\| 104 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 105 \[unit 1582\]

umāsakhyāṃ vismayaḥ syādāścaryyagarvayoḥ pumān \|

vinayā tu balāyāṃ strī śikṣāyāṃ praṇatau pumān \|\| 105 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 106 \[unit 1583\]

viṣaye gocare deśe tathā janapade'pi ca \|

prabandhād yasya yo jñātastatra rūpādike pumān \|\| 106 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 107 \[unit 1584\]

viśalyo'gniśikhādantīguḍūcītripuṭāsu ca \|

śvaśuryyo devare śyāle śāṇḍilyaḥ pāvakāntare \|\| 107 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 108 \[unit 1585\]

munibhede ca mālūre śāleyaḥ syānmisau pumān \|

triṣu śālyudbhave kṣetre syāt śīrṣaṇyantu śīrṣake \|\| 108 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 109 \[unit 1586\]

sukeśe puṃsi śaileyaṃ tālaparṇyāñca saindhave \|

śailaje nā tu madhupe śilātulye'nyaliṅgakam \|\| 109 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 110 \[unit 1587\]

saṃstyāyaḥ sanniveśe ca saṃsthāne vistṛtāvapi \|

sannayaḥ samacāye syāt pṛṣṭhasthāyibale pumān \|\| 110 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 111 \[unit 1588\]

samayaḥ śapathācārasiddhānteṣu tathā dhiyi \|

kriyākāre ca nirdeśe saṅkete kālabhāṣayoḥ \|\| 111 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 112 \[unit 1589\]

saraṇyustu pumān vārivāhe syānmātariśvani \|

sāmarthyaṃ yogyatāyāṃ syācchaktāvapi napuṃsakam \|\| 112 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 113 \[unit 1590\]

saurabhyantu maneājñatve saugandhe guṇagaurave \|

saubhāgyaṃ subhagatve syād yogabhede napuṃsakam \|\| 113 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / 114 \[unit 1591\]

hiraṇyaṃ retasi dravye śātakumbhavarāṭayoḥ \|

akṣaye mānabhede syadakupye ca napuṃsakam \|\| 114 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yatrikam / fragment 1592

hṛdayaṃ mānase vukkorasorapi napuṃsakam \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 115 \[unit 1593\]

yacatuṣkam

bhavedanuśayo dveṣe paścāttāpānubandhayoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 116 \[unit 1594\]

avaśyāyastu nīhāre'pyabhimāne'pi puṃsyayam \|

athāvasāyaḥ śeṣe syāt samāptau niścaye'pi ca \|\| 116 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 117 \[unit 1595\]

apasavyaṃ triliṅgastu dakṣiṇapratikūlayoḥ \|

antaśayyā mṛtau bhūmiśayyāyāṃ pitṛkānane \|\| 117 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 118 \[unit 1596\]

upakāryyā rājasadmanyupakārocite'nyavat \|

jalāśayo jalādhāre syāduśīre napuṃsakam \|\| 118 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 119 \[unit 1597\]

taṇḍulīyaḥ śākabhede viḍaṅgatarutāpyayoḥ \|

tṛṇaśūnyaṃ mallikāyāṃ tathā syāt ketakīphale \|\| 119 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 120 \[unit 1598\]

dākṣiṇātyo nārikele triṣu dakṣiṇadigbhave \|

dhanañjayo'rjune vahnināgabhiddehamārute \|\| 120 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 121 \[unit 1599\]

nirāmayastu puṃsi syādiḍikke triṣu nīruji \|

paridhāyo janasthāne paricchedanitambayoḥ \|\| 121 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 122 \[unit 1600\]

pratiśrayaḥ sabhāyāñcāśraye pratibhayaṃ bhaye \|

ghore'pi pāñcajanyastu viṣṇuśaṅkhe hutāśane \|\| 122 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 123 \[unit 1601\]

prāvṛṣeṇyaḥ pumān nīpe prāvṛṭkālabhave triṣu \|

pauruṣeyaḥ kṛte puṃsāṃ vikāre puruṣasya ca \|\| 123 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 124 \[unit 1602\]

triṣu nā saṅghavadhayoḥ puruṣasya padāntare \|

phalodayaḥ pumān lābhe tridiveśālaye'pi ca \|\| 124 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 125 \[unit 1603\]

bhāgadheyaṃ mataṃ bhāgye bhāgapratyāyayoḥ pumān \|

mahālayo vihāre syāt tīrthe ca paramātmani \|\| 125 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 126 \[unit 1604\]

mahodayaḥ kānyakubje cādhipatyāpavargayoḥ \|

mahāmūlyaḥ padmarāge nā mahārghye'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 126 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 127 \[unit 1605\]

mārjārīyaḥ smṛtaḥ śūdre viḍāle kāyaśodhane \|

rauhiṇeyo'nyavad vatse nā budhe ca halāyudhe \|\| 127 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 128 \[unit 1606\]

bileśayastu puṃliṅgo mūṣake ca bhujaṅgame \|

vainateyastu garuḍe syāt prabhākarasārathau \|\| 128 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 129 \[unit 1607\]

samparāyaḥ samīke syādāpaduttarakālayoḥ \|

samāhvayastu paśubhiḥ khagairdyūte ca saṅgare \|\| 129 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 130 \[unit 1608\]

bhavet samudayaḥ saṅghe saṅgame ca samudgame \|

samudāyaḥ samūhe syād yuddhe samucchrayaḥ pumān \|\| 130 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / 131 \[unit 1609\]

virodhotsedhayoḥ sthūloccayorgaṇḍopale'pi ca \|

gajānāṃ madhyamagate'pyasākalyavaraṇḍayoḥ \|\| 131 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yacatuṣkam / fragment 1610

hiraṇmayaḥ surajyeṣṭhe nā suvarṇamaye triṣu \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam

## yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam / 132 \[unit 1611\]

yapañcakam

kālānusāryyaṃ śaileye kālīye śiṃśapādrume \|\| 132 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam / 133 \[unit 1612\]

dugdhatālīyamityetad dugdhāmrakṣīraphenayoḥ \|

atha pravacanīyañca syāt pravaktṛpravācyayoḥ \|\| 133 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yapañcakam / fragment 1613

vṛṣākapāyī śrīgaurīvarījīvantikāsu ca \|

## yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam

## yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam / 134 \[unit 1614\]

yaṣaṭkam

pratyudgamanīyamupastheye dhautāṃśukadvaye \|\| 134 \|\|

## yāntavargaḥ / yaṣaṭkam / fragment 1615

viśvaksenapriyā lakṣmyāṃ vārāhyāmapi yoṣiti \|

iti yāntavargaḥ \|

## rāntavargaḥ / raikam

## rāntavargaḥ / raikam / 1 \[unit 1616\]

raikam

raḥ smṛtaḥ pāvake tīkṣṇe rāḥ puṃsi svarṇavittayoḥ \|

śrīrveśaracanāśobhābhāratīsaraladrume \|\| 1 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / raikam / 2 \[unit 1617\]

lakṣmyāṃ trivargasampattividhopakaraṇeṣu ca \|

vibhūtau ca matau ca strī sruḥ striyāṃ nirjjhare srave \|\| 2 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 3 \[unit 1618\]

araṃ śīghre ca cakrāṅge śīghrage punaranyavat \|

agraṃ purastādupari parimāṇe palasya ca \|\| 3 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 4 \[unit 1619\]

ālambane samūhe ca prānte ca syānnapuṃsakam \|

adhike ca pradhāne ca prathame cābhidheyavat \|\| 4 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 5 \[unit 1620\]

asraḥ koṇe kace puṃsi klībamaśruṇi śoṇite \|

astraṃ praharaṇe cāpe karabāle napuṃsakam \|\| 5 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 6 \[unit 1621\]

abhraṃ meghe ca gagane dhātubhede ca kāñcane \|

adriḥ śailadrumārke nāthāṅghrirnā pādamūlayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 7 \[unit 1622\]

ārā carmaprabhedinyāṃ puṃsi bhaume śanaiścare \|

ārdrā nakṣatrabhede syāt striyāṃ klinne'bhidheyavat \|\| 7 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 8 \[unit 1623\]

āruḥ puṃsi tarorbhede tathā karkaṭadaṃṣṭriṇoḥ \|

indraḥ śakrādityabhede yogabhedāntarātmani \|\| 8 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 9 \[unit 1624\]

indrā phaṇiñjhake strī syādirā bhūvāksurāmbuṣu \|

ugraḥ śūdrāsute kṣatrādrudre puṃsi triṣūtkaṭe \|\| 9 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 10 \[unit 1625\]

strī vacākṣudrayoruṣṭrī karkaryyāṃ karabhe pumān \|

usro vṛṣe ca kiraṇe'pyusrārjunyupacitrayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 11 \[unit 1626\]

aindriḥ kāke jayante nā'thauḍrāḥ puṃbhūmni nīvṛti \|

oḍrastu tarubhede syāt kṣaraṃ nīre kṣaro'mbude \|\| 11 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 12 \[unit 1627\]

karo varṣopale raśmau pāṇau pratyāyaśuṇḍayoḥ \|

kadrustriṣu svarṇapiṅge nāgānāṃ mātari striyām \|\| 12 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 13 \[unit 1628\]

karo vadhe niścaye ca valau yatne yatāvapi \|

tuṣāraśaile'pi pumān striyāṃ dūtyāṃ prasevake \|\| 13 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 14 \[unit 1629\]

suvarṇakārikāyāñca bandhanāgārabandhayoḥ \|

kṣāro rasāntare dhūrtte lavaṇe kācabhasmanoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 15 \[unit 1630\]

kāriḥ striyāṃ kriyāyāṃ syādvācyaliṅgastu śilpini \|

kārurviśvakarmaṇi nā triṣu kārakaśilpinoḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 16 \[unit 1631\]

kṣīraṃ dugdhe jale kīraḥ śuke puṃbhūmni nīvṛti \|

kururnṛpāntare bhakte pumān puṃbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 16 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 17 \[unit 1632\]

kṣudraḥ syādadhamakrūrakṛpaṇālpeṣu vācyavat \|

kṣudrā vyaṅgānaṭīkaṇṭakārikāsaraghāsu ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 18 \[unit 1633\]

cāṅgerīveśyayorhiṃsrāmakṣikāmātrayorapi \|

kṣuraḥ syācchedanadravye kokilākṣe ca gokṣure \|\| 18 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 19 \[unit 1634\]

krūrastu kaṭhine ghore nṛśaṃse'pyabhidheyavat \|

kṛcchramākhyātamābhīle pāpasāntapanādinoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 20 \[unit 1635\]

kṣetraṃ śarīre kedāre siddhasthānakalatrayoḥ \|

kroṣṭrī śṛgālikākṛṣṇavidārīlāṅgalīṣu ca \|\| 20 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 21 \[unit 1636\]

kṣaudraṃ madhuni pānīye kharaḥ syāttīkṣṇadharmayoḥ \|

gardabhe strī devatāḍe kharurdarpe hare haye \|\| 21 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 22 \[unit 1637\]

dante puṃsi triṣu śvete khuraḥ koladale śaphe \|

garī kharāyāṃ karaṇe klībaṃ nopaviṣe viṣe \|\| 22 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 23 \[unit 1638\]

gātraṃ gajāgrajaṅghādibhāge'pyaṅge kalevare \|

gīḥ strī bhāṣāsarasvatyorgirirnā netrarugbhidi \|\| 23 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 24 \[unit 1639\]

adrau giriyake yoṣid gīrṇau pūjye punastriṣu \|

gundrastejanake strī tu priyaṅgau bhadramustake \|\| 24 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 25 \[unit 1640\]

gurustriliṅgyāṃ mahati durjarālaghunorapi \|

pumān niṣekādikare pitrādau suramantriṇi \|\| 25 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 26 \[unit 1641\]

gṛdhraḥ khagāntare puṃsi vācyaliṅgo'tha lubdhake \|

gotrā bhūgavyayorgotraḥ śaile gotraṃ kulākhyayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 27 \[unit 1642\]

sambhāvanīyabodhe ca kānanacchatravartmasu \|

gauraḥ śvete'ruṇe pīte viśuddhe cābhidheyavat \|\| 27 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 28 \[unit 1643\]

nā śvetasarṣape candre na dvayoḥ padmakeśare \|

gaurī tvasañjātarajaḥkanyāśaṅkarabhāryyayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 29 \[unit 1644\]

rocanīrajanīpiṅgāpriyaṅguvasudhāsu ca \|

āpagāyā viśeṣe ca yādasāṃpatiyoṣiti \|\| 29 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 30 \[unit 1645\]

ghasrastu divase hiṃsre ghoro bhīme hare'pi ca \|

caro'kṣadyūtabhede ca bhaume cāre trase cale \|\| 30 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 31 \[unit 1646\]

candraḥ karpūrakāmpillasudhāṃśusvarṇavāriṣu \|

cakraḥ koke pumān klībaṃ vajraṃ sainyarathāṅgayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 32 \[unit 1647\]

rāṣṭre dambhāntare kumbhakāropakaraṇāstrayoḥ \|

jalāvartte'pyatha caruḥ pumān havyānnabhāṇḍayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 33 \[unit 1648\]

cāraḥ piyālavṛkṣe syādgatau bandhāpasarpayoḥ \|

cārurbṛhaspatau puṃsi śobhane tvabhidheyavat \|\| 33 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 34 \[unit 1649\]

citrākhuparṇīgoḍumbāsubhadrādantikāsu ca \|

māyāyāṃ sarpanakṣatranadībhedeṣu ca striyām \|\| 34 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 35 \[unit 1650\]

tilakālekhyayoḥ klībaṃ karburādbhutayorapi \|

tadyuktayostvanyaliṅgaṃ cīrī jhillyāṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 35 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 36 \[unit 1651\]

gostane vastrabhede ca rekhālikhanabhedayoḥ \|

cukraṃ vṛkṣāmle cāṅgeryyāṃ strī puṃsyamle'mlavetase \|\| 36 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 37 \[unit 1652\]

caitraṃ mṛtadevakule nā bhūbhṛnmāsabhedayoḥ \|

cauraḥ pāṭaccare'pi syāt corapuṣpauṣadhāvapi \|\| 37 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 38 \[unit 1653\]

kṣatrā misāvaticchatre kustumburuśilīndhrayoḥ \|

napuṃsakañcātapatre chidraṃ dūṣaṇarandhrayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 39 \[unit 1654\]

jārī syādoṣadhībhede striyāmupapatau pumān \|

jīrastu jaraṇe khaḍge ṭāro laṅgaturaṅgayoḥ \|\| 39 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 40 \[unit 1655\]

tarastu jaraṇe puṃsi kṛśānau peṭake tarī \|

tantraṃ kuṭumbakṛtye syāt siddhānte cauṣadhottame \|\| 40 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 41 \[unit 1656\]

pradhāne tantuvāye ca śāstrabhede paricchade \|

śrutiśākhāntare hetāvubhayārthaprayojake \|\| 41 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 42 \[unit 1657\]

itikarttavyatāyāñca tantrī vīṇāguṇe matā \|

amṛtādehaśirayostandrī nidrāpramīlayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 43 \[unit 1658\]

tāro vānarabhinmuktāviśuddhyoḥ śuddhamauktike \|

nā nakṣatre'kṣimadhye ca na nā rūpyaṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 43 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 44 \[unit 1659\]

strī buddhadevatābhede bāligīṣpatibhāryyayoḥ \|

triliṅgo'tyuccaśabde ca tāmraṃ śūlve'ruṇe'pi ca \|\| 44 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 45 \[unit 1660\]

tīvrā tu kaṭurohiṇyāṃ rājikāmaṇḍadūrvayoḥ \|

triṣvatyuṣṇe nitānte ca kaṭau tīrantu rodhasi \|\| 45 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 46 \[unit 1661\]

puṃsi trapuṇi totrantu prājane veṇuke'pi ca \|

daro'strī sādhvase gartte kandare tu darī matā \|\| 46 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 47 \[unit 1662\]

darāvyayaṃ manāgarthe dasraḥ khare'śvinīsute \|

dāruḥ syāt pittale kāṣṭhe devadārau napuṃsakam \|\| 47 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 48 \[unit 1663\]

dvāraṃ nirgamane'pi syādabhyupāye dharo girau \|

kārpāsatūlake kūrmarāje vasvantare'pi ca \|\| 48 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 49 \[unit 1664\]

dharā viśvambharāyāñca strī garbhāśayamedasoḥ \|

dhātrī jananyāmalakīvasumatyupamātṛṣu \|\| 49 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 50 \[unit 1665\]

dhārā sainyāgrimaskande turaṅgagatipañcake \|

ghaṭādicchidrasantatyoḥ prapāte syād dravasya ca \|\| 50 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 51 \[unit 1666\]

khaḍgāderniśitamukhe dhāro grāvāntare'pyṛṇe \|

dhīrā dhairyyānvite svaire budhe klībantu kuṅkume \|\| 51 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 52 \[unit 1667\]

striyāṃ pravaṇatulyāyāṃ naro'je manuje'rjune \|

klībantu rāmakarpūre nakraṃ syādagradāruṇi \|\| 52 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 53 \[unit 1668\]

nāsāyāṃ puṃsi kumbhīre nārastarṇakanīrayoḥ \|

nībraṃ nemau valīkendvoḥ revatībhe'pi kānane \|\| 53 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 54 \[unit 1669\]

netraṃ mathiguṇe vastrabhede mūle drumasya ca \|

rathe cakṣuṣi nadyāñca netrī nāḍyāñca yoṣiti \|\| 54 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 55 \[unit 1670\]

paraḥ śreṣṭhoridūrānyottare klībantu kevale \|

patrantu vāhane parṇe syāt pakṣe śarapakṣiṇoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 56 \[unit 1671\]

pāraṃ parataṭe prānte na strī pārā nadībhidi \|

pārī pūre ca karkaryyāṃ pādarajjau ca dantinaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 57 \[unit 1672\]

pātraṃ sruvādau parṇe ca bhājane rājamantriṇi \|

tīradvayāntare yogye puruḥ prājye'bhidheyavat \|\| 57 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 58 \[unit 1673\]

puṃsi syād devaloke ca nṛpabhedaparāgayoḥ \|

puraṃ napuṃsakaṃ gehe dehapāṭīlaputrayoḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 59 \[unit 1674\]

puṣpādīnāṃ dalāvṛttau nā guggulau na nā puri \|

puṇḍro daityaviśeṣekṣubhedayoratimuktake \|\| 59 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 60 \[unit 1675\]

citre kṛmau puṇḍarīke puṃbhūmni nīvṛdantare \|

pūro jalasamūhe syād vraṇasaṃśuddhikhādyayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 61 \[unit 1676\]

potraṃ vajre mukhāgre ca śūkarasya halasya ca \|

pauraṃ triṣu purodbhūte kattṛṇe tu napuṃsakam \|\| 61 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 62 \[unit 1677\]

varo jāmātari vṛtau devatāderabhīpsite \|

ṣiḍge puṃsi triṣu śreṣṭhe kuṅkume tu napuṃsakam \|\| 62 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 63 \[unit 1678\]

varī proktā śatāvaryyāṃ varā ca syāt phalatrike \|

manāgiṣṭe varaṃ klībe kecidāhustadavyayam \|\| 63 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 64 \[unit 1679\]

babhrurvaiśvānare śūlapāṇau ca garuḍadhvaje \|

viśāle nakule puṃsi piṅgale tvabhidheyavat \|\| 64 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 65 \[unit 1680\]

vakraḥ śanaiścare puṃsi puṭabhede napuṃsakam \|

triṣu krūre ca kuṭile vāraḥ sūryyādivāsare \|\| 65 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 66 \[unit 1681\]

dvāre hare kubjavṛkṣe vṛndāvasarayoḥ kṣaṇe \|

vārī syād gajabandhanyāṃ kalasyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 66 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 67 \[unit 1682\]

vārirvāggajabandhanyoḥ strī klībe'mbuni bālake \|

vīro rasaviśeṣe puṃsyuttare subhaṭe triṣu \|\| 67 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 68 \[unit 1683\]

strī surākṣīrakākolītāmalakyelavāluke \|

patiputravatīrambhāgambhārīdugdhikāsu ca \|\| 68 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 69 \[unit 1684\]

malapūkṣīravidāryyoḥ klībaṃ śṛṅgyāṃ nale'pi ca \|

bharuḥ svarṇe hare puṃsi bharo'tiśayabhārayoḥ \|\| 69 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 70 \[unit 1685\]

bhadraḥ śive khañjarīṭe vṛṣabhe ca kadambake \|

karijātiviśeṣe nā klībaṃ maṅgalamustayoḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 71 \[unit 1686\]

kāñcane ca striyāṃ rāsnākṛṣṇāvyomanadīṣu ca \|

tithibhede prasāriṇyāṃ kaṭphalānantayorapi \|\| 71 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 72 \[unit 1687\]

triṣu śreṣṭhe ca sādhau syānna puṃsi karaṇāntare \|

bhāraḥ syād vīvaghe viṣṇau palānāṃ dvisahasrake \|\| 72 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 73 \[unit 1688\]

bhīrurārtte triliṅgaḥ syād varayoṣiti yoṣiti \|

bhūrirnā vāsudeve ca hare ca parameṣṭhini \|\| 73 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 74 \[unit 1689\]

napuṃsakaṃ suvarṇe ca prājye syād vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

mantro vedaviśeṣe syād devādīnāñca sādhane \|\| 74 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 75 \[unit 1690\]

guhyavāde'pi ca pumān marurnā giridhanvanoḥ \|

mātrā karṇavibhūṣāyāṃ vitte māne paricchade \|\| 75 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 76 \[unit 1691\]

akṣarāvayave svalpe klībaṃ kārtsnye'vadhāraṇe \|

mārā mṛtyau smare vighne mārī caṇḍyāṃ janakṣaye \|\| 76 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 77 \[unit 1692\]

māriḥ strī māraṇe varṣe mitraṃ suhṛdi na dvayoḥ \|

sūryye puṃsi murā gandhadravye daityāntare pumān \|\| 77 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 78 \[unit 1693\]

yātrā tu yāpane'pi syād gamanotsavayoḥ striyām \|

randhrantu dūṣaṇe chidre rāṣṭraṃ syādupavarttane \|\| 78 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 79 \[unit 1694\]

upadrave klībapuṃsoḥ rururnā mṛgadaityayoḥ \|

retraṃ retasi pīyūṣe paṭavāse ca sūtake \|\| 79 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 80 \[unit 1695\]

rodhro nā sāvare klībamaparādhe ca kilviṣe \|

raudro dharme rase caṇḍyāṃ strī tīvre bhīṣaṇe triṣu \|\| 80 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 81 \[unit 1696\]

vajraṃ syād bālake dhātryāṃ klībaṃ yogāntare pumān \|

vajrā snuhyāṃ guḍūcyāñca vajrī snuhyantare smṛtā \|\| 81 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 82 \[unit 1697\]

dambholau harike'pyastrī badhraṃ trapuvaratrayoḥ \|

vaprastāte pumānastrī reṇau kṣetre caye taṭe \|\| 82 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 83 \[unit 1698\]

vaktraṃ mukhe vṛttabhede vyagro vyāsakta ākule \|

vāśro nā divase klībaṃ mandire ca catuṣpathe \|\| 83 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 84 \[unit 1699\]

vyāghraḥ syāt puṃsi śārdūle raktairaṇḍakarañjayoḥ \|

śreṣṭhe narādyuttarasthaḥ kaṇṭakāryyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 84 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 85 \[unit 1700\]

vṛtro ripau dhane dhvānte tailabhede ca dānave \|

veraṃ kalevare klībaṃ vārtākau kuṅkume'pi ca \|\| 85 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 86 \[unit 1701\]

voḍrī strī paṇapāde nā gonasāhau jhaṣāntare \|

śarastejanake bāṇe dadhyagre nā śaraṃ jale \|\| 86 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 87 \[unit 1702\]

śastraṃ lauhāstrayoḥ klībaṃ churikāyāntu yoṣiti \|

śakraḥ pumān devarāje kuṭajārjunabhūruhoḥ \|\| 87 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 88 \[unit 1703\]

śadrirnā'mbhodhare jiṣṇau śarurnā koṣavajrayoḥ \|

śārirnā'kṣopakaraṇe striyāṃ śakunikāntare \|\| 88 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 89 \[unit 1704\]

yuddhārthagajaparyyāṇe vyavahārāntare'pi ca \|

śāraḥ syāt śabale vācyaliṅgaḥ puṃsi samīraṇe \|\| 89 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 90 \[unit 1705\]

akṣopakaraṇe śāstraṃ na dvayorāgamājñayoḥ \|

śigrurnā śākamātre ca śeābhāñjanamahīruhe \|\| 90 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 91 \[unit 1706\]

śiro nā pippalīmūle syād dhamanyāñca yoṣiti \|

śīghraṃ nalade cakrāṅge klībaṃ drutagatau triṣu \|\| 91 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 92 \[unit 1707\]

śubhraṃ syādabhrake klībamuddīptaśuklayostriṣu \|

śukraḥ syād bhārgave jyeṣṭhamāse vaiśvānare pumān \|\| 92 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 93 \[unit 1708\]

reto'kṣirugbhidoḥ klībaṃ śūraḥ syād yādave bhaṭe \|

svaruḥ puṃsi yūpakhaṇḍe bhidure'pyadhare śare \|\| 93 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 94 \[unit 1709\]

satraṃ yajñe sadādānācchādanāraṇyaketave \|

saro dadhyagragatyornā svaro nāsāsamīraṇe \|\| 94 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 95 \[unit 1710\]

udāttādāvakārādau ṣaḍrajādau ca dhvanau pumān \|

sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca majjni puṃsi jale dhane \|\| 95 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 96 \[unit 1711\]

nyāyye klībaṃ triṣu vare sāndraṃ vane ghane mṛdau \|

sphāraḥ syāt puṃsi vikaṭe kanakādeśca budbude \|\| 96 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 97 \[unit 1712\]

sipro nidāghasalile siprā tu saridantare \|

sthirā bhūśālaparṇyornā śanau mokṣe'cale triṣu \|\| 97 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 98 \[unit 1713\]

sīro'rkahalayoḥ puṃsi surā caṣakamadyayoḥ \|

puṃliṅgastridiveśe syāt sūtraṃ tantuvyavasthayoḥ \|\| 98 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 99 \[unit 1714\]

śāstrādisūtraṇāgranthe svairaḥ svacchandamandayoḥ \|

hariścandrārkavātāśvaśukabhekayamāhiṣu \|\| 99 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 100 \[unit 1715\]

kapau siṃhe hare'jeṃ'śau śakre lokāntare pumān \|

vācyavat piṅgaharitorhāro muktāvalau yudhi \|\| 100 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 101 \[unit 1716\]

hāriḥ pathikasantānadyūtādibhaṅgayoḥ striyām \|

hiṃsrā tvelāvalīmāṃsyoḥ striyāṃ syād ghātuke'nyavat \|\| 101 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / radvikam / 102 \[unit 1717\]

hīrā tailāmbukālakṣmyoḥ puṃsi śaṅkaravajrayoḥ \|

horā lagne'pi rāśyarddhe rekhāśāstrabhidorapi \|\| 102 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 103 \[unit 1718\]

ratrikam

amarastridaśe'pyasthisaṃhāre kuliśadrume \|

strī guḍūcyamarāvatyoḥ sthūṇādūrvājarāyuṣu \|\| 103 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 104 \[unit 1719\]

aparaṃ gajapaścārddhe syājjarāyau tu yoṣiti \|

itarasminnapi tathā'rvācīne'pyanyaliṅgakam \|\| 104 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 105 \[unit 1720\]

adharastu pumānoṣṭhe hīne'nūrddhve vācyacat \|

adhvaraḥ sāvadhāne syād vasubhede kratau pumān \|\| 105 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 106 \[unit 1721\]

antaramavakāśāvadhiparidhānāntarddhibhedatādarthye \|

chidrātmīyavināvahiravasaramadhyātmasadṛśeṣu \|\| 106 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 107 \[unit 1722\]

avaraṃ gajāntyajaṅghādideśe carame triṣu \|

ambaraṃ na dvayorvyomni sugandhyantaravastrayoḥ \|\| 107 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 108 \[unit 1723\]

aṅgāramulmuke na strī puṃliṅgastu mahīsute \|

aṅkuro rudhire lomni pānīye'bhinavodbhidi \|\| 108 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 109 \[unit 1724\]

ajiraṃ prāṅgaṇe vāte viṣaye dardure tanau \|

strī caṇḍyāmakṣaraṃ brahmavarṇayoḥ puṃsi śaṅkare \|\| 109 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 110 \[unit 1725\]

aśiro vītihotre syādrākṣase bhāskare pumān \|

aṇḍīraḥ puruṣe śakte'raraṃ chadakapāṭayoḥ \|\| 110 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 111 \[unit 1726\]

avīrā'patiputrāyāṃ strī sattvarahite triṣu \|

aguru klībaṃ joṅgakaśiṃśapayorvācyavallaghuni \|\| 111 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 112 \[unit 1727\]

asurā rajanīrāśyorasuro devāribhāskarayoḥ \|

ākaro nivahotpattisthānaśreṣṭheṣu kathyate \|\| 112 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 113 \[unit 1728\]

ādhāraścādhikaraṇe'pyālabāle'mbudhāraṇe \|

āsāraḥ syāt prasaraṇe vegavṛṣṭau suhṛdvale \|\| 113 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 114 \[unit 1729\]

āhāraḥ syādāharaṇe bhojane ca pumānayam \|

āśarastu pumān vaiśvānare ca rajanīcare \|\| 114 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 115 \[unit 1730\]

ākāra iṅgitākṛtyoritaro'nyatra pāmare \|

itvaryyasatyāṃ pathike krūrakarmaṇi ca triṣu \|\| 115 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 116 \[unit 1731\]

īśvaro manmathe śambhau nā''ḍhye svāmini vācyavat \|

īśvarī ceśvaromāyāmudaraṃ jaṭhare yudhi \|\| 116 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 117 \[unit 1732\]

udāro dātṛmahatordakṣiṇe cābhidheyavat \|

urvarā sarvaśasyāḍhyabhūmau syād bhūmimātrake \|\| 117 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 118 \[unit 1733\]

uttarā digviśeṣe ca snuṣāyāmarjunasya ca \|

virāṭasya sute nā syādūrddhvodīcyottame triṣu \|\| 118 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 119 \[unit 1734\]

napuṃsakaṃ prativākye'thoddhāraścoddhṛtāvṛṇe \|

ṛkṣaraṃ vāridhārāyāmṛkṣaraścartviji smṛtaḥ \|\| 119 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 120 \[unit 1735\]

ekāgramanyaliṅgaṃ syādekatāne'pyanākule \|

auśīraṃ śayanāsanacāmaradaṇḍe'pyuśīraje proktam \|\| 120 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 121 \[unit 1736\]

krakaraḥ karīravṛkṣe dīne krakace ca pakṣibhede ca \|

kadaraḥ śvetakhadire krakacavyādhibhedayoḥ \|\| 121 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 122 \[unit 1737\]

karburaṃ salile hemni karburaḥ pāparakṣasoḥ \|

karburā kṛṣṇavṛntāyāṃ śabale punaranyavat \|\| 122 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 123 \[unit 1738\]

karparaḥ syāt kapāle ca śastrabhedakaṭāhayoḥ \|

kandharo vārivāhe syād grīvāyāṃ kandharā matā \|\| 123 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 124 \[unit 1739\]

karvaraḥ kathito vyāghre śivāyāmapi karvarī \|

kañjaro jaṭhare sūryye viriñcau vāraṇe munau \|\| 124 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 125 \[unit 1740\]

karīro vaṃśāṅkure'strī vṛkṣabhidghaṭayoḥ pumān \|

karīrī cīrikāyāñca dantamūle ca dantinām \|\| 125 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 126 \[unit 1741\]

kaṭitraṃ raśanāyāñca carmāṅge kīṭavāsasi \|

kacchurā śūkaśimbyāñca śaṭīduṣparśayorapi \|\| 126 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 127 \[unit 1742\]

kacchuraḥ puṃścale kacchuyukte ca kavarī punaḥ \|

pṛthvītuṅgayoḥ keśaveśe kavaraṃ lavaṇāmlayoḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 128 \[unit 1743\]

kavaryyumāyāṃ nā rakṣaḥpāpayorbheṣajāntare \|

karmāro jātibhede ca tvacisāre ca puṃsyayam \|\| 128 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 129 \[unit 1744\]

kaṇeruḥ karṇikāre ca kariṇīveśyayoḥ striyām \|

kaccaraṃ kutsite vācyaliṅgaṃ takre napuṃsakam \|\| 129 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 130 \[unit 1745\]

karkarī bhāṇḍabhede nā darpaṇe kaṭhine triṣu \|

kaḍāraḥ piṅgale dāse kalatraṃ śroṇibhāryyayoḥ \|\| 130 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 131 \[unit 1746\]

durgasthāne nṛpādīnāṃ kaṭaprūḥ puṃsi rākṣase \|

vidyādhare mahādeve tathā syādakṣadevate \|\| 131 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 132 \[unit 1747\]

kandarastvaṅkuśe puṃsi guhāyāñca napuṃsakam \|

karburaḥ syāt pumān śaṭyāṃ suvarṇa tu napuṃsakam \|\| 132 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 133 \[unit 1748\]

kāśmīraṃ kuṅkume'pi syāt ṭaṅkapuṣkaramūlayoḥ \|

kāverī syāt sāridbhede puṇyanārīharidrayoḥ \|\| 133 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 134 \[unit 1749\]

kāntāro'strī mahāraṇye vile durgamavartmani \|

puṃsi syādikṣubhede'tha kiśoro'śvasya śāvake \|\| 134 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 135 \[unit 1750\]

tailaparṇyoṣadhau ca syāt taruṇāvasthasūryyayoḥ \|

kirmīro nāgaraṅge ca karbure rākṣasāntare \|\| 135 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 136 \[unit 1751\]

kiṃśārurnā śasyaśūke viśikhe kaṅkapakṣiṇi \|

kuharaṃ gahvare chidre klībaṃ nāgāntare pumān \|\| 136 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 137 \[unit 1752\]

kukkuraḥ sārameye nā granthiparṇe napuṃsakam \|

kūrparaḥ syāt kaphoṇau ca jānunyapi ca puṃsyayam \|\| 137 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 138 \[unit 1753\]

kuñjaro'nekape keśe strī dhātakyāñca pāṭalau \|

kumāraḥ syāt śuke skande yuvarāje'śvacārake \|\| 138 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 139 \[unit 1754\]

bālake varuṇadrau nā na dvayorjātyakāñcane \|

kumārī śailatanayānavamālyornadībhidi \|\| 139 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 140 \[unit 1755\]

sahāparājitākanyājambudvīpeṣu ca striyām \|

kuṭhārurnā drame kīśe kuṭṭāraṃ kevale rate \|\| 140 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 141 \[unit 1756\]

kuberaḥ syāt puṃsi nandīvṛkṣe puṇyajaneśvare \|

kūbarastriṣu cārau nā kubjake strī yugandhare \|\| 141 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 142 \[unit 1757\]

keśaraṃ hiṅgule klībaṃ kiñjalke na striyāṃ pumān \|

siṃhasaṭāyāṃ punnāge bakule nāgakeśare \|\| 142 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 143 \[unit 1758\]

kedāro'drau śive kṣetre bhūmibhedālavālayoḥ \|

kenāraḥ kumbhinarake śiraḥkapolasandhiṣu \|\| 143 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 144 \[unit 1759\]

koṭiraḥ puṃsi nakule śatrugopakaśakrayoḥ \|

koṭṭāro nāgare kūpe puṣkariṇyāśca pāṭake \|\| 144 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 145 \[unit 1760\]

khapuraḥ kramuke bhadramustake kalaśe'pi ca \|

kharparastaskare dhūrtte bhikṣābhāṇḍakapālayoḥ \|\| 145 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 146 \[unit 1761\]

kharjūraṃ rūpyakhalayorvṛścike nā drume dvayoḥ \|

khadirī śākabhede strī nā candre dantadhāvane \|\| 146 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 147 \[unit 1762\]

khaṇḍābhramabhraleśe syāt tathā dantakṣatāntare \|

khiṅkhirastu śivābhede khaṭvāṅge vāribālake \|\| 147 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 148 \[unit 1763\]

gaṇeruḥ karṇikārādrau kariṇīveśyayoḥ striyām \|

gargaro mīnabhede strī manthanyāmatha gahvaram \|\| 148 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 149 \[unit 1764\]

guhāgahanadambheṣu nikuñje tu pumānayam \|

gāndhāraḥ puṃsi sindūre rāgadeśaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 149 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 150 \[unit 1765\]

gāyatrī tripadādevīcchandobhitkhadireṣu ca \|

gopuraṃ dvāri pūrdvāri kaivarttīmustake'pi ca \|\| 150 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 151 \[unit 1766\]

ghargharo nā caladvāridhvānolūkanadāntare \|

strī kṣudraghaṇṭyāṃ vīṇāyā bhede svarāntare triṣu \|\| 151 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 152 \[unit 1767\]

camaraṃ cāmare strī tu mañjarī mṛgabhedayoḥ \|

candiro'nekape candre catvaraṃ sthaṇḍile'ṅgane \|\| 152 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 153 \[unit 1768\]

caṅkuraḥ syādrathe vṛkṣe cāturo netragocare \|

cāṭukāriniyantrośca triṣu dākṣye tu cāturī \|\| 153 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 154 \[unit 1769\]

cakragaṇḍau ca puṃsi syāccāmaraṃ camarāpi ca \|

daṇḍe ca bālavyajane cikurastaralāgasoḥ \|\| 154 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 155 \[unit 1770\]

pakṣivṛkṣabhidoḥ keśe gṛhababhrau sarīsṛpe \|

chidiraḥ pāvake rajjau karabāle paraśvadhe \|\| 155 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 156 \[unit 1771\]

chiduraśchedanadravye dhūrtte vairiṇi ca triṣu \|

jaṭharo na striyāṃ kukṣau vṛddhakarkaṭayostriṣu \|\| 156 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 157 \[unit 1772\]

jarjaraḥ śaivale śakradhvaje triṣu jarāture \|

jambāraḥ prasthapuṣpe syāt tathā dantaśaṭhadrume \|\| 157 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 158 \[unit 1773\]

jalendraḥ puṃsi varuṇe jambhale ca mahodadhau \|

jharjharaḥ syāt kaliyuge vādyabhāṇḍe nadāntare \|\| 158 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 159 \[unit 1774\]

jhallarī jhalarī ca dve huḍakke bālacakrake \|

ṭaṭṭarī syān mṛṣāvāde lampāpaṭahavādyayoḥ \|\| 159 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 160 \[unit 1775\]

ṭaṅkāro vismaye puṃsi prasiddhau śiñjinīdhvanau \|

ṭagaraṣṭaṅkaṇakṣāre helāvibhramagocare \|\| 160 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 161 \[unit 1776\]

nā'nyavat kekarākṣe syād ḍiṅgarī ḍaṅgare'pi ca \|

kṣepe tamisraṃ timire kope strī tu tamastatau \|\| 161 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 162 \[unit 1777\]

kṛṣṇapakṣaniśāyāñca tvakpatrantu varāṅgake \|

strī kāravyāñca timiraṃ dhvānte netrāmayāntare \|\| 162 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 163 \[unit 1778\]

dhīvaro nā'mbudhau vyādhe tumburī kukkurastriyām \|

dhanyāke'pi tuṣārastu śītale śīkare hime \|\| 163 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 164 \[unit 1779\]

tuvarastu kaṣāye syāt kākṣyāḍhakyostuvaryyapi \|

tūvaro'śmaśrupuruṣe'prauḍhaśṛṅgagave'pi ca \|\| 164 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 165 \[unit 1780\]

puruṣavyañjanatyakte syāt kaṣāyarase'pi ca \|

daharo bhrātari svalpe bhūṣikāyāñca bālake \|\| 165 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 166 \[unit 1781\]

danturastūnnatarade tathonnatānate triṣu \|

dardaraḥ parvate puṃsi triṣvīṣadbhagnabhājane \|\| 166 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 167 \[unit 1782\]

dardurastoyade bheke vādyabhāṇḍādribhedayoḥ \|

dardurā caṇḍikāyāṃ syād grāmajāle napuṃsakam \|\| 167 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 168 \[unit 1783\]

daṇḍāro vahane mattavāraṇe śarayantrake \|

kumbhakārasya cakre ca dvāparaḥ saṃśaye yuge \|\| 168 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 169 \[unit 1784\]

dāsero dāsikāpatye triṣu puṃsi kramelake \|

durddharaḥ puṃsi narakāntare svādṛṣabhauṣadhe \|\| 169 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 170 \[unit 1785\]

daityāriḥ puṃsi sāmānyadeve ca garuḍadhvaje \|

dhūsarī kinnarībhede nā khare triṣu pāṇḍure \|\| 170 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 171 \[unit 1786\]

narendrastu mahīpāle viṣavaidye ca puṃsyayam \|

narmarā tu darībhastrāsaralāniṣkalāsu ca \|\| 171 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 172 \[unit 1787\]

nāgaraṃ mustake śuṇṭhyāṃ vidagdhe nagarodbhave \|

nikaro nivahe sāre nyāyadeyadhane nidhau \|\| 172 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 173 \[unit 1788\]

nirjaraḥ syāt pumān deve jarātyakte tu vācyavat \|

nirjarā tu guḍūcyāñca tālaparṇyāmapi striyām \|\| 173 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 174 \[unit 1789\]

nirjharastu sahasrāṃśuturaṅge tuṣapāvake \|

nikāraḥ syāt paribhave dhānyasyotkṣepaṇe'pi ca \|\| 174 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 175 \[unit 1790\]

nirdaraṃ nirbhare sāre'nyavattu kaṭhine'trape \|

syānnīvaro vāṇijake vāstavye ca pumānayam \|\| 175 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 176 \[unit 1791\]

pravaraṃ santatau gotre klībaṃ śreṣṭhe tu vācyavat \|

prakharaṃ hayasannāhe kukkure'śvatare'pi ca \|\| 176 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 177 \[unit 1792\]

puṃsi triṣvatyantakhare pavitraṃ varṣaṇe kuśe \|

tāmre payasi ca klībaṃ medhye syādabhidheyavat \|\| 177 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 178 \[unit 1793\]

pradaro rogabhede syād vidāre śarabhaṅgayoḥ \|

prakaraḥ syāt pumān saṅghe vikīrṇakusumādiṣu \|\| 178 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 179 \[unit 1794\]

napuṃsakaṃ joṅgake strī nāṭyāṅge catvarāvanau \|

paṅkāraḥ śaivale setau sopāne jalakubjake \|\| 179 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 180 \[unit 1795\]

prastaro grāvṇi ca maṇau prakārastulyabhedayoḥ \|

padāraḥ pādadhūlau syāt pādālinde'pi puṃsyayam \|\| 180 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 181 \[unit 1796\]

prasaraḥ praṇaye vege syāt paṭīrastu mūlake \|

kedāre veṇusāre ca vāride titaunyapi \|\| 181 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 182 \[unit 1797\]

syādraṅge vātike puṃsi pāmaraḥ khalanīcayoḥ \|

pāṇḍuraḥ syānmarubake varṇo nā tadvati triṣu \|\| 182 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 183 \[unit 1798\]

pārparo bhaktaśikthe syāt kīnāśe rājayakṣmaṇi \|

jarāṭe'pi kadambasya keśare ca gadāntare \|\| 183 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 184 \[unit 1799\]

prāntaraṃ vipine dūraśūnyavartmani koṭare \|

piṭharaḥ sthālyāṃ nā klībaṃ mustāmanthānadaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 184 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 185 \[unit 1800\]

piñjaro'śvāntare pīte klībaṃ svarṇe ca pītane \|

piṇḍāraḥ kṣapaṇe gope mahiṣīrakṣake drume \|\| 185 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 186 \[unit 1801\]

pīvaraḥ kacchape sthūle puṣkaraṃ khe'mbupadmayoḥ \|

turyyavaktre khaḍgaphale hastihastāgrakāṇḍayoḥ \|\| 186 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 187 \[unit 1802\]

kuṣṭhauṣadhe dvīpatīrthabhedayośca napuṃsakam \|

nā roganāgavihaganṛpabhedeṣu vāruṇau \|\| 187 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 188 \[unit 1803\]

baṇṭharaḥ sthagikārajjau lāṅgūle kukkurasya ca \|

karīrakoṣe tālasya pallave ca payodhare \|\| 188 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 189 \[unit 1804\]

bandhuraṃ mukuṭe puṃsi strīcihnatailakalkayoḥ \|

bandhūke badhire haṃse triṣu syādramyanamrayoḥ \|\| 189 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 190 \[unit 1805\]

bandhūrā paṇyayoṣāyāṃ striyāṃ puṃbhūmni śaktuṣu \|

bandhūraḥ śobhane namre varatrā varddhīkakṣayoḥ \|\| 190 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 191 \[unit 1806\]

bhramaraḥ kāmuke bhṛṅge bhrāmaraṃ madhuni smṛtam \|

puṃsi prastarabhede'tha bhāskaro'gnau prabhākare \|\| 191 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 192 \[unit 1807\]

bhāryyāruḥ śailabhede syān mṛgabhede ca tatra ca \|

krīḍayā parabhāryyāyāṃ putro yenopapāditaḥ \|\| 192 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 193 \[unit 1808\]

bhṛṅgārī jhillikāyāṃ syāt kanakālau punaḥ pumān \|

matsarā makṣikāyāṃ syān mātsaryyakrodhayoḥ pumān \|\| 193 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 194 \[unit 1809\]

asahyaparasampattau kṛpaṇe cābhidheyavat \|

madhurā śatapuṣpāyāṃ miśreyānagarībhidoḥ \|\| 194 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 195 \[unit 1810\]

madhukarkaṭikābhedāmadhulīyaṣṭikāsu ca \|

klībaṃ viṣe puṃsi rase tadvat svādupriye'nyavat \|\| 195 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 196 \[unit 1811\]

mandarastu pumān manthaśaile mandārapādape \|

vācyavad bahale mande mandāraḥ syāt suradrume \|\| 196 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 197 \[unit 1812\]

pāribhadre'rkaparṇe ca madāro hastidhūrttayoḥ \|

mandiraṃ nagare'gāre klībaṃ nā makarālaye \|\| 197 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 198 \[unit 1813\]

mantharaḥ koṣaphalayorbādhamanthānayoḥ pumān \|

kusumbhyāṃ na dvayormande pṛthau vakre'bhidheyavat \|\| 198 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 199 \[unit 1814\]

makaro yādaso bhede nidhirāśiprabhedayoḥ \|

mayūro barhicūḍāyāmapāmārge śikhaṇḍini \|\| 199 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 200 \[unit 1815\]

mahendraḥ parvate śakre madhudro'lau ca kāmuke \|

mandurā vājiśālāyāṃ śayanīyārthavastuni \|\| 200 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 201 \[unit 1816\]

masūrā masurā vā nā veśyāvrīhiprabhedayoḥ \|

masūrī pāparoge syādupadhāne punaḥ pumān \|\| 201 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 202 \[unit 1817\]

marmaro vastraparṇādisvane strī potadāruṇi \|

mañjarī tilakadrūrumuktayorvallarī dvayoḥ \|\| 202 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 203 \[unit 1818\]

makuraḥ syāt mukuravad darpaṇe bakuladrume \|

kulāladaṇḍe māṭharo vyāse'rkapāripārśvike \|\| 203 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 204 \[unit 1819\]

mārjāra otau khaṭṭāśe mihiraḥ sūryavṛddhayoḥ \|

mudgaraṃ kallikābhede puṃsi loṣṭrādibhedane \|\| 204 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 205 \[unit 1820\]

mudiraḥ kāmuke meghe muhiraḥ kāmamūrkhayoḥ \|

murmurastuṣavahnau syānmanmathe ravivājini \|\| 205 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 206 \[unit 1821\]

mṛgāriḥ syāt puṃsi kaṇṭhīravaśārdūlayorapi \|

rudhiro'ṅgārake puṃsi klībantu kuṅkumāsṛjoḥ \|\| 206 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 207 \[unit 1822\]

vadarā gṛṣṭikārpāsyorelāparṇyāṃ striyāṃ pumān \|

kārpāsasyāsthni badarī kolau klībantu tatphale \|\| 207 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 208 \[unit 1823\]

vaṭaraḥ kukkuṭe vastre śaṭhe ca vaśiraḥ pumān \|

kinihīhastipippalyoḥ klībe'bdhilavaṇe smṛtaḥ \|\| 208 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 209 \[unit 1824\]

varvaraḥ pāmare keśe cakrale nīvṛdantare \|

phañjikāyāṃ pumān śākabhedapuṣpabhidoḥ striyām \|\| 209 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 210 \[unit 1825\]

varkaraḥ parihāse syācchāge yuvapaśāvapi \|

valluraṃ syād vanakṣetre gahanoṣarayorapi \|\| 210 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 211 \[unit 1826\]

valluro triṣu saṃśuṣkamāṃsaśūkaramāṃsayoḥ \|

vārdaraṃ kṛṣṇalābījadakṣiṇāvarttaśaṅkhayoḥ \|\| 211 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 212 \[unit 1827\]

nīre ca kākaciñcāyāṃ bhāratyāñca napuṃsakam \|

vāgarastu gatātaṅke mumukṣau vātaveṣṭake \|\| 212 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 213 \[unit 1828\]

viśārade viṣāṇe'pi nirṇaye vārake'pi ca \|

vāsarastu pumān nāgaviśeṣe divase'striyām \|\| 213 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 214 \[unit 1829\]

vāsurā vāsitāyāṃ syād vāsateyabhuvi striyām \|

viṣṭaraḥ kuśamuṣṭau syādāsane ca mahīruhe \|\| 214 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 215 \[unit 1830\]

vistaro vākprapañce syād vistāre praṇaye'pi ca \|

viduro nāgare dhīre kauravāṇāñca mantriṇi \|\| 215 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 216 \[unit 1831\]

vidhuraṃ syāt praviśleṣe na dvayorvikale triṣu \|

rasālāyāṃ strī vidāro jalocchvāsājidāraṇe \|\| 216 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 217 \[unit 1832\]

vidārī śīlaparṇyāñca rogabhedekṣugandhayoḥ \|

vivaraṃ dūṣaṇe gartte visaraḥ prasare vraje \|\| 217 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 218 \[unit 1833\]

vistāro vistṛtau stambe vikāro vikṛtau ruji \|

vihārī bhramaṇe skandhe līlāyāṃ sugatālaye \|\| 218 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 219 \[unit 1834\]

śabaro mlecchabhede ca pānīye śaṅkare'pi ca \|

śambaraṃ salile puṃsi mṛgadaityaviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 219 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 220 \[unit 1835\]

śambarī cākhuparṇyāṃ syāt śarvarī yāminīstriyoḥ \|

śarkarā khaṇḍavikṛtāvupalākarparāṃśayoḥ \|\| 220 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 221 \[unit 1836\]

śarkarā'nvitadeśe'pi rugbhede sakale'pi ca \|

śakvarī chandaso bhede nadīmekhalayorapi \|\| 221 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 222 \[unit 1837\]

śaṇīraṃ śīṇamadhyasthapuline dardarītaṭe \|

śvaśuraḥ pūjye strī brāhmyāṃ śārīraṃ dehaje vṛṣe \|\| 222 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 223 \[unit 1838\]

śārkaraḥ syād dugdhaphenaśarkarānvitadeśayoḥ \|

śāṅkarastu balīvarde chandobhede napuṃsakam \|\| 223 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 224 \[unit 1839\]

śārvarantvandhatamase ghātuke bhedyaliṅgakam \|

śālāraṃ syād hastinakhe sopane pakṣipañjare \|\| 224 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 225 \[unit 1840\]

śāvarī śūkaśimbyāṃ syāt puṃsi pāpāparādhayoḥ \|

lodhre ca śāṅkariḥ puṃsi kārttikeye gaṇādhipe \|\| 225 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 226 \[unit 1841\]

śikharo'strī drumāgre'driśṛṅge pulakakakṣayoḥ \|

pakvadāḍimabījābhamāṇikyaśakalā'grayoḥ \|\| 226 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 227 \[unit 1842\]

śiśiro nā hime na strī ṛtubhede jaḍe triṣu \|

śilīndraṃ kandalīpuṣpe karake nā jhaṣāntare \|\| 227 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 228 \[unit 1843\]

drubhede strī tu vihagībhede gaṇḍūpadīmṛdoḥ \|

śīkaraṃ śarale vātasṛtāmbukaṇayoḥ pumān \|\| 228 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 229 \[unit 1844\]

śuṣiraṃ vaṃśyādivādye vivare ca napuṃsakam \|

mūṣike nā striyāṃ nalyoṣadhau randhrānvite triṣu \|\| 229 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 230 \[unit 1845\]

śṛṅgāraḥ surate nāṭyarase ca gajamaṇḍane \|

napuṃsakaṃ lavaṅge'pi nāgasambhavacūrṇayoḥ \|\| 230 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 231 \[unit 1846\]

śambaro daityahariṇamatsyaśailajināntare \|

napuṃsakantu salilabauddhavrataviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 231 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 232 \[unit 1847\]

saṅkāro'gnicaṭatkāre sammārjanyavapuñjite \|

navadūṣitakanyāyāṃ saṅkārī punarucyate \|\| 232 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 233 \[unit 1848\]

saṃskāraḥ pratiyatne'nubhave mānasakarmaṇi \|

saṃstaraḥ prastare yajñe saṅgaro yudhi cāpadi \|\| 233 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 234 \[unit 1849\]

kriyākāre viṣe cāṅgīkāre klībaṃ śamīphale \|

sambhāraḥ sambhṛtau saṅghe sāmudraṃ dehalakṣaṇe \|\| 234 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 235 \[unit 1850\]

samudraje'nyaliṅgo'tha sāvitraḥ śaṅkare vasau \|

sāvitrī satyavatpatnyāṃ brahmapatnyumayoripi \|\| 235 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 236 \[unit 1851\]

sindūrastarubhede syāt sindūraṃ raktacūrṇake \|

sindūrī rocanāraktacelikādhātakīṣu ca \|\| 236 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 237 \[unit 1852\]

sunārastu śunīstanye sarpāṇḍakalaviṅkayoḥ \|

sundarī tarubhinnārībhidoḥ strī rucire'nyavat \|\| 237 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 238 \[unit 1853\]

sairandhrī paraveśmasthaśilpakṛtsvavaśastriyām \|

varṇasaṅkarasambhūtastrīmahallikayorapi \|\| 238 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / 239 \[unit 1854\]

sauvīraṃ kāñjike sroteā'ñjane ca badarīphale \|

nā tu nīvṛti hāridraṃ haridrārañjite triṣu \|\| 239 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / ratrikam / fragment 1855

nā nīpavṛkṣe hiṇḍīraḥ phenavārttākupūruṣe \|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 240 \[unit 1856\]

racatuṣkam

aruṣkaro vraṇakṛti triṣu bhallātake pumān \|\| 240 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 241 \[unit 1857\]

bhavedabhimaro yuddhe vadhe svabalasādhvase \|

anuttaraṃ triṣu śreṣṭhe pratijalpavivarjite \|\| 241 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 242 \[unit 1858\]

alaṅkārastu hārādāvupamādāvalaṅkṛtau \|

avahāraḥ pumān caure dyūtayuddhādiviśrame \|\| 242 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 243 \[unit 1859\]

nimantraṇopanetavyadravye grāhākṣayādasi \|

avatāro'vataraṇe puṣkariṇyāditīrthayoḥ \|\| 243 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 244 \[unit 1860\]

avaskāro'pi varcaske guhye'thāvasaraḥ pumān \|

prastāve mantrabhede ca varṣaṇe cāpi kīrttitaḥ \|\| 244 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 245 \[unit 1861\]

upalādāvakūpāraḥ kūrmarāje mahodadhau \|

asipatraḥ khaḍgakoṣe pumānikṣau ca nārake \|\| 245 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 246 \[unit 1862\]

bhavedaśvataro vegasare nāgāntare'pi ca \|

abhihāro'bhiyoge ca cauryye sannahane'pi ca \|\| 246 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 247 \[unit 1863\]

agnihotro'gnihaviṣorarddhacandrocandro nakhakṣate \|

galahaste bāṇabhede kṛṣṇatrivṛti tu striyām \|\| 247 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 248 \[unit 1864\]

āḍambarastūryyapakṣmasaṃrambhe gajagarjite \|

ātmavīraḥ prāṇavati śyālake ca vidūṣake \|\| 248 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 249 \[unit 1865\]

indīvaraṃ kuvalaye śatāvaryyāntu yoṣiti \|

udumbarastu dehalyāṃ vṛkṣabhede ca ṣaṇḍake \|\| 249 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 250 \[unit 1866\]

kuṣṭhabhede'pi ca pumān tāmre tu syānnapuṃsakam \|

upahvaraṃ samīpe syādekānte ca napuṃsakam \|\| 250 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 251 \[unit 1867\]

uddanturastriṣūttuṅge karālotkaṭadaṇḍayoḥ \|

upakāraścopakṛtau vikīrṇakusumādiṣu \|\| 251 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 252 \[unit 1868\]

audumbaraḥ śrāddhadeve rogabhede napuṃsakam \|

atha karmakaro bhṛtye vetanājīvini triṣu \|\| 252 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 253 \[unit 1869\]

kīnāśe puṃsi mūrvāyāṃ bimbikāyāmapi striyām \|

kalikārastu dhūmyāṭe karañje pītamastake \|\| 253 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 254 \[unit 1870\]

karṇikāraḥ pumānāragvadhadrau ca drumotpale \|

karṇapūraḥ śirīṣe syānnīlotpalāvataṃsayoḥ \|\| 254 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 255 \[unit 1871\]

karavīraḥ kṛpāṇe syād daityabhedāśvamārayoḥ \|

karavīryyaditiśreṣṭhagavīputravatīṣu ca \|\| 255 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 256 \[unit 1872\]

kaṭambharā prasāraṇyāṃ rohiṇyāṃ gajayoṣiti \|

kalaviṅkāyāṃ golāyāṃ varṣābhūmūrvayorapi \|\| 256 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 257 \[unit 1873\]

kādambarastu dadhyagre madyabhede napuṃsakam \|

strī vāruṇīparabhṛtābhāratīśārikāsu ca \|\| 257 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 258 \[unit 1874\]

kālañjaro yogicakramelake bhairave girau \|

kumbhakārī kulatthyāñca puṃliṅgo ghaṭakārake \|\| 258 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 259 \[unit 1875\]

kṛṣṇasārā śiṃśapāyāṃ puṃsi snuhyāṃ mṛgāntare \|

girisāraḥ pumān lohe vaṅge malayaparvate \|\| 259 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 260 \[unit 1876\]

ghanasārastu karpūre dakṣiṇāvarttapārade \|

carācaraṃ vācyaliṅgamiṅge jagati na dvayoḥ \|\| 260 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 261 \[unit 1877\]

carmakāraḥ pādukṛti striyāṃ carmakaṣauṣadhau \|

atha cakradharo'je'hau nā triṣu grāmajālini \|\| 261 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 262 \[unit 1878\]

citraṭīro vidhau bhālamaṅkitaṃ yena tatra ca \|

ghaṇṭākarṇopahārāya hatacchāgāsrabindubhiḥ \|\| 262 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 263 \[unit 1879\]

tālapatrantu tāḍaṅke raṇḍāyāṃ tālapatryapi \|

tuṅgabhadrā nadībhede striyāṃ puṃsi madotkaṭe \|\| 263 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 264 \[unit 1880\]

tuṇḍikerī tu kārpāsyāṃ bimbikāyāmapi striyām \|

tulādhārastulārāśau puṃsi vāṇijake triṣu \|\| 264 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 265 \[unit 1881\]

atha toyadharo mustāsuniṣaṇṇauṣadhīghane \|

daṇḍayātrā digvijaye saṃyānavarayātrayoḥ \|\| 265 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 266 \[unit 1882\]

atho daśapuraṃ deśe plave'pi syānnapuṃsakam \|

daṇḍadharaḥ pumān pṛthvīnāthe pretādhipe'pi ca \|\| 266 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 267 \[unit 1883\]

digambaraḥ syāt kṣapaṇe nagne tamasi śaṅkare \|

durodaraṃ dyūtabhede kṛtakṛtpaṇayoḥ pumān \|\| 267 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 268 \[unit 1884\]

dehayātrā yamapurīgamane bhojane'pi ca \|

dvaimāturo jarāsandhavāraṇānanayoḥ pumān \|\| 268 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 269 \[unit 1885\]

dharādharo harau śaile dhārādharo'simeghayoḥ \|

dhārāṅkurastu nāsīre śīkare'pi ghanopale \|\| 269 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 270 \[unit 1886\]

dhārttarāṣṭro'sitāsyāṅghrihaṃse kauravasarpayoḥ \|

dhundhumāraḥ śakragope gṛhadhūme padālike \|\| 270 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 271 \[unit 1887\]

dhurandharo dhavadrau nā vācyaliṅgastu dhūrvahe \|

dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ surājñi syānnāgakṣatriyabhedayoḥ \|\| 271 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 272 \[unit 1888\]

dhṛtarāṣṭrī haṃsapatnyāṃ nabhaścaro vihaṅgame \|

vidyādharo ghane vāte niśācarastu rakṣasi \|\| 272 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 273 \[unit 1889\]

pherupecakasarpeṣu pāṃśulāyāṃ niśācarī \|

niṣadvarastu jambāle niśāyāntu niṣadvarī \|\| 273 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 274 \[unit 1890\]

nīlāmbaraḥ pralambaghne kauṇape ca śanaiścare \|

bhavet parikaraḥ saṅghe paryyaṅkaparivārayoḥ \|\| 274 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 275 \[unit 1891\]

pragāḍhagātrikābandhe samārambhavivekayoḥ \|

atha pakṣacaraścandre pṛthakcārigaje'pi ca \|\| 275 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 276 \[unit 1892\]

bhavet pratisaro mantrabhede mālye ca kaṅkaṇe \|

vraṇaśuddhau camūpṛṣṭhe puṃsi na strī tu maṇḍane \|\| 276 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 277 \[unit 1893\]

ārakṣe karasūtre ca niyeājye tvanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

bhavet parisaro mṛtyau vidhāvapi ca puṃsyayam \|\| 277 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 278 \[unit 1894\]

paramparo mṛgabhede prapautre tanayasya ca \|

paramparā paripāṭyāṃ hiṃsāsantānayorapi \|\| 278 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 279 \[unit 1895\]

payodharaḥ koṣikāre nārikele stane'pi ca \|

kaśerumeghayoḥ puṃsi prabhākarā'gnisūryyayoḥ \|\| 279 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 280 \[unit 1896\]

pratīhāro dvāri dvāḥsthe dvāḥsthitāyāntu yoṣiti \|

parivāraḥ parijane khaḍgakoṣe paricchade \|\| 280 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 281 \[unit 1897\]

pārāvāraḥ samudre nā taṭadvaye napuṃsakam \|

pātraṭīrastu puṃliṅgo yuktavyāpāramantriṇi \|\| 281 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 282 \[unit 1898\]

lohakāṃsyarajatapātre siṃhāṇe pāvake'pi ca \|

pāribhadrastu mandāre nimbadrau devadāruṇi \|\| 282 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 283 \[unit 1899\]

pītāmbarastu śailūṣe puṃsi kaiṭabhasūdane \|

pītasāro malayaje gomedakamaṇāvapi \|\| 283 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 284 \[unit 1900\]

pūrṇapātraṃ vastupūrṇapātre varddhāpake'pi ca \|

balabhadrā trāyamāṇākumāryyoḥ puṃsi sīriṇi \|\| 284 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 285 \[unit 1901\]

brahmaputraḥ kṣetrabhede nadabhede ca puṃsyayam \|

vakranakrastu piśune tathaiva śukapakṣiṇi \|\| 285 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 286 \[unit 1902\]

vārvaṭīro raṅga āmrāsthnyaṅkure gaṇikāsute \|

vārakīrastu puṃsi syād bhāragrāhiṇi vāḍave \|\| 286 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 287 \[unit 1903\]

yūkāyāṃ veṇivedhanyāṃ nīrājitahaye'pi ca \|

bindutantraḥ pumān śāriphalake ca turaṅgake \|\| 287 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 288 \[unit 1904\]

vīrabhadro'śvamedhāśve vīraśreṣṭhe ca vīraṇe \|

syād vīrataraṃ vīraṇe vīraśreṣṭhe śare ca nā \|\| 288 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 289 \[unit 1905\]

mahāvīrastu garuḍe śūre siṃhe makhānale \|

vajre śvetaturaṅge ca sañcānavihage'pi ca \|\| 289 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 290 \[unit 1906\]

mahāmātraḥ samṛddhe cāmātye hastipakādhipe \|

maṇicchidrā tu medāyāmṛṣabhākhyauṣadhe'pi ca \|\| 290 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 291 \[unit 1907\]

maheśvaro mahādeve kathito'dhīśvare'pi ca \|

mṛganetrā rātribhede viśeṣe yoṣitastathā \|\| 291 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 292 \[unit 1908\]

rathakārastu māhiṣyāt karaṇīje ca takṣaṇi \|

rāgasūtraṃ tulāsūtre paṭṭasūtre napuṃsakam \|\| 292 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 293 \[unit 1909\]

lambodaraḥ syāduddhāne gaṇānāmadhipe'pi ca \|

lakṣmīputrastu puṃliṅgaḥ kāmadeve turaṅgame \|\| 293 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 294 \[unit 1910\]

vyavahāro drubhede syānnyāye'pi ca paṇe sthitau \|

atha vyatikaraḥ puṃsi vyasanavyatiṣaṅgayoḥ \|\| 294 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 295 \[unit 1911\]

vātaputro mahādhūrtte bhīmasene hanūmati \|

viśvambharo'cyute śakre puṃsi viśvambharā bhuvi \|\| 295 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 296 \[unit 1912\]

viśvakadrastriṣu khale dhvānākheṭaśunoḥ pumān \|

vibhāvarī niśārātryoḥ kuṭṭanyāṃ vakrayoṣiti \|\| 296 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 297 \[unit 1913\]

vivādavastraguṇṭhyāñca vibhākaro'gnisūryyayoḥ \|

vītihotro'nale'rke ca śatapatraḥ śikhaṇḍini \|\| 297 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 298 \[unit 1914\]

dārvāghāṭe sārase ca kamale tu napuṃsakam \|

śatāvarī tu śuṇṭyāṃ syādindīvaryyāmapi striyām \|\| 298 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 299 \[unit 1915\]

śiśumāro'mbusambhūtajantau tārātmakācyute \|

bhavetsahacaro jhiṇṭyāṃ dvayoranucare triṣu \|\| 299 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 300 \[unit 1916\]

samudrāruḥ pumān grāhe setubandhe timiṅgile \|

samprahāro gatau yuddhe khiradaṃṣṭro bhujaṅgame \|\| 300 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / 301 \[unit 1917\]

varāhākṛtiviṣṇau ca sukumāro mṛdau triṣu \|

puṃsi puṇḍrābhidhānekṣau sūtradhāraḥ śacīpatau \|\| 301 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / racatuṣkam / fragment 1918

nāndyanantarasañcāripātraśilpiprabhedayoḥ \|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 302 \[unit 1919\]

tamālapatraṃ tāpicche tilake patrake'pi ca \|\| 302 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 303 \[unit 1920\]

tālīśapatraṃ bhūmyāmalakītālīśayoḥ smṛtam \|

syāt pāṃśucāmaraḥ puṃsi durvāñcitataṭībhuvi \|\| 303 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 304 \[unit 1921\]

varddhāpake praśaṃsāyāṃ puroṭau dhūligucchake \|

syāt pādacatvaraśchāge saikate pippale'pi ca \|\| 304 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 305 \[unit 1922\]

karake paradoṣaikapravaktṛpuruṣe'pi ca \|

pītakāveramityetat kuṅkume pittale'pi ca \|\| 305 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 306 \[unit 1923\]

syādrājavadaraṃ raktāmalake lavaṇe'pi ca \|

vasvokasārendrapure kuberanalinīpuroḥ \|\| 306 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 307 \[unit 1924\]

vipratīsāra uddiṣṭaḥ kaukṛtye'nuśaye ruṣi \|

bhavet samabhihārastu paunaḥpunyabhṛśārthayoḥ \|\| 307 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / 308 \[unit 1925\]

sarvatobhadra ityuktaḥ kāvyacitre gṛhāntare \|

nimbe nā sarvatobhadrā gāmbhārīnaṭayoṣitoḥ \|\| 308 \|\|

## rāntavargaḥ / rapañcakam / fragment 1926

iti rāntavargaḥ \|

## lāntavargaḥ / laikam

## lāntavargaḥ / laikam / 1 \[unit 1927\]

laḥ śakre lā tu dāne syād grahaṇe'pi nigadyate \|

lī śleṣaṇe ca capale glaurdharaṇyāṃ niśāpatau \|\| 1 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 2 \[unit 1928\]

ladvikam

amlo rasasya bhede syādāmlī cāṅgerikauṣadhau \|

aliḥ surāpuṣpalihoḥ puṃsyālirviśadāśaye \|\| 2 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 3 \[unit 1929\]

triṣu striyāṃ vayasyāyāṃ setau paṅktau prakīrttitā \|

ālurgalantikāyāṃ strī klībaṃ mūle ca bhelake \|\| 3 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 4 \[unit 1930\]

ilā kalave saumyasya dharitryāṃ gavi vāci ca \|

ollastu śūraṇe puṃsi syādārdre vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 5 \[unit 1931\]

kalā syān mūlavivṛddhau śilpādāvaṃśamātrake \|

ṣoḍaśāṃśe ca candrasya kalanākālamānayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 6 \[unit 1932\]

kalaṃ śukre triṣvajīrṇe cāvyaktamadhuradhvanau \|

kaliḥ strī kalikāyāṃ nā śarājikalahe yuge \|\| 6 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 7 \[unit 1933\]

kālo mṛtau mahākāle samaye yamakṛṣṇayoḥ \|

kālā tu kṛṣṇātrivṛtāmañjiṣṭhānīlinīṣu ca \|\| 7 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 8 \[unit 1934\]

kālī gauryyāṃ kṣīrakīṭe kālikāmātṛbhedayoḥ \|

kīlo leśe dvayoḥ stambhajvālākaphoṇiśaṅkuṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 9 \[unit 1935\]

kulaṃ janapade gotre sajātīyagaṇe'pi ca \|

bhavane ca tanau klībaṃ kaṇṭakāryyauṣadhau kulī \|\| 9 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 10 \[unit 1936\]

atha kūlaṃ taṭe stūpe sainyapṛṣṭhataḍāgayoḥ \|

kolaṃ koliphale klībaṃ pippalīcavyayoḥ striyām \|\| 10 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 11 \[unit 1937\]

nā'ṅkapālau śanau citre varāhotsaṅgamelake \|

khalaṃ bhūsthānakalkeṣu nīcakrūrādhame triṣu \|\| 11 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 12 \[unit 1938\]

khallo vastraprabhede syād gartte carmaṇi cātake \|

khallī tu hastapādāvamardanākhyaruji striyām \|\| 12 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 13 \[unit 1939\]

khilamaprahate'pi syāt sārasaṃkṣiptavedhasoḥ \|

galaḥ sarjarase kaṇṭhe gulaḥ syādaikṣave pumān \|\| 13 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 14 \[unit 1940\]

galī tu guṭikorogabhidoḥ snuhyāṃ gulā smṛtā \|

golā godāvarīsakhyoḥ kunaṭīdurgayoḥ striyām \|\| 14 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 15 \[unit 1941\]

patrāñjane maṇḍale cāliñcare bālakhelane \|

calā lakṣmyāṃ pumān kampe kampayukte'bhidheyavat \|\| 15 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 16 \[unit 1942\]

cillaḥ pumān klinnanetre klinnākṣe'pyabhidheyavat \|

ātatāyini samproktaścillaṃ syāt kṣudravāstuke \|\| 16 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 17 \[unit 1943\]

cullī citāyāmuddhāne striyāṃ celo'dhame triṣu \|

napuṃsakantu vasane chalaṃ skhalitaśāṭhyayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 18 \[unit 1944\]

challī vīrudhi santāne valkale kusumāntare \|

jalaṃ gokalane nīre hrīvere'pyanyavajjaḍe \|\| 18 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 19 \[unit 1945\]

jālaṃ gavākṣa ānāye kṣārake dantavṛndayoḥ \|

jālo nīpadrume jālī paṭolikauṣadhau striyām \|\| 19 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 20 \[unit 1946\]

jhalā syādātapasyormau duhitaryyapi ca striyām \|

jhilī cīryyātaparucorvarttyāmudvarttanāṃśuke \|\| 20 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 21 \[unit 1947\]

talaṃ svarūpe'nūrdhve'strī klībaṃ jyāghātavāraṇe \|

kānane kāryyabīje ca puṃsi tālamahīruhe \|\| 21 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 22 \[unit 1948\]

capeṭe ca tsarau tantrīghāte savyena pāṇinā \|

tallī taruṇyāṃ tallastu jalādhārāntare pumān \|\| 22 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 23 \[unit 1949\]

tālaḥ karatale'ṅguṣṭhamadhyamābhyāñca sammite \|

gītakālakriyāmāne karāsphāle drumāntare \|\| 23 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 24 \[unit 1950\]

vādyabhāṇḍe ca kāṃsyasya tsarau tālyajaṭauṣadhau \|

klībantu haritāle syāt tulā sādṛśyamānayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 25 \[unit 1951\]

gṛhāṇāṃ dārubandhāya pīṭhikāyāmapīṣyate \|

rāśau palaśate bhāṇḍe tūlaṃ syāt brahmadāruṇi \|\| 25 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 26 \[unit 1952\]

ākāśe'tha picau na strī dalamutsedhakhaṇḍayoḥ \|

śastrīcchede'pyapadravye patre'pyatha duliḥ striyām \|\| 26 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 27 \[unit 1953\]

kamaṭhyāṃ nā munau dolā nīlyāṃ yānāntare'pi ca \|

nalaḥ poṭagale rājñi pitṛdeve kapīśvare \|\| 27 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 28 \[unit 1954\]

kamale'pi ca naṭyāñca krameṇa klībayoṣitoḥ \|

nālā na nā padmadaṇḍe nālī śākakaḍambake \|\| 28 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 29 \[unit 1955\]

nīlantu nīlarakte nā nidhyadrivānarāntare \|

nīlī rugbhedanīlinyoḥ palamunmānamāṃsayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 30 \[unit 1956\]

pallyalpagrāmakuṭyoḥ strī pallaḥ sthūlakusūlake \|

pāliḥ karṇalatāgre'śrau paṅktāvaṅkaprabhedayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 31 \[unit 1957\]

chātrādideye strī pālo yūkāsaśmaśruyoṣitoḥ \|

pillaḥ pumān klinnanetre klinnākṣe punaranyavat \|\| 31 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 32 \[unit 1958\]

pīluḥ pumān prasūne syāt paramāṇau mataṅgaje \|

asthikhaṇḍe ca tālasya kāṇḍapādapabhedayoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 33 \[unit 1959\]

pulaḥ syāt pulake puṃsi vipule'pyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

phalaṃ jātīphale śasye hetūtthe vyuṣṭilābhayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 34 \[unit 1960\]

triphalāyāñca kakkole priyaṅgau tu phalī smṛtā \|

phālaḥ puṃsi mahādeve kālindībhedane'pi ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 35 \[unit 1961\]

klībaṃ sīropakaraṇe triṣu kārpāsavāsasi \|

balirdaityaprabhede ca karacāmaradaṇḍayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 36 \[unit 1962\]

upahāre pumān strī tu jarayā ślathacarmaṇi \|

gṛhadāruprabhede ca jaṭharāvayave'pi ca \|\| 36 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 37 \[unit 1963\]

bale gandharase rūpe sthāmani sthaulyasainyayoḥ \|

pumān halāyudhe daityaprabhede vāyase'pi ca \|\| 37 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 38 \[unit 1964\]

balayukte'nyaliṅgaḥ syād vāṭyālake tu yoṣiti \|

vallī syādajamodāyāṃ vratatyāmapi yoṣiti \|\| 38 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 39 \[unit 1965\]

bālo nā kuntale'śvasya kariṇaścāpi bāladhau \|

nārikele haridrāyāṃ mallikābhidyapi striyām \|\| 39 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 40 \[unit 1966\]

vācyaliṅgo'rbhake mūrkhe hrībere punnapuṃsakam \|

alaṅkārāntare medhye bālī bālā truṭau striyām \|\| 40 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 41 \[unit 1967\]

velā kāle ca sīmāyamabdheḥ kūlavikārayoḥ \|

akliṣṭamaraṇe rāge īśvarasya ca bhojane \|\| 41 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 42 \[unit 1968\]

bhallaḥ syāt puṃsi bhallūke śastrabhede punardvayoḥ \|

bhallātakyāṃ striyāṃ bhallī bhālaṃ tejolalāṭayoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 43 \[unit 1969\]

bhelaḥ plave munau puṃsi bhīrāvajñe ca vācyavat \|

bholo mīnāntare puṃsi triṣu kāmādivihvale \|\| 43 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 44 \[unit 1970\]

malo'strī pāpaviṭkiṭṭe kṛpaṇe tvabhidheyavat \|

mallaḥ pātre kapole ca matsyabhede balīyasi \|\| 44 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 45 \[unit 1971\]

mālaṃ kṣetre striyāṃ pṛkkāsrajorjātyantare pumān \|

māluḥ patralatānāryyoḥ striyāṃ mūlaṃ śiphādyayoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 46 \[unit 1972\]

mūlavitte'ntike vā nā bhe melā melake masau \|

mauliḥ kirīṭe dhammille cūḍāyāmanapuṃsakam \|\| 46 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 47 \[unit 1973\]

nā'śokadrau striyāṃ bhūmau līlā kelivilāsayoḥ \|

śṛṅgārabhāvaceṣṭāyāṃ lolaḥ syād vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 48 \[unit 1974\]

satṛṣṇe cañcale lolā jihvākamalayoḥ striyām \|

vyālo duṣṭagaje sarpe śvāpade nānyavat khale \|\| 48 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 49 \[unit 1975\]

bilaṃ chidre guhāyāñca pumānuccaiḥśravohaye \|

śalantu śalale puṃsi bhṛṅgikṣetrabhidovidhau \|\| 49 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 50 \[unit 1976\]

śāluḥ kaṣāyadravye syāt caurakākhyauṣadhau pumān \|

śālistu kalamādau ca gandhamārjārake pumān \|\| 50 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 51 \[unit 1977\]

śālā duskandhaśākhāyāṃ gṛhagehaikadeśayoḥ \|

nā jhaṣe śilamuñche syād gaṇḍūpadyāṃ śilī matā \|\| 51 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 52 \[unit 1978\]

stambhaśīrṣe śilāśilyau śilā tu prastare matā \|

tathā manaḥśilāyāñca dvārādhaḥsthitadāruṇi \|\| 52 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 53 \[unit 1979\]

śīlaṃ svabhāve sadvṛtte śuklo yogāntare site \|

napuṃsakantu rajate śūlo'strī roga āyudhe \|\| 53 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 54 \[unit 1980\]

mṛtyuketanayogeṣu śuklā syāt paṇyayoṣiti \|

śailo bhūbhṛti śailaṃ tu śaileye tārkṣyaśailake \|\| 54 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 55 \[unit 1981\]

sālaḥ pādapamātre syāt prākāre sarjapādape \|

sthūlaṃ bhājanabhede'pi sthālī syāt pāṭalokhayoḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / 56 \[unit 1982\]

sthūlaṃ kūṭe'tha niṣprajñe pīvare'pyanyaliṅgakaḥ \|

hālo hale pumān hālā madirāyāñca yoṣiti \|\| 56 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / ladvikam / fragment 1983

helā striyāmavajñāyāṃ vilāse vīrayoṣitām \|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 57 \[unit 1984\]

latrikam

analo vasubhede'gnāvanilo vasuvātayoḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 58 \[unit 1985\]

argalā triṣu kallole'ntardaṇḍavārayorna nā \|

arālaḥ kuṭile sarjarase samadadantini \|\| 58 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 59 \[unit 1986\]

aṅgulī karaśākhāyāṃ karṇikāyāṃ gajasya ca \|

acalā vasudhāyāṃ syādacalaḥ śailakīlayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 60 \[unit 1987\]

amalantvabhrake klībaṃ lakṣmyāṃ strī nirmale triṣu \|

añjalistu pumān hastasampuṭe kuḍave'pi ca \|\| 60 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 61 \[unit 1988\]

avelastvapalāpe syādavelā pūgacarvite \|

ābhīlaṃ na dvayoḥ kṛcchre vācyaliṅgaṃ bhayānake \|\| 61 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 62 \[unit 1989\]

ilvalā tārakābhede nā bhede daityamatsyayoḥ \|

utpalī tuṣacarpaṭyāṃ klībaṃ kuṣṭhaprasūnayoḥ \|\| 62 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 63 \[unit 1990\]

upalaḥ prastare ratne śarkarāyāntu yoṣiti \|

ujjvalo dīptaśṛṅgāraviśadeṣu vikāśini \|\| 63 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 64 \[unit 1991\]

utphullaṃ karaṇe strīṇāmuttāne'pi vikasvare \|

uttāla utkaṭe śreṣṭhe vikarāle plavaṅgame \|\| 64 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 65 \[unit 1992\]

kamalaṃ salile tāmre jalaje klomni bheṣaje \|

mṛgabhede tu kamalaḥ kamalā śrīvarastriyoḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 66 \[unit 1993\]

kapilā reṇukāyāñca śiṃśapāgoviśeṣayoḥ \|

puṇḍarīkakariṇyāṃ strī varṇabhede triliṅgakam \|\| 66 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 67 \[unit 1994\]

nā'nale vāsudeve ca munibhede ca kukkure \|

kambalo nāgarāje syāt sāsnāprāvārayorapi \|\| 67 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 68 \[unit 1995\]

kṛmāvapyuttarāsaṅge salile tu napuṃsakam \|

karālo danture tuṅge bhīṣaṇe cābhidheyavat \|\| 68 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 69 \[unit 1996\]

sasarjarasataile nā klībaṃ kṛṣṇakuṭherake \|

kandalaṃ triṣu kapāle'pyuparāge navāṅkure \|\| 69 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 70 \[unit 1997\]

kaladhvanau kandalī tu mṛgagulmaprabhedayoḥ \|

kadalā kadalau pṛśnyāṃ kadalī kadalau punaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 71 \[unit 1998\]

rambhāvṛkṣe'tha kadalī patākāmṛgabhedayoḥ \|

kalā ḍimbikāyāñca śālmalībhūruhe'pi ca \|\| 71 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 72 \[unit 1999\]

kapālo'strī śiro'sthni syād ghaṭādeḥ śakale vraje \|

kaṇṭhālā tu dvayordroṇīprabhede nā kramelake \|\| 72 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 73 \[unit 2000\]

kallolaḥ puṃsi harṣe syādullolavairiṇorapi \|

kāmalā rogabhede vā nā nā maruvasantayoḥ \|\| 73 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 74 \[unit 2001\]

kāmuke vācyaliṅgo'tha kākolaṃ narakāntare \|

nā kulāle droṇakāke viṣabhede tu na striyām \|\| 74 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 75 \[unit 2002\]

kāhalā vādyabhāṇḍasya bhede cāpsarasāṃ bhidi \|

kāhalī tu taruṇyāṃ syāt kālaścaraṇāyudhe \|\| 75 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 76 \[unit 2003\]

śabdamātre'pi puṃliṅgastriṣu śuṣke bhṛśe khale \|

kiṭṭālaḥ puṃsi tāmrasya kalase lohagūthake \|\| 76 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 77 \[unit 2004\]

kīlālaṃ rudhire toye kuśalaḥ śikṣite triṣu \|

kṣeme ca sukute cāpi paryyāptau ca napuṃsakam \|\| 77 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 78 \[unit 2005\]

kuvalañcotpale muktāphale ca badarīphale \|

kuḍmalo mukule puṃsi na dvayornarakāntare \|\| 78 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 79 \[unit 2006\]

kuṭilā tagarapādyāṃ strī bhugne ca vācyaliṅgakaḥ \|

kuntalaścaṣake bāle yave puṃbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 79 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 80 \[unit 2007\]

kulālaḥ kukkubhe kumbhakāre strī tvañjanāntare \|

kumbhīlaḥ śālamīne ca cauraślokārthacaurayoḥ \|\| 80 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 81 \[unit 2008\]

kuddālaḥ syāt pumān bhūmidāraṇe yugapatrake \|

kucelā viddhakarṇyāṃ strī vācyavattu kuvāsasi \|\| 81 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 82 \[unit 2009\]

kukūlaṃ śaṅkusaṅgīrṇaśvabhre nā tu tuṣānale \|

kuṇḍalaṃ karṇabhūṣāyāṃ pāśe'pi valaye'pi ca \|\| 82 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 83 \[unit 2010\]

kāñcanadruguḍūcyoḥ strī kevalaḥ kuhane pumān \|

napuṃsakantu nirṇīte vācyavaccaikakṛtsnayoḥ \|\| 83 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 84 \[unit 2011\]

kevalī jñānabhede'tha komalaṃ mṛdule jale \|

kohalo vādyabhede syān nāṭyaśāstrapravaktari \|\| 84 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 85 \[unit 2012\]

granthilastu karīradrau vikaṅkatatarau pumān \|

saṃgranthau triṣu gandholirbhadrāśaṭyośca yoṣiti \|\| 85 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 86 \[unit 2013\]

garalaṃ tṛṇapūle ca viṣe māne napuṃsakam \|

gopālo nṛpagopeśe gokīlo musale hale \|\| 86 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 87 \[unit 2014\]

gaurilastu pumān lauhacūrṇe syād gaurasarṣape \|

capalaḥ pārade mīne caurake prastarāntare \|\| 87 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 88 \[unit 2015\]

capalā kamalāvidyutpuṃścalīpippalīṣu ca \|

napuṃsakantu śīghre syād vācyavat tarale cale \|\| 88 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 89 \[unit 2016\]

catvālo homakuṇḍe syāt puṃsi darbhe'pi dṛśyate \|

candilaḥ puṃsi vāstūkaśāke bhage ca nāpite \|\| 89 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 90 \[unit 2017\]

cañcalā tu taḍillakṣmyoścañcalaḥ kāmuke'nile \|

cūḍālī tūccaṭāyāṃ strī cūḍāvati ca vācyavat \|\| 90 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 91 \[unit 2018\]

chagalaṃ nīlavastre nā chāge strī vṛddhadārake \|

jambālaḥ śaivale paṅke jagalo madanadrume \|\| 91 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 92 \[unit 2019\]

medake piṣṭamadye ca puṃsi dhūrtte'bhidheyavat \|

jaṭilā pippalīmāṃsyorjaṭāyukte ca vācyavat \|\| 92 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 93 \[unit 2020\]

jaṅgalaṃ nirjanasthāne triliṅgyāṃ piśite'striyām \|

jambhalaḥ puṃsi jambīre buddhadevāntare'pi ca \|\| 93 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 94 \[unit 2021\]

jambūlastu pumān jambūviṭape krakacacchade \|

jāṅgulī viṣavidyāyāṃ jāṅgulaṃ jālinīphale \|\| 94 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 95 \[unit 2022\]

jāṅgalī śūkaśimbyāṃ strī jāṅgalo nā kapiñjale \|

taralaṃ cañcale ṣiḍge bhāsvare'pi triliṅgakam \|\| 95 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 96 \[unit 2023\]

hāramadhyamaṇau puṃsi yavāgūsurayoḥ striyām \|

taṇḍulaḥ syād viḍaṅge ca dhānyādinikare pumān \|\| 96 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 97 \[unit 2024\]

tamālastilake khaḍge tāpicche varuṇadrume \|

tāmbūlī nāgavallyāṃ strī kramuke tu napuṃsakam \|\| 97 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 98 \[unit 2025\]

tātalo ruji pāke ca lohakūṭe manojave \|

tumulaḥ kalivṛkṣe nā klībantu raṇasaṅkule \|\| 98 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 99 \[unit 2026\]

taitilo gaṇḍake puṃsi klīvantu karaṇāntare \|

dukūlaṃ ślakṣṇavastre syāt kṣaume ca dhavalā gavi \|\| 99 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 100 \[unit 2027\]

vṛṣaśreṣṭhe pumān vācyaliṅgaḥ śukle ca sundare \|

nakulī kukkuṭīmāṃsyoḥ paśupāṇḍavayoḥ pumān \|\| 100 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 101 \[unit 2028\]

nākulī kukkuṭīkande rāsnāyāṃ cavike striyām \|

nābhīlaṃ vaṅkṣaṇe nāryāḥ kṛcchragarbhāṇḍayorapi \|\| 101 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 102 \[unit 2029\]

niculastu nicole syādijjalākhyamahīruhe \|

nirmalaṃ syāttu nirmāmye cābhrake ca napuṃsakam \|\| 102 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 103 \[unit 2030\]

malahīne'nyaliṅgañca nistalaṃ varttule cale \|

niṣkalastu kalāśūnye naṣṭavīryye ca vācyavat \|\| 103 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 104 \[unit 2031\]

nepālī tu navamālīkunaṭīsuvahāsu ca \|

pakṣale tilacūrṇe ca paṅke māṃse napuṃsakam \|\| 104 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 105 \[unit 2032\]

nā rākṣase'tha paṭalaṃ piṭake ca paricchade \|

chardirdṛgrogatilake klībaṃ vṛnde punarna nā \|\| 105 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 106 \[unit 2033\]

pratalaṃ pātālabhede tatāṅgulikare pumān \|

paṭolaṃ vastrabhede nauṣadhau jyotsnyāṃ tu yoṣiti \|\| 106 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 107 \[unit 2034\]

pravālo'strī kisalaye vīṇādaṇḍe ca vidrume \|

pāñcālī putrikāgītyoḥ striyāṃ pumbhūmni nīvṛti \|\| 107 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 108 \[unit 2035\]

pāṃśulaḥ puṃścale śambhoḥ khaṭvāṅge stryasatībhuvoḥ \|

pākalaṃ kuṣṭhabhaiṣajye puṃsi syāt kuñjarajvare \|\| 108 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 109 \[unit 2036\]

pācalaṃ pācane nāgnau rādhanadravyavātayoḥ \|

pāṭalā pāṭalau strī syādasya puṣpe punarna nā \|\| 109 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 110 \[unit 2037\]

āśudhānye pumān śvetaraktavarṇe'pi vācyavat \|

pātālaṃ nāgaloke syādvivare vaḍavānale \|\| 110 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 111 \[unit 2038\]

pātilī vāgurāyāṃ syānnārīpātraprabhedayoḥ \|

pippalaṃ salile vastracchedabhede ca nā tarau \|\| 111 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 112 \[unit 2039\]

niraṃśuke pakṣibhede kaṇāyāṃ pippalī matā \|

piculo jhāvuke'pi syādijjale jalavāyase \|\| 112 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 113 \[unit 2040\]

piṅgalo nāgabhidrudracaṇḍāṃśupāripārśvike \|

nidhibhede kapāvagnau puṃsi syāt kapile'nyavat \|\| 113 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 114 \[unit 2041\]

striyāṃ veśyāviśeṣe ca kariṇyāṃ kumudasya ca \|

pittalā toyapippalyāṃ stryanyavat pittasaṃyute \|\| 114 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 115 \[unit 2042\]

klībantu taijasadravye picchalo vijile'nyavat \|

strī potikośiṃśapayoḥ śālmalīsindhubhedayoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 116 \[unit 2043\]

pudgalaḥ sundarākāre triṣu puṃsyātmadehayoḥ \|

peṣalo rucire dakṣe phenilo'riṣṭapādape \|\| 116 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 117 \[unit 2044\]

nāsaphene triṣu klībaṃ kolīmadanayoḥ phale \|

bahulā nīlikāyāṃ syādelāyāṃ gavi yoṣiti \|\| 117 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 118 \[unit 2045\]

kṛttikāsu striyāṃ bhūmni vihāyasi napuṃsakam \|

puṃsyagnau kṛṣṇapakṣe ca vācyavat prājyakṛṣṇayoḥ \|\| 118 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 119 \[unit 2046\]

baralā vāralā cāpi gandholīhaṃsakāntayoḥ \|

bārddalaṃ durddine melānandāyāṃ vārddalaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 119 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 120 \[unit 2047\]

maṅgalā sitadūrvāyāmumāyāṃ puṃsi bhūmije \|

napuṃsakantu kalyāṇe sarvvārtharakṣaṇe'pi ca \|\| 120 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 121 \[unit 2048\]

maṇḍalaṃ paridhau koṭhe deśe dvādaśarājasu \|

klībe'tha nivahe bimbe triṣu puṃsi tu kukkure \|\| 121 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 122 \[unit 2049\]

mañjulaṃ triṣu mañjau nā jalaraṅkau napuṃsakam \|

jalāñcale nikuñje ca mahilā phalinīstriyoḥ \|\| 122 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 123 \[unit 2050\]

mātulo vrīhibhinmātṛbhrātrośca madanadrume \|

dhūstūre mācalo vandicaure ca grāharāgayoḥ \|\| 123 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 124 \[unit 2051\]

mūṣalaṃ syādayo'gre ca punnapuṃsakayoḥ striyām \|

tālamūlyāmākhuparṇīgṛhagodhikayorapi \|\| 124 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 125 \[unit 2052\]

mṛṇālaṃ nalade klībaṃ punnapuṃsakayorbise \|

mekhalā khaḍgabandhe syāt kāñcīśailanitambayoḥ \|\| 125 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 126 \[unit 2053\]

yamalaṃ yugale klībaṃ yamalī coṭikādvaye \|

rasālā rasanādūrvvāvidārīmārjitāsu ca \|\| 126 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 127 \[unit 2054\]

rasālaṃ sihlake bole rasālaścekṣucūtayoḥ \|

rāmilo ramaṇe kāme lāṅgulaṃ pucchaśephayoḥ \|\| 127 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 128 \[unit 2055\]

lāṅgalī toyapippalyāṃ klībantu kusumāntare \|

godāraṇe tṛṇarājagṛhadāruviśeṣayoḥ \|\| 128 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 129 \[unit 2056\]

lohalaḥ śṛṅkhalācāryye'vyaktavācini ca triṣu \|

vañjulaḥ puṃsi tiniśe vetasāśokayorapi \|\| 129 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 130 \[unit 2057\]

vaṇṭālo yuddhabhede ca naukāyāñca khanitrake \|

vātalaḥ puṃsi vātyāyāṃ vācyavanmārutāsahe \|\| 130 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 131 \[unit 2058\]

vāmilo dāmbhike vāme triṣu syād vimalā striyām \|

śātalāyāṃ bhuvo bhede nirmmale tvabhidheyavat \|\| 131 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 132 \[unit 2059\]

vipulaḥ pṛthule'gādhe merupaścimabhūdhare \|

vidulastu pumānambuvetase vetase'pi ca \|\| 132 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 133 \[unit 2060\]

viḍālo netrapiṇḍe syād vṛṣadaṃśakake pumān \|

viśālā tvindrāvāruṇyāmujjayinyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 133 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 134 \[unit 2061\]

mṛgapakṣibhidoḥ puṃsi pṛthule tvabhidheyavat \|

vṛṣalo gṛñjane śūdre candragupte'pi rājani \|\| 134 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 135 \[unit 2062\]

śakalaṃ tvaci khaṇḍe syād rāgavastuni valkale \|

śambalo'strī sambalavat kulapātheyamatsare \|\| 135 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 136 \[unit 2063\]

śayāluḥ syādajagare nidrāśīle ca kukkure \|

śraddhālurdohadinyāṃ strī śraddhāyukte tu yācyavat \|\| 136 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 137 \[unit 2064\]

śyāmalaḥ pippale kṛṣṇe śārdūlo rākṣasāntare \|

vyāghre ca paśubhede ca sattame tūttarasthitaḥ \|\| 137 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 138 \[unit 2065\]

śālmalistarubhede syād dvīpabhede'pi ca dvayoḥ \|

śrīphalaḥ puṃsi mālūre dhātrīnīlikayoḥ striyām \|\| 138 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 139 \[unit 2066\]

śīvalaṃ syācca śaivāle śaileye ca napuṃsakam \|

śītalaṃ puṣpakāśīśe śailaje malayodbhave \|\| 139 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 140 \[unit 2067\]

pumānasanaparṇyāṃ syāt śiśire vācyaliṅgakam \|

śṛgālo vañcake daityabhede nā ḍamare striyām \|\| 140 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 141 \[unit 2068\]

śṛṅkhalā puṃskaṭīvastrabandhe ca nigaḍe triṣu \|

śaivalaṃ padmakāṣṭhe syāt śaivāle'pi pumānayam \|\| 141 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 142 \[unit 2069\]

śauṣkalaḥ śuṣkamāṃsasya paṇake piśitāśini \|

ṣaṇḍālī tailamāne ca sarasīkāmukastriyoḥ \|\| 142 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 143 \[unit 2070\]

saralaḥ pūtikāṣṭhe nā'thodārāvakrayostriṣu \|

saṅkulaṃ triṣu vispaṣṭavāci vyāpte'tha saptalā \|\| 143 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 144 \[unit 2071\]

navamālīcarmakaṣāguñjāsu pāṭalau striyām \|

sandhilā tu suruṅgāyāṃ nadīmadirayoḥ striyām \|\| 144 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 145 \[unit 2072\]

sidhmalā matsyavikṛtau vācyavattu kilāsini \|

suvelaḥ praṇate śānte triṣu nā parvatāntare \|\| 145 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / 146 \[unit 2073\]

sutalo'ṭṭalikābandhanāgalokaprabhedayoḥ \|

hiṅgulo varṇakadravye nā bhaṇṭākyāntu hiṅgulī \|\| 146 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / latrikam / fragment 2074

hemalaḥ svarṇakāre syāt kṛkalāse śilāntare \|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 147 \[unit 2075\]

lacatuṣkam

akṣamālā'kṣasūtre syādarundhatyāmapi striyām \|\| 147 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 148 \[unit 2076\]

aṅkapālī parīrambhe dhātrīvedikayoḥ striyām \|

athātibalaḥ prabale triṣu strī tvauṣadhībhidi \|\| 148 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 149 \[unit 2077\]

udūkhalaṃ guggulau syādulūkhale'pi na dvayoḥ \|

ekāṣṭhīlā vanatiktikauṣadhau puṃsi bakapuṣpe ca \|\| 149 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 150 \[unit 2078\]

kalakala uktaḥ kolāhale tathā sālaniryyāse \|

atha karmaphalaṃ karmaraṅgakarmavipākayoḥ \|\| 150 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 151 \[unit 2079\]

kandarālaḥ pumān gardabhāṇḍe plakṣatarāvapi \|

kamaṇḍaluḥ syāt karake na strī nā plakṣapādape \|\| 151 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 152 \[unit 2080\]

kutūhalaṃ kautuke syāt praśaste'pi ca dṛśyate \|

khatamālastu puṃliṅgo dhūme'pi ca balāhake \|\| 152 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 153 \[unit 2081\]

gaṇḍaśailo lalāṭe syāt cyutasthūlopale gireḥ \|

gandhaphalyapi gundrāyāṃ campakasya ca korake \|\| 153 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 154 \[unit 2082\]

cakravālo'dribhede syāt cakravālantu maṇḍale \|

jalāñcalaṃ svato vārinirgame śaivale'pi ca \|\| 154 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 155 \[unit 2083\]

dalāmalaṃ marubake damane'pi napuṃsakam \|
