dhvaninālā tu vīṇāyāṃ veṇukāhalayorapi \|\| 155 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 156 \[unit 2084\]

syāt parimalo'timardātimaneāharagandhayoścāpi \|

suratopamardavikasaccharīrarāgādisaurabhe puṃsi \|\| 156 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 157 \[unit 2085\]

atha poṭagalaḥ puṃsi nale ca kāśamatsyayoḥ \|

bhaved bahuphalo nīpe nāmalakyāntu yoṣiti \|\| 157 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 158 \[unit 2086\]

bhasmatūlaṃ grāmakūṭe pāṃśuvarṣe hime'pi ca \|

bhadrakālī tu gandholyāṃ kātyāyanyāmapi striyām \|\| 158 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 159 \[unit 2087\]

mahākālo mahādeve kimpāke pramathāntare \|

bhavenmadakalo mattebhamadāvyaktavācinoḥ \|\| 159 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 160 \[unit 2088\]

mahābalaṃ sīsake'tibalāyāṃ strī balottare \|

triliṅgaṃ maṇimālā tu hāre dantakṣatāntare \|\| 160 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 161 \[unit 2089\]

mahānīlo bhṛṅgarāje maṇināgaviśeṣayoḥ \|

muktāphalantu karpūre mauktike lavalīphale \|\| 161 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 162 \[unit 2090\]

syān mṛtyuphalā kadalyāṃ mahākālaphale pumān \|

bhaved yavaphalo veṇau māṃsīkuṭajayorapi \|\| 162 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 163 \[unit 2091\]

rajasvalā puṣpavatyāṃ striyāṃ syāt saurabhe pumān \|

vātakeliḥ kalālāpe ṣiḍgadantakṣate pumān \|\| 163 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 164 \[unit 2092\]

atha vāyuphalaṃ śakrakārmuke karake'pi ca \|

bhaved vicakilo mallīprabhede madane pumān \|\| 164 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 165 \[unit 2093\]

bṛhannalā guḍākeśe mahāpoṭagale pumān \|

sadāphalaḥ skandaphale nārikele'pyudumbare \|\| 165 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 166 \[unit 2094\]

sinīvālī tu dṛṣṭendukalāmādurgayorapi \|

atha sauvarcalaṃ sarjakṣāre ca lavaṇāntare \|\| 166 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / 167 \[unit 2095\]

hastimallo'bhramātaṅge śaṅkhanāge vināyake \|

haritālaṃ dhātubhede strī durvākāśarekhayoḥ \|\| 167 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lacatuṣkam / fragment 2096

halāhalo brahmasarpe'ñjanāyāṃ nā viṣe'striyām \|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam

## lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 168 \[unit 2097\]

lapañcakam

āsutīvala ākhyātaḥ kanyāpālakayajvanoḥ \|\| 168 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 169 \[unit 2098\]

uddaṇḍapālaḥ puṃsi syāt sarpamatsyaprabhedayoḥ \|

ekakuṇḍala ākhyāto balabhadre dhanādhipe \|\| 169 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / 170 \[unit 2099\]

kṛpīṭapāla uddiṣṭaḥ kenipātasamudrayoḥ \|

syāt pāṇḍukambalaḥ śvetaprāvāragrāvabhedayoḥ \|\| 170 \|\|

## lāntavargaḥ / lapañcakam / fragment 2100

bhavet suratavālī tu dūtikāyāṃ śiraḥsraji \|

iti lāntavargaḥ \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam / 1 \[unit 2101\]

vaikam

vaḥ sāntvane ca vāte ca varuṇe ca nigadyate \|

vaṃ pracetasi jānīyādivārthe ca tadavyayam \|\| 1 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vaikam / fragment 2102

svaḥ syāt puṃsyātmani jñātau triṣvātmīye dhane'striyām \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 2 \[unit 2103\]

vadvikam

avirnāthe ravau meṣe śailamūṣikakambale \|\| 2 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 3 \[unit 2104\]

aśvaḥ puñjātibhede ca turage ca pumānayam \|

urddhvaṃ syāducchrite tuṅge copariṣṭādapi smṛtam \|\| 3 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 4 \[unit 2105\]

kṣavaḥ kṣute rājakāyāṃ kavirvālmīkiśukrayoḥ \|

sūrau kāvyakare puṃsi syāt khalīne tu yoṣiti \|\| 4 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 5 \[unit 2106\]

kaṇvaṃ pāpe munau puṃsi kiṇvaṃ bīje ca śīdhunaḥ \|

pāpe klībaṃ napuṃsake ṣaṇḍe'nyavadavikrame \|\| 5 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 6 \[unit 2107\]

syāttu grīvā kandharāyāṃ tacchirāyāñca yoṣiti \|

cārvī tu śobhanābuddhyorjyotsnādhanadabhāryyayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 7 \[unit 2108\]

chaviḥ śobhārucoryoṣijjavo vegavati triṣu \|

puṃliṅgastu bhaved vege coṇḍrapuṣpe javā smṛtā \|\| 7 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 8 \[unit 2109\]

jīvaḥ prāṇini vṛttau ca vṛkṣabhede bṛhaspatau \|

jīvā jīvantikāmaurvīvacāśiñjitabhūmiṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 9 \[unit 2110\]

na strī tu jīvite tattvaṃ svarūpe paramātmani \|

syād vilamvitanṛtye ca dravaḥ pradrāvanarmaṇeāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 10 \[unit 2111\]

rase'pi davadāvau tu vanavahnau vane'pyubhau \|

dvandvaṃ rahasye kalahe tathā mithunayugmayoḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 11 \[unit 2112\]

dārvī dāruharidrāyāṃ tathā gojihvikauṣadhau \|

dyauḥ strī svarge ca gagane divaṃ klībaṃ tayoḥ smṛtam \|\| 11 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 12 \[unit 2113\]

devo meghe sure rājñi syān napuṃsakamindriye \|

devī kṛtābhiṣekāyāṃ tejanīpṛkkayorapi \|\| 12 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 13 \[unit 2114\]

dhavaḥ pumān nare dhūrtte patyau vṛkṣāntare'pi ca \|

dhruvaḥ śaṅkau hare viṣṇau vaṭe ceāttānapādaje \|\| 13 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 14 \[unit 2115\]

vasuyogabhidoḥ puṃsi klībaṃ niścitatarkayoḥ \|

strī mūrvāḍhyoḥ śāparṇyāṃ gītisrugbheyostriṣu \|\| 14 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 15 \[unit 2116\]

santate śāśvate cātha navaṃ navye pumān stutau \|

nīvī paripaṇe strīṇāṃ kaṭīvasanavandhane \|\| 15 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 16 \[unit 2117\]

pakvaṃ pariṇate'pi syād vināśābhimukhe triṣu \|

plavaḥ syāt plavane bhele bheke'vau śvapace'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 17 \[unit 2118\]

śākhāmṛge ca kulake pravaṇe parkaṭīdrume \|

kāraṇḍavākhyavihage śabde plutagatau pumān \|\| 17 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 18 \[unit 2119\]

kaivartīmustake gandhatṛṇe'pi syān napuṃsakam \|

pārśvaṃ kakṣādhare cakropānte parśugaṇe'pi ca \|\| 18 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 19 \[unit 2120\]

prādhvantu praṇate cātidūravartmani bandhane \|

pṛthvī bhūmau mahatyāñca tvakpatryāṃ kṛṣṇajīrake \|\| 19 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 20 \[unit 2121\]

bhavaḥ kṣemeśasaṃsāre sattāyāṃ prāptijanmanoḥ \|

bhāvaḥ sattāsvabhāvābhiprāyaceṣṭātmajanmasu \|\| 20 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 21 \[unit 2122\]

kriyālīlāpadārtheṣu vibhūtibudhajantuṣu \|

ratyādau cātha revā syān nīlyāṃ ratyāṃ nadībhidi \|\| 21 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 22 \[unit 2123\]

lavo leśe vināśe ca chedane rāmanandane \|

laṭvā karañjabhede syāt phale vādye khagāntare \|\| 22 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 23 \[unit 2124\]

laghvī lāghavayuktāyāṃ prabhede syandanasya ca \|

viśvā tvativiṣāyāṃ strī jagati syān napuṃsakam \|\| 23 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 24 \[unit 2125\]

na nā śuṇṭhyāṃ puṃsi devaprabhedeṣvakhile triṣu \|

bilvaṃ phale śrīphale nā śavaḥ syāt kuṇape pumān \|\| 24 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 25 \[unit 2126\]

napuṃsakantu pānīye śivirbhūrjje nṛpe pumān \|

śivo mokṣe mahādeve kīlakagrahayogayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 26 \[unit 2127\]

bāluke guggulau vede puṇḍarīkadrume pumān \|

sukhakṣemajale klībaṃ śivā jhāṭāmalauṣadhau \|\| 26 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 27 \[unit 2128\]

abhayāmalakīgaurīpherusaktuphalāsvapi \|

śulbaṃ tāmre yajñakarmmaṇyācāre jalasannidhau \|\| 27 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 28 \[unit 2129\]

sattvaṃ guṇe piśācādau bale dravyasvabhāvayoḥ \|

ātmatve vyavasāyā'sucitteṣvastrī tu jantuṣu \|\| 28 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / 29 \[unit 2130\]

savo yajñe ca santāne sāntvaṃ dākṣiṇyasāmanoḥ \|

sruvā dvayoryajñapātre śallakīmūrvvayoḥ striyām \|\| 29 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vadvikam / fragment 2131

hava ājñādhvarāhvāne hrasvo nyakkharvvayostriṣu \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 30 \[unit 2132\]

vatrikam

akṣīvaṃ vaśire śigrau nā'matte punaranyavat \|\| 30 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 31 \[unit 2133\]

abhāvo maraṇe'sattve āhavo yuddhayajñayoḥ \|

ārttavaṃ strīrajaḥpuṣpe klībaṃ syādṛtuje triṣu \|\| 31 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 32 \[unit 2134\]

āśravo'ṅgīkṛtau kleśe nā'nyavat vacanasthite \|

uddhavo yādavabhidi mahe ca kratupāvake \|\| 32 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 33 \[unit 2135\]

utsavo maha utseke icchāprasavakopayoḥ \|

kāravī madhurādīpyatvakpatrīkṛṣṇajīrake \|\| 33 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 34 \[unit 2136\]

kitavastu pumān matte vañcake kanakāhvaye \|

keśavo'je ca punnāge puṃsi keśavati triṣu \|\| 34 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 35 \[unit 2137\]

kaitavaṃ tu chale dyūte kairavaḥ kitave ripau \|

napuṃsakañca kumude candrikāyāntu kairavī \|\| 35 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 36 \[unit 2138\]

gāṇḍīvo gāṇḍivaścāstrī kāmuke'rjunakārmuke \|

gālavastu munau lodhre tāṇḍavo'strī tṛṇāntare \|\| 36 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 37 \[unit 2139\]

bhaveduddhatanṛtye ca tridivā saridantare \|

puṃsi svarge dīdivirnā dhiṣaṇe'nne tadastriyām \|\| 37 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 38 \[unit 2140\]

niṣpāvaḥ śūrpapavane rājamāṣe kaḍaṅgake \|

pavane śimbikāyāṃ nā nirvikalpe'nyaliṅgakaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 39 \[unit 2141\]

nihnivaḥ puṃsi nikṛtāvaviśvāsāpalāpayoḥ \|

pallavo'strī kisalaye viṭape vistare vane \|\| 39 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 40 \[unit 2142\]

śṛṅgāre'laktarāge ca prabhāvaḥ śāntitejasoḥ \|

pañcatvaṃ pañcānāṃ bhāve prāṇānāmatyaye'pi ca \|\| 40 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 41 \[unit 2143\]

prabhavo janmamūle syājjanmahetau parākrame \|

jñānasya cādimasthāne prasavo garbhamocane \|\| 41 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 42 \[unit 2144\]

utpāde syādapatye'pi phale ca kusume'pi ca \|

prasevaḥ puṃsi vīṇā'ṅge syūte ca pārthive nṛpe \|\| 42 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 43 \[unit 2145\]

pārthivī tu sītāyāṃ strī pṛthivyā vikṛtau triṣu \|

puṅgavaḥ syād balīvarde prabhede'pyauṣadhasya ca \|\| 43 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 44 \[unit 2146\]

uttarasthaḥ punaḥ śreṣṭhe pheravo jambuke'srape \|

ballavaḥ sūpakāre syād bhīmasene ca godruhi \|\| 44 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 45 \[unit 2147\]

bāndhavo jñātisuhṛdorbhārgavo gajadhanvinoḥ \|

śukre paraśurāme ca bhārgavī pārvatīśriyoḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 46 \[unit 2148\]

dūrvāyāṃ bhairavaḥ puṃsi śaṅkare bhīṣaṇe triṣu \|

mādhavo'je madhau rādhe yādave nā striyāṃ misau \|\| 46 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 47 \[unit 2149\]

madhuśarkarāvāsantīkuṭṭanīmadirāsu ca \|

gomahiṣyādisampattau yādavaḥ puṃsi keśave \|\| 47 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 48 \[unit 2150\]

rājīvaṃ naline nā tu bhede hariṇamīnayoḥ \|

rāghavo'bdhermahāmīnabhede ca raghuvaṃśaje \|\| 48 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 49 \[unit 2151\]

rauravo narake ghore vaḍavā dvijayoṣiti \|

aśvāyāṃ kumbhadāsyāñca nārījātyantare'pi ca \|\| 49 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 50 \[unit 2152\]

vāḍavaṃ karaṇe strīṇāṃ ghoṭakaughe napuṃsakam \|

pātāle na striyāṃ puṃsi brāhmaṇe vaḍavānale \|\| 50 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 51 \[unit 2153\]

vibhavo raimokṣaiśvaryye vidravo vidrutau dhiyi \|

vibhāvaḥ syāt paricaye rasasyoddīpanādiṣu \|\| 51 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 52 \[unit 2154\]

śātravaṃ bhāvasaṃhatyoḥ śatrūṇāṃ śātravo dviṣi \|

ṣāḍavo gānarasayoḥ sacivo mantriṇi smṛtaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 53 \[unit 2155\]

sahāye sambhavo hetāvutpattau melake'pi ca \|

ādhārānatiriktatve'pyādheyasya nigadyate \|\| 53 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / 54 \[unit 2156\]

sugrīvo vāsudevasya haye śākhāmṛgeśvare \|

suṣavī kāravelle syāt kṛṣṇajīrakajīrayoḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vatrikam / fragment 2157

saindhavo'strī māṇimanthe nā'śve sindhubhave triṣu \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 2158\]

vacatuṣkam

anubhāvaḥ prabhāve syānniścaye bhāvabodhake \|\| 55 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 2159\]

bhavedabhiṣavaḥ snāne madye sandhānayajñayoḥ \|

apahnavastu puṃliṅgaḥ smṛtaḥ snehāpalāpayoḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 2160\]

ādīnavaḥ pumān doṣe parikleśadurantayoḥ \|

upaplavaḥ saiṃhikeye viplavotpātayorapi \|\| 57 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 2161\]

kuśīlavastu vālmīkau naṭayācakayorapi \|

jalabilvaḥ karkaṭe syāt pañcāṅge jalacatvare \|\| 58 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 2162\]

jīvañcīvaścakore syād drumabhede'pi puṃsyayam \|

dhāmārgavastu puṃsi syādapāmārge ca ghoṣake \|\| 59 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 2163\]

pāriplavaścākule syāñcañcale ca parābhavaḥ \|

tiraskāre vināśe ca puṃsi pāraśavaḥ pumān \|\| 60 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 2164\]

parastrītanaye śastre dvijāt śūdrāsute'pi ca \|

puṭagrīvastu puṃliṅgo gargarītāmrakumbhayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 2165\]

baladevo bale vāte trāyamāṇauṣadhau striyām \|

ratidevastu nṛpaterbhede ca garuḍadhvaje \|\| 62 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 63 \[unit 2166\]

rohitāśvaścitrabhānau hariścandranṛpātmaje \|

atha śītaśivaṃ klībaṃ śaleyamāṇimanthayoḥ \|\| 63 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / 64 \[unit 2167\]

puṃsi śaktuphalāvṛkṣe tathā madhurikauṣadhau \|

sahadevā balādaṇḍotpalayoḥ śārivauṣadhau \|\| 64 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vacatuṣkam / fragment 2168

sahadevī tu sarpākṣyāṃ puṃsi syāt pāṇḍave punaḥ \|

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam / 65 \[unit 2169\]

vapañcakam

āśitambhavamannādye tṛptau syādāśitambhavaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## vāntavargaḥ / vapañcakam / fragment 2170

iti vāntavargaḥ \|

## śāntavargaḥ / śaikam

## śāntavargaḥ / śaikam / 1 \[unit 2171\]

śaikam

śaṃ vadanti budhāḥ śreyaḥ śaśca śastraṃ nigadyate \|

śīśca śayanamityāhuḥ śāstraṃ śā ca nigadyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 2 \[unit 2172\]

śadvikam

aṃśurarkaprabhosreṣu nā''śā tṛṣṇādiśoḥ striyām \|

āśurdhānyāntare śīghre'pīśā lāṅgaladaṇḍake \|\| 2 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 3 \[unit 2173\]

īśaḥ prabhau mahādeve kāśī vārāṇasīpure \|

na striyāṃ tṛṇabhede syāt kīśo digambare kapau \|\| 3 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 4 \[unit 2174\]

kuśī phāle kuśā rajjvāṃ triṣu pāpiṣṭhamattayoḥ \|

yoktrarāmasutadvīpe nā darbhe'strī kuśaṃ jale \|\| 4 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 5 \[unit 2175\]

keśaḥ syāt puṃsi varuṇe hrībere kuntale'pi ca \|

kleśo duḥkhe'pi kope'pi vyavasāye'pi dṛśyate \|\| 5 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 6 \[unit 2176\]

kośo'strī kudmale pātre divye khaḍgapidhānake \|

jātikoṣe'rthasaṅghāte peśyāṃ śabdādisaṅgrahe \|\| 6 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 7 \[unit 2177\]

darśastu syādamāvasyāyāgabhedāvalokane \|

daṃśaḥ kīṭaviśeṣe ca varmadaṃśanayoḥ pumān \|\| 7 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 8 \[unit 2178\]

daśā'vasthādīpavarttyorvastrānte bhūmni yoṣiti \|

dṛk striyāṃ darśane netre buddhau ca triṣu vīkṣake \|\| 8 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 9 \[unit 2179\]

nāśaḥ palāyane'pi syānnidhānānupalambhayoḥ \|

niśā dāruharidrāyāṃ syāt triyāmāharidrayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 10 \[unit 2180\]

paśurmṛgādidevāje nāvyayaṃ paśu darśane \|

pāśaḥ keśādipūrvaḥ syāt tatsaṅghe karṇapūrvakaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 11 \[unit 2181\]

sukarṇe ca svasāmarthyān mṛgapakṣyādibandhane \|

pāṃśurdhūlau ca śasyārthacirasañcitagomaye \|\| 11 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 12 \[unit 2182\]

peśī supakvakalike māṃsyāṃ khaḍgapidhānake \|

māṃsapiṇḍyāmaṇḍabhede rāśirmeṣādipuñjayoḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 13 \[unit 2183\]

vaṃśaḥ puṃsi kule veṇau pṛṣṭhāvayavavargayoḥ \|

vaśā vandhyāsutāyoṣāstrīgavīkariṇīṣu ca \|\| 13 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 14 \[unit 2184\]

triṣvāyatte klībamāyattattve cecchāprabhutvayoḥ \|

viṭ puṃsi manuje vaiśye veśo veśyāgṛhe gṛhe \|\| 14 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / 15 \[unit 2185\]

nepathye ca śaśo vole lodhre nṛpaśubhedayoḥ \|

sparśo rujāyāṃ dāne ca sparśane sparśake'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śadvikam / fragment 2186

sparśaḥ syāt samparāye ca praṇidhāvapi puṃsyayam \|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 16 \[unit 2187\]

śatrikam

ādarśo darpaṇe ṭīkāpratipustakayorapi \|\| 16 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 17 \[unit 2188\]

uḍḍīśo granthabhede syāduḍḍīśaścandrikāpatau \|

upāṃśurjapabhede syādupāṃśu vijane'vyayam \|\| 17 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 18 \[unit 2189\]

kapiśastriṣu śyāve strī mādhavyāṃ sihvake pumān \|

karkaśaḥ kāśamarddekṣukāmpilyeṣvapyasau pumān \|\| 18 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 19 \[unit 2190\]

triṣu sāhasike krūre dṛḍhāmasṛṇanirddaye \|

kīnāśaḥ karṣakakṣudropāṃśughātiṣu vācyavat \|\| 19 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 20 \[unit 2191\]

yame nā kuliśo na strī dambholau nā jhaṣāntare \|

giriśo'dripatau vācaspatiśaṅkarayoḥ pumān \|\| 20 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 21 \[unit 2192\]

tuṅgīśastu pumān gaurīvallabhe himadīdhitau \|

duḥsparśo dhanvayāse nā kaṇṭakāryyāṃ striyāṃ triṣu \|

kharasparśe'tha nistriṃśaḥ khaṇḍe nā nirddaye triṣu \|\| 21 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 22 \[unit 2193\]

nirveśastu pumān bhoge vetane mūrcchane'pi ca \|

niveśaḥ puṃsi vinyāse śibirodvāhayorapi \|\| 22 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 23 \[unit 2194\]

nideśaḥ śāsane'pi syāt kathanopāntayorapi \|

nīkāśo niścaye tulye palāśaṃ chadane matam \|\| 23 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 24 \[unit 2195\]

śaṭīkiṃśukarakṣaḥsu puṃsi syād harite triṣu \|

prakāśastu sphuṭe khyāte prahāsātapayorapi \|\| 24 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 25 \[unit 2196\]

pradeśo deśamātre syāt tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasammite \|

bhittāvapi ca piṅgāśī nīlikāyāṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 25 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 26 \[unit 2197\]

jātyasvarṇe pumān pallīpatau matsyāntare'pi ca \|

vāliśaśca śiśau mūrkhe bhūkeśaḥ śaivale vaṭe \|\| 26 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 27 \[unit 2198\]

bhūkeśyavalguje'pi syāt lomaśo munimeṣayoḥ \|

lomānvite striyāṃ kākajaṅghāmāṃsīvacāsu ca \|\| 27 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 28 \[unit 2199\]

śūkaśimbāmahāmedākāśīśe śākinībhidi \|

vivaśastriṣvavaśyātmāriṣṭaduṣṭadhiyorapi \|\| 28 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 29 \[unit 2200\]

vikāśaḥ puṃsi vijane prakāśe sadṛśaṃ same \|

ucite cātha saṃveśaḥ svāpastrīratabandhayoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śatrikam / 30 \[unit 2201\]

sukhāśo varuṇe rājatiniśe sukhabhojane \|

hatāśo nirddaye cāśārahite piśune'pi ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 31 \[unit 2202\]

śacatuṣkam

apadeśaḥ pumān lakṣye nimittavyājayorapi \|

apabhraṃśastu patane bhāṣābhedāpaśabdayoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 32 \[unit 2203\]

āśrayāśaḥ pumān vahnau triṣu cāśrayanāśake \|

upasparśaḥ sparśamātre snānācamanayorapi \|\| 32 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 33 \[unit 2204\]

upadaṃśo vidaṃśe ca meḍhrarogāntare'pi ca \|

krūradṛk piśune vācyaliṅgaḥ puṃsi śanaiścare \|\| 33 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 34 \[unit 2205\]

khaṇḍaparśuḥ parśurāme śaṅkare cūrṇalepini \|

khaṇḍāmalakabhaiṣajye siṃhikātanaye'pi nā \|\| 34 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 35 \[unit 2206\]

jīviteśo yame puṃsi triṣu syājjīviteśvare \|

nāgapāśaḥ pumān strīṇāṃ karaṇe varuṇāyudhe \|\| 35 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 36 \[unit 2207\]

pañcadaśī tvamāvāsyāpaurṇamāsyośca yoṣiti \|

pratisparśaḥ sahāye syād vārttāharapurogayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 37 \[unit 2208\]

pariveśo veṣṭane syāt paridhāvapi puṃsyayam \|

pādapāśī khaḍḍukāyāṃ śṛṅkhalāyāmapi striyām \|\| 37 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / 38 \[unit 2209\]

puroḍāśo havirbhede camasyāṃ piṣṭakasya ca \|

hutaśeṣe ca bhūmispṛk puṃsi mānuṣavaiśyayoḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## śāntavargaḥ / śacatuṣkam / fragment 2210

iti śāntavargaḥ \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣaikam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣaikam / 1 \[unit 2211\]

ṣaikam

ṣaḥ kace puṃsi vijñeyaḥ śreṣṭhe syādabhidheyavat \|

kṣaḥ saṃvartte rākṣase ca narasiṃhe ca vidyuti \|\| 1 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣaikam / fragment 2212

kṣetre nāśe kṣetrapāle kṣirnivāse gatau kṣaye \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 2 \[unit 2213\]

ṣadvikam

akṣo jñātārthaśakaṭavyavahāreṣu pāśake \|\| 2 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 3 \[unit 2214\]

rudrākṣendrākṣayoḥ sarpe bibhītakatarāvapi \|

cakre karṣe pumān klībaṃ tutthe sauvarcalendriye \|\| 3 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 4 \[unit 2215\]

uṣā bāṇasutārātryoruṣaḥ kāmini guggulau \|

rātriśeṣe uṣāyāntu kecidāhustadavyayam \|\| 4 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 5 \[unit 2216\]

uṣaḥ kṣāramṛttikāyāṃ prabhāte'pi pumānayam \|

tatsandhāyāñca randhre ca candanādrau śravovile \|\| 5 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 6 \[unit 2217\]

ṛṣirvede vaśiṣṭhādau dīdhitau ca pumānayam \|

ṛkṣaḥ parvatabhede syād bhallūke śoṇake pumān \|\| 6 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 7 \[unit 2218\]

kṛtavedhane'nyaliṅgo nakṣatre punnapuṃsakam \|

kakṣā syādantarīyasya paścādañcalapallave \|\| 7 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 8 \[unit 2219\]

sparddhāspade nā dormūle kacchavīruttṛṇeṣu ca \|

karṇo na karṣaṇe mānaprabhede punnapusaṃkam \|\| 8 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 9 \[unit 2220\]

karṣo pumān karīṣāgnau striyāṃ kulyeṣṭikhātayoḥ \|

kākṣī tuvarikāyāñca saurāṣṭramṛdyapi striyām \|\| 9 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 10 \[unit 2221\]

koṣo'strī kudmale pātre divye khaḍgapidhānake \|

jātikoṣe'rthasaṅghāte peśyāṃ śabdādisaṅgrahe \|\| 10 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 11 \[unit 2222\]

ghoṣa ābhīrapallyāṃ syād geāpāladhvanighoṣake \|

kāṃsye cāmbudanāde nā ghoṣā madhurikauṣadhau \|\| 11 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 12 \[unit 2223\]

cokṣo gīte śucau dakṣe tathā tīkṣṇamanojñayoḥ \|

jhaṣā nāgabalāyāṃ strī tāpamatsyāṭavīṣu nā \|\| 12 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 13 \[unit 2224\]

tarṣo lipsodanyayostviṭ kāntau vāci rucau striyām \|

tuṣo dhānyatvagakṣadrostṛṣā lipsātṛṣoḥ striyām \|\| 13 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 14 \[unit 2225\]

tṛṭ lipsāyāmudanyāyāṃ smaraputryāmapi striyām \|

dakṣastriṣu paṭau puṃsi tāmracūḍe prajāpatau \|\| 14 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 15 \[unit 2226\]

munibhede haravṛṣe drumabhede striyāṃ bhuvi \|

doṣaḥ syād dūṣaṇe pāpe doṣā rātrau bhuje'pi ca \|\| 15 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 16 \[unit 2227\]

dhvaṅkṣo matsyāt khage kāke takṣake bhikṣuke'pi ca \|

dhvāṅkṣī kakkolikāyāṃ syānnyukṣaṃ kārtsnye tṛṇe'pi ca \|\| 16 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 17 \[unit 2228\]

klībe'tha puṃsi mahiṣe nikṛṣṭe punaranyavat \|

plakṣo jaṭīgardabhāṇḍadvīpabhitkuñjarāśane \|\| 17 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 18 \[unit 2229\]

pakṣo māsārddhake pārṣṇigrahe sādhyavirodhayoḥ \|

keśādeḥ parato vṛnde bale sakhisahāyayoḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 19 \[unit 2230\]

cullīrandhre patattre ca vāje kuñjarapārśvayoḥ \|

prekṣā nṛtyekṣaṇe buddhau praiṣaḥ kleśe ca mardane \|\| 19 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 20 \[unit 2231\]

unmāde preṣaṇe pauṣo māse tatpūrṇimātithau \|

striyāṃ bhikṣā bhṛtau yāñcāsevābhikṣitavastuṣu \|\| 20 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 21 \[unit 2232\]

māṣo vrīhyantare mūrkhe mānatvagdoṣabhedayoḥ \|

miṣaṃ vyāje sparddhane nā meṣo rāśyantare huḍe \|\| 21 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 22 \[unit 2233\]

bhaiṣajyabhidi mokṣastu muktipāṭalimocane \|

yakṣo guhyakamātre ca guhyakādhīśvare'pi ca \|\| 22 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 23 \[unit 2234\]

rakṣā jaturakṣaṇayoḥ rūkṣastvapremṇyacikkaṇe \|

lakṣā na puṃsi saṅkhyāyāṃ klībaṃ vyājaśaravyayoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 24 \[unit 2235\]

varṣo'strī bhāratādau ca jambūdvīpābdavṛṣṭiṣu \|

prāvṛṭkāle striyāṃ bhūmni viṭ strī vyāpanaviṣṭhayoḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 25 \[unit 2236\]

viṣaṃ jale'tiviṣāyāṃ striyāṃ kṣveḍe tu na striyām \|

vṛṣo dharme valīvarde śṛṅgyāṃ puṃrāśibhedayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 26 \[unit 2237\]

śreṣṭhe syāduttarasthaśca vāsāmūṣikaśukrale \|

tathā vāstusthānabhede pumānayaṃ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 27 \[unit 2238\]

vṛṣā mūṣikaparṇyāñca yatīnāmāsane vṛṣī \|

vyoṣaṃ syāt trikaṭudravye karibhede pumānayam \|\| 27 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / 28 \[unit 2239\]

śuṣiḥ śoṣe bile strī syāt śeṣaḥ saṅkarṣaṇe vadhe \|

anante nā prasāde na svanirmālyārpaṇe striyām \|\| 28 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣadvikam / fragment 2240

upayuktetare na strī śoṣo yakṣmaṇi śoṣaṇe \|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 29 \[unit 2241\]

ṣatrikam

adhyakṣo'dhikṛte puṃsi pratyakṣe tvabhidheyavat \|\| 29 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 30 \[unit 2242\]

abhiṣuḥ pragrahe raśmau nākarṣo dyūta indriye \|

pāśake śāriphalake kodaṇḍābhyāsavastuni \|\| 30 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 31 \[unit 2243\]

ākarṣaṇe'pi puṃsi syādāmiṣaṃ punnapuṃsakam \|

bhogyavastuni sambhoge'pyutkoce palale'pi ca \|\| 31 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 32 \[unit 2244\]

uṣṇīṣantu śiroveṣṭe kirīṭe lakṣaṇāntare \|

utprekṣā'napadhāne'pi kāvyālaṅkaraṇāntare \|\| 32 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 33 \[unit 2245\]

kalmaṣaṃ kilviṣe klībaṃ puṃsi syān narakāntare \|

kalmāṣo yātudhāne ca kṛṣṇapāṇḍarakṛṣṇayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 34 \[unit 2246\]

kaluṣaṃ tvāvile pāpe kilviṣaṃ pāparogayoḥ \|

aparādhe'pi kulmāṣaṃ kāñjike yāvake pumān \|\| 34 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 35 \[unit 2247\]

gaṇḍūṣo mukhapūrttībhapuṣkaraprasṛtonmite \|

gavākṣī śakravāruṇyāṃ gavākṣo jālake kapau \|\| 35 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 36 \[unit 2248\]

gorakṣo nāgaraṅge syād gavāñca parirakṣake \|

jigīṣā jetumicchāyāṃ vyavasāyaprakarṣayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 37 \[unit 2249\]

tarīṣaḥ śobhanākāre bhele'bdhivyavasāyayoḥ \|

tarīṣī śakrakanyāyāṃ puṃsi svarge mahodadhau \|\| 37 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 38 \[unit 2250\]

tārīṣī cendrakanyāyāṃ nā svargāmbudhikāñcane \|

nahuṣo nāgabhede syāt somavaṃśanṛpe'pi ca \|\| 38 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 39 \[unit 2251\]

nikaṣaḥ śāṇaphalake nikaṣā yātumātari \|

nimeṣanimiṣau kālaprabhede'kṣinimīlane \|\| 39 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 40 \[unit 2252\]

paruṣaṃ karbure rūkṣe niṣṭhuroktau ca vācyavat \|

pradoṣaḥ samaye doṣe pratyuṣo'harmukhe vasau \|\| 40 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 41 \[unit 2253\]

pīyūṣaṃ saptadivasāvadhikṣīre tathā'mṛte \|

puruṣaḥ pūruṣe sāṅkhyajñe ca punnāgapādape \|\| 41 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 42 \[unit 2254\]

pauruṣaṃ puruṣasya syād bhāve karmaṇi tejasi \|

ūrdhvavistṛtadoḥpāṇinṛmāne tvabhidheyavat \|\| 42 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 43 \[unit 2255\]

mahiṣī kṛtābhiṣekāsairibhyoroṣadhībhidi \|

māriṣaḥ śākabhidyāryye nāṭyoktau puṃsi yoṣiti \|\| 43 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 44 \[unit 2256\]

dakṣāmbāyāṃ mṛgākṣī tu viśālāmṛganetrayoḥ \|

raktākṣaḥ kāsare krūre pārāvatacakorayoḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 45 \[unit 2257\]

rauhiṣaṃ kattṛṇe klībaṃ puṃsi syād hariṇāntare \|

viśleṣo vidhure'yoge śuśrūṣā syādupāsane \|\| 45 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 46 \[unit 2258\]

kathane śrotumicchāyāṃ śailūṣo naṭabilvayoḥ \|

saṃharṣastu pramode'pi sparddhāyāñca prabhañjane \|\| 46 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣatrikam / 47 \[unit 2259\]

saṅgharṣaḥ syāt pumān ghṛṣṭau sparddhāyāmapi dṛśyate \|

samīkṣā tu striyāṃ tattve buddhāvapi nibhālane \|\| 47 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 2260\]

ṣacatuṣkam

anukarṣo rathādhaḥsthadāruṇyapyanukarṣaṇe \|

ambarīṣaṃ raṇe bhrāṣṭre klībaṃ puṃsi nṛpāntare \|\| 48 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 49 \[unit 2261\]

narakasya prabhede ca kiśore bhāskare'pi ca \|

āmrātake'nutāpe cānimeṣo matsyadevayoḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 50 \[unit 2262\]

anutarṣaḥ surāpānapātre tṛṣṇābhilāṣayoḥ \|

ahidviḍ garuḍe śakre mayūre nakule pumān \|\| 50 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 51 \[unit 2263\]

alambuṣaḥ prahaste ca chardane rākṣāsāntare \|

alambuṣā tu muṇḍīryyāṃ svargavārāṅganāntare \|\| 51 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 2264\]

atha kimpuruṣo lokabhedakinnarayoḥ pumān \|

devavṛkṣaḥ saptaparṇīmandārādiṣu guggulī \|\| 52 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 2265\]

nandighoṣo'rjunarathe ghoṣe bandijanasya ca \|

pariveṣastu puṃliṅgaḥ paridhau pariveṣaṇe \|\| 53 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 2266\]

parighoṣo nināde syādavācye jaladadhvanau \|

palaṅkaṣā gokṣurake rāsnāguggulukiṃśuke \|\| 54 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 2267\]

muṇḍīrīlākṣayośca strī rākṣase tu palaṅkaṣaḥ \|

vīravṛkṣo bhallātakakakubhadrumayoḥ pumān \|\| 55 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 2268\]

bhūtavṛkṣastu śākheṭadrume śyonākapādape \|

mahāghoṣā tu karkaṭaśṛṅgyā puṃsyatighoṣaṇe \|\| 56 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 2269\]

klībaṃ haṭṭe rājavṛkṣaḥ suvarṇākapiyālayoḥ \|

vātarūṣastu vātūlotkacayoḥ śakrakārmuke \|\| 57 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 2270\]

(1)viśālākṣo hare tārkṣye nā sunetre'bhidheyavat \|

sakaṭākṣo varākṣe nā kaṭākṣasahite triṣu \|\| 58 \|\|

## ṣāntavargaḥ / ṣacatuṣkam / fragment 2271

iti ṣāntavargaḥ \|

(1) viśālākṣaṃ-kha. \|

## sāntavargaḥ / saikam

## sāntavargaḥ / saikam / 1 \[unit 2272\]

saikam

sa jīvecche pañcavaktre bhṛgau bhasi niśāpatau \|

śaktau tu sā striyāṃ sustu prasave garbhamocane \|\| 1 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 2 \[unit 2273\]

sadvikam

kaṃso'strī taijasadravye kāṃsye māne'sure tu nā \|

kāsūrvikalavāci syāt tathā śaktyāyudhe striyām \|\| 2 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 3 \[unit 2274\]

gutsaḥ syāt stabake stambe hārabhidgranthiparṇayoḥ \|

goso boloṣasoścāsa ikṣupakṣibhidoḥ pumān \|\| 3 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 4 \[unit 2275\]

trāso bhaye maṇerdoṣe dāsī bāṇābhujiṣyayoḥ \|

dāso bhṛtye ca śūdre ca jñātātmani ca dhīvare \|\| 4 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 5 \[unit 2276\]

nāsā tu nāsikāyāñca dvārorddhvadāruṇi striyām \|

prasūraśvājananyośca kandalīvīrudhoḥ striyām \|\| 5 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 6 \[unit 2277\]

vasurnā devabhedāgnibhāyoktrabakarājasu \|

klībe vṛddhyauṣadhe śyāmarairatne madhure triṣu \|\| 6 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 7 \[unit 2278\]

bhāsaḥ puṃsi prabhāyāṃ syād viśeṣe vihagasya ca \|

bhās prabhāve mayūkhe strī bhāścandramāsayoḥ pumān \|\| 7 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 8 \[unit 2279\]

māṃsaṃ syādāmiṣe klībaṃ kakkolījaṭayoḥ striyām \|

misiḥ strī madhurāmāṃsyoḥ śatapuṣpājamodayoḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 9 \[unit 2280\]

mṛtsā kākṣyāṃ śreṣṭhamṛdi raso gandharase jale \|

śṛṅgārādau viṣe vīryye tiktādau dravarāgayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 10 \[unit 2281\]

dehadhātuprabhede ca pāradasvādayoḥ pumān \|

striyāntu rasanāpāṭhāśallakīkaṅgubhūmiṣu \|\| 10 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 11 \[unit 2282\]

rāsaḥ kolāhale dhvāne bhāṣāśṛṅkhalake'pi ca \|

krīḍābhede ca gopānāṃ vatsaḥ putrādivarṣayoḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 12 \[unit 2283\]

tarṇake norasi klībaṃ vyāso nā vistṛtau munau \|

śaṃsā vacasi vāñchāyāṃ haṃsaḥ syān mānasaukasi \|\| 12 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / 13 \[unit 2284\]

nirlobhanṛpaviṣṇvarke paramātmani matsare \|

yogibhede mantrabhede śarīramarudantare \|\| 13 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sadvikam / fragment 2285

turaṅgamaprabhede'pi hiṃsā cauryyādighātayoḥ \|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 14 \[unit 2286\]

satrikam

alasā haṃsapadyāṃ nā pādarogadrubhedayoḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 15 \[unit 2287\]

kriyāmande triṣvathārcirmayūkhaśikhayorna nā \|

adas parasminnatra syādabhyāso'bhyasane'ntike \|\| 15 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 16 \[unit 2288\]

āgo'parādhe pāpe syādāśvāsaḥ puṃsi nirvṛtau \|

ākhyāyikāparicchede cāśīrdante marudbhujām \|\| 16 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 17 \[unit 2289\]

hitasyāśaṃsane strī syādiṣvāsaḥ kārmuke pumān \|

triṣu syāt kṣepake ceṣorucchvāsaḥ prāṇane'pi ca \|\| 17 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 18 \[unit 2290\]

ākhyāyikāparicchede'pyāśvāse'pi pumānayam \|

uttaṃsaḥ karṇapūre'pi śikhare'pi ca na striyām \|\| 18 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 19 \[unit 2291\]

uṣas pratyūṣasi klībaṃ pitṛprasvāñca yoṣiti \|

uras vakṣasi ca śreṣṭhe'pyenaḥ pāpāparādhayoḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 20 \[unit 2292\]

ojas dīptāvavaṣṭambhe prakāśabalayorapi \|

okas āśrayamātre ca mandire ca napuṃsakam \|\| 20 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 21 \[unit 2293\]

kīkasaḥ kṛmijātau syāt puṃsi kulye napuṃsakam \|

camaso yajñapātrasya bhede'strī piṣṭake striyām \|\| 21 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 22 \[unit 2294\]

chandas padye ca vede ca svairācārābhilāṣayoḥ \|

jyāyān vṛddhe'tipraśaste jyotiragnau divākare \|\| 22 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 23 \[unit 2295\]

pumān napuṃsakaṃ dṛṣṭau syān nakṣatraprakāśayoḥ \|

taro bale ca vege ca tapo lokāntare'pi ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 24 \[unit 2296\]

cāndrāyaṇādau dharme ca pumān śiśiramāghayoḥ \|

tamo dhvānte guṇe śoke klībaṃ vā nā vidhuntude \|\| 24 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 25 \[unit 2297\]

tāmasī niśi durgāyāṃ tāmasī bhujage khale \|

tejo dīptau prabhāve ca syāt parākramaretasoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 26 \[unit 2298\]

dhanuḥ piyāle nā na strī rāśibhede śarāsane \|

dhanurddhare triṣu nabhaḥ klībaṃ pumān ghane \|\| 26 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 27 \[unit 2299\]

ghrāṇaśrāvaṇavarṣāsu bisatantau patadgrahe \|

panasaḥ kaṇṭakīphale kaṇṭake vānarāntare \|\| 27 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 28 \[unit 2300\]

striyāṃ rogaprabhede syāt payaḥ syāt kṣīranīrayoḥ \|

pāyasastu klībapuṃsoḥ śrīvāsaparamānnayoḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 29 \[unit 2301\]

pukkasī kalikānīlyoḥ pukkasaḥ śvapace'dhame \|

bībhatso nā'rjjune krūraghṛṇātmavikṛte triṣu \|\| 29 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 30 \[unit 2302\]

bhūyān triṣu bahutare punararthe tvado'vyayam \|

manas citte manīṣāyāṃ maha utsavatejasoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 31 \[unit 2303\]

mānasaṃ sarasi svānte rabhaso vegaharṣayoḥ \|

rahas tattve rate guhye rajas klībaṃ guṇāntare \|\| 31 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 32 \[unit 2304\]

ārttave ca parāge ca reṇumātre ca dṛśyate \|

rākṣaso yātudhāne syāccaṇḍayāṃ rākṣasī smṛtā \|\| 32 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 33 \[unit 2305\]

repāḥ syādadhame krūre kṛpaṇe'pyabhidheyavat \|

retas śukre pārade ca rodaśca rodasīti ca \|\| 33 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 34 \[unit 2306\]

divi bhūmau pṛthak ca syāt sahyoktyāpyetayostathā \|

lālasautsukyatṛṣṇātirekayācñāsu ca dvayoḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 35 \[unit 2307\]

vayas pakṣiṇi bālyādau yauvane ca napuṃsakam \|

varcco napuṃsakaṃ rūpe viṣṭhāyāmapi tejasi \|\| 35 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 36 \[unit 2308\]

puṃsi candrasya tanaye varhis puṃsi hutāśane \|

na strī kuśe vapus klībaṃ tanau śastākṛtāvapi \|\| 36 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 37 \[unit 2309\]

vataṃsastu pumān karṇapūraśekharayorapi \|

vāhaso jalaniryāne śayālau suniṣaṇṇake \|\| 37 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 38 \[unit 2310\]

vāyaso'guruvṛkṣe ca śrīvāsadhvāṅkṣayoḥ pumān \|

kākoḍumbarikāyāñca kākamācyāñca vāyasī \|\| 38 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 39 \[unit 2311\]

vidvānātmavidi prājñe paṇḍite cābhidheyavat \|

vilāso hārabhede syāllīlāyāmapi puṃsyayam \|\| 39 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 40 \[unit 2312\]

vītaṃso bandhanopāye mṛgāṇāṃ pakṣiṇāmapi \|

teṣāmapi ca viśvāsahetoḥ prāvaraṇe'pi ca \|\| 40 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 41 \[unit 2313\]

vedhāḥ puṃsi hṛṣīkeśe budhe ca parameṣṭhini \|

śiras pradhāne senāgre śikhare mastake'pi ca \|\| 41 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 42 \[unit 2314\]

śrīvāso vṛkadhūpe syāt paṅkaje madhusūdane \|

śreyo muktau śubhe gharme'tipraśaste ca vācyavat \|\| 42 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 43 \[unit 2315\]

śreyasī karipippalyāmabhayāpāṭhayorapi \|

saho bale jyotiṣi ca puṃsi hemantamārgayoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 44 \[unit 2316\]

sarasī ville kāsāre saro nīrataḍāgayoḥ \|

syāt samāsastu puṃliṅgaḥ saṅkṣepe ca samarthane \|\| 44 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 45 \[unit 2317\]

sārasaḥ pakṣibhedendvoḥ klībantu sarasīruhe \|

sāhasantu balātkārakṛtakāryye dame'pi ca \|\| 45 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / 46 \[unit 2318\]

surasantu triṣu svādau parṇāse tu napuṃsakam \|

strī rāsnānāgamātrośca sroto'mbuvega indriye \|\| 46 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / satrikam / fragment 2319

havirhotavyamātre ca sarpiṣyapi napuṃsakam \|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 47 \[unit 2320\]

sacatuṣkam

adhivāso nivāse syāt saṃskāre dhūpanādibhiḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 48 \[unit 2321\]

avadhvaṃsaḥ parityāge nindane'pyavacūrṇane \|

avataṃso na striyāṃ syāt karṇapūre ca śekhare \|\| 48 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 49 \[unit 2322\]

agaukāḥ puṃsi śarabhe pakṣipañcāsyayorapi \|

udarccirutprabhe vācyaliṅgaḥ puṃsi hutāśane \|\| 49 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 50 \[unit 2323\]

kalahaṃsastu kādambe rājahaṃse nṛpottame \|

kanīyānatiyūni syādatyalpānujayostriṣu \|\| 50 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 51 \[unit 2324\]

kumbhīnasaḥ krūrasarpe striyāṃ lavaṇamātari \|

bhaved ghanarasaḥ sāndraniryyāse moraṭe'mbuni \|\| 51 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 52 \[unit 2325\]

karpūre pīluparṇyāñca samyak siddharase'pi ca \|

candrahāso daśagrīvakarabāle'simātrake \|\| 52 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 53 \[unit 2326\]

jaṭāyus puṃsi sampāteḥ kanīyasi ca guggulau \|

atha tāmarasaṃ padme tāmrakāñcanayorapi \|\| 53 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 54 \[unit 2327\]

trisrotā jahnukanayāyāṃ srotasvatyantare'pi ca \|

divokāśca divaukāśca puṃsi deve ca cātake \|\| 54 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 55 \[unit 2328\]

dīrghāyuḥ śālmalīvṛkṣe vāyase jīvakadrume \|

mārkaṇḍeye ca puṃsi syāccirajīvini vācyavat \|\| 55 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 56 \[unit 2329\]

nagaukāḥ puṃsi śarabhe pakṣipañcāsyayorapi \|

niḥśreyasantu kalyāṇamokṣayoḥ śaṅkare pumān \|\| 56 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 57 \[unit 2330\]

nīlāñjasā'psarobhede saridbhede ca vidyuti \|

pracetāḥ pāśini munau nā prahṛṣṭahṛdi triṣu \|\| 57 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 58 \[unit 2331\]

punarvasurnā munibhidyaje dvitve tu bhāntare \|

paurṇamāsaḥ pumān yajñabhede strī pūrṇimātithau \|\| 58 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 59 \[unit 2332\]

mahārasaḥ syāt kharjure koṣakārakaśeruṇoḥ \|

malīmasastu maline puṣpakāśīśalohayoḥ \|\| 59 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 60 \[unit 2333\]

bhavenmadhurasā drākṣāmūrvikādugdhikāsu ca \|

yamasvasā tu durgāyāṃ yamunāyāmapi striyām \|\| 60 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 61 \[unit 2334\]

yavīyānanuje'pi syādatiyūni ca vācyavat \|

rāserasastu goṣṭyāṃ syād rāsaśṛṅgārayorapi \|\| 61 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 62 \[unit 2335\]

rasasiddhau rasāvāse ṣaṣṭhījāgarake'pi ca \|

rājahaṃsastu kādambe kalahaṃse nṛpottame \|\| 62 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 63 \[unit 2336\]

varīyān yogabhicchreṣṭhavariṣṭheṣvatiyūni ca \|

vihāyāḥ śakunau puṃsi gagane punnapuṃsakam \|\| 63 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 64 \[unit 2337\]

vibhāvasuḥ pumān sūryye hārabhede ca pāvake \|

śvaḥśreyasantu kalyāṇe paramātmani śarmmaṇi \|\| 64 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 65 \[unit 2338\]

atha sarvaraso vādyabhāṇḍabhede ca dhūnake \|

saptārcciḥ pāvake puṃsi krūracakṣuṣi tu triṣu \|\| 65 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 66 \[unit 2339\]

sādhīyānatibāḍhe syādatisādhau tu vācyavat \|

siddharaso rase puṃsi dhātuprabhṛtiṣu triṣu \|\| 66 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sacatuṣkam / 67 \[unit 2340\]

sumanāḥ puṣpamālatyoḥ striyāṃ nā dhīradevayoḥ \|

sumedhāstu striyāṃ jyotiṣmatyāṃ triṣu subuddhini \|\| 67 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam

## sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam / 68 \[unit 2341\]

divyacakṣuḥ sugandhasya bhede nā'ndhe sulocane \|

syānnabhaścamasaścandre citrāpūpendrajālayoḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / sapañcakam / fragment 2342

hiṅguniryyāsa ityeṣa nimbe hiṅagurase'pi ca \|

## sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam

## sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam / 69 \[unit 2343\]

saṣaṭkam

hiraṇyaretāḥ puṃsi syād divākarahavirbhujoḥ \|\| 69 \|\|

## sāntavargaḥ / saṣaṭkam / fragment 2344

iti sāntavargaḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / haikam

## hāntavargaḥ / haikam / 1 \[unit 2345\]

haikam

haḥ śive salile śūnye dhāraṇe maṅgale'pi ca \|

gagane nakulīśe ca rakte nāke ca varṇyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 2 \[unit 2346\]

hadvikam

ahirvṛtrāsure sarpe puṃsīhodyamavāñchayoḥ \|

kuhūḥ strī kokilālāpanaṣṭhendukaladarśayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 3 \[unit 2347\]

graho'nugrahanirbandhagrahaṇeṣu raṇodyame \|

sūryyādau pūtanādau ca saiṃhikeyaparāgayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 4 \[unit 2348\]

grāho grahe'vahāre ca guhaḥ ṣaṇmāture guhā \|

siṃhapucchyāṃ ca gartte ca parvatādeśca gahvare \|\| 4 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 5 \[unit 2349\]

gṛhaṃ gṛhāśca puṃbhūmni kalatre'pi ca sadmani \|

nāhastu bandhane kūṭe proho gajāṅghriparvaṇoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 6 \[unit 2350\]

vācyavannipuṇe tarke barhaṃ picche dale'striyām \|

bahu syāt tryādisaṅkhyāsu vipule'pyabhidheyavat \|\| 6 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 7 \[unit 2351\]

mahī nadyantare bhūmau maha utsavatejasoḥ \|

atha meāho nṛliṅgaḥ syādavidyāyāñca murcchane \|\| 7 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 8 \[unit 2352\]

loho'strī śastrake lauhe joṅgake sarvataijase \|

vahaḥ syād vṛṣabhaskandhe vāhe gandhavahe'pi ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 9 \[unit 2353\]

vāho bhuje pumān mānabhedāśvavṛṣavāyuṣu \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 10 \[unit 2354\]

vyūhaḥ syād balavinyāse nirmāṇe vṛndatarkayoḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 11 \[unit 2355\]

daṇḍotpalāmudgaparṇīkumārīpṛthivīṣu ca \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hadvikam / 12 \[unit 2356\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 13 \[unit 2357\]

hatrikam

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 14 \[unit 2358\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 15 \[unit 2359\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 16 \[unit 2360\]

jāyamānaviṣāṇāgramahiṣīśāvake'pi ca \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 17 \[unit 2361\]

dātyūhastu pumān kālakaṇṭacātakapakṣiṇoḥ \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 18 \[unit 2362\]

pragrāhastu tūlāsūtre vṛṣādipragrahe'pi ca \|

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 21 \[unit 2365\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 22 \[unit 2366\]

## hāntavargaḥ / hatrikam / 23 \[unit 2367\]

praśne sambhāvanāyāñca kāmam cā'numatau smṛtam \|\| 58 \|\|

prakāme cāpyasūyāyāṃ tathā'nugamane'pi ca \|

## avyayāni / matrikam / 60 \[unit 2465\]

nūnantu niścite tarke smaraṇe vākyapūraṇe \|

param niyoge kṣepe ca prādhvam narmānukūlayoḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

radvikam

uryyūrī cāpyubhau śabdau vistāre'ṅgīkṛtāvapi \|

durniṣedhe ca kaṣṭe ca nirnirṇayaniṣedhayoḥ \|\| 66 \|\|

## avyayāni / radvikam / 67 \[unit 2477\]

itthambhūtākhyānabhāgavīpsāliṅganalakṣaṇe \|\| 67 \|\|

## avyayāni / radvikam / 68 \[unit 2478\]

doṣākhyāne nirasane pūjāvyāptyośca bhūṣaṇe \|

parā vimokṣaprādhānyaprātilomyeṣu dharṣaṇe \|\| 68 \|\|

## avyayāni / radvikam / 69 \[unit 2479\]

ratrikam

## avyayāni / ratrikam / 71 \[unit 2482\]

uraryūruryyūrarī ca vistāre'ṅgākṛtau trayam \|

nakirniṣedhopamayoḥ prāduḥ prākāśya iṣyate \|\| 71 \|\|

## avyayāni / ratrikam / 72 \[unit 2483\]

sacatuṣkam

## avyayāni / haikam

## avyayāni / haikam / 85 \[unit 2503\]

haikam

ha syāt sambodhane pādapūraṇe ca vinigrahe \|

niyeāge ca kṣipāyāṃ syāt kutsāyāmapi dṛśyate \|\| 85 \|\|

## avyayāni / haikam / 86 \[unit 2504\]

hā viṣāde ca śoke ca kutsā duḥkhārthayorapi \|

hi pādapūraṇe hetau viśeṣe'pyavadhāraṇe \|\| 86 \|\|

## avyayāni / haikam / 87 \[unit 2505\]

praśne hetvapadeśe ca sambhramāsūyayorapi \|

hī duḥkhe hetāvākhyāto viṣāde vismaye'pi ca \|\| 87 \|\|

## avyayāni / haikam / 88 \[unit 2506\]

he sambodhana āhvāne'pyasūyādau ca dṛśyate \|

hai sambodhana āhvāne hohauśabdau tathaitayoḥ \|\| 88 \|\|

## avyayāni / hadvikam

## avyayāni / hadvikam / 89 \[unit 2507\]

hadvikam

aha praśaṃsākṣipayorniyoge ca vinigrahe \|

aho dhigarthe śāke ca karuṇārthaviṣādayoḥ \|\| 89 \|\|

## avyayāni / hadvikam / 90 \[unit 2508\]

sambodhane praśaṃsāyāṃ vismaye padapūraṇe \|

asūyāyāṃ vitarke syādāha kṣepaniyogayoḥ \|\| 90 \|\|

## avyayāni / hadvikam / 91 \[unit 2509\]

āho praśne vitarke ca saha sākalya iṣyate \|

vidyamāne ca sādṛśyayaugapadyasamṛddhiṣu \|\| 91 \|\|

## avyayāni / hadvikam / 92 \[unit 2510\]

sambandhe ca tathā hīhīśabdo vismayahāsyayoḥ \|

hehai sambodhane hūtau hohauśabdau tathaitayoḥ \|\| 92 \|\|

## avyayāni / hatrikam / 93 \[unit 2511\]

hatrikam

## avyayāni / hatrikam / fragment 2512

avyayānekārthavargaḥ samāptaḥ \|

## upasaṃhāraḥ

## upasaṃhāraḥ / closing

upasaṃhāraḥ \|

utpalinīśabdārṇavasaṃsārāvartanāmamālākhyān \|

bhāgurivararuciśāśvatavopālitarantidevaharakoṣān \|\|

umaraśubhāṅkahalāyudhagovardhanarabhasapālakṛtakoṣān \|

rudrāmaradattājayagaṅgādharadharaṇikoṣāṃśca \|\|

hārāvalyabhidhānaṃ trikāṇḍaśeṣaṃ ca ratnamālāṃ ca \|

api bahudoṣaṃ viśvaprakāśakeāṣaṃ ca suvicārya \|\|

vāgbhaṭamādhavavācaspatidharmavyāḍitārapālākhyān \|

api viśvarūpavikramādityanāmaliṅgāni suvicārya \|\|

kātyāyanavāmanacandragomiracitāni liṅgaśāstrāṇi \|

pāṇinipadānuśāsanapurāṇakāvyādikaṃ ca sunirūpya \|\|

ṣaṭśatagāthākoṣapraṇayanavikhyātakauśalenā'yam \|

medinikareṇa koṣaḥ prāṇakarasūnunā racitaḥ \|\|

iti śrīprāṇakarasūnuśrīmedinikaranirmito nānārthaśabdakośākhyo medinīkośaḥ samāptaḥ \|
